    -- ============================================================================
-- KINETIC OS v2.6 — BLINDAJE TOTAL DE SEGURIDAD, RLS, TRIGGERS Y STRIPE ACID
-- Archivo: sql/blindaje_total_seguridad_rls_triggers_stripe.sql
-- Destino: Supabase SQL Editor (Proyecto gcppxrlzxlgkuapktxvl)
-- Tablas Reales: public.perfiles, public.afiliados, public.servicios,
--   public.reservas, public.equipos_kinetic, public.equipo_miembros,
--   public.equipo_mensajes, public.mensajes_directos, public.admin_audit_logs,
--   public.fundadores_waitlist, public.stripe_processed_events,
--   public.qr_totp_replay_nonces
-- ============================================================================

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. FUNCIÓN DE ADMIN MAESTRO ENDURECIDA (EMAIL CONFIRMADO O ROL VERIFICADO)
-- ============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin()
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND (
        (
          u.email_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
            'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
            'administrador@kinetic.com',
            'kinetic@kinetic.com'
          )
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  );
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin() TO authenticated, anon;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. VECTOR 1: TRIGGER ANTI-ESCALADA Y ANTI-AUTO-INYECCIÓN EN public.perfiles
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.perfiles
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS email TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS telefono TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS rfc_fiscal TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS avatar_url TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS rol TEXT DEFAULT 'atleta',
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tipo_cuenta TEXT DEFAULT 'atleta',
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS nivel TEXT DEFAULT 'Acceso Base',
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_membresia NUMERIC DEFAULT 0 CHECK (kc_membresia >= 0),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_topup NUMERIC DEFAULT 0 CHECK (kc_topup >= 0),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS saldo_kc NUMERIC DEFAULT 0 CHECK (saldo_kc >= 0),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS xp_total INTEGER DEFAULT 0 CHECK (xp_total >= 0),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS racha_dias INTEGER DEFAULT 0 CHECK (racha_dias >= 0),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recompensas_reclamadas TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS favoritos_ids TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS radar_publico BOOLEAN DEFAULT true;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_perfiles_privilegios_y_boveda()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_is_trusted_rpc BOOLEAN := (current_setting('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', true) = 'true');
  v_is_backend_superuser BOOLEAN := (
    (SELECT auth.uid()) IS NULL
    AND current_user IN ('postgres', 'supabase_admin', 'service_role')
  ) OR (COALESCE((SELECT auth.role()), '') = 'service_role');
  v_is_master_admin BOOLEAN := COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false);
BEGIN
  NEW.kc_membresia := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(NEW.kc_membresia, 0));
  NEW.kc_topup := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(NEW.kc_topup, 0));
  NEW.saldo_kc := NEW.kc_membresia + NEW.kc_topup;

  IF v_is_trusted_rpc OR v_is_backend_superuser OR v_is_master_admin THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
    IF NEW.id IS DISTINCT FROM (SELECT auth.uid()) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Operacion denegada: solo puedes crear tu propio perfil.'
        USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    IF LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(NEW.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
       OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(NEW.tipo_cuenta, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador') THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Escalada de privilegios bloqueada en registro.'
        USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    IF NEW.kc_membresia > 0 OR NEW.kc_topup > 0 OR NEW.saldo_kc > 0 THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Auto-inyeccion de saldo bloqueada en registro.'
        USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;

    NEW.rol := CASE
      WHEN LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(NEW.rol, ''))) IN ('afiliado', 'club', 'especialista')
        THEN LOWER(TRIM(NEW.rol))
      ELSE 'atleta'
    END;
    NEW.tipo_cuenta := CASE
      WHEN LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(NEW.tipo_cuenta, ''))) IN ('afiliado', 'club', 'especialista', 'b2b')
        THEN LOWER(TRIM(NEW.tipo_cuenta))
      ELSE 'atleta'
    END;
    NEW.nivel := CASE
      WHEN NEW.rol IN ('afiliado', 'club', 'especialista') THEN 'Partner B2B'
      ELSE 'Acceso Base'
    END;
    NEW.xp_total := 0;
    NEW.racha_dias := 0;
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN
    IF NEW.id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.id
       OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(NEW.rol, ''))) IS DISTINCT FROM LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(OLD.rol, '')))
       OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(NEW.tipo_cuenta, ''))) IS DISTINCT FROM LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(OLD.tipo_cuenta, '')))
       OR COALESCE(NEW.nivel, '') IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(OLD.nivel, '')
       OR COALESCE(NEW.kc_membresia, 0) IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(OLD.kc_membresia, 0)
       OR COALESCE(NEW.kc_topup, 0) IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(OLD.kc_topup, 0)
       OR COALESCE(NEW.saldo_kc, 0) IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(OLD.saldo_kc, 0)
       OR COALESCE(NEW.xp_total, 0) IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(OLD.xp_total, 0)
       OR COALESCE(NEW.perdones_mensuales_usados, 0) IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(OLD.perdones_mensuales_usados, 0)
    THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Modificacion directa de rol, nivel o saldos bloqueada fuera de RPC autorizado.'
        USING ERRCODE = '42501';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_guard_perfiles_privilegios_y_boveda ON public.perfiles;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_guard_perfiles_privilegios_y_boveda
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.perfiles
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_perfiles_privilegios_y_boveda();

-- RPC autorizado para baja/anonimización voluntaria (Cumplimiento Apple Guideline 5.1.1(v))
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.anonimizar_y_cerrar_cuenta_propia()
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sesion requerida.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);

  UPDATE public.perfiles
  SET nombre = 'Usuario Desactivado',
      email = 'anonimo_' || SUBSTRING(v_uid::text FROM 1 FOR 8) || '@kineticapp.com.mx',
      telefono = NULL,
      rfc_fiscal = NULL,
      avatar_url = NULL,
      waiver_firmado = false,
      radar_publico = false,
      kc_membresia = 0,
      kc_topup = 0,
      saldo_kc = 0
  WHERE id = v_uid;

  RETURN true;
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.anonimizar_y_cerrar_cuenta_propia() TO authenticated;

-- 3. VECTOR 4: PROTECCIÓN PII / LFPDPPP EN public.perfiles Y public.afiliados
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.perfiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DO $$
DECLARE
  r_pol RECORD;
BEGIN
  FOR r_pol IN
    SELECT policyname FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'perfiles'
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS %I ON public.perfiles', r_pol.policyname);
  END LOOP;
END $$;

CREATE POLICY "perfiles_select_titular_o_admin"
ON public.perfiles FOR SELECT TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE POLICY "perfiles_insert_titular_o_admin"
ON public.perfiles FOR INSERT TO authenticated
WITH CHECK ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE POLICY "perfiles_update_titular_o_admin"
ON public.perfiles FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE POLICY "perfiles_delete_titular_o_admin"
ON public.perfiles FOR DELETE TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

-- Vista pública sin PII ni saldos para Leaderboard/Squads (respeta Modo Fantasma radar_publico)
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.perfiles_publicos
WITH (security_barrier = true)
AS
SELECT
  p.id,
  p.nombre,
  p.avatar_url,
  p.nivel,
  COALESCE(p.xp_total, 0) AS xp_total,
  COALESCE(p.racha_dias, 0) AS racha_dias,
  COALESCE(p.radar_publico, true) AS radar_publico
FROM public.perfiles p
WHERE COALESCE(p.radar_publico, true) = true
  AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, 'atleta'))) NOT IN ('admin', 'fundador');

REVOKE ALL ON public.perfiles_publicos FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT SELECT ON public.perfiles_publicos TO authenticated;

-- RPC para Recepción B2B: entrega ficha visual sin PII fiscal ni saldos ajenos al escanear QR
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.obtener_perfil_checkin_b2b(p_reserva_id UUID)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
  v_res RECORD;
  v_perf RECORD;
BEGIN
  SELECT id, usuario_id, negocio_nombre, monto_puerta
    INTO v_res
  FROM public.reservas
  WHERE id = p_reserva_id;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;

  IF v_caller IS DISTINCT FROM v_res.usuario_id
     AND NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false)
     AND NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
       WHERE a.propietario_id = v_caller
         AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(v_res.negocio_nombre, '')))
     ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado para consultar esta reserva.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT id, nombre, avatar_url, nivel,
         (COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) + COALESCE(kc_topup, 0)) >= COALESCE(v_res.monto_puerta, 0) AS cubre_puerta
    INTO v_perf
  FROM public.perfiles
  WHERE id = v_res.usuario_id;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('id', v_res.usuario_id, 'nombre', 'Atleta Kinetic', 'nivel', 'Acceso Base', 'cubre_puerta', true);
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'id', v_perf.id,
    'nombre', COALESCE(v_perf.nombre, 'Atleta Kinetic'),
    'avatar_url', v_perf.avatar_url,
    'nivel', COALESCE(v_perf.nivel, 'Acceso Base'),
    'cubre_puerta', COALESCE(v_perf.cubre_puerta, false)
  );
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.obtener_perfil_checkin_b2b(UUID) TO authenticated;

-- Blindaje de public.afiliados y ocultamiento de columnas fiscales a anon
ALTER TABLE public.afiliados ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DO $$
DECLARE
  r_pol RECORD;
  v_safe_cols TEXT[];
BEGIN
  FOR r_pol IN
    SELECT policyname FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'afiliados'
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS %I ON public.afiliados', r_pol.policyname);
  END LOOP;

  SELECT array_agg(quote_ident(column_name))
    INTO v_safe_cols
  FROM information_schema.columns
  WHERE table_schema = 'public'
    AND table_name = 'afiliados'
    AND column_name NOT IN (
      'rfc', 'regimen_fiscal', 'clabe_bancaria', 'csf_validada',
      'email_contacto', 'cambios_pendientes_b2b', 'audit_checklists', 'strikes_operativos'
    );

  IF v_safe_cols IS NOT NULL AND array_length(v_safe_cols, 1) > 0 THEN
    EXECUTE 'REVOKE SELECT ON public.afiliados FROM anon';
    EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT (%s) ON public.afiliados TO anon', array_to_string(v_safe_cols, ', '));
  END IF;
END $$;

CREATE POLICY "afiliados_select_radar"
ON public.afiliados FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated
USING (true);

CREATE POLICY "afiliados_insert_propietario_o_admin"
ON public.afiliados FOR INSERT TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE POLICY "afiliados_update_propietario_o_admin"
ON public.afiliados FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
USING (propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK (propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE POLICY "afiliados_delete_admin"
ON public.afiliados FOR DELETE TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

ON public.servicios FOR INSERT TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (
  (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
  OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
    WHERE a.propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(servicios.negocio_nombre, '')))
  )
);

CREATE POLICY "servicios_update_socio_o_admin"
ON public.servicios FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
USING (
  (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
  OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
    WHERE a.propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(servicios.negocio_nombre, '')))
  )
)
WITH CHECK (
  (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
  OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
    WHERE a.propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(servicios.negocio_nombre, '')))
  )
);

CREATE POLICY "servicios_delete_socio_o_admin"
ON public.servicios FOR DELETE TO authenticated
USING (
  (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
  OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
    WHERE a.propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(servicios.negocio_nombre, '')))
  )
);

-- 4. VECTOR 2: PRIVACIDAD ESTRICTA 1-A-1 Y CIERRE DE FUGA EN admin_audit_logs
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.admin_audit_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DELETE FROM public.admin_audit_logs
WHERE accion LIKE '__KINETIC_DM_V25__:%'
   OR accion LIKE '__KINETIC_SQUAD_MSG_V25__:%';

DO $$
DECLARE
  r_pol RECORD;
BEGIN
  FOR r_pol IN
    SELECT policyname FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'admin_audit_logs'
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS %I ON public.admin_audit_logs', r_pol.policyname);
  END LOOP;
END $$;

CREATE POLICY "admin_audit_logs_select_exclusivo_admin"
ON public.admin_audit_logs FOR SELECT TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE POLICY "admin_audit_logs_insert_exclusivo_admin"
ON public.admin_audit_logs FOR INSERT TO authenticated
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

ALTER TABLE public.mensajes_directos ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DO $$
DECLARE
  r_pol RECORD;
BEGIN
  FOR r_pol IN
    SELECT policyname FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'mensajes_directos'
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS %I ON public.mensajes_directos', r_pol.policyname);
  END LOOP;
END $$;

CREATE POLICY "Privacidad absoluta 1 a 1 lectura"
ON public.mensajes_directos FOR SELECT TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = sender_id OR (SELECT auth.uid()) = recipient_id);

CREATE POLICY "Privacidad absoluta 1 a 1 envio"
ON public.mensajes_directos FOR INSERT TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (
  (SELECT auth.uid()) = sender_id
  AND sender_id <> recipient_id
  AND LENGTH(TRIM(COALESCE(mensaje, ''))) BETWEEN 1 AND 2000
);

CREATE POLICY "Privacidad absoluta 1 a 1 lectura_update"
ON public.mensajes_directos FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = recipient_id)
WITH CHECK ((SELECT auth.uid()) = recipient_id);

CREATE POLICY "Privacidad absoluta 1 a 1 borrado"
ON public.mensajes_directos FOR DELETE TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = sender_id OR (SELECT auth.uid()) = recipient_id);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_mensajes_directos_inmutables()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.sender_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.sender_id
     OR NEW.recipient_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.recipient_id
     OR NEW.mensaje IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.mensaje THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'El contenido de un mensaje directo es inmutable.'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_guard_mensajes_directos_inmutables ON public.mensajes_directos;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_guard_mensajes_directos_inmutables
BEFORE UPDATE ON public.mensajes_directos
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_mensajes_directos_inmutables();

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. VECTOR 5: BÓVEDA, STRIPE IDEMPOTENTE ACID, ESCROW 40/60 Y QR TOTP 60s
-- ============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_mem NUMERIC;
  v_top NUMERIC;
  v_nivel TEXT;
BEGIN
  IF NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado: requiere privilegios de Admin Maestro Kinetic.'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);

  SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base')
    INTO v_mem, v_top, v_nivel
  FROM public.perfiles
  WHERE id = p_user_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Perfil % no encontrado.', p_user_id;
  END IF;

  IF LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')) = 'membresia' THEN
    v_mem := GREATEST(0, v_mem + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  ELSE
    v_top := GREATEST(0, v_top + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  END IF;

  IF p_nuevo_nivel IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel)) > 0 THEN
    v_nivel := TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel);
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.perfiles
  SET kc_membresia = v_mem,
      kc_topup = v_top,
      saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top,
      nivel = v_nivel
  WHERE id = p_user_id;

  INSERT INTO public.admin_audit_logs (accion, cantidad_kc)
  VALUES (
    format('ADMIN_VAULT_OP user=%s wallet=%s delta=%s', p_user_id, COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup'), COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0)),
    COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0)
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'kc_membresia', v_mem,
    'kc_topup', v_top,
    'saldo_kc', v_mem + v_top,
    'nivel', v_nivel
  );
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.stripe_processed_events (
  event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  user_id UUID REFERENCES public.perfiles(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  billetera_destino TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  monto_kc NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (monto_kc >= 0),
  nivel_asignado TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_stripe_processed_events_user_id
  ON public.stripe_processed_events (user_id);

ALTER TABLE public.stripe_processed_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Lectura auditoria stripe_processed_events Admin Maestro" ON public.stripe_processed_events;
CREATE POLICY "Lectura auditoria stripe_processed_events Admin Maestro"
ON public.stripe_processed_events FOR SELECT TO authenticated
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.procesar_webhook_stripe_kc(
  p_event_id TEXT,
  p_event_type TEXT,
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad_kc NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$ DECLARE v_mem NUMERIC; v_top NUMERIC; v_nivel TEXT; v_now TIMESTAMPTZ := NOW(); BEGIN IF p_event_id IS NULL OR LENGTH(TRIM(p_event_id)) < 4 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Identificador de evento Stripe invalido.'; END IF;    IF COALESCE(p_cantidad_kc, 0) <= 0 OR MOD(p_cantidad_kc, 10) <> 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Candado financiero Kinetic: p_cantidad_kc debe ser positivo y multiplo exacto de 10.'; END IF;    INSERT INTO public.stripe_processed_events ( event_id, event_type, user_id, billetera_destino, monto_kc, nivel_asignado, metadata, processed_at ) VALUES ( TRIM(p_event_id), COALESCE(p_event_type, 'payment_intent.succeeded'), p_user_id, LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')), p_cantidad_kc, p_nuevo_nivel, COALESCE(p_metadata, '{}'::jsonb), v_now ) ON CONFLICT (event_id) DO NOTHING;    IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'duplicate_ignored', 'event_id', TRIM(p_event_id)); END IF;    PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);    SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base') INTO v_mem, v_top, v_nivel FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = p_user_id FOR UPDATE;    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Usuario % no existe en public.perfiles.', p_user_id; END IF;    IF LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')) = 'membresia' THEN v_mem := v_mem + p_cantidad_kc; ELSE v_top := v_top + p_cantidad_kc; END IF;    IF p_nuevo_nivel IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel)) > 0 THEN v_nivel := TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel); END IF;    UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_membresia = v_mem, kc_topup = v_top, saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top, nivel = v_nivel, fecha_activacion_membresia = CASE WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')) = 'membresia' THEN v_now ELSE fecha_activacion_membresia END WHERE id = p_user_id;    RETURN jsonb_build_object( 'status', 'processed', 'event_id', TRIM(p_event_id), 'user_id', p_user_id, 'kc_membresia', v_mem, 'kc_topup', v_top, 'saldo_kc', v_mem + v_top, 'nivel', v_nivel ); END; $$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.procesar_webhook_stripe_kc(TEXT, TEXT, UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT, JSONB) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.procesar_webhook_stripe_kc(TEXT, TEXT, UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT, JSONB) TO service_role;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.procesar_reserva_escrow(
  p_usuario_id UUID,
  p_negocio_nombre TEXT,
  p_servicio_nombre TEXT,
  p_precio_kc NUMERIC,
  p_servicio_id UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$ DECLARE v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid()); v_precio_real NUMERIC; v_cupo_max INTEGER; v_ocupados INTEGER; v_escrow NUMERIC; v_puerta NUMERIC; v_mem NUMERIC; v_top NUMERIC; v_use_mem NUMERIC; v_use_top NUMERIC; v_reserva_id UUID; v_qr_token TEXT; BEGIN IF v_caller IS NULL OR (p_usuario_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_caller AND NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false)) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado para reservar a nombre de otro atleta.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;    IF p_servicio_id IS NOT NULL THEN SELECT COALESCE(precio_kc, 0), COALESCE(cupo_maximo, 30) INTO v_precio_real, v_cupo_max FROM public.servicios WHERE id = p_servicio_id FOR UPDATE;      IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'La experiencia seleccionada ya no existe.'; END IF;      SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_ocupados FROM public.reservas WHERE servicio_id = p_servicio_id AND LOWER(COALESCE(estado, 'activa')) IN ('activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada');      IF v_ocupados >= v_cupo_max THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'CUPO_AGOTADO: No quedan lugares disponibles en esta sesion.'; END IF; ELSE v_precio_real := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(p_precio_kc, 0)); END IF;    IF v_precio_real < 0 OR (v_precio_real > 0 AND MOD(v_precio_real, 10) <> 0) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Precio de servicio invalido (debe ser multiplo de 10 KC).'; END IF;    v_escrow := ROUND(v_precio_real * 0.40); v_puerta := GREATEST(0, v_precio_real - v_escrow);    PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);    SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) INTO v_mem, v_top FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = p_usuario_id FOR UPDATE;    IF (v_mem + v_top) < v_escrow THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SALDO_INSUFICIENTE: Tu boveda no cubre el 40%% de custodia (% KC).', v_escrow; END IF;    v_use_mem := LEAST(v_mem, v_escrow); v_use_top := v_escrow - v_use_mem;    UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_membresia = v_mem - v_use_mem, kc_topup = v_top - v_use_top, saldo_kc = (v_mem - v_use_mem) + (v_top - v_use_top) WHERE id = p_usuario_id;    v_qr_token := encode(gen_random_bytes(16), 'hex');    INSERT INTO public.reservas ( usuario_id, servicio_id, negocio_nombre, servicio, precio_kc, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup, estado, qr_token ) VALUES ( p_usuario_id, p_servicio_id, TRIM(COALESCE(p_negocio_nombre, '')), TRIM(COALESCE(p_servicio_nombre, '')), v_precio_real, v_escrow, v_puerta, v_use_mem, v_use_top, 'confirmada', v_qr_token ) RETURNING id INTO v_reserva_id;    RETURN jsonb_build_object( 'id', v_reserva_id, 'qr_token', v_qr_token, 'precio_kc', v_precio_real, 'monto_escrow', v_escrow, 'monto_puerta', v_puerta, 'escrow_membresia', v_use_mem, 'escrow_topup', v_use_top ); END; $$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.procesar_reserva_escrow(UUID, TEXT, TEXT, NUMERIC, UUID) TO authenticated;

ALTER TABLE public.reservas
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS qr_usado_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NULL;

ALTER TABLE public.reservas ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DO $$ DECLARE r_pol RECORD; BEGIN FOR r_pol IN SELECT policyname FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'reservas' LOOP EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY IF EXISTS %I ON public.reservas', r_pol.policyname); END LOOP; END $$;

CREATE POLICY "reservas_select_acotado"
ON public.reservas FOR SELECT TO authenticated
USING (
  usuario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
  OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
  OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
    WHERE a.propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(reservas.negocio_nombre, '')))
  )
);

CREATE POLICY "reservas_insert_titular_o_admin"
ON public.reservas FOR INSERT TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (usuario_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE POLICY "reservas_update_titular_socio_admin"
ON public.reservas FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
USING (
  usuario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
  OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
  OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
    WHERE a.propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(reservas.negocio_nombre, '')))
  )
)
WITH CHECK (
  usuario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
  OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
  OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a
    WHERE a.propietario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(reservas.negocio_nombre, '')))
  )
);

CREATE POLICY "reservas_delete_titular_o_admin"
ON public.reservas FOR DELETE TO authenticated
USING (usuario_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_reservas_anti_bypass()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$ DECLARE v_is_trusted_rpc BOOLEAN := (current_setting('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', true) = 'true'); v_srv_precio NUMERIC := 0; BEGIN IF v_is_trusted_rpc OR COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false) THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;    IF NEW.servicio_id IS NOT NULL THEN SELECT COALESCE(precio_kc, 0) INTO v_srv_precio FROM public.servicios WHERE id = NEW.servicio_id; END IF;    IF v_srv_precio > 0 OR COALESCE(NEW.precio_kc, 0) > 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Bypass de custodia bloqueado: toda reserva con costo debe procesarse via procesar_reserva_escrow.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;    NEW.precio_kc := 0; NEW.monto_escrow := 0; NEW.monto_puerta := 0; RETURN NEW; END; $$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_guard_reservas_anti_bypass ON public.reservas;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_guard_reservas_anti_bypass
BEFORE INSERT ON public.reservas
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_reservas_anti_bypass();

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.qr_totp_replay_nonces (
  reserva_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.reservas(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  totp_step BIGINT NOT NULL,
  qr_nonce TEXT NOT NULL,
  consumed_by UUID,
  consumed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (reserva_id, totp_step, qr_nonce)
);

ALTER TABLE public.qr_totp_replay_nonces ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.liquidar_checkin_qr_totp(
  p_reserva_id UUID,
  p_qr_token TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_totp_step BIGINT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_qr_nonce TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$ DECLARE v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid()); v_current_step BIGINT := FLOOR(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM NOW()) / 60); v_step BIGINT := COALESCE(p_totp_step, v_current_step); v_res RECORD; v_mem NUMERIC; v_top NUMERIC; v_use_mem NUMERIC; v_use_top NUMERIC; BEGIN IF p_totp_step IS NOT NULL AND ABS(v_current_step - p_totp_step) > 1 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'QR_EXPIRADO: El codigo optico de 60s ha caducado. Solicita el frame actual.'; END IF;    SELECT * INTO v_res FROM public.reservas WHERE id = p_reserva_id FOR UPDATE;    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Reserva no encontrada.'; END IF;    IF NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false) AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM public.afiliados a WHERE a.propietario_id = v_caller AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.nombre, ''))) = LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(v_res.negocio_nombre, ''))) ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Recepcion no autorizada: esta reserva pertenece a otra sede.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;    IF v_res.qr_usado_at IS NOT NULL OR LOWER(COALESCE(v_res.estado, '')) IN ('completada', 'usada', 'cancelada') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'QR_YA_UTILIZADO: Este pase ya fue liquidado o cancelado previamente.'; END IF;    IF p_qr_token IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(p_qr_token)) > 0 AND COALESCE(v_res.qr_token, '') <> '' AND TRIM(p_qr_token) <> TRIM(v_res.qr_token) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Token QR invalido para esta reserva.'; END IF;    INSERT INTO public.qr_totp_replay_nonces (reserva_id, totp_step, qr_nonce, consumed_by) VALUES (p_reserva_id, v_step, COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(p_qr_nonce), ''), v_step::text), v_caller);    PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);    IF COALESCE(v_res.monto_puerta, 0) > 0 THEN SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) INTO v_mem, v_top FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = v_res.usuario_id FOR UPDATE;      IF (v_mem + v_top) < v_res.monto_puerta THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SALDO_PUERTA_INSUFICIENTE: El atleta requiere % KC para liquidar el 60%% restante.', v_res.monto_puerta; END IF;      v_use_mem := LEAST(v_mem, v_res.monto_puerta); v_use_top := v_res.monto_puerta - v_use_mem;      UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_membresia = v_mem - v_use_mem, kc_topup = v_top - v_use_top, saldo_kc = (v_mem - v_use_mem) + (v_top - v_use_top) WHERE id = v_res.usuario_id; END IF;    UPDATE public.reservas SET estado = 'completada', monto_puerta = 0, qr_usado_at = NOW() WHERE id = p_reserva_id;    RETURN jsonb_build_object( 'status', 'checked_in', 'reserva_id', p_reserva_id, 'checked_in_at', NOW() ); END; $$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.liquidar_checkin_qr_totp(UUID, TEXT, BIGINT, TEXT) TO authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.liquidar_pase_escrow(p_reserva_id UUID)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$ BEGIN RETURN public.liquidar_checkin_qr_totp(p_reserva_id, NULL, NULL, NULL); END; $$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.liquidar_pase_escrow(UUID) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. RPCS AUTORIZADOS DE ATLETA (CANCELACIÓN 72H, COFRES/TROFEOS Y DOWNGRADE)
-- ============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cancelar_reserva_escrow_72h(p_reserva_id UUID)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
  v_res RECORD;
  v_perf RECORD;
  v_event_ts TIMESTAMPTZ;
  v_hours_until NUMERIC := 999;
  v_mins_created NUMERIC := 999;
  v_month_key TEXT := to_char(NOW(), 'YYYY-MM');
  v_used_forgive INTEGER := 0;
  v_max_forgive INTEGER := 0;
  v_should_refund BOOLEAN := false;
  v_use_forgive BOOLEAN := false;
  v_mem_refund NUMERIC := 0;
  v_top_refund NUMERIC := 0;
  v_total_refund NUMERIC := 0;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO v_res FROM public.reservas WHERE id = p_reserva_id FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'refunded', false, 'refundAmount', 0, 'newState', 'cancelada');
  END IF;

  IF v_caller IS NULL OR (v_res.usuario_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_caller AND NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado para cancelar esta reserva.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF LOWER(COALESCE(v_res.estado, '')) IN ('cancelada', 'completada', 'no_show') THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'refunded', false, 'refundAmount', 0, 'newState', v_res.estado);
  END IF;

  SELECT * INTO v_perf FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = v_res.usuario_id FOR UPDATE;

  BEGIN
    v_event_ts := COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(COALESCE(v_res.fecha_clase::text, '')), '')::timestamptz, v_res.created_at + INTERVAL '96 hours');
  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    v_event_ts := NOW() + INTERVAL '96 hours';
  END;

  v_hours_until := EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (v_event_ts - NOW())) / 3600.0;
  v_mins_created := EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - COALESCE(v_res.created_at, NOW()))) / 60.0;

  v_max_forgive := CASE
    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(v_perf.nivel, '')) LIKE '%plat%' OR LOWER(COALESCE(v_perf.nivel, '')) LIKE '%leyenda%' THEN 3
    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(v_perf.nivel, '')) LIKE '%gold%' OR LOWER(COALESCE(v_perf.nivel, '')) LIKE '%oro%' THEN 2
    WHEN LOWER(COALESCE(v_perf.nivel, '')) LIKE '%silver%' OR LOWER(COALESCE(v_perf.nivel, '')) LIKE '%plata%' THEN 1
    ELSE 0
  END;

  v_used_forgive := CASE
    WHEN COALESCE(v_perf.mes_perdones_control, '') = v_month_key THEN GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_perf.perdones_mensuales_usados, 0))
    ELSE 0
  END;

  v_mem_refund := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_res.escrow_membresia, 0));
  v_top_refund := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_res.escrow_topup, 0));
  v_total_refund := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_res.monto_escrow, v_mem_refund + v_top_refund));
  IF (v_mem_refund + v_top_refund) = 0 AND v_total_refund > 0 THEN
    v_mem_refund := v_total_refund;
  END IF;

  IF v_total_refund <= 0 OR COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false) OR v_mins_created <= 5 OR v_hours_until >= 72 THEN
    v_should_refund := true;
  ELSIF v_hours_until >= 4 AND v_hours_until < 72 AND v_used_forgive < v_max_forgive THEN
    v_should_refund := true;
    v_use_forgive := true;
  END IF;

  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);

  UPDATE public.reservas
  SET estado = 'cancelada',
      perdon_vip_aplicado = CASE WHEN v_use_forgive THEN true ELSE COALESCE(perdon_vip_aplicado, false) END,
      monto_escrow = CASE WHEN v_should_refund THEN 0 ELSE monto_escrow END
  WHERE id = p_reserva_id;

  IF v_should_refund THEN
    UPDATE public.perfiles
    SET kc_membresia = COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) + v_mem_refund,
        kc_topup = COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) + v_top_refund,
        saldo_kc = COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) + v_mem_refund + COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) + v_top_refund,
        perdones_mensuales_usados = CASE WHEN v_use_forgive THEN v_used_forgive + 1 ELSE v_used_forgive END,
        mes_perdones_control = v_month_key
    WHERE id = v_res.usuario_id;
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'ok', true,
    'refunded', v_should_refund,
    'refundAmount', CASE WHEN v_should_refund THEN v_total_refund ELSE 0 END,
    'usedVipForgiveness', v_use_forgive,
    'remainingForgiveness', GREATEST(0, v_max_forgive - (CASE WHEN v_use_forgive THEN v_used_forgive + 1 ELSE v_used_forgive END)),
    'newState', 'cancelada'
  );
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reclamar_recompensa_kineti(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_reto_id TEXT,
  p_kc_reward NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  p_xp_reward INTEGER DEFAULT 0
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
  v_claim_key TEXT;
  v_claimed TEXT[];
  v_safe_kc NUMERIC := 0;
  v_safe_xp INTEGER := 0;
  v_mem NUMERIC;
  v_top NUMERIC;
  v_xp INTEGER;
BEGIN
  IF v_caller IS NULL OR (p_user_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_caller AND NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado para reclamar recompensas ajenas.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  v_claim_key := LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p_reto_id, '')));
  IF v_claim_key = '' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Identificador de reto requerido.';
  END IF;

  IF v_claim_key = 'recompensa_diaria' THEN
    v_claim_key := 'recompensa_diaria_' || to_char(NOW(), 'YYYY-MM-DD');
    v_safe_kc := 0;
  ELSE
    v_safe_kc := FLOOR(LEAST(50, GREATEST(0, COALESCE(p_kc_reward, 0))) / 10.0) * 10;
  END IF;

  v_safe_xp := LEAST(300, GREATEST(0, COALESCE(p_xp_reward, 0)));

  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);

  SELECT COALESCE(recompensas_reclamadas, '{}'), COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(xp_total, 0)
    INTO v_claimed, v_mem, v_top, v_xp
  FROM public.perfiles
  WHERE id = p_user_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF v_claim_key = ANY(v_claimed) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'duplicate', true, 'kc_topup', v_top, 'xp_total', v_xp);
  END IF;

  v_claimed := array_append(v_claimed, v_claim_key);
  v_top := v_top + v_safe_kc;
  v_xp := v_xp + v_safe_xp;

  UPDATE public.perfiles
  SET kc_topup = v_top,
      saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top,
      xp_total = v_xp,
      recompensas_reclamadas = v_claimed
  WHERE id = p_user_id;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'duplicate', false, 'kc_topup', v_top, 'xp_total', v_xp);
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cambiar_nivel_downgrade_propio(p_nuevo_nivel TEXT)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
  v_cur_nivel TEXT;
  v_cur_w INTEGER;
  v_new_w INTEGER;
  v_clean TEXT := TRIM(COALESCE(p_nuevo_nivel, 'Acceso Base'));
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sesion requerida.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  SELECT COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base') INTO v_cur_nivel FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = v_uid FOR UPDATE;

  v_cur_w := CASE
    WHEN LOWER(v_cur_nivel) LIKE '%plat%' THEN 4
    WHEN LOWER(v_cur_nivel) LIKE '%gold%' OR LOWER(v_cur_nivel) LIKE '%oro%' THEN 3
    WHEN LOWER(v_cur_nivel) LIKE '%silver%' OR LOWER(v_cur_nivel) LIKE '%plata%' THEN 2
    WHEN LOWER(v_cur_nivel) LIKE '%bronz%' OR LOWER(v_cur_nivel) LIKE '%bronce%' THEN 1
    ELSE 0
  END;

  v_new_w := CASE
    WHEN LOWER(v_clean) = 'platinum' THEN 4
    WHEN LOWER(v_clean) = 'gold' THEN 3
    WHEN LOWER(v_clean) = 'silver' THEN 2
    WHEN LOWER(v_clean) = 'bronze' THEN 1
    WHEN LOWER(v_clean) = 'acceso base' THEN 0
    ELSE 99
  END;

  IF v_new_w >= v_cur_w THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Solo se permite bajar de rango sin cargo; los ascensos requieren checkout.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);
  UPDATE public.perfiles SET nivel = v_clean WHERE id = v_uid;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'nivel', v_clean);
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cancelar_reserva_escrow_72h(UUID) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.reclamar_recompensa_kineti(UUID, TEXT, NUMERIC, INTEGER) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cambiar_nivel_downgrade_propio(TEXT) TO authenticated;

NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';

    FILE: src/__tests__/adversarial/bovedaSecurity2FA.adversarial.test.ts

    // === src/__tests__/adversarial/bovedaSecurity2FA.adversarial.test.ts === //
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { getTopUpPricingForLevel } from '../../components/boveda/TopUpPackagesSection';
import {
  extractJwtSessionId,
  isValidTotpSixDigitCode,
  normalizeActiveSessionsList,
  parseUserAgentDeviceSummary,
  sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode,
  validateSatCfdi40Fields,
} from '../../services/kineticSecurityMfaService';
import { encodeUint8ArrayToBase64 } from '../../utils/kineticBinaryMediaHelper';

declare const describe: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;
declare const it: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;

const expectTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string): void => {
  if (!cond) throw new Error(msg);
};
const expectEq = <T>(actual: T, expected: T, msg: string): void => {
  if (actual !== expected) throw new Error(`${msg} (expected=${String(expected)}, actual=${String(actual)})`);
};

function toBase64Url(jsonObj: unknown): string {
  const raw = JSON.stringify(jsonObj);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) bytes[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i) & 0xff;
  return encodeUint8ArrayToBase64(bytes).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
}

describe('Bóveda v2.6.6 — Seguridad 2FA TOTP, Sesiones Activas, CFDI 4.0 & Top-Up (fast-check 500x)', () => {
  it('Propiedad 1: sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode e isValidTotpSixDigitCode rechazan basura e idempotencia 000000..999999', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 999_999 }), (num) => {
        const validCode = String(num).padStart(6, '0');
        expectEq(sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode(validCode), validCode, 'idempotent sanitize on valid 6-digit');
        expectEq(sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode(sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode(validCode)), validCode, 'double idempotence');
        expectTrue(isValidTotpSixDigitCode(validCode), 'valid 6-digit accepted');
      }),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.oneof(
          fc.string(),
          fc.constant('123 45'),
          fc.constant('🔥123456'),
          fc.constant('12345'),
          fc.constant('1234567'),
          fc.constant(null as unknown),
          fc.constant(undefined as unknown),
          fc.integer() as fc.Arbitrary<unknown>
        ),
        (raw) => {
          const sanitized = sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode(raw);
          expectTrue(/^\d{0,6}$/.test(sanitized), 'sanitized output only 0..6 digits');
          expectEq(sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode(sanitized), sanitized, 'sanitize idempotent on arbitrary input');
          const shouldBeValid = typeof raw === 'string' && /^\d{6}$/.test(raw);
          expectEq(isValidTotpSixDigitCode(raw), shouldBeValid, 'strict 6-digit validation');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 2: extractJwtSessionId y normalizeActiveSessionsList filtran poison keys, enmascaran IPv4 y ordenan isCurrent[0]', () => {
    const validUuidArb = fc.uuid();
    const ipv4Arb = fc
      .tuple(fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 254 }), fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 255 }), fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 255 }), fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 254 }))
      .map(([a, b, c, d]) => `${a}.${b}.${c}.${d}`);

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(fc.anything(), validUuidArb, (garbage, validSid) => {
        const resGarbage = extractJwtSessionId(typeof garbage === 'string' ? garbage : (garbage as any));
        expectTrue(resGarbage === null || /^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/.test(resGarbage), 'never throws on arbitrary JWT input');

        const token = `${toBase64Url({ alg: 'HS256' })}.${toBase64Url({ session_id: validSid.toUpperCase(), aal: 'aal2' })}.sig`;
        expectEq(extractJwtSessionId(token), validSid.toLowerCase(), 'extracts lowercase UUID session_id');
      }),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            session_id: validUuidArb,
            user_agent: fc.string({ maxLength: 160 }),
            ip: ipv4Arb,
            aal: fc.constantFrom('aal1', 'aal2', 'invalid'),
            updated_at: fc.integer({ min: 1_700_000_000_000, max: 1_850_000_000_000 }).map((ms) => new Date(ms).toISOString()),
          }),
          { minLength: 1, maxLength: 12 }
        ),
        ipv4Arb,
        validUuidArb,
        (rows, poisonIp, poisonUuid) => {
          const currentId = rows[rows.length - 1].session_id;
          const poisonedProto = JSON.parse(`{"__proto__":{"polluted":true},"session_id":"${poisonUuid}","ip":"${poisonIp}"}`);
          const poisonedCtor: Record<string, unknown> = { session_id: poisonUuid, ip: poisonIp };
          Object.defineProperty(poisonedCtor, 'constructor', { value: 'evil', enumerable: true });

          const mixedInput = [null, undefined, 'bad', ...rows, rows[0], poisonedProto, poisonedCtor];
          const normalized = normalizeActiveSessionsList(mixedInput, currentId, false);

          expectTrue(normalized.length >= 1, 'at least valid sessions preserved');
          expectTrue(normalized[0].isCurrent === true, 'current session always at index 0');
          expectEq(normalized[0].id, currentId.toLowerCase(), 'index 0 matches currentId');

          const seen = new Set<string>();
          const seenDevices = new Set<string>();
          for (const item of normalized) {
            expectTrue(!seen.has(item.id), 'deduplicated session_id');
            seen.add(item.id);
            const devKey = `${item.deviceLabel.toLowerCase()}|${item.ipMasked}`;
            expectTrue(!seenDevices.has(devKey), 'deduplicated same browser/device+ip session');
            seenDevices.add(devKey);
            expectTrue(!/aal1|aal2/i.test(item.aalBadge), 'clean label without raw AAL1/AAL2');
            expectTrue(!/\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+/.test(item.ipMasked), 'raw IPv4 never leaked');
            expectTrue(item.ipMasked.endsWith('.***.***'), 'IPv4 masked as a.b.***.***');
            expectTrue(item.deviceLabel.length < 48, 'clean device label <48 chars');
          }
          const uaCheck = parseUserAgentDeviceSummary('Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 17_0) Safari/605.1', true);
          expectTrue(uaCheck.label.length < 48 && uaCheck.icon === 'smartphone', 'UA summary bounded');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 3: validateSatCfdi40Fields acepta RFC 12/13, CP 5 y CLABE 18 válidos y rechaza corruptos', () => {
    const rfcLettersArb = fc.array(fc.constantFrom(...'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ&Ñ'.split('')), { minLength: 3, maxLength: 4 }).map((a) => a.join(''));
    const rfcDateArb = fc.array(fc.constantFrom(...'0123456789'.split('')), { minLength: 6, maxLength: 6 }).map((a) => a.join(''));
    const rfcHomoArb = fc.array(fc.constantFrom(...'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789'.split('')), { minLength: 3, maxLength: 3 }).map((a) => a.join(''));
    const cpArb = fc.array(fc.constantFrom(...'0123456789'.split('')), { minLength: 5, maxLength: 5 }).map((a) => a.join(''));
    const clabeArb = fc.array(fc.constantFrom(...'0123456789'.split('')), { minLength: 18, maxLength: 18 }).map((a) => a.join(''));

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(rfcLettersArb, rfcDateArb, rfcHomoArb, cpArb, clabeArb, fc.boolean(), (l, d, h, cp, clabe, isB2B) => {
        const validRfc = `${l}-${d} ${h}`.toLowerCase();
        const res = validateSatCfdi40Fields({ rfc: validRfc, cp, clabe, isB2B });
        expectTrue(res.ok, 'valid SAT CFDI 4.0 payload accepted');
        expectEq(res.cleanRfc, `${l}${d}${h}`, 'clean uppercase RFC');
        expectEq(res.cleanCp, cp, 'clean 5-digit CP');
        expectEq(res.cleanClabe, clabe, 'clean 18-digit CLABE');

        const badRfc = validateSatCfdi40Fields({ rfc: '123INVALID', cp, clabe, isB2B });
        expectTrue(!badRfc.ok && badRfc.errorCode === 'INVALID_RFC', 'corrupt RFC rejected');

        const badCp = validateSatCfdi40Fields({ rfc: `${l}${d}${h}`, cp: '1234', clabe, isB2B });
        expectTrue(!badCp.ok && badCp.errorCode === 'INVALID_CP', '4-digit CP rejected');

        const badClabeB2B = validateSatCfdi40Fields({ rfc: `${l}${d}${h}`, cp, clabe: '1234567890', isB2B: true });
        expectTrue(!badClabeB2B.ok && badClabeB2B.errorCode === 'INVALID_CLABE', 'short B2B CLABE rejected');
      }),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 4: getTopUpPricingForLevel preserva 0% recargo VIP 1:1 y +20% Acceso Base sin centavos ni negativos', () => {
    const vipLevelArb = fc.constantFrom('Bronze', 'Bronce', 'Silver', 'Plata', 'Gold', 'Oro', 'Platinum', 'Platino');
    const freeLevelArb = fc.constantFrom('Acceso Base', 'Base Access', 'Ninguno', '');

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(fc.double({ min: -5000, max: 50_000, noNaN: false }), vipLevelArb, freeLevelArb, (rawAmount, vipLvl, freeLvl) => {
        const vipRes = getTopUpPricingForLevel(rawAmount, vipLvl, 1500);
        expectTrue(vipRes.isVipMember, 'VIP recognized');
        expectEq(vipRes.surchargeMxn, 0, 'VIP 0% surcharge');
        expectEq(vipRes.totalMxn, vipRes.baseMxn, 'VIP 1:1 parity');
        expectTrue(Number.isInteger(vipRes.totalMxn) && vipRes.totalMxn >= 0, 'VIP integer non-negative');

        const freeRes = getTopUpPricingForLevel(rawAmount, freeLvl, 0);
        expectTrue(!freeRes.isVipMember, 'Free / Base Access recognized');
        expectEq(freeRes.surchargeMxn, Math.round(freeRes.baseMxn * 0.2), '+20% exact surcharge on Base Access');
        expectEq(freeRes.totalMxn, freeRes.baseMxn + freeRes.surchargeMxn, 'total = base + surcharge');
        expectTrue(Number.isInteger(freeRes.totalMxn) && Number.isInteger(freeRes.surchargeMxn) && freeRes.totalMxn >= 0, 'Free integer non-negative');
      }),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });
});

import fc from 'fast-check';
import {
  KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP,
  hasConfiguredOfficialWhatsApp,
} from '../../components/BovedaSupportAndReferralModals';
import { formatVenueCountLabel } from '../../services/bovedaB2BDataHelper';
import { localizeVipTierName } from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { deduplicateSavedAccountsMap, sanitizeSavedAccount } from '../../services/kineticAuthHelpers';
import { buildQrCancelCtaLabel } from '../../services/kineticCancellationPolicyHelper';
import {
  buildConciergePureReply,
  classifyConciergeSemanticIntent,
} from '../../services/kineticConciergeIntentEngine';
import {
  isExplicitKineticSignOut,
  markExplicitKineticSignOut,
  resetExplicitKineticSignOut,
  subscribeExplicitKineticSignOut,
} from '../../services/kineticSessionPersistence';
import { formatSatRegimenLabel } from '../../styles/billingCfdi.styles';

const g = globalThis as Record<string, unknown>;
const describeSuite = typeof g.describe === 'function'
  ? (g.describe as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
  : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();
const itCase = typeof g.it === 'function'
  ? (g.it as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
  : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();

const assertTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string) => {
  if (!cond) throw new Error(msg);
};

describeSuite('KINETIC OS v2.7.5 Sprint 2 — 34 Hallazgos + D6 CEO (fast-check 500x)', () => {
  itCase(
    'Propiedad 1: deduplicateSavedAccountsMap fusiona case-insensitive sin secretos y subscribeExplicitKineticSignOut notifica en 0ms',
    () => {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.emailAddress(),
          fc.string({ minLength: 4, maxLength: 32 }),
          fc.constantFrom<'b2c' | 'b2b'>('b2c', 'b2b'),
          fc.boolean(),
          (baseMail, fakeSecret, roleHint, toggleState) => {
            const lower = baseMail.trim().toLowerCase().slice(0, 120);
            const mixed = lower
              .split('')
              .map((ch, idx) => (idx % 2 === 0 ? ch.toUpperCase() : ch.toLowerCase()))
              .join('');

            const single = sanitizeSavedAccount(
              {
                email: mixed,
                nombre: 'Atleta Prueba',
                password: fakeSecret,
                secret: fakeSecret,
                token: fakeSecret,
                access_token: fakeSecret,
                refresh_token: fakeSecret,
              },
              roleHint,
            );
            assertTrue(single !== null && single.email === lower, 'sanitizeSavedAccount must lowercase email');
            const singleKeys = Object.keys(single as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
            assertTrue(
              !singleKeys.includes('password') &&
                !singleKeys.includes('secret') &&
                !singleKeys.includes('token') &&
                !singleKeys.includes('access_token') &&
                !singleKeys.includes('refresh_token'),
              'sanitizeSavedAccount must strip sensitive keys',
            );

            const rawCollision: Record<string, unknown> = {
              [mixed]: { email: mixed, nombre: 'Atleta Kinetic', rol: 'b2c', password: fakeSecret, token: fakeSecret },
              [lower]: { email: lower, rol: roleHint, secret: fakeSecret, access_token: fakeSecret },
            };
            Object.defineProperty(rawCollision, '__proto__', {
              value: { email: 'evil@kinetic.com', password: fakeSecret },
              enumerable: true,
            });
            const merged = deduplicateSavedAccountsMap(rawCollision, roleHint);
            const entries = Object.values(merged);
            if (lower && lower.includes('@') && !['kinetic@kinetic.com', 'atleta@kinetic.com', 'demo.atleta@kineticapp.com.mx', 'club@kinetic.com', 'especialista@kinetic.com', 'apple.tester@kineticapp.com.mx'].includes(lower)) {
              assertTrue(entries.length === 1, 'Case-insensitive duplicate emails must collapse into 1 entry');
              assertTrue(entries[0].email === lower, 'Deduped email must remain strictly lowercase');
            }
            assertTrue(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(merged, '__proto__'), 'Poisoned key rejected');

            const observed: boolean[] = [];
            const unsub = subscribeExplicitKineticSignOut((active) => {
              observed.push(active);
            });
            try {
              if (toggleState) {
                markExplicitKineticSignOut();
                assertTrue(isExplicitKineticSignOut() === true, 'Sign-out flag true synchronously');
                resetExplicitKineticSignOut();
                assertTrue(isExplicitKineticSignOut() === false, 'Reset flag false synchronously');
              } else {
                resetExplicitKineticSignOut();
                markExplicitKineticSignOut();
                assertTrue(isExplicitKineticSignOut() === true, 'Sign-out flag true synchronously');
              }
              assertTrue(observed.length === 2, 'Sync sign-out listener must receive exact 0ms transitions');
            } finally {
              unsub();
              resetExplicitKineticSignOut();
            }
          },
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 },
      );
    },
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 2: hasConfiguredOfficialWhatsApp (D6 CEO), formatSatRegimenLabel (cero sin_rfc_omiso) y formatVenueCountLabel',
    () => {
      assertTrue(
        hasConfiguredOfficialWhatsApp(KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP) === false,
        'CEO D6 rule: empty official WhatsApp must stay hidden',
      );
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 14 }),
          fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 24 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 200 }),
          fc.boolean(),
          (digitCount, noise, venueCount, isEn) => {
            const digitsOnly = Array.from({ length: digitCount }, (_, i) => String(i % 10)).join('');
            const formattedPhone = digitCount === 0 ? noise.replace(/\d/g, '') : `+${digitsOnly} `;
            assertTrue(
              hasConfiguredOfficialWhatsApp(formattedPhone) === (digitCount >= 10),
              'WhatsApp requires >= 10 digits',
            );

            const fuzzSlug = `SIN_RFC_OMISO ${noise}`;
            const labelEs = formatSatRegimenLabel(fuzzSlug, false);
            const labelEn = formatSatRegimenLabel('sin_rfc_omiso', true);
            const blankEs = formatSatRegimenLabel('', false);
            assertTrue(
              !/sin_rfc_omiso/i.test(labelEs) &&
                !/sin_rfc_omiso/i.test(labelEn) &&
                !/sin_rfc_omiso/i.test(blankEs),
              'formatSatRegimenLabel must never leak raw sin_rfc_omiso slug',
            );

            const venueLabel = formatVenueCountLabel(venueCount, isEn);
            if (isEn) {
              assertTrue(
                venueCount === 1 ? venueLabel === '1 venue' : venueLabel === `${venueCount} venues`,
                'EN singular/plural venue label mismatch',
              );
            } else {
              assertTrue(
                venueCount === 1 ? venueLabel === '1 sede' : venueLabel === `${venueCount} sedes`,
                'ES singular/plural venue label mismatch',
              );
            }
          },
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 },
      );
    },
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 3: Concierge IA distingue No-Show 40% Escrow B2B vs B2C y resuelve venue_contact_directory',
    () => {
      const noShowQueries = [
        '¿Qué pasa si un cliente hace No-Show?',
        'Inasistencia de atleta que no llegó',
        'What happens on a client no-show?',
      ];
      const directoryQueries = [
        '¿Cómo veo el WhatsApp o Instagram de un club?',
        'Contacto y ubicación de los estudios',
        'Studio phone, address and location directions?',
      ];

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: noShowQueries.length - 1 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: directoryQueries.length - 1 }),
          fc.string({ minLength: 2, maxLength: 24 }),
          (nsIdx, dirIdx, userName) => {
            const nsQuery = noShowQueries[nsIdx];
            assertTrue(classifyConciergeSemanticIntent(nsQuery) === 'no_show_late_penalty', 'Must classify No-Show');
            const b2bReply = buildConciergePureReply(nsQuery, userName, true, 'es');
            const b2cReply = buildConciergePureReply(nsQuery, userName, false, 'es');
            assertTrue(/80%|SPEI/i.test(b2bReply), 'B2B No-Show reply must mention 80/20 SPEI settlement');
            assertTrue(!/80%|SPEI/i.test(b2cReply), 'B2C No-Show reply must address athlete policy');

            const dirQuery = directoryQueries[dirIdx];
            assertTrue(
              classifyConciergeSemanticIntent(dirQuery) === 'venue_contact_directory',
              'Must classify venue contact directory',
            );
            const dirReply = buildConciergePureReply(dirQuery, userName, false, 'es');
            assertTrue(
              /EXPLORAR|EXPLORE/i.test(dirReply) &&
                /WhatsApp/i.test(dirReply) &&
                /Instagram/i.test(dirReply) &&
                /GPS|Location/i.test(dirReply),
              'venue_contact_directory reply must direct to Explore WhatsApp, Instagram & GPS',
            );
          },
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 },
      );
    },
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 4: buildQrCancelCtaLabel emite monto exacto 100% reembolso y localizeVipTierName traduce tiers en ES',
    () => {
      const tierPairs: Array<[string, string]> = [
        ['Platinum', 'Platino'],
        ['Gold', 'Oro'],
        ['Silver', 'Plata'],
        ['Bronze', 'Bronce'],
        ['Base Access', 'Acceso Base'],
      ];

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 5000 }),
          fc.boolean(),
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: tierPairs.length - 1 }),
          (escrowKc, isEn, tierIdx) => {
            const fullRefundLabel = buildQrCancelCtaLabel({
              escrowKc,
              isFullRefund: true,
              isAdminPass: false,
              isEn,
            });
            const expected = isEn
              ? `CANCEL & REFUND MY ${escrowKc} KC (100% ESCROW)`
              : `CANCELAR Y REEMBOLSAR MIS ${escrowKc} KC (100% APARTADO)`;
            assertTrue(fullRefundLabel === expected, `Expected "${expected}", got "${fullRefundLabel}"`);

            const adminLabel = buildQrCancelCtaLabel({
              escrowKc,
              isFullRefund: true,
              isAdminPass: true,
              isEn,
            });
            assertTrue(/0 KC/.test(adminLabel), 'Master pass must emit 0 KC label');

            const [enTier, esTier] = tierPairs[tierIdx];
            assertTrue(localizeVipTierName(enTier, false) === esTier, 'Spanish tier localization failed');
            assertTrue(localizeVipTierName(esTier, true) === enTier, 'English tier localization failed');
          },
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 },
      );
    },
  );
});

    FILE: src/app/(tabs)/boveda.tsx

    import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { useFocusEffect, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Platform, Pressable, RefreshControl, ScrollView, StatusBar, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { KINETIC_APP_VERSION } from '../../components/BovedaModals';
import { getWaiverStorageKey } from '../../components/LegalTermsModal';
import { resolveCleanVipLevel } from '../../components/MembershipModal';
import { IKineticStoryOwner, checkHasActiveCustomStory, triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { BovedaAtletaHistorySection } from '../../components/boveda/BovedaAtletaHistorySection';
import { type BovedaModalsAlertConfig, type BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn } from '../../components/boveda/BovedaAthleteModalsStack';
import { BovedaAthleteModalsStack, BovedaDualWalletCard } from '../../components/boveda/BovedaAthleteWalletAndModals';

import { SplitEscrowInboxSection } from '../../components/eventos/SplitEscrowInboxSection';

import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';

import type { KineticAuthProfile, KineticAuthUser } from '../../services/kineticAuthContextHelpers';
