const LOGOUT_DATA_SET: Record<string, unknown> = { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'light', kineticOnMedia: 'true' } };

const makeModalFlagSetter = <T extends Record<string, unknown>, K extends keyof T>(
  setter: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<T>>,
  key: K
): React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<T[K]>> => (action) => {
  setter((prev) => ({
    ...prev,
    [key]: typeof action === 'function' ? (action as (p: T[K]) => T[K])(prev[key]) : action,
  }));
};

const BovedaAtleta = ({ user, profile, insets, router }: {
  user: KineticAuthUser | null;
  profile: KineticAuthProfile | null;
  insets: { top: number; bottom: number };
  router: ReturnType<typeof useRouter>;
}) => {
  const { refreshProfile, signOut } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, t, tx, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const bv = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseBv, resolvedTheme, ['passCardOverlay', 'passBadgePill', 'passTimePill']),
    [resolvedTheme],
  );
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const [uiFlags, setUiFlags] = useState<{
    isSettingsOpen: boolean; isFiscalModalOpen: boolean; isDossierOpen: boolean; isMembershipModalOpen: boolean;
    isLegalModalOpen: boolean; isReferralOpen: boolean; isAiConciergeOpen: boolean; isHumanSupportOpen: boolean;
    isStoryOpen: boolean; autoUploadStory: boolean; settingsInitialSection: 'preferencias' | 'legal' | 'seguridad';
    hasActiveStoryRing: boolean; hasSignedWaiver: boolean; hasScrolled: boolean; isSigningOutTap: boolean;
  }>({
    isSettingsOpen: false, isFiscalModalOpen: false, isDossierOpen: false, isMembershipModalOpen: false,
    isLegalModalOpen: false, isReferralOpen: false, isAiConciergeOpen: false, isHumanSupportOpen: false,
    isStoryOpen: false, autoUploadStory: false, settingsInitialSection: 'preferencias',
    hasActiveStoryRing: false, hasSignedWaiver: Boolean(profile?.waiver_firmado), hasScrolled: false, isSigningOutTap: false,
  });
  const {
    isSettingsOpen, isFiscalModalOpen, isDossierOpen, isMembershipModalOpen,
    isLegalModalOpen, isReferralOpen, isAiConciergeOpen, isHumanSupportOpen,
    isStoryOpen, autoUploadStory, settingsInitialSection,
    hasActiveStoryRing, hasSignedWaiver, hasScrolled, isSigningOutTap,
  } = uiFlags;
  const flagSetters = useMemo(() => ({
    setIsSettingsOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isSettingsOpen'), setIsFiscalModalOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isFiscalModalOpen'),
    setIsDossierOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isDossierOpen'), setIsMembershipModalOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isMembershipModalOpen'),
    setIsLegalModalOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isLegalModalOpen'), setIsReferralOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isReferralOpen'),
    setIsAiConciergeOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isAiConciergeOpen'), setIsHumanSupportOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isHumanSupportOpen'),
    setIsStoryOpen: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isStoryOpen'), setAutoUploadStory: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'autoUploadStory'),
    setSettingsInitialSection: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'settingsInitialSection'), setHasActiveStoryRing: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'hasActiveStoryRing'),
    setHasSignedWaiver: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'hasSignedWaiver'), setHasScrolled: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'hasScrolled'),
    setIsSigningOutTap: makeModalFlagSetter(setUiFlags, 'isSigningOutTap'),
  }), []);
  const {
    setIsSettingsOpen, setIsFiscalModalOpen, setIsDossierOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen,
    setIsLegalModalOpen, setIsReferralOpen, setIsAiConciergeOpen, setIsHumanSupportOpen,
    setIsStoryOpen, setAutoUploadStory, setSettingsInitialSection,
    setHasActiveStoryRing, setHasSignedWaiver, setHasScrolled, setIsSigningOutTap,
  } = flagSetters;
  const [selectedRateReserva, setSelectedRateReserva] = useState<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null);
  const isSettingsOpenRef = useRef(false);
  isSettingsOpenRef.current = isSettingsOpen;
  const returnToSettingsLegalRef = useRef(false);
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<BovedaModalsAlertConfig>({
    visible: false, title: '', message: '', type: 'alert',
    confirmText: isEn ? 'GOT IT' : 'ENTENDIDO', cancelText: isEn ? 'STAY' : 'PERMANECER', onConfirm: null,
  });

  const showTacticalAlert = useCallback<BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn>(
    (title, message, type = 'alert', onConfirm = null, confirmText = isEn ? 'GOT IT' : 'ENTENDIDO', cancelText = isEn ? 'STAY' : 'PERMANECER') =>
      setAlertConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type, confirmText, cancelText, onConfirm }),
    [isEn],
  );

  const {
    refreshing, reservas, isFetchingReservas, hasInitialLoaded, activeReservations, historyReservations, completedCount, totalEscrowLockedKc,
    billingCycleInfo, isGhostMode, toggleGhostModeBoveda, selectedQR, setSelectedQR, isQRReady, setIsQRReady,
    selectedReceipt, setSelectedReceipt, isHistorialExpanded, setIsHistorialExpanded, isProcessingPayment,
    setIsProcessingPayment, processingPlanId, setProcessingPlanId, isCanceling, setIsCanceling, qrTimeLeft,
    qrPayload, isRegeneratingQR, isWithinWindow, windowMessage, isExportingReceiptPdf, exportReceiptToPdf,
    fetchData, getCostoTotal, onRefresh,
  } = useBovedaAtleta(user, profile, refreshProfile);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      fetchData(true, false);
    }, [fetchData]),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    bovedaService.syncActivePassesSnapshot(user?.id, activeReservations);
  }, [user?.id, activeReservations]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    return bovedaService.subscribeUserReservasRealtimeAndFocus(user.id, () => {
      fetchData(true, true);
      refreshProfile?.();
    });
  }, [user?.id, fetchData, refreshProfile]);

  const cleanVipLevel = useMemo(
    () => resolveCleanVipLevel(profile?.nivel, profile?.kc_membresia),
    [profile?.nivel, profile?.kc_membresia],
  );
  const noSel: ViewStyle | null = Platform.OS === 'web'
    ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle)
    : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    const openFn = () => {
      try {
        if (isSettingsOpenRef.current) {
          returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = true;
          setIsSettingsOpen(false);
        }
        setIsFiscalModalOpen(true);
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    };
    const gObj = globalThis as Record<string, unknown>;
    gObj.__kineticOpenBovedaBilling = openFn;
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function') {
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:open-boveda-billing', openFn);
    }
    return () => {
      if (gObj.__kineticOpenBovedaBilling === openFn) {
        delete gObj.__kineticOpenBovedaBilling;
      }
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.removeEventListener === 'function') {
        window.removeEventListener('kinetic:open-boveda-billing', openFn);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const athleteScrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const hasUserScrolledRef = useRef(false);
  const bovedaTimersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    hasUserScrolledRef.current = false;
    athleteScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
  }, [user?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    fetchData(true, false);
    if (!profile) refreshProfile?.();
  }, [user?.id, profile, fetchData, refreshProfile]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasUserScrolledRef.current) return;
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (!hasUserScrolledRef.current) athleteScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
    });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [hasInitialLoaded, profile?.id]);
  const scheduleBovedaTimeout = useCallback((fn: () => void, ms: number) => {
    const tid = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    bovedaTimersRef.current.push(tid);
    return tid;
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    bovedaTimersRef.current.forEach((tm) => clearTimeout(tm));
    bovedaTimersRef.current = [];
  }, []);

  const refreshStoryRingState = useCallback(async () => {
    const ownerId = String(user?.id || '');
    if (!ownerId) { setHasActiveStoryRing(false); return; }
    try { setHasActiveStoryRing(await checkHasActiveCustomStory(ownerId)); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; setHasActiveStoryRing(false); }
  }, [user?.id, setHasActiveStoryRing]);

  useEffect(() => { refreshStoryRingState(); }, [refreshStoryRingState]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profile?.waiver_firmado) { setHasSignedWaiver(true); return; }
    if (!user?.id) { setHasSignedWaiver(false); return; }
    let active = true;
    setHasSignedWaiver(false);
    AsyncStorage.getItem(getWaiverStorageKey(user.id))
      .then((val) => { if (active) setHasSignedWaiver(Boolean(val)); })
      .catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [profile?.waiver_firmado, user?.id, setHasSignedWaiver]);

  const { handlePurchase, cancelarReserva } = useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel({
    user, profile, isEn, tx, cleanVipLevel, refreshProfile, fetchData, getCostoTotal,
    isProcessingPayment, setIsProcessingPayment, setProcessingPlanId, isCanceling, setIsCanceling,
    setIsMembershipModalOpen, setSelectedQR, scheduleBovedaTimeout, showTacticalAlert,
  });

  const handleSignOut = () => {
    if (isSigningOutTap) return;
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    setAlertConfig({
      visible: true, title: t('vault.sign_out'), message: t('vault.sign_out_confirm_q'), type: 'confirm',
      confirmText: t('vault.sign_out'), cancelText: t('vault.stay'), immediateConfirm: true,
      onConfirm: async () => {
        setIsSigningOutTap(true);
        await yieldNextUiFrame();
        markExplicitKineticSignOut();
        bovedaTimersRef.current.forEach((tm) => clearTimeout(tm));
        bovedaTimersRef.current = [];
        setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false, onConfirm: null }));
        setIsSettingsOpen(false); setIsFiscalModalOpen(false); setIsDossierOpen(false);
        setIsMembershipModalOpen(false); setIsLegalModalOpen(false); setIsReferralOpen(false);
        setIsAiConciergeOpen(false); setIsHumanSupportOpen(false); setIsStoryOpen(false);
        setIsQRReady(false); setSelectedQR(null); setSelectedReceipt(null); setSelectedRateReserva(null);
        try { await signOut?.(); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally { setIsSigningOutTap(false); }
      },
    });
  };

  const {
    kcMembresia = 0, kcTopup = 0, saldoTotal = 0, daysUntilCut = 30,
    nextCutDateLabel = '--', perdonesMaximos = 0, perdonesDisponibles = 0,
  } = billingCycleInfo || {};
  const isMasterAdmin = isKineticMasterAdmin(user?.email, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta);
  const athleteName = resolveOfficialKineticName(profile?.nombre, isMasterAdmin, isEn ? 'Kinetic Athlete' : 'Atleta Kinetic');
  const displayTier = isMasterAdmin
    ? getDisplayRole(profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.email, isEn)
    : localizeVipTierName(cleanVipLevel, isEn);
  const storyOwnerAthlete = useMemo<IKineticStoryOwner>(() => ({
    id: String(user?.id || athleteName), name: athleteName, subtitle: `${displayTier} • ${isEn ? 'Kinetic Member' : 'Miembro Kinetic'}`,
    avatarUrl: profile?.avatar_url || null, isAthleteAccount: !isMasterAdmin, isOfficialKinetic: isMasterAdmin,
    autoStartUpload: autoUploadStory, discipline: 'Fuerza', canUpload: true,
  }), [user?.id, athleteName, displayTier, profile?.avatar_url, autoUploadStory, isEn, isMasterAdmin]);

  const handlePullRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsHistorialExpanded(false);
    athleteScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true });
    await Promise.allSettled([onRefresh(), refreshProfile?.()]);
  }, [onRefresh, refreshProfile, setIsHistorialExpanded]);

  const handleOpenSettingsFromTop = useCallback(() => {
    if (
      isSettingsOpen || isFiscalModalOpen || isDossierOpen || isMembershipModalOpen ||
      isLegalModalOpen || isReferralOpen || isAiConciergeOpen || isHumanSupportOpen ||
      isStoryOpen || Boolean(selectedQR) || Boolean(selectedReceipt) || Boolean(selectedRateReserva) || Boolean(alertConfig?.visible)
    ) return;
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    setSettingsInitialSection('preferencias');
    returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = false;
    setIsSettingsOpen(true);
  }, [
    isSettingsOpen, isFiscalModalOpen, isDossierOpen, isMembershipModalOpen,
    isLegalModalOpen, isReferralOpen, isAiConciergeOpen, isHumanSupportOpen,
    isStoryOpen, selectedQR, selectedReceipt, selectedRateReserva, alertConfig?.visible,
  ]);

  const anyModalOpen = isSettingsOpen || isFiscalModalOpen || isDossierOpen || isMembershipModalOpen || isLegalModalOpen || isReferralOpen || isAiConciergeOpen || isHumanSupportOpen || isStoryOpen || Boolean(selectedQR) || Boolean(selectedReceipt) || Boolean(selectedRateReserva) || Boolean(alertConfig?.visible);

  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor={themeColors.background} />
      <View style={[bv.topBar, { paddingTop: insets.top + 14, backgroundColor: themeColors.background, borderBottomWidth: hasScrolled ? 1 : 0, borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.22)' }]}>
        <View>
          <Text style={[bv.headerSub, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{t('vault.sub')}</Text>
          <Text style={[bv.headerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('vault.title')}</Text>
        </View>
        <TouchableOpacity
          accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={tx('⚙️ AJUSTES', '⚙️ SETTINGS')}
          style={[bv.storyTopBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }, noSel]}
          onPress={handleOpenSettingsFromTop}
        >
          <Text selectable={false} style={[bv.storyTopBtnText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('⚙️ AJUSTES', '⚙️ SETTINGS')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        ref={athleteScrollRef} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} scrollEventThrottle={16}
        onScroll={(e) => {
          const scrolled = Number(e?.nativeEvent?.contentOffset?.y || 0) > 4;
          if (scrolled) hasUserScrolledRef.current = true;
          setHasScrolled((prev) => (prev === scrolled ? prev : scrolled));
        }}
        contentContainerStyle={[bv.scrollContent, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 120 }]}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={handlePullRefresh} tintColor="#00F0FF" colors={['#00F0FF']} progressBackgroundColor={isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#080C12'} />}
      >
        <BovedaClockwiseRefreshIndicator visible={refreshing} isLight={isLight} />
        <BovedaAthleteIdentityCard
          avatarUrl={profile?.avatar_url} hasActiveStoryRing={hasActiveStoryRing}
          athleteName={athleteName} email={user?.email} displayTier={displayTier}
          onOpenDossier={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; setIsDossierOpen(true); }}
          onPressAvatar={(e: { stopPropagation?: () => void }) => { e?.stopPropagation?.(); if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Light'); setAutoUploadStory(!hasActiveStoryRing); setIsStoryOpen(true); }}
        />

        <BovedaDualWalletCard
          saldoTotal={saldoTotal} displayTier={displayTier} kcMembresia={kcMembresia} kcTopup={kcTopup}
          nextCutDateLabel={nextCutDateLabel} daysUntilCut={daysUntilCut} hasActiveMembership={billingCycleInfo.hasActiveMembership} totalEscrowLockedKc={totalEscrowLockedKc}
          isBalanceHydrating={Boolean(user?.id && !profile && user?.user_metadata?.kc_membresia === undefined && !hasInitialLoaded)}
          isEscrowHydrating={!hasInitialLoaded && isFetchingReservas}
          perdonesMaximos={perdonesMaximos} perdonesDisponibles={perdonesDisponibles}
          onOpenMembership={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; setIsMembershipModalOpen(true); }}
        />

        <SplitEscrowInboxSection userId={user?.id} onAcceptInvite={() => router.push('/(tabs)/eventos' as never)} />

        <BovedaAthletePassesSection
          activeReservations={activeReservations} isFetchingReservas={isFetchingReservas}
          hasInitialLoaded={hasInitialLoaded} reservasLength={reservas.length}
          onExploreEvents={() => router.push('/(tabs)/eventos' as never)}
          onInstantOpenQr={(r: Record<string, unknown>) => { if (anyModalOpen) return; setSelectedQR(r); setIsQRReady(true); }}
          onOpenPassQr={(r: Record<string, unknown>) => { if (anyModalOpen) return; setSelectedQR(r); scheduleBovedaTimeout(() => setIsQRReady(true), 120); }}
        />

        <BovedaAtletaHistorySection
          historyReservations={historyReservations} isHistorialExpanded={isHistorialExpanded}
          setIsHistorialExpanded={setIsHistorialExpanded}
          setSelectedReceipt={(rec: Record<string, unknown> | null) => { if (rec && anyModalOpen) return; setSelectedReceipt(rec); }}
          getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal}
          onRateReserva={(res: Record<string, unknown>) => { if (res && anyModalOpen) return; setSelectedRateReserva(res); }}
        />

        <Text style={[bv.sectionTitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
          {tx('CONCIERGE, SOPORTE Y COMUNIDAD', 'CONCIERGE, SUPPORT & COMMUNITY')}
        </Text>
        <UnifiedMenuSection
          isB2B={false}
          hasSignedWaiver={hasSignedWaiver}
          onOpenSettings={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsSettingsOpen(true); }}
          onOpenFiscal={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsFiscalModalOpen(true); }}
          onOpenReferrals={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsReferralOpen(true); }}
          onOpenLegal={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsLegalModalOpen(true); }}
          onOpenConcierge={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsAiConciergeOpen(true); }}
          onOpenSupport={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsHumanSupportOpen(true); }}
        />
        <Pressable
          accessibilityRole="button" onPress={handleSignOut} disabled={isSigningOutTap} {...LOGOUT_DATA_SET}
          style={({ pressed }) => [
            bv.logoutBtn,
            {
              backgroundColor: pressed ? '#991B1B' : '#DC2626', borderColor: pressed ? '#EF4444' : '#F87171',
              borderTopColor: pressed ? '#F87171' : '#FCA5A5', borderBottomColor: pressed ? '#450A0A' : '#7F1D1D',
              opacity: isSigningOutTap ? 0.8 : 1, elevation: 0,
            },
            pressed && { transform: [{ translateY: 1.5 }] }, noSel,
          ]}
        >
          {isSigningOutTap ? <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#FFFFFF" /> : <Feather name="log-out" size={16} color="#FFFFFF" />}
          <Text selectable={false} style={[bv.logoutText, { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>{t('vault.sign_out')}</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={[bv.footerVersionText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{KINETIC_APP_VERSION}</Text>
      </ScrollView>

      <BovedaAthleteModalsStack
        user={user} profile={profile} cleanVipLevel={cleanVipLevel} isEn={isEn} athleteName={athleteName}
        reservas={reservas} completedCount={completedCount} totalEscrowLockedKc={totalEscrowLockedKc}
        billingCycleInfo={billingCycleInfo} isGhostMode={isGhostMode} toggleGhostModeBoveda={toggleGhostModeBoveda}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert} refreshProfile={refreshProfile}
        isDossierOpen={isDossierOpen} setIsDossierOpen={setIsDossierOpen} isSettingsOpen={isSettingsOpen} setIsSettingsOpen={setIsSettingsOpen}
        settingsInitialSection={settingsInitialSection} setSettingsInitialSection={setSettingsInitialSection} returnToSettingsLegalRef={returnToSettingsLegalRef}
        isFiscalModalOpen={isFiscalModalOpen} setIsFiscalModalOpen={setIsFiscalModalOpen} isAiConciergeOpen={isAiConciergeOpen} setIsAiConciergeOpen={setIsAiConciergeOpen}
        isHumanSupportOpen={isHumanSupportOpen} setIsHumanSupportOpen={setIsHumanSupportOpen} isReferralOpen={isReferralOpen} setIsReferralOpen={setIsReferralOpen}
        selectedQR={selectedQR} setSelectedQR={setSelectedQR} isQRReady={isQRReady} setIsQRReady={setIsQRReady}
        isWithinWindow={isWithinWindow} qrPayload={qrPayload} isRegeneratingQR={isRegeneratingQR} windowMessage={windowMessage} qrTimeLeft={qrTimeLeft} isCanceling={isCanceling}
        getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal} cancelarReserva={cancelarReserva} selectedReceipt={selectedReceipt} setSelectedReceipt={setSelectedReceipt}
        exportReceiptToPdf={exportReceiptToPdf} isExportingReceiptPdf={isExportingReceiptPdf} isMembershipModalOpen={isMembershipModalOpen} setIsMembershipModalOpen={setIsMembershipModalOpen}
        handlePurchase={handlePurchase} isProcessingPayment={isProcessingPayment} processingPlanId={processingPlanId} isLegalModalOpen={isLegalModalOpen} setIsLegalModalOpen={setIsLegalModalOpen}
        hasSignedWaiver={hasSignedWaiver} setHasSignedWaiver={setHasSignedWaiver} isStoryOpen={isStoryOpen} setIsStoryOpen={setIsStoryOpen} setAutoUploadStory={setAutoUploadStory}
        storyOwnerAthlete={storyOwnerAthlete} setHasActiveStoryRing={setHasActiveStoryRing}
        selectedRateReserva={selectedRateReserva} setSelectedRateReserva={setSelectedRateReserva} alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

export default function BovedaScreen() {
  const { user, profile, isLoading } = useAuth();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const router = useRouter();
  const hasMountedOnceRef = useRef(false);
  const lastB2BModeRef = useRef(false);
  const appMeta = (user?.app_metadata || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (isLoading && !profile && !appMeta.rol && !appMeta.tipo_cuenta && !hasMountedOnceRef.current) {
    return <BovedaScannerSkeleton topInset={insets.top} />;
  }
  const computedB2B =
    hasAnyKineticMasterAdminRole(user?.email, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, appMeta.rol, appMeta.tipo_cuenta) ||
    hasAnyKineticB2BRole(profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, appMeta.rol, appMeta.tipo_cuenta);
  if (profile || appMeta.rol || appMeta.tipo_cuenta || (!isLoading && user)) {
    hasMountedOnceRef.current = true;
    lastB2BModeRef.current = computedB2B;
  }
  const effectiveB2B = profile || appMeta.rol || appMeta.tipo_cuenta || (!isLoading && user) ? computedB2B : lastB2BModeRef.current;
  if (effectiveB2B) return <DashboardB2B user={user} profile={profile} insets={insets} router={router} />;
  return <BovedaAtleta user={user} profile={profile} insets={insets} router={router} />;
}

    FILE: src/components/BillingCfdiModal.tsx

    // === src/components/BillingCfdiModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Keyboard, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useSettingsBoveda } from '../hooks/useBoveda';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { maskClabeAccount, validateSatCfdi40Fields } from '../services/kineticSecurityMfaService';
import {
  CFDI_USES_CATALOG,
  SAT_REGIMES_CATALOG,
  baseCfdiStyles,
  formatSatRegimenLabel,
  getCfdiUseEntry,
  getSatRegimeEntry,
} from '../styles/billingCfdi.styles';
export { formatSatRegimenLabel };
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { renderInWebPortal } from '../utils/kineticWebPortal';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from './shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

const NO_SEL: Record<string, string> | undefined = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : undefined;

interface BillingCfdiModalProps {
  visible?: boolean;
  onClose?: () => void;
  user?: { id?: string; email?: string | null } | null;
  profile?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  isB2B?: boolean;
  refreshProfile?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
  triggerHaptic?: (type?: string) => void;
  inlineOverlay?: boolean;
  portalOnWeb?: boolean;
}

export const BillingCfdiModal = ({
  visible, onClose, user, profile, isB2B = false, refreshProfile, showTacticalAlert, triggerHaptic: hapticProp,
  inlineOverlay = false, portalOnWeb = true,
}: BillingCfdiModalProps) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const cfdiStyles = React.useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseCfdiStyles, resolvedTheme, ['primaryCyanBtn']), [resolvedTheme]);
  const fireHaptic = hapticProp || triggerHaptic;
  const scrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const [keyboardHeight, setKeyboardHeight] = useState(0);
  const [openPicker, setOpenPicker] = useState<'regimen' | 'cfdi' | null>(null);
  const [inlineAlert, setInlineAlert] = useState<{ title: string; msg: string } | null>(null);
  const [isEditingClabe, setIsEditingClabe] = useState(false);
  const [hasScrolled, setHasScrolled] = useState(false);
  const attenuatedPlaceholder = isLightMode ? 'rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.46)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.44)';
  const saveBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const timersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);
  const scheduleCfdiTimeout = useCallback((fn: () => void, ms: number) => { timersRef.current.push(setTimeout(fn, ms)); }, []);

  useEffect(() => () => { timersRef.current.forEach(clearTimeout); timersRef.current = []; }, []);

  const {
    clabe, setClabe, rfc, setRfc, razonSocial, setRazonSocial,
    regimenFiscal, setRegimenFiscal, codigoPostalFiscal, setCodigoPostalFiscal,
    usoCfdi, setUsoCfdi, emailFacturacion, setEmailFacturacion,
    saveFiscalCache, isSaving, setIsSaving,
  } = useSettingsBoveda(user, isB2B, profile);

  const wasVisibleRef = useRef(false);
  const lastUserIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) { wasVisibleRef.current = false; return; }
    const uid = String(user?.id || '');
    if (!wasVisibleRef.current || lastUserIdRef.current !== uid) {
      wasVisibleRef.current = true;
      lastUserIdRef.current = uid;
      setOpenPicker(null);
      setInlineAlert(null);
      setIsEditingClabe(false);
      if (!emailFacturacion && user?.email) setEmailFacturacion(user.email);
    }
  }, [visible, emailFacturacion, user?.id, user?.email, setEmailFacturacion]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const showEv = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillShow' : 'keyboardDidShow';
    const hideEv = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillHide' : 'keyboardDidHide';
    const s1 = Keyboard.addListener(showEv, (e) => setKeyboardHeight(e.endCoordinates?.height || 260));
    const s2 = Keyboard.addListener(hideEv, () => setKeyboardHeight(0));
    return () => { s1.remove(); s2.remove(); };
  }, []);

  const handleSaveFiscalData = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    if (!user?.id || saveBusyRef.current) return;
    setInlineAlert(null);
    const satCheck = validateSatCfdi40Fields({ rfc, cp: codigoPostalFiscal, clabe, isB2B });

    if (isB2B && satCheck.cleanClabe.length !== 18) {
      fireHaptic?.('Warning');
      setInlineAlert({
        title: tx('CLABE DE 18 DÍGITOS REQUERIDA', '18-DIGIT CLABE REQUIRED'),
        msg: tx(
          'La cuenta CLABE interbancaria exacta de 18 dígitos numéricos es obligatoria para dispersiones SPEI B2B.',
          'An exact 18-digit numeric interbank CLABE account is required for B2B weekly SPEI payouts.'
        ),
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!satCheck.ok) {
      fireHaptic?.('Warning');
      if (satCheck.errorCode === 'INVALID_RFC') {
        setInlineAlert({
          title: tx('RFC CON FORMATO INVÁLIDO', 'INVALID TAX ID (RFC) FORMAT'),
          msg: tx(
            'El RFC debe contener 12 o 13 caracteres alfanuméricos válidos con homoclave SAT (ej. XAXX010101000).',
            'Your Mexican Tax ID (RFC) must contain 12 or 13 valid alphanumeric SAT characters (e.g. XAXX010101000).'
          ),
        });
      } else if (satCheck.errorCode === 'INVALID_CP') {
        setInlineAlert({
          title: tx('CÓDIGO POSTAL FISCAL INVÁLIDO', 'INVALID TAX POSTAL CODE'),
          msg: tx(
            'El Código Postal fiscal debe contener exactamente 5 dígitos numéricos.',
            'Your Tax Postal Code must contain exactly 5 numeric digits.'
          ),
        });
      } else if (isB2B) {
        setInlineAlert({
          title: tx('CLABE DE 18 DÍGITOS INVÁLIDA', 'INVALID 18-DIGIT CLABE'),
          msg: tx(
            'Verifica que tu cuenta CLABE contenga exactamente 18 dígitos numéricos.',
            'Verify that your interbank CLABE contains exactly 18 numeric digits.'
          ),
        });
      }
      return;
    }

    const rawBillingEmail = emailFacturacion.trim().toLowerCase().slice(0, 120);
    const cleanBillingEmail = rawBillingEmail || String(user?.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (cleanBillingEmail && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(cleanBillingEmail)) {
      fireHaptic?.('Warning');
      setInlineAlert({
        title: tx('CORREO FISCAL INVÁLIDO', 'INVALID BILLING EMAIL'),
        msg: tx('Ingresa un correo electrónico válido para recibir tus comprobantes CFDI 4.0.', 'Enter a valid email address to receive your CFDI 4.0 tax invoices.'),
      });
      return;
    }
    saveBusyRef.current = true;
    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      await saveFiscalCache({
        rfc: satCheck.cleanRfc,
        razonSocial: razonSocial.trim(),
        regimenFiscal: regimenFiscal.trim(),
        codigoPostalFiscal: satCheck.cleanCp,
        usoCfdi,
        emailFacturacion: cleanBillingEmail,
      });
      const basePerfilesPayload = {
        rfc_fiscal: satCheck.cleanRfc || null,
        razon_social_fiscal: razonSocial.trim() || null,
        regimen_fiscal_receptor: regimenFiscal.trim() || null,
        cp_fiscal: satCheck.cleanCp || null,
        uso_cfdi: usoCfdi,
      };
      const { error: pErr } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles')
        .update({ ...basePerfilesPayload, email_facturacion: cleanBillingEmail || null })
        .eq('id', user.id);
      if (pErr && String(pErr.message || '').toLowerCase().includes('email_facturacion')) {
        const { error: fallbackErr } = await supabase.from('perfiles').update(basePerfilesPayload).eq('id', user.id);
        if (fallbackErr) throw fallbackErr;
      } else if (pErr) {
        throw pErr;
      }
      if (isB2B) {
        const { error: afErr } = await supabase
          .from('afiliados')
          .update({ clabe_bancaria: satCheck.cleanClabe || null, rfc: satCheck.cleanRfc || null })
          .eq('propietario_id', user.id);
        if (afErr) throw afErr;
      }
      await refreshProfile?.();
      setIsEditingClabe(false);
      fireHaptic?.('Success');
      onClose?.();
      scheduleCfdiTimeout(() => {
        showTacticalAlert?.(
          isB2B ? tx('DATOS B2B Y CLABE GUARDADOS', 'B2B & CLABE INFO SAVED') : tx('DATOS DE FACTURACIÓN GUARDADOS', 'BILLING INFO SAVED'),
          isB2B
            ? tx('Tu cuenta CLABE SPEI y parámetros fiscales quedaron sincronizados.', 'Your SPEI CLABE account and tax parameters have been synced.')
            : tx('Tus datos para emisión de comprobantes CFDI 4.0 quedaron guardados.', 'Your CFDI 4.0 tax invoicing details have been saved.'),
          'success',
        );
      }, 240);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      fireHaptic?.('Error');
      const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
      setInlineAlert({
        title: tx('FRICCIÓN AL GUARDAR', 'SAVE ERROR'),
        msg: language === 'en' ? 'Unable to update tax billing information.' : (errMsg || 'No se pudieron actualizar los datos fiscales.'),
      });
    } finally {
      saveBusyRef.current = false;
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const sheetContent = (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      style={{ width: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      pointerEvents="box-none"
    >
      <View
        style={[
          globalStyles.bottomSheet,
          KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
          {
            minHeight: 0,
            flexShrink: 1,
            backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : themeColors.surface,
            borderColor: isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.25)',
            borderTopColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan,
            paddingBottom: 0,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader visible={Boolean(visible)} onClose={onClose} />
        <View
          style={{
            alignItems: 'center',
            marginBottom: 8,
            paddingBottom: 8,
            borderBottomWidth: hasScrolled ? 1 : 0,
            borderBottomColor: isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.22)',
          }}
        >
          <Feather name={isB2B ? 'credit-card' : 'file-text'} size={22} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text style={cfdiStyles.modalTitle}>{isB2B ? tx('CLABE SPEI (80%) Y FACTURACIÓN SAT', 'SPEI CLABE (80%) & TAX BILLING') : tx('DATOS DE FACTURACIÓN SAT (CFDI 4.0)', 'TAX & BILLING INFO (CFDI 4.0)')}</Text>
          <Text style={cfdiStyles.modalSub}>
            {isB2B
              ? tx('Registra tu cuenta CLABE de 18 dígitos y tu RFC para recibir tus dispersiones semanales SPEI.', 'Register your 18-digit CLABE account and Tax ID (RFC) to receive weekly SPEI payouts.')
              : tx('Opcional para Atletas: Registra tu Constancia de Situación Fiscal solo si requieres factura CFDI 4.0 de tus Membresías o recargas Top-Up.', 'Optional for Athletes: Register your Mexican Tax ID (RFC) only if you require CFDI 4.0 tax invoicing for VIP Memberships or Top-Ups.')}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <ScrollView
          ref={scrollRef}
          style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}
          nestedScrollEnabled={true}
          bounces={false}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          onScroll={(e) => {
            const nextScrolled = Number(e?.nativeEvent?.contentOffset?.y || 0) > 4;
            setHasScrolled((prev) => (prev === nextScrolled ? prev : nextScrolled));
          }}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: keyboardHeight > 0 ? keyboardHeight + 24 : 18 }}
        >
            {isB2B && (
              <>
                <Text style={cfdiStyles.fieldLabel}>{tx('CLABE INTERBANCARIA (18 DÍGITOS PARA SPEI) *', 'INTERBANK CLABE (18 DIGITS FOR SPEI) *')}</Text>
                {!isEditingClabe && /^\d{18}$/.test(String(clabe || '').trim()) ? (
                  <View style={[cfdiStyles.inputBox, { justifyContent: 'space-between' }]}>
                    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flex: 1 }}>
                      <Feather name="credit-card" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                      <Text style={[cfdiStyles.inputText, { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {maskClabeAccount(clabe)}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); setIsEditingClabe(true); }} style={{ paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(2,132,199,0.1)' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.12)' }}>
                      <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' }}>{tx('EDITAR', 'EDIT')}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <View style={cfdiStyles.inputBox}>
                    <Feather name="credit-card" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    <TextInput
                      style={[cfdiStyles.inputText, { fontSize: 16 }]} value={clabe}
                      onChangeText={(txt) => setClabe(txt.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 18))}
                      keyboardType="number-pad" autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect={false} importantForAutofill="no" maxLength={18}
                      placeholder={tx('18 dígitos CLABE sin espacios', '18-digit CLABE number')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
                    />
                  </View>
                )}
              </>
            )}

            <Text style={cfdiStyles.fieldLabel}>{tx('RFC (CON HOMOCLAVE)', 'TAX ID / RFC')}{isB2B ? ' *' : tx(' (OPCIONAL)', ' (OPTIONAL)')}</Text>
            <View style={cfdiStyles.inputBox}>
              <Feather name="hash" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <TextInput
                style={[cfdiStyles.inputText, { fontSize: 16 }]} value={rfc}
                onChangeText={(txt) => setRfc(txt.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-ZÑ&0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 13))}
                autoCapitalize="characters" autoCorrect={false} importantForAutofill="no" maxLength={13}
                placeholder="XAXX010101000" placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              />
            </View>

            <Text style={cfdiStyles.fieldLabel}>{tx('RAZÓN SOCIAL O NOMBRE FISCAL (COMO APARECE EN TU CSF)', 'LEGAL TAX NAME (AS SHOWN ON TAX CERTIFICATE)')}</Text>
            <View style={cfdiStyles.inputBox}>
              <Feather name="user" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <TextInput
                style={[cfdiStyles.inputText, { fontSize: 16 }]} value={razonSocial}
                onChangeText={(txt) => setRazonSocial(txt.slice(0, 140))} autoCorrect={false} importantForAutofill="no" maxLength={140}
                placeholder={tx('Nombre o Razón Social (sin S.A.)', 'Legal Name (no corp. suffix)')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              />
            </View>

            <Text style={cfdiStyles.fieldLabel}>{tx('RÉGIMEN FISCAL SAT', 'SAT TAX REGIME')}{isB2B ? '' : tx(' (OPCIONAL)', ' (OPTIONAL)')}</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity style={[cfdiStyles.inputBox, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); Keyboard.dismiss(); setOpenPicker((p) => (p === 'regimen' ? null : 'regimen')); }}>
              <Feather name="briefcase" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <Text selectable={false} style={[cfdiStyles.inputLabelVal, !regimenFiscal && { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={2}>
                {!isB2B && !String(regimenFiscal || '').trim() ? tx('Seleccionar régimen fiscal SAT (Opcional)', 'Select SAT tax regime (Optional)') : formatSatRegimenLabel(regimenFiscal, language === 'en')}
              </Text>
              <Feather name={openPicker === 'regimen' ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
            {openPicker === 'regimen' && (
              <View style={[cfdiStyles.pickerListCard, isLightMode && cfdiStyles.pickerListCardLight]}>
                {SAT_REGIMES_CATALOG.map((item) => {
                  const selected = getSatRegimeEntry(regimenFiscal)?.code === item.code;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity key={item.code} style={[cfdiStyles.pickerOptionRow, selected && cfdiStyles.pickerOptionRowActive, isLightMode && selected && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF' }, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); setRegimenFiscal(item.es); setOpenPicker(null); }}>
                      <Text selectable={false} style={[cfdiStyles.pickerOptionTxt, { color: selected ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx(item.es, item.en)}</Text>
                      {selected && <Feather name="check" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />}
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            )}

            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
              <View style={{ width: 124 }}>
                <Text style={cfdiStyles.fieldLabel}>{tx('CÓDIGO POSTAL', 'POSTAL CODE')}</Text>
                <View style={cfdiStyles.inputBox}>
                  <Feather name="map-pin" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <TextInput
                    style={[cfdiStyles.inputText, { fontSize: 16 }]} value={codigoPostalFiscal}
                    onChangeText={(txt) => setCodigoPostalFiscal(txt.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 5))}
                    keyboardType="number-pad" autoCapitalize="none" importantForAutofill="no" maxLength={5}
                    placeholder={tx('Ej. 23450', 'e.g. 23450')} placeholderTextColor={attenuatedPlaceholder}
                  />
                </View>
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={cfdiStyles.fieldLabel}>{tx('USO DE CFDI', 'CFDI TAX USE')}</Text>
                <TouchableOpacity style={[cfdiStyles.inputBox, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); Keyboard.dismiss(); setOpenPicker((prev) => (prev === 'cfdi' ? null : 'cfdi')); }}>
                  <Feather name="check-square" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text selectable={false} style={cfdiStyles.inputLabelVal} numberOfLines={2}>{tx(getCfdiUseEntry(usoCfdi).es, getCfdiUseEntry(usoCfdi).en)}</Text>
                  <Feather name={openPicker === 'cfdi' ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
            {openPicker === 'cfdi' && (
              <View style={[cfdiStyles.pickerListCard, isLightMode && cfdiStyles.pickerListCardLight]}>
                {CFDI_USES_CATALOG.map((item) => {
                  const selected = getCfdiUseEntry(usoCfdi).code === item.code;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity key={item.code} style={[cfdiStyles.pickerOptionRow, selected && cfdiStyles.pickerOptionRowActive, isLightMode && selected && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF' }, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); setUsoCfdi(item.es); setOpenPicker(null); }}>
                      <Text selectable={false} style={[cfdiStyles.pickerOptionTxt, { color: selected ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx(item.es, item.en)}</Text>
                      {selected && <Feather name="check" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />}
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            )}

            <Text style={cfdiStyles.fieldLabel}>{tx('CORREO ELECTRÓNICO PARA ENVÍO DE XML Y PDF', 'EMAIL FOR XML & PDF INVOICE DELIVERY')}</Text>
            <View style={cfdiStyles.inputBox}>
              <Feather name="mail" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <TextInput
                style={[cfdiStyles.inputText, { fontSize: 16 }]} value={emailFacturacion}
                onChangeText={(txt) => setEmailFacturacion(txt.slice(0, 120))} keyboardType="email-address" autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect={false} maxLength={120}
                placeholder={tx('correo@kinetic.com', 'billing@kinetic.com')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
                onFocus={() => scheduleCfdiTimeout(() => scrollRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true }), 150)}
              />
            </View>

            {Boolean(inlineAlert) && (
              <View style={[cfdiStyles.inlineAlertBox, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFBEB', borderColor: '#D97706' }]}>
                <Feather name="alert-triangle" size={16} color={isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FFB300'} style={{ marginTop: 1 }} />
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text style={[cfdiStyles.inlineAlertTitle, { color: isLightMode ? '#92400E' : '#FFB300' }]}>{inlineAlert?.title}</Text>
                  <Text style={[cfdiStyles.inlineAlertMsg, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{inlineAlert?.msg}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            )}
          </ScrollView>

          <KineticErgonomicFooter
            onBack={() => onClose?.()}
            rightAction={{ label: isB2B ? tx('GUARDAR DATOS Y CLABE', 'SAVE TAX & CLABE DETAILS') : tx('GUARDAR FACTURACIÓN', 'SAVE BILLING'), icon: 'check', tone: 'cyan', loading: isSaving, onPress: handleSaveFiscalData }}
            style={{ marginHorizontal: -18 }}
          />
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    if (!visible) return null;
    const webOverlayAttrs: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } : {};
    const overlayPos = Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? ('fixed' as const) : ('absolute' as const);
    const webSize = Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? ({ width: '100vw', height: '100dvh' } as unknown as Record<string, string>) : {};
    const inlineWrap = (
      <View
        {...webOverlayAttrs}
        style={{ position: overlayPos, top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, ...webSize, zIndex: 9940, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5)' : 'rgba(5, 5, 5, 0.92)', justifyContent: 'flex-end', elevation: 0 }}
      >
        <Pressable style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }} onPress={onClose} />
        {sheetContent}
      </View>
    );
    return Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? renderInWebPortal(inlineWrap) : inlineWrap;
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(visible)} onBackdropPress={onClose} onBackButtonPress={onClose} avoidKeyboard={true}
      animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false} useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background} backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92} style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
    >
      {sheetContent}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export { HumanSupportModal, KINETIC_OFFICIAL_SUPPORT_EMAIL, KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP } from './BovedaSupportAndReferralModals';
export { KineticConciergeModal } from './boveda/KineticConciergeAndSupportModals';
export { ReferralModal, ReferralPromoModal } from './boveda/ReferralModals';

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaActivePassCard.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/BovedaActivePassCard.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Platform, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { baseBv, formatPassDate } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import {
  buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel,
  type DigitalPassRecordInput,
} from '../../utils/kineticDigitalPassViewModelLeaf';
import { AttentionShakeView, SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as Record<string, string>)
  : null;

export type ActivePassCardReserva = DigitalPassRecordInput & {
  estado?: string | null;
  asistio?: boolean | null;
  calificacion_estrellas?: number | null;
  calificacion?: number | null;
};

export interface IActivePassCardProps<T extends ActivePassCardReserva = ActivePassCardReserva> {
  reserva: T;
  isAdminPass?: boolean;
  onOpenQR: (res: T) => void;
  onRate?: (res: T) => void;
}

function ActivePassCardInner<T extends ActivePassCardReserva = ActivePassCardReserva>(
  { reserva, isAdminPass, onOpenQR, onRate }: IActivePassCardProps<T>,
) {
    const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, t, tx, td, tDisc, language } = useKineticPreferences();
    const bv = useMemo(
      () =>
        adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseBv, resolvedTheme, [
          'passCardOverlay',
          'passBadgePill',
          'passTimePill',
          'qrMiniActionBtn',
          'qrMiniActionText',
        ]),
      [resolvedTheme],
    );
    const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const vm = useMemo(
      () => buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel(reserva, { isEn, isAdminOverride: isAdminPass }),
      [reserva, isEn, isAdminPass],
    );
    const coverUri = resolveCategoryImage(vm.disciplineLabel, vm.sessionTitle);
    const fechaTxt = vm.fechaClaseIso ? formatPassDate(vm.fechaClaseIso, language) : vm.fechaDisplayLabel;
    const horaTxt = vm.scheduleLabel.split(' ')[0] || '08:00';
    const isCompleted = reserva?.estado === 'completada' || Boolean(reserva?.asistio);
    const ratingVal = reserva?.calificacion_estrellas || reserva?.calificacion || null;

    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={[
          bv.passCardWrap,
          localSt.cardSize,
          isLight && {
            backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
            borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
            borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
            borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
            shadowColor: '#0F172A',
            shadowOpacity: 0.08,
          },
          NO_SEL,
        ]}
        activeOpacity={0.88}
        onPress={() => {
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          if (isCompleted && onRate) onRate(reserva);
          else onOpenQR(reserva);
        }}
      >
        <View style={[bv.passCardTop, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9' }]}>
          <SmartImage source={{ uri: coverUri }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
          <View style={[bv.passCardOverlay, isLight && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)' }]} />
          <View style={bv.passBadgeRow}>
            <View
              style={[
                bv.passBadgePill,
                isLight && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94)', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
                vm.isMasterPass && { borderColor: isLight ? '#7C3AED' : COLORS.purple },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  bv.passBadgeLabel,
                  isLight && { color: '#0369A1' },
                  vm.isMasterPass && { color: isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#E0AAFF' },
                ]}
              >
                {isCompleted
                  ? tx('EVENTO COMPLETADO', 'EVENT COMPLETED')
                  : vm.isMasterPass
                    ? `${vm.headerTitle} • 0 KC`
                    : vm.headerTitle}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Text
              style={[
                bv.passTimePill,
                isLight && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94)', color: '#0F172A', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#E2E8F0' },
              ]}
            >
              {fechaTxt} • {horaTxt}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[localSt.cardBody, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF' }]}>
          <View style={localSt.topInfoRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, paddingRight: 8 }}>
              <Text style={[localSt.discTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {tDisc(vm.disciplineLabel).toUpperCase()}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[bv.passTitleText, isLight && { color: '#0F172A' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {td(vm.sessionTitle)}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[bv.passClubSub, isLight && { color: '#475569' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                📍 {vm.venueName}
              </Text>
            </View>
            {isCompleted ? (
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={(e) => {
                  e?.stopPropagation?.();
                  triggerHaptic('Selection');
                  if (onRate) onRate(reserva);
                  else onOpenQR(reserva);
                }}
                style={[
                  localSt.rateBtn,
                  isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFBEB', borderColor: '#F59E0B', borderTopColor: '#FDE68A', borderBottomColor: '#D97706' },
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
              >
                <Feather name="star" size={11} color={isLight ? '#D97706' : '#FBBF24'} />
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FDE68A' }}>
                  {ratingVal ? `★ ${ratingVal}/5 ★` : tx('CALIFICAR', 'RATE')}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : (
              <AttentionShakeView active={true}>
                <View style={[bv.qrMiniActionBtn, NO_SEL]} {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}>
                  <Feather name="maximize" size={12} color="#050505" />
                  <Text selectable={false} style={bv.qrMiniActionText}>{t('vault.open_qr')}</Text>
                </View>
              </AttentionShakeView>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={localSt.metaChipRow}>
            {vm.stationBadge && (
              <View style={[localSt.stationChip, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#059669' }]}>
                <Feather name="map-pin" size={10} color={isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green} />
                <Text style={[localSt.stationChipTxt, { color: isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green }]}>
                  {vm.stationBadge}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
            {vm.masterPassBadge && (
              <View style={[localSt.masterChip, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F5F3FF', borderColor: '#7C3AED' }]}>
                <Text style={[localSt.stationChipTxt, { color: isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#E0AAFF' }]}>
                  {vm.masterPassBadge}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          <Text style={[localSt.metaLine, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            🕒 {vm.scheduleLabel} • {vm.zoneAndCoachLabel}
          </Text>

          <View style={[localSt.splitBoxesRow, isLight && { borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
            <View style={[localSt.splitBox, isLight && localSt.splitBoxLight]}>
              <Text style={[localSt.splitBoxLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {vm.depositLabel}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[localSt.splitBoxVal, { color: vm.isMasterPass ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {vm.depositValueLabel}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[localSt.splitBox, isLight && localSt.splitBoxLight]}>
              <Text style={[localSt.splitBoxLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {vm.settlementLabel}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[localSt.splitBoxVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {vm.settlementValueLabel}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
}

export const ActivePassCard = memo(ActivePassCardInner) as typeof ActivePassCardInner;

const localSt = StyleSheet.create({
  cardSize: { width: 294 },
  cardBody: { padding: 12, gap: 6 },
  topInfoRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },
  discTxt: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7 },
  metaChipRow: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6 },
  stationChip: {
    flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, paddingHorizontal: 7, paddingVertical: 3, borderRadius: 7,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.12)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.45)', elevation: 0,
  },
  masterChip: {

    backgroundColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.16)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(224, 170, 255, 0.45)', elevation: 0,

  metaLine: { fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '700' },
  splitBoxesRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, paddingTop: 6, borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)' },
  splitBox: {
    flex: 1, minWidth: 0, borderRadius: 9, paddingVertical: 6, paddingHorizontal: 8, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)', elevation: 0,
  },

    backgroundColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.16)', borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: '#D97706', borderTopColor: '#FCD34D',

  },
});

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaAthleteModalsStack.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/BovedaAthleteModalsStack.tsx === //
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { KineticAuthProfile, KineticAuthUser } from '../../services/kineticAuthContextHelpers';
import { formatPassDate, getDisplayRole } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import {
  BillingCfdiModal,
  HumanSupportModal,
  KineticConciergeModal,
  MembershipModal,
  ProfileDossierModal,
  QRModal,
  ReceiptModal,
  ReferralModal,
  SettingsModal,
  TacticalAlertModal,
} from '../BovedaModals';
import { LegalTermsModal } from '../LegalTermsModal';
import { type IKineticStoryOwner, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KineticStoriesModal } from '../shared/KineticStoriesModal';
import { BovedaRateEventModal } from './BovedaRateEventModal';

export interface BovedaModalsAlertConfig {
  visible: boolean;
  title?: string;
  message?: string;
  type?: string;
  confirmText?: string;
  cancelText?: string;
  immediateConfirm?: boolean;
  onConfirm?: (() => void) | null;
}

export type BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn = (
  title: string,
  message: string,
  type?: string,
  onConfirm?: (() => void) | null,
  confirmText?: string,
  cancelText?: string
) => void;

export interface BovedaAthleteModalsStackProps {
  user: KineticAuthUser | null;
  profile: KineticAuthProfile | null;
  cleanVipLevel: string;
  isEn: boolean;
  athleteName: string;
  reservas: Record<string, unknown>[];
  completedCount: number;
  totalEscrowLockedKc: number;
  billingCycleInfo: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  isGhostMode: boolean;
  toggleGhostModeBoveda: (alertFn: BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn) => void;
  showTacticalAlert: BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  isDossierOpen: boolean;
  setIsDossierOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isSettingsOpen: boolean;
  setIsSettingsOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  settingsInitialSection?: 'preferencias' | 'legal' | 'seguridad';
  setSettingsInitialSection?: (s: 'preferencias' | 'legal' | 'seguridad') => void;
  returnToSettingsLegalRef?: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  isFiscalModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsFiscalModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isAiConciergeOpen: boolean;
  setIsAiConciergeOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isHumanSupportOpen: boolean;
  setIsHumanSupportOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isReferralOpen: boolean;
  setIsReferralOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  selectedQR: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  setSelectedQR: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => void;
  isQRReady: boolean;
  setIsQRReady: (v: boolean) => void;
  isWithinWindow: boolean;
  qrPayload: string;
  isRegeneratingQR: boolean;
  windowMessage: string;
  qrTimeLeft: number;
  isCanceling: boolean;
  getCostoTotal: (item: Record<string, unknown>) => number;
  cancelarReserva: (item: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
  selectedReceipt: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  setSelectedReceipt: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => void;
  exportReceiptToPdf: (rec: Record<string, unknown>, alertFn: BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn) => void;
  isExportingReceiptPdf: boolean;
  isMembershipModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsMembershipModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  handlePurchase: (plan: Record<string, unknown>, tipo?: 'membresia' | 'topup') => Promise<void> | void;
  isProcessingPayment: boolean;
  processingPlanId: string | null;
  isLegalModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsLegalModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  hasSignedWaiver: boolean;
  setHasSignedWaiver: (v: boolean) => void;
  isStoryOpen: boolean;
  setIsStoryOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  setAutoUploadStory: (v: boolean) => void;
  storyOwnerAthlete: IKineticStoryOwner;
  setHasActiveStoryRing: (v: boolean) => void;
  selectedRateReserva: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  setSelectedRateReserva?: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => void;
  onReservaRated?: (reservaId: string, stars: number, comment?: string) => void;
  alertConfig: BovedaModalsAlertConfig;
  setAlertConfig: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<BovedaModalsAlertConfig>>;
}

export const BovedaAthleteModalsStack = ({
  user, profile, cleanVipLevel, isEn, athleteName, reservas, completedCount, totalEscrowLockedKc,
  billingCycleInfo, isGhostMode, toggleGhostModeBoveda, showTacticalAlert, refreshProfile,
  isDossierOpen, setIsDossierOpen, isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen, settingsInitialSection,
  setSettingsInitialSection, returnToSettingsLegalRef, isFiscalModalOpen, setIsFiscalModalOpen,
  isAiConciergeOpen, setIsAiConciergeOpen, isHumanSupportOpen, setIsHumanSupportOpen,
  isReferralOpen, setIsReferralOpen, selectedQR, setSelectedQR, isQRReady, setIsQRReady,
  isWithinWindow, qrPayload, isRegeneratingQR, windowMessage, qrTimeLeft, isCanceling,
  getCostoTotal, cancelarReserva, selectedReceipt, setSelectedReceipt, exportReceiptToPdf,
  isExportingReceiptPdf, isMembershipModalOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen, handlePurchase,
  isProcessingPayment, processingPlanId, isLegalModalOpen, setIsLegalModalOpen,
  hasSignedWaiver, setHasSignedWaiver, isStoryOpen, setIsStoryOpen, setAutoUploadStory,
  storyOwnerAthlete, setHasActiveStoryRing, selectedRateReserva, setSelectedRateReserva,
  onReservaRated, alertConfig, setAlertConfig,
}: BovedaAthleteModalsStackProps) => {
  const [isMembershipCooling, setIsMembershipCooling] = useState<boolean>(Boolean(isMembershipModalOpen));
  const wasMembershipOpenRef = useRef<boolean>(Boolean(isMembershipModalOpen));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isMembershipModalOpen) {
      wasMembershipOpenRef.current = true;
      setIsMembershipCooling(true);
      return;
    }
    if (wasMembershipOpenRef.current) {
      wasMembershipOpenRef.current = false;
      setIsMembershipCooling(true);
      const t = setTimeout(() => setIsMembershipCooling(false), 185);
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }
    setIsMembershipCooling(false);
  }, [isMembershipModalOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (alertConfig?.visible && isMembershipModalOpen && !isProcessingPayment) {
      setIsMembershipModalOpen?.(false);
    }
  }, [alertConfig?.visible, isMembershipModalOpen, isProcessingPayment, setIsMembershipModalOpen]);

  const safeAlertConfig =
    alertConfig?.visible && (isMembershipModalOpen || isMembershipCooling)
      ? { ...alertConfig, visible: false }
      : alertConfig;

  const reopenLegalFromSettings = () => {
    if (returnToSettingsLegalRef?.current) {
      returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = false;
      setSettingsInitialSection?.('legal');
      setIsSettingsOpen(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <ProfileDossierModal
        visible={isDossierOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsDossierOpen(false)}
        user={user}
        profile={{ ...profile, nivel: cleanVipLevel }}
        isB2B={false}
        displayRole={getDisplayRole(
          typeof profile?.rol === 'string' ? profile.rol : undefined,
          typeof profile?.tipo_cuenta === 'string' ? profile.tipo_cuenta : undefined,
          typeof user?.email === 'string' ? user.email : undefined,
          isEn
        )}
        reservasCount={reservas.length}
        completedCount={completedCount}
        totalEscrowLockedKc={totalEscrowLockedKc}
        billingCycleInfo={billingCycleInfo}
        isGhostMode={isGhostMode}
        onToggleGhostMode={() => toggleGhostModeBoveda(showTacticalAlert)}
        onOpenSettings={() => setIsSettingsOpen(true)}
        onOpenMembership={() => setIsMembershipModalOpen(true)}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <SettingsModal
        visible={isSettingsOpen}
        initialSection={settingsInitialSection || 'preferencias'}
        onClose={() => {
          setIsSettingsOpen(false);
          setSettingsInitialSection?.('preferencias');
        }}
        user={user}
        profile={profile}
        isB2B={false}
        refreshProfile={refreshProfile}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        alreadySigned={hasSignedWaiver}
        onLegalAccepted={() => { setHasSignedWaiver(true); refreshProfile?.(); }}
        onOpenFiscal={() => {
          if (returnToSettingsLegalRef) returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = true;
          setIsSettingsOpen(false);
          setIsFiscalModalOpen(true);
        }}
        onOpenLegal={() => {
          if (returnToSettingsLegalRef) returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = true;
          setIsSettingsOpen(false);
          setIsLegalModalOpen(true);
        }}
        onOpenReferrals={() => setIsReferralOpen(true)}
        onOpenAiConcierge={() => setIsAiConciergeOpen(true)}
        onOpenHumanSupport={() => setIsHumanSupportOpen(true)}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <BillingCfdiModal
        visible={isFiscalModalOpen}
        onClose={() => { setIsFiscalModalOpen(false); reopenLegalFromSettings(); }}
        user={user}
        profile={profile}
        isB2B={false}
        refreshProfile={refreshProfile}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <KineticConciergeModal
        visible={isAiConciergeOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAiConciergeOpen(false)}
        isB2B={false}
        userName={athleteName}
        onSwitchToHuman={() => setIsHumanSupportOpen(true)}
      />
      <HumanSupportModal
        visible={isHumanSupportOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsHumanSupportOpen(false)}
        userName={athleteName}
        userEmail={typeof user?.email === 'string' ? user.email : undefined}
        isB2B={false}
        onSwitchToConcierge={() => setIsAiConciergeOpen(true)}
      />
      <ReferralModal
        visible={isReferralOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsReferralOpen(false)}
        user={user}
        profile={profile}
        refreshProfile={refreshProfile}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <QRModal
        selectedQR={selectedQR}
        isQRReady={isQRReady}
        isWithinWindow={isWithinWindow}
        isAdminPass={false}
        qrPayload={qrPayload}
        isRegeneratingQR={isRegeneratingQR}
        windowMessage={windowMessage}
        qrTimeLeft={qrTimeLeft}
        isCanceling={isCanceling}
        formatPassDate={formatPassDate}
        getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal}
        cancelarReserva={cancelarReserva}
        onClose={() => { setIsQRReady(false); setSelectedQR(null); }}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <ReceiptModal
        selectedReceipt={selectedReceipt}
        onClose={() => setSelectedReceipt(null)}
        formatPassDate={formatPassDate}
        getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal}
        athleteName={athleteName}
        onExportPdf={(rec: Record<string, unknown>) => exportReceiptToPdf(rec, showTacticalAlert)}
        isExportingPdf={isExportingReceiptPdf}
        onRated={onReservaRated}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <MembershipModal
        isMembershipModalOpen={isMembershipModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsMembershipModalOpen(false)}
        handlePurchase={handlePurchase}
        isProcessingPayment={isProcessingPayment}
        processingPlanId={processingPlanId}
        currentLevel={cleanVipLevel}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <LegalTermsModal
        isVisible={isLegalModalOpen}
        onClose={() => { setIsLegalModalOpen(false); reopenLegalFromSettings(); }}
        initialTab="atleta"
        userId={typeof user?.id === 'string' ? user.id : undefined}
        userName={athleteName}
        alreadySigned={hasSignedWaiver}
        onAccept={() => { setHasSignedWaiver(true); refreshProfile?.(); }}
      />
      <KineticStoriesModal
        visible={isStoryOpen}
        owner={storyOwnerAthlete}
        onStoriesUpdated={(hasStories: boolean) => setHasActiveStoryRing(hasStories)}
        onClose={() => { setIsStoryOpen(false); setAutoUploadStory(false); }}
      />
      <BovedaRateEventModal
        visible={Boolean(selectedRateReserva)}
        reserva={selectedRateReserva}
        onClose={() => setSelectedRateReserva?.(null)}
        onRated={onReservaRated}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
      />
      <TacticalAlertModal alertConfig={safeAlertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
    </>
  );
};

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaAthleteWalletAndModals.tsx

import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { localizeVipTierName } from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { baseBv } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KineticDeepParallaxCard3D } from '../shared/KineticDeepParallaxCard3D';

export { BovedaAthleteModalsStack } from './BovedaAthleteModalsStack';

export interface BovedaDualWalletCardProps {

const TOP_UP_DATA_SET: Record<string, unknown> =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'light', kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } : {};

export const BovedaDualWalletCard = ({
  saldoTotal,
  displayTier,
  kcMembresia,
  kcTopup,
  nextCutDateLabel,
  daysUntilCut,
  hasActiveMembership = false,
  totalEscrowLockedKc,
  isBalanceHydrating = false,
  isEscrowHydrating = false,
  perdonesMaximos,
  perdonesDisponibles,
  onOpenMembership,
}: BovedaDualWalletCardProps) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, language, t, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const bv = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseBv, resolvedTheme, ['topUpBtn', 'topUpBtnText']),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const tierLabel = localizeVipTierName(displayTier, isEn);
  const noSel: ViewStyle | null = Platform.OS === 'web'
    ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle)
    : null;

  return (
    <KineticDeepParallaxCard3D
      isLightMode={isLight}
      accentColor={COLORS.cyan}
      secondaryColor={COLORS.purple}
      maxOffsetPx={6}
      style={[
        bv.balanceCardAthlete,
        isLight && {

        )}

          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => {
            triggerHaptic('Selection');
            onOpenMembership('membresias');
          }}

            bv.walletSplitPill,
            !isLight && { elevation: 0 },
            isLight && {
              backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9',
              borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
              borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
            },

          ]}

            onOpenMembership('topup');

            bv.walletSplitPill,
            { borderColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.35)' },

              borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED',

            {tx('TOP-UP (ROLLOVER)', 'TOP-UP (ROLLOVER)')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[bv.walletSplitVal, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
            {isBalanceHydrating ? '— KC' : `${kcTopup} KC`}

            {tx('No caduca nunca', 'Never expires')}
          </Text>

      </View>
      <View style={bv.cutInfoStrip}>

          🕒 {hasActiveMembership ? tx('Corte:', 'Cycle:') : tx('Ciclo mensual:', 'Monthly cycle:')} {nextCutDateLabel} ({daysUntilCut}d)
        </Text>
        <Text style={[bv.cutInfoText, { color: themeColors.textMuted, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>

          marginBottom: 12,

            fontSize: 10,
            fontWeight: '800',
            textAlign: 'center',
          }}

          🛡️{' '}

            shadowOpacity: 0.36,
          },
          noSel,
        ]}
