    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaAtletaHistorySection.tsx

    import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { LayoutAnimation, Platform, Pressable, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  IRatedReservaEntry,
  formatLocalReservaDate,
  hydrateLocalRatingsMap,
  resolveReservaRating,
  subscribeRatingUpdates,
} from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { baseBv } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { BovedaRateEventModal } from './BovedaRateEventModal';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;

interface HistorySectionProps {
  historyReservations: any[];
  isHistorialExpanded: boolean;
  setIsHistorialExpanded: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
  setSelectedReceipt: (item: any) => void;
  getCostoTotal: (item: any) => number;
  onRateReserva?: (item: any) => void;
}

export const BovedaAtletaHistorySection = memo(
  ({
    historyReservations,
    isHistorialExpanded,
    setIsHistorialExpanded,
    setSelectedReceipt,
    getCostoTotal,
    onRateReserva,
  }: HistorySectionProps) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, language, t, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const bv = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseBv, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
    const [ratedCache, setRatedCache] = useState<Record<string, IRatedReservaEntry>>({});
    const [fallbackRateItem, setFallbackRateItem] = useState<any | null>(null);
    const [historyFilter, setHistoryFilter] = useState<'all' | 'completed' | 'noshow' | 'canceled'>('all');

    const historyCounts = useMemo(() => {
      let completed = 0, noshow = 0, canceled = 0;
      (historyReservations || []).forEach((item: any) => {
        const st = String(item?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (st === 'completada') completed += 1;
        else if (st === 'no_show' || st === 'penalizada' || st === 'penalizado') noshow += 1;
        else if (st === 'cancelada' || st === 'eliminada' || st === 'rechazada') canceled += 1;
      });
      return { all: (historyReservations || []).length, completed, noshow, canceled };
    }, [historyReservations]);

    const filteredHistory = useMemo(() => {
      if (historyFilter === 'all') return historyReservations || [];
      return (historyReservations || []).filter((item: any) => {
        const st = String(item?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (historyFilter === 'completed') return st === 'completada';
        if (historyFilter === 'noshow') return st === 'no_show' || st === 'penalizada' || st === 'penalizado';
        return st === 'cancelada' || st === 'eliminada' || st === 'rechazada';
      });
    }, [historyFilter, historyReservations]);

    useEffect(() => {
      hydrateLocalRatingsMap().then(setRatedCache).catch(() => {});
      return subscribeRatingUpdates((reservaId, stars, comment) => {
        setRatedCache((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          [reservaId]: { stars, comment, updatedAt: Date.now() },
        }));
      });
    }, [historyReservations]);

    return (
      <>
        <Pressable
          accessibilityRole="button"
          hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
          style={({ pressed }) => [
            bv.historyHeaderBtn,
            { width: '100%' },
            isLight && {
              backgroundColor: pressed ? '#F1F5F9' : '#FFFFFF',
              borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
              borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
            },
            !isLight && pressed && { opacity: 0.85 },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
          onPress={() => {
            triggerHaptic('Selection');
            if (Platform.OS !== 'web') LayoutAnimation.configureNext(LayoutAnimation.Presets.easeInEaseOut);
            setIsHistorialExpanded((p: boolean) => !p);
          }}
        >
          <View pointerEvents="none" style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flex: 1 }}>
            <Feather name="file-text" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[bv.historyHeaderTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {t('vault.history_passes')} ({historyReservations.length})
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Feather
            name={isHistorialExpanded ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'}
            size={18}
            color={themeColors.cyanText}
          />
        </Pressable>
        {isHistorialExpanded && (
          <View style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6, marginBottom: 10 }}>
              {(
                [
                  { k: 'all', lbl: `${tx('Todos', 'All')} (${historyCounts.all})` },
                  { k: 'completed', lbl: `${tx('Completados', 'Completed')} (${historyCounts.completed})` },
                  { k: 'noshow', lbl: `${tx('No-Shows', 'No-Shows')} (${historyCounts.noshow})` },
                  { k: 'canceled', lbl: `${tx('Cancelados', 'Canceled')} (${historyCounts.canceled})` },
                ] as const
              ).map((chip) => {
                const active = historyFilter === chip.k;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={chip.k}
                    activeOpacity={0.82}
                    onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setHistoryFilter(chip.k); }}
                    style={{
                      paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 5, borderRadius: 9, borderWidth: 1, elevation: 0,
                      borderColor: active ? (chip.k === 'noshow' ? '#F59E0B' : themeColors.cyanText) : (isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.14)'),
                      backgroundColor: active ? (chip.k === 'noshow' ? (isLight ? '#FEF3C7' : 'rgba(245,158,11,0.16)') : (isLight ? '#ECFEFF' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.12)')) : (isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)'),
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: active ? (chip.k === 'noshow' ? (isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24') : themeColors.cyanText) : themeColors.textMuted }}>
                      {chip.lbl}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>
            {filteredHistory.length === 0 ? (
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 11.5, textAlign: 'center', paddingVertical: 12 }}>
                {tx('Sin registros en esta categoría.', 'No records in this folder.')}
              </Text>
            ) : filteredHistory.map((item: any) => {
              const stLower = String(item.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
              const isCancel = stLower === 'cancelada';
              const isNoShow = stLower === 'no_show' || stLower === 'penalizada';
              const isCompleted = stLower === 'completada';
              const { stars: ratedStars, comment: ratedComment } = resolveReservaRating(item, ratedCache);
              const usedPerdon = Boolean(item.perdon_vip_aplicado);
              const totalVal = getCostoTotal(item);
              const rawEscrowDb = Number(item.monto_escrow || 0);
              const isRetainedCancel = isCancel && rawEscrowDb > 0 && !usedPerdon;
              const escrowSplitSum = Number(item.escrow_membresia || 0) + Number(item.escrow_topup || 0);
              const refundedEscrowVal =
                totalVal <= 0 && rawEscrowDb <= 0
                  ? 0
                  : isRetainedCancel
                  ? rawEscrowDb
                  : escrowSplitSum > 0
                  ? escrowSplitSum
                  : rawEscrowDb > 0
                  ? rawEscrowDb
                  : Math.round(totalVal * 0.4);
              const amountColor = isRetainedCancel || isNoShow
                ? isLight ? '#B45309' : COLORS.yellow
                : isCancel
                ? themeColors.cyanText
                : isLight ? '#059669' : COLORS.green;

              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={item.id}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  style={[
                    bv.historyItemRow,
                    !isLight && { elevation: 0 },
                    isLight && {
                      backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                      borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                      borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                      borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
                    },
                    NO_SEL,
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => setSelectedReceipt(item)}
                >
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
                    <Text style={[bv.passTitleText, isLight && { color: '#0F172A' }]}>
                      {item.servicio || item.nombre_servicio || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session')}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[bv.passClubSub, isLight && { color: '#475569' }]}>
                      {formatLocalReservaDate(item, language)} •{' '}
                      {item.negocio_nombre || tx('Kinetic Oficial', 'Kinetic Official')}
                    </Text>
                    {isCompleted && (
                      <View style={{ marginTop: 8, alignSelf: 'flex-start', maxWidth: '100%' }}>
                        <TouchableOpacity
                          activeOpacity={0.8}
                          onPress={(e) => {
                            e?.stopPropagation?.();
                            triggerHaptic('Selection');
                            if (onRateReserva) onRateReserva(item);
                            else setFallbackRateItem(item);
                          }}
                          style={[
                            {
                              alignSelf: 'flex-start',
                              flexDirection: 'row',
                              alignItems: 'center',
                              gap: 6,
                              paddingVertical: 5,
                              paddingHorizontal: 10,
                              borderRadius: 8,
                              borderWidth: 1.5,
                              elevation: 0,
                              borderColor: isLight ? '#F59E0B' : '#D97706',
                              borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FDE68A' : '#FCD34D',
                              borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#D97706' : '#78350F',
                              backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFBEB' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.14)',
                            },
                            NO_SEL,
                          ]}
                        >
                          <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, color: isLight ? '#D97706' : '#FBBF24' }}>★</Text>
                          <Text
                            style={{
                              fontSize: 10,
                              fontWeight: '900',
                              color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FDE68A',
                              letterSpacing: 0.3,
                            }}
                          >
                            {ratedStars
                              ? tx(`CALIFICADO: ★ ${ratedStars}/5 • EDITAR`, `RATED: ★ ${ratedStars}/5 • EDIT`)
                              : tx('CALIFICAR EXPERIENCIA (1 A 5 ★)', 'RATE EXPERIENCE (1 TO 5 ★)')}
                          </Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                        {ratedStars && ratedComment ? (
                          <Text
                            numberOfLines={2}
                            style={{
                              marginTop: 4,
                              fontSize: 10.5,
                              fontStyle: 'italic',
                              color: isLight ? '#64748B' : '#94A3B8',
                            }}
                          >
                            “{ratedComment}”
                          </Text>
                        ) : null}
                      </View>
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: amountColor,
                        fontSize: 13.5,
                        fontWeight: '900',
                        fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'],
                      }}
                    >
                      {isRetainedCancel || isNoShow
                        ? `-${refundedEscrowVal} KC`
                        : isCancel
                        ? totalVal <= 0 && refundedEscrowVal <= 0
                          ? '0 KC'
                          : `+${refundedEscrowVal} KC`
                        : `${totalVal} KC`}
                    </Text>
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: themeColors.textMuted,
                        fontSize: 9.5,
                        lineHeight: 15,
                        fontWeight: '800',
                        marginTop: 2,
                      }}
                    >
                      {isRetainedCancel
                        ? tx('ANTICIPO 40% RETENIDO • PDF ›', '40% DEPOSIT RETAINED • PDF ›')
                        : isCancel
                        ? totalVal <= 0 && refundedEscrowVal <= 0
                          ? tx('PASE MAESTRO CANCELADO (0 KC) • PDF ›', 'MASTER PASS CANCELLED (0 KC) • PDF ›')
                          : usedPerdon
                            ? tx('PERDÓN VIP • REEMBOLSO 40% ›', 'VIP FORGIVENESS • 40% REFUND ›')
                            : tx('REEMBOLSO 40% • PDF ›', '40% REFUND • PDF ›')
                        : isNoShow
                          ? tx('GARANTÍA 40% NO-SHOW • PDF ›', '40% NO-SHOW GUARANTEE • PDF ›')
                          : tx('RECIBO / PDF ›', 'RECEIPT / PDF ›')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        )}
        <BovedaRateEventModal
          visible={Boolean(fallbackRateItem)}
          reserva={fallbackRateItem}
          onClose={() => setFallbackRateItem(null)}
        />
      </>
    );
  },
);

export const DashboardB2B = ({ user, profile, insets, router }: BovedaB2BDashboardProps) => {
  const { refreshProfile, signOut } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, t, tx, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const bv = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseBv, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const noSel: ViewStyle | null = Platform.OS === 'web'
    ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle)
    : null;
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isFiscalModalOpen, setIsFiscalModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isDossierOpen, setIsDossierOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isLegalModalOpen, setIsLegalModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isAiConciergeOpen, setIsAiConciergeOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isHumanSupportOpen, setIsHumanSupportOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isStoryOpen, setIsStoryOpen] = useState(false);
  const [autoUploadStory, setAutoUploadStory] = useState(false);
  const [settingsInitialSection, setSettingsInitialSection] = useState<'preferencias' | 'legal' | 'seguridad'>('preferencias');
  const isSettingsOpenRef = useRef(false);
  isSettingsOpenRef.current = isSettingsOpen;
  const returnToSettingsLegalRef = useRef(false);
  const [selectedRateReserva, setSelectedRateReserva] = useState<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null);
  const [hasSignedWaiver, setHasSignedWaiver] = useState(Boolean(profile?.waiver_firmado));
  const [hasScrolled, setHasScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [isSigningOutTap, setIsSigningOutTap] = useState(false);
  const cancelBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<BovedaModalsAlertConfig>({
    visible: false, title: '', message: '', type: 'alert',
    confirmText: isEn ? 'GOT IT' : 'ENTENDIDO', cancelText: isEn ? 'STAY' : 'PERMANECER', onConfirm: null,
  });

  const showTacticalAlert = useCallback<BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn>(
    (title, message, type = 'alert', onConfirm = null, confirmText = isEn ? 'GOT IT' : 'ENTENDIDO', cancelText = isEn ? 'STAY' : 'PERMANECER') => {
      setAlertConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type, confirmText, cancelText, onConfirm });
    },
    [isEn]
  );

  const {
    hasHydratedB2B, refreshing, isProfileIncomplete, afData, allAfiliados, reservasB2B,
    metricas, masterPassSummary, ceoWeeklyReport, isAdmin, onRefresh, fetchB2BData,
  } = useDashboardB2B(user, profile);
  const [isExportingSpei, setIsExportingSpei] = useState(false);
  const b2bMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const {
    activeReservations, historyReservations, completedCount, totalEscrowLockedKc, isHistorialExpanded, setIsHistorialExpanded,
    selectedQR, setSelectedQR, isQRReady, setIsQRReady, selectedReceipt, setSelectedReceipt,
    isCanceling, setIsCanceling, qrTimeLeft, qrPayload, isRegeneratingQR, isWithinWindow, windowMessage,
    isExportingReceiptPdf, exportReceiptToPdf, getCostoTotal, fetchData: fetchAdminPasses,
  } = useBovedaAtleta(user, profile, refreshProfile);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      if (!hasUserScrolledRef.current) b2bScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
      fetchB2BData(false);
      fetchAdminPasses(true, false);
    }, [fetchAdminPasses, fetchB2BData]),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const reloadPasses = () => { void fetchAdminPasses(true, true); };
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(reloadPasses);
    const unsubRoot = subscribeKineticRootRefresh(reloadPasses);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function') {
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', reloadPasses);
      return () => { unsubRadar(); unsubRoot(); window.removeEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', reloadPasses); };
    }
    return () => { unsubRadar(); unsubRoot(); };
  }, [fetchAdminPasses]);

  const effectiveIsAdmin = Boolean(
    isAdmin || isKineticMasterAdmin(user?.email, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta),
  );
  const b2bScrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const hasUserScrolledRef = useRef(false);
  const b2bQrTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const b2bAlertTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => { hasUserScrolledRef.current = false; b2bScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false }); }, [user?.id]);
  useEffect(() => { if (user?.id) { fetchB2BData(true); if (!profile) refreshProfile?.(); } }, [user?.id, effectiveIsAdmin, profile, fetchB2BData, refreshProfile]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasUserScrolledRef.current) return;
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => { if (!hasUserScrolledRef.current) b2bScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false }); });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [hasHydratedB2B, profile?.id]);
  const scheduleB2BTimeout = useCallback((fn: () => void, ms: number) => {
    if (b2bAlertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(b2bAlertTimerRef.current);
    b2bAlertTimerRef.current = setTimeout(fn, ms);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    b2bMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      b2bMountedRef.current = false;
      if (b2bQrTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(b2bQrTimerRef.current);
      if (b2bAlertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(b2bAlertTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  const anyModalOpen = isSettingsOpen || isFiscalModalOpen || isDossierOpen || isLegalModalOpen || isAiConciergeOpen || isHumanSupportOpen || isStoryOpen || Boolean(selectedQR) || Boolean(selectedReceipt) || Boolean(selectedRateReserva) || Boolean(alertConfig?.visible);
  const displayRole = getDisplayRole(profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.email, isEn);
  const activeName = resolveOfficialKineticName(profile?.nombre, effectiveIsAdmin, isEn ? 'Affiliate Partner' : 'Socio Afiliado');
  const handlePullRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    b2bScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true });
    await Promise.allSettled([onRefresh(), fetchAdminPasses(true, true), refreshProfile?.()]);
  }, [onRefresh, fetchAdminPasses, refreshProfile]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    if (profile?.waiver_firmado) { setHasSignedWaiver(true); return () => { active = false; }; }
    setHasSignedWaiver(false);
    if (user?.id) {
      AsyncStorage.getItem(getWaiverStorageKey(user.id)).then((val) => { if (active) setHasSignedWaiver(Boolean(val)); }).catch(() => { if (active) setHasSignedWaiver(false); });
    }
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [profile?.waiver_firmado, user?.id]);

  const storyOwnerB2B = useMemo<IKineticStoryOwner>(() => ({
    id: String(user?.id || activeName), name: activeName, subtitle: `${displayRole} • ${isEn ? 'Kinetic Network' : 'Red Kinetic'}`,
    avatarUrl: profile?.avatar_url || null, isOfficialKinetic: effectiveIsAdmin, autoStartUpload: autoUploadStory, discipline: 'Fuerza', canUpload: true,
  }), [user?.id, activeName, displayRole, profile?.avatar_url, effectiveIsAdmin, autoUploadStory, isEn]);

  const handleCancelPass = useCallback(async (reservaTarget: Record<string, unknown>) => {
    const uid = typeof user?.id === 'string' ? user.id : '';
    const rid = reservaTarget?.id != null ? String(reservaTarget.id) : '';
    if (!rid || !uid || cancelBusyRef.current) return;
    cancelBusyRef.current = true;
    const reembolso = Number(reservaTarget.monto_escrow) || 0;
    setIsCanceling(true);
    try {
      let resMsg = '';
      try {
        const res = await bovedaService.cancelarReserva(rid, uid, reembolso);
        resMsg = res?.message || '';
      } catch (innerErr: unknown) {
        if (reembolso <= 0) {
          const { data: fbRows, error: fbErr } = await supabase
            .from('reservas')
            .update({ estado: 'cancelada' })
            .eq('id', rid)
            .eq('usuario_id', uid)
            .eq('monto_escrow', 0)
            .select('id');
          if (fbErr || !fbRows?.length) throw innerErr;
        } else {
          throw innerErr;
        }
      }
      notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
      await Promise.all([fetchAdminPasses(true, true), refreshProfile?.()]);
      setIsQRReady(false);
      setSelectedQR(null);
      triggerHaptic('Success');
      scheduleB2BTimeout(() => showTacticalAlert(
        tx('PASE CANCELADO', 'PASS CANCELLED'),
        isEn ? 'Reservation released successfully.' : (resMsg || 'La reserva fue liberada correctamente.'),
        'success'
      ), 180);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
      setIsQRReady(false);
      setSelectedQR(null);
      scheduleB2BTimeout(() => showTacticalAlert(
        tx('FRICCIÓN', 'NOTICE'),
        isEn ? 'Could not cancel the pass.' : (msg || 'No se pudo cancelar el pase.'),
        'error'
      ), 180);
    } finally {
      cancelBusyRef.current = false;
      setIsCanceling(false);
    }
  }, [user?.id, setIsCanceling, fetchAdminPasses, refreshProfile, setIsQRReady, setSelectedQR, scheduleB2BTimeout, showTacticalAlert, tx, isEn]);

  const handleSignOut = () => {
    if (isSigningOutTap) return;
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    setAlertConfig({
      visible: true,
      title: t('vault.sign_out_operator'),
      message: t('vault.sign_out_confirm_q'),
      type: 'confirm',
      confirmText: t('vault.sign_out'),
      cancelText: t('vault.stay'),
      immediateConfirm: true,
      onConfirm: async () => {
        setIsSigningOutTap(true);
        await yieldNextUiFrame();
        markExplicitKineticSignOut();
        if (b2bQrTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(b2bQrTimerRef.current);
        if (b2bAlertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(b2bAlertTimerRef.current);
        setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false, onConfirm: null }));
        setIsSettingsOpen(false); setIsFiscalModalOpen(false); setIsDossierOpen(false);
        setIsLegalModalOpen(false); setIsAiConciergeOpen(false);
        setIsHumanSupportOpen(false); setIsStoryOpen(false); setIsQRReady(false);
        setSelectedQR(null); setSelectedReceipt(null); setSelectedRateReserva(null);
        try { await signOut?.(); } catch {} finally { setIsSigningOutTap(false); }
      },
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const openFn = () => {
      try {
        if (isSettingsOpenRef.current) {
          returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = true;
          setIsSettingsOpen(false);
        }
        setIsFiscalModalOpen(true);
      } catch {}
    };
    const gObj = globalThis as Record<string, unknown>;
    gObj.__kineticOpenBovedaBilling = openFn;
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.addEventListener('kinetic:open-boveda-billing', openFn);
    return () => {
      if (gObj.__kineticOpenBovedaBilling === openFn) {
        delete gObj.__kineticOpenBovedaBilling;
      }
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.removeEventListener('kinetic:open-boveda-billing', openFn);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor={themeColors.background} />
      <View
        style={[
          bv.topBar,
          {
            paddingTop: insets.top + 14,
            backgroundColor: themeColors.background,
            borderBottomWidth: hasScrolled ? 1 : 0,
            borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.22)',
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View>
          <Text style={[bv.headerSub, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{t('vault.exec_sub')}</Text>
          <Text style={[bv.headerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {effectiveIsAdmin ? t('vault.master_title') : t('vault.b2b_title')}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <TouchableOpacity
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel={tx('⚙️ AJUSTES', '⚙️ SETTINGS')}
          style={[
            bv.storyTopBtn,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' },
            noSel,
          ]}
          onPress={() => {
            if (anyModalOpen) return;
            triggerHaptic('Light');
            setSettingsInitialSection('preferencias');
            returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = false;
            setIsSettingsOpen(true);
          }}
        >
          <Text selectable={false} style={[bv.storyTopBtnText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('⚙️ AJUSTES', '⚙️ SETTINGS')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        ref={b2bScrollRef}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        onScroll={(e) => {
          const scrolled = Number(e?.nativeEvent?.contentOffset?.y || 0) > 4;
          if (scrolled) hasUserScrolledRef.current = true;
          setHasScrolled((prev) => (prev === scrolled ? prev : scrolled));
        }}
        contentContainerStyle={[bv.scrollContent, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 120 }]}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={handlePullRefresh}
            tintColor="#00F0FF"
            colors={['#00F0FF']}
            progressBackgroundColor={isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#080C12'}
          />
        }
      >
        <BovedaClockwiseRefreshIndicator visible={refreshing} isLight={isLight} />
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            bv.identityCard,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
            noSel,
          ]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => { if (!anyModalOpen) setIsDossierOpen(true); }}
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[bv.avatarCircleBox, noSel]}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={(e) => {
              e?.stopPropagation?.();
              if (anyModalOpen) return;
              triggerHaptic('Light');
              setAutoUploadStory(false);
              setIsStoryOpen(true);
            }}
          >
            {profile?.avatar_url ? (
              <SmartImage source={{ uri: profile.avatar_url }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
            ) : (
              <Image source={KINETIC_LOGO} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 14 }}>
            <Text style={[bv.identityName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{activeName}</Text>
            <Text style={[bv.identityEmail, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>{user?.email || ''}</Text>
            <View style={[bv.rolePill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
              <Text style={[bv.rolePillText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{displayRole}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          <Feather name="chevron-right" size={18} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <BovedaB2BRevenueCard
          bv={bv}
          effectiveIsAdmin={effectiveIsAdmin}
          hasHydratedB2B={hasHydratedB2B}
          isProfileIncomplete={isProfileIncomplete}
          metricas={metricas}
          afData={afData}
          allAfiliados={allAfiliados}
          reservasB2B={reservasB2B}
          masterPassSummary={masterPassSummary}
          ceoWeeklyReport={ceoWeeklyReport}
          activeName={activeName}
          user={user}
          profile={profile}
          isExportingSpei={isExportingSpei}
          setIsExportingSpei={setIsExportingSpei}
          onOpenFiscalModal={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = false; setIsFiscalModalOpen(true); }}
          showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
          mountedRef={b2bMountedRef}
        />

        <BovedaB2BPassesAndHistoryBlock
          bv={bv} activeReservations={activeReservations} historyReservations={historyReservations}
          isHistorialExpanded={isHistorialExpanded} setIsHistorialExpanded={setIsHistorialExpanded}
          isAdminPass={effectiveIsAdmin} isCanceling={isCanceling} getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal}
          onInstantOpenQr={(r) => { if (anyModalOpen) return; setSelectedQR(r); setIsQRReady(true); }}
          onOpenPassQr={(r) => { if (anyModalOpen) return; setSelectedQR(r); if (b2bQrTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(b2bQrTimerRef.current); b2bQrTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setIsQRReady(true), 120); }}
          onCancelPass={handleCancelPass} setSelectedReceipt={(rec: Record<string, unknown> | null) => { if (!anyModalOpen || rec === null) setSelectedReceipt(rec); }}
          onRateReserva={(res: Record<string, unknown> | null) => { if (!anyModalOpen || res === null) setSelectedRateReserva(res); }} onExploreEvents={() => router.push('/(tabs)/eventos' as never)}
        />

        <Text style={[bv.sectionTitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
          {tx('CONCIERGE Y SOPORTE', 'CONCIERGE & SUPPORT')}
        </Text>
        <UnifiedMenuSection
          isB2B={true}
          hasSignedWaiver={hasSignedWaiver}
          onOpenSettings={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Light'); setIsSettingsOpen(true); }}
          onOpenFiscal={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Light'); setIsFiscalModalOpen(true); }}
          onOpenLegal={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Light'); setIsLegalModalOpen(true); }}
          onOpenConcierge={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Light'); setIsAiConciergeOpen(true); }}
          onOpenSupport={() => { if (anyModalOpen) return; triggerHaptic('Light'); setIsHumanSupportOpen(true); }}
        />
        <Pressable
          accessibilityRole="button"
          onPress={handleSignOut}
          disabled={isSigningOutTap}
          {...LOGOUT_DATA_SET}
          style={({ pressed }) => [
            bv.logoutBtn,
            { backgroundColor: pressed ? '#991B1B' : '#DC2626', borderColor: pressed ? '#EF4444' : '#F87171', borderTopColor: pressed ? '#F87171' : '#FCA5A5', borderBottomColor: pressed ? '#450A0A' : '#7F1D1D', opacity: isSigningOutTap ? 0.8 : 1, elevation: 0 },
            pressed && { transform: [{ translateY: 1.5 }] },
            noSel,
          ]}
        >
          {isSigningOutTap ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#FFFFFF" />
          ) : (
            <Feather name="log-out" size={16} color="#FFFFFF" />
          )}
          <Text selectable={false} style={[bv.logoutText, { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>{t('vault.sign_out_operator')}</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={[bv.footerVersionText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{KINETIC_APP_VERSION}</Text>
      </ScrollView>

      <BovedaB2BModalsStack
        user={user} profile={profile} refreshProfile={refreshProfile} displayRole={displayRole}
        activeName={activeName} effectiveIsAdmin={effectiveIsAdmin} metricas={metricas}
        activeReservations={activeReservations} completedCount={completedCount} totalEscrowLockedKc={totalEscrowLockedKc}
        isDossierOpen={isDossierOpen} setIsDossierOpen={setIsDossierOpen} isSettingsOpen={isSettingsOpen} setIsSettingsOpen={setIsSettingsOpen}
        settingsInitialSection={settingsInitialSection} setSettingsInitialSection={setSettingsInitialSection}
        returnToSettingsLegalRef={returnToSettingsLegalRef} fetchB2BData={fetchB2BData} scheduleB2BTimeout={scheduleB2BTimeout}
        hasSignedWaiver={hasSignedWaiver} setHasSignedWaiver={setHasSignedWaiver} showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        isFiscalModalOpen={isFiscalModalOpen} setIsFiscalModalOpen={setIsFiscalModalOpen} isAiConciergeOpen={isAiConciergeOpen}
        setIsAiConciergeOpen={setIsAiConciergeOpen} isHumanSupportOpen={isHumanSupportOpen} setIsHumanSupportOpen={setIsHumanSupportOpen}
        isLegalModalOpen={isLegalModalOpen} setIsLegalModalOpen={setIsLegalModalOpen} selectedQR={selectedQR} setSelectedQR={setSelectedQR}
        isQRReady={isQRReady} setIsQRReady={setIsQRReady} isWithinWindow={isWithinWindow} qrPayload={qrPayload}
        isRegeneratingQR={isRegeneratingQR} windowMessage={windowMessage} qrTimeLeft={qrTimeLeft} isCanceling={isCanceling}
        getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal} handleCancelPass={handleCancelPass} selectedReceipt={selectedReceipt} setSelectedReceipt={setSelectedReceipt}
        exportReceiptToPdf={exportReceiptToPdf} isExportingReceiptPdf={isExportingReceiptPdf} selectedRateReserva={selectedRateReserva}
        setSelectedRateReserva={setSelectedRateReserva} isStoryOpen={isStoryOpen} setIsStoryOpen={setIsStoryOpen}
        setAutoUploadStory={setAutoUploadStory} storyOwnerB2B={storyOwnerB2B} alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaB2BModalsStack.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/BovedaB2BModalsStack.tsx === //
import React from 'react';
import type { KineticAuthProfile, KineticAuthUser } from '../../services/kineticAuthContextHelpers';
import { formatPassDate } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import {
  BillingCfdiModal,
  HumanSupportModal,
  KineticConciergeModal,
  ProfileDossierModal,
  QRModal,
  ReceiptModal,
  SettingsModal,
  TacticalAlertModal,
} from '../BovedaModals';
import { LegalTermsModal } from '../LegalTermsModal';
import { type IKineticStoryOwner, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KineticStoriesModal } from '../shared/KineticStoriesModal';
import { BovedaRateEventModal } from './BovedaRateEventModal';
import type { BovedaModalsAlertConfig, BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn } from './BovedaAthleteModalsStack';

export interface BovedaB2BModalsStackProps {
  user: KineticAuthUser | null;
  profile: KineticAuthProfile | null;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  displayRole: string;
  activeName: string;
  effectiveIsAdmin: boolean;
  metricas: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  activeReservations: Record<string, unknown>[];
  completedCount: number;
  totalEscrowLockedKc: number;
  isDossierOpen: boolean;
  setIsDossierOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isSettingsOpen: boolean;
  setIsSettingsOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  settingsInitialSection: 'preferencias' | 'legal' | 'seguridad';
  setSettingsInitialSection: (s: 'preferencias' | 'legal' | 'seguridad') => void;
  returnToSettingsLegalRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  fetchB2BData: (silent?: boolean) => void;
  scheduleB2BTimeout: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => void;
  hasSignedWaiver: boolean;
  setHasSignedWaiver: (v: boolean) => void;
  showTacticalAlert: BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn;
  isFiscalModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsFiscalModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isAiConciergeOpen: boolean;
  setIsAiConciergeOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isHumanSupportOpen: boolean;
  setIsHumanSupportOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isLegalModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsLegalModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  selectedQR: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  setSelectedQR: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => void;
  isQRReady: boolean;
  setIsQRReady: (v: boolean) => void;
  isWithinWindow: boolean;
  qrPayload: string;
  isRegeneratingQR: boolean;
  windowMessage: string;
  qrTimeLeft: number;
  isCanceling: boolean;
  getCostoTotal: (item: Record<string, unknown>) => number;
  handleCancelPass: (reservaTarget: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<void>;
  selectedReceipt: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  setSelectedReceipt: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => void;
  exportReceiptToPdf: (rec: Record<string, unknown>, alertFn: BovedaShowTacticalAlertFn) => void;
  isExportingReceiptPdf: boolean;
  selectedRateReserva: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  setSelectedRateReserva: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => void;
  isStoryOpen: boolean;
  setIsStoryOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  setAutoUploadStory: (v: boolean) => void;
  storyOwnerB2B: IKineticStoryOwner;
  alertConfig: BovedaModalsAlertConfig;
  setAlertConfig: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<BovedaModalsAlertConfig>>;
}

export const BovedaB2BModalsStack = ({
  user, profile, refreshProfile, displayRole, activeName, effectiveIsAdmin, metricas,
  activeReservations, completedCount, totalEscrowLockedKc, isDossierOpen, setIsDossierOpen,
  isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen, settingsInitialSection, setSettingsInitialSection,
  returnToSettingsLegalRef, fetchB2BData, scheduleB2BTimeout, hasSignedWaiver, setHasSignedWaiver,
  showTacticalAlert, isFiscalModalOpen, setIsFiscalModalOpen, isAiConciergeOpen, setIsAiConciergeOpen,
  isHumanSupportOpen, setIsHumanSupportOpen, isLegalModalOpen, setIsLegalModalOpen,
  selectedQR, setSelectedQR, isQRReady, setIsQRReady, isWithinWindow, qrPayload, isRegeneratingQR,
  windowMessage, qrTimeLeft, isCanceling, getCostoTotal, handleCancelPass, selectedReceipt,
  setSelectedReceipt, exportReceiptToPdf, isExportingReceiptPdf, selectedRateReserva,
  setSelectedRateReserva, isStoryOpen, setIsStoryOpen, setAutoUploadStory, storyOwnerB2B,
  alertConfig, setAlertConfig,
}: BovedaB2BModalsStackProps) => {
  const reopenLegalFromSettings = () => {
    if (returnToSettingsLegalRef.current) {
      returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = false;
      setSettingsInitialSection('legal');
      setIsSettingsOpen(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <ProfileDossierModal
        visible={isDossierOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsDossierOpen(false)}
        user={user}
        profile={profile}
        isB2B={true}
        displayRole={displayRole}
        reservasCount={activeReservations.length}
        completedCount={completedCount}
        totalEscrowLockedKc={totalEscrowLockedKc}
        metricas={metricas}
        onOpenSettings={() => {
          setIsDossierOpen(false);
          scheduleB2BTimeout(() => setIsSettingsOpen(true), 220);
        }}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <SettingsModal
        visible={isSettingsOpen}
        initialSection={settingsInitialSection}
        onClose={() => { setIsSettingsOpen(false); setSettingsInitialSection('preferencias'); fetchB2BData(); }}
        user={user}
        profile={profile}
        isB2B={true}
        refreshProfile={refreshProfile}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        alreadySigned={hasSignedWaiver}
        onLegalAccepted={() => { setHasSignedWaiver(true); refreshProfile?.(); }}
        onOpenFiscal={() => { returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = true; setIsSettingsOpen(false); setIsFiscalModalOpen(true); }}
        onOpenLegal={() => { returnToSettingsLegalRef.current = true; setIsSettingsOpen(false); setIsLegalModalOpen(true); }}
        onOpenAiConcierge={() => setIsAiConciergeOpen(true)}
        onOpenHumanSupport={() => setIsHumanSupportOpen(true)}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <BillingCfdiModal
        visible={isFiscalModalOpen}
        onClose={() => { setIsFiscalModalOpen(false); fetchB2BData(); reopenLegalFromSettings(); }}
        user={user}
        profile={profile}
        isB2B={true}
        refreshProfile={refreshProfile}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <KineticConciergeModal
        visible={isAiConciergeOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAiConciergeOpen(false)}
        isB2B={true}
        userName={activeName}
        onSwitchToHuman={() => setIsHumanSupportOpen(true)}
      />
      <HumanSupportModal
        visible={isHumanSupportOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsHumanSupportOpen(false)}
        userName={activeName}
        userEmail={typeof user?.email === 'string' ? user.email : ''}
        isB2B={true}
        onSwitchToConcierge={() => setIsAiConciergeOpen(true)}
      />
      <QRModal
        selectedQR={selectedQR}
        isQRReady={isQRReady}
        isWithinWindow={isWithinWindow}
        isAdminPass={effectiveIsAdmin}
        qrPayload={qrPayload}
        isRegeneratingQR={isRegeneratingQR}
        windowMessage={windowMessage}
        qrTimeLeft={qrTimeLeft}
        isCanceling={isCanceling}
        formatPassDate={formatPassDate}
        getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal}
        cancelarReserva={handleCancelPass}
        onClose={() => { setIsQRReady(false); setSelectedQR(null); }}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <ReceiptModal
        selectedReceipt={selectedReceipt}
        onClose={() => setSelectedReceipt(null)}
        formatPassDate={formatPassDate}
        getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal}
        athleteName={activeName}
        onExportPdf={(rec: Record<string, unknown>) => exportReceiptToPdf(rec, showTacticalAlert)}
        isExportingPdf={isExportingReceiptPdf}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
      <BovedaRateEventModal
        visible={Boolean(selectedRateReserva)}
        reserva={selectedRateReserva}
        onClose={() => setSelectedRateReserva(null)}
      />
      <LegalTermsModal
        isVisible={isLegalModalOpen}
        onClose={() => { setIsLegalModalOpen(false); reopenLegalFromSettings(); }}
        initialTab="b2b"
        userId={typeof user?.id === 'string' ? user.id : undefined}
        userName={activeName}
        alreadySigned={hasSignedWaiver}
        onAccept={() => { setHasSignedWaiver(true); refreshProfile?.(); }}
      />
      <KineticStoriesModal
        visible={isStoryOpen}
        owner={storyOwnerB2B}
        onClose={() => { setIsStoryOpen(false); setAutoUploadStory(false); }}
      />
      <TacticalAlertModal alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
    </>
  );
};

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaB2BPassesAndHistoryBlock.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/BovedaB2BPassesAndHistoryBlock.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { KineticDualConfirmModal } from '../shared/KineticDualConfirmModal';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { AutoPopReceptionQrBanner } from '../shared/KineticDisruptiveWidgets';
import { BovedaAtletaHistorySection } from './BovedaAtletaHistorySection';
import { ActivePassCard } from './BovedaActivePassCard';

type B2BPassRow = Record<string, unknown> & {
  id?: string | number | null;
  servicio?: string | null;
  nombre_servicio?: string | null;
  servicios?: { titulo?: string | null } | null;
};

interface IBovedaB2BPassesAndHistoryProps {
  bv: Record<string, unknown>;
  activeReservations: B2BPassRow[];
  historyReservations: B2BPassRow[];
  isHistorialExpanded: boolean;
  setIsHistorialExpanded: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
  isAdminPass: boolean;
  isCanceling: boolean;
  getCostoTotal: (r: B2BPassRow) => number;
  onInstantOpenQr: (r: B2BPassRow) => void;
  onOpenPassQr: (r: B2BPassRow) => void;
  onCancelPass: (r: B2BPassRow) => void;
  setSelectedReceipt: (r: B2BPassRow) => void;
  onRateReserva: (r: B2BPassRow) => void;
  onExploreEvents: () => void;
}

export const BovedaB2BPassesAndHistoryBlock: React.FC<IBovedaB2BPassesAndHistoryProps> = React.memo(({
  bv, activeReservations, historyReservations, isHistorialExpanded, setIsHistorialExpanded,
  isAdminPass, isCanceling, getCostoTotal, onInstantOpenQr, onOpenPassQr,
  onCancelPass, setSelectedReceipt, onRateReserva, onExploreEvents,
}) => {
    const { themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
    const [pendingCancelPass, setPendingCancelPass] = useState<B2BPassRow | null>(null);
    const noSel: Record<string, string> | undefined =
      Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : undefined;

    if (!isAdminPass && historyReservations.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    return (
      <View style={styles.wrapper}>
        {isAdminPass && (
          <>
            <Text style={[(bv as { sectionTitle?: object }).sectionTitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('MIS PASES ACTIVOS (PASE QR DINÁMICO)', 'MY ACTIVE PASSES (DYNAMIC QR PASS)')}
              {activeReservations.length > 0 ? ` (${activeReservations.length})` : ''}
            </Text>

            {activeReservations.length > 0 ? (
              <View style={styles.activeBlock}>
                <AutoPopReceptionQrBanner
                  primaryReservation={activeReservations[0] as never}
                  reservations={activeReservations as never}
                  activeCount={activeReservations.length}
                  onInstantOpenQr={onInstantOpenQr as never}
                />
                <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.carouselRow}>
                  {activeReservations.map((res) => (
                    <View key={String(res.id)} style={styles.passColumn}>
                      <ActivePassCard reserva={res} isAdminPass={isAdminPass} onOpenQR={onOpenPassQr} />
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        accessibilityRole="button"
                        disabled={isCanceling}
                        activeOpacity={0.82}
                        style={[
                          styles.quickCancelBtn,
                          isLight && styles.quickCancelBtnLight,
                          isCanceling && { opacity: 0.55 },
                          noSel,
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => {
                          triggerHaptic('Warning');
                          setPendingCancelPass(res);
                        }}
                      >
                        <Feather name="x-circle" size={13} color={isLight ? '#B91C1C' : '#FCA5A5'} />
                        <Text
                          selectable={false}
                          numberOfLines={1}
                          adjustsFontSizeToFit
                          minimumFontScale={0.8}
                          style={[styles.quickCancelTxt, isLight && { color: '#B91C1C' }]}
                        >
                          {tx('Cancelar Reserva', 'Cancel Reservation')}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                </ScrollView>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.emptyCard,
                  isLight && {
                    backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                    borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
                  },
                  noSel,
                ]}
              >
                <View style={[styles.emptyIconCircle, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4' }]}>
                  <Feather name="shield" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                </View>
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                    {tx('Sin pases activos reservados', 'No active passes booked')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.emptySub, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                    {tx(
                      'Reserva en Eventos o Explorar para activar tu Pase QR Dinámico 60s aquí.',
                      'Book in Events or Explore to activate your live 60s Dynamic QR Pass here.'
                    )}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  accessibilityRole="button"
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  style={[styles.exploreBtn, noSel]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    onExploreEvents();
                  }}
                >
                  <Text selectable={false} style={styles.exploreBtnTxt}>
                    {tx('EVENTOS', 'EVENTS')}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}
          </>
        )}

        <BovedaAtletaHistorySection
          historyReservations={historyReservations}
          isHistorialExpanded={isHistorialExpanded}
          setIsHistorialExpanded={setIsHistorialExpanded}
          setSelectedReceipt={setSelectedReceipt}
          getCostoTotal={getCostoTotal}
          onRateReserva={onRateReserva}
        />

        <KineticDualConfirmModal
          visible={Boolean(pendingCancelPass)}
          title={tx('Cancelar Reserva', 'Cancel Reservation')}
          message={tx(
            'Tu lugar reservado se liberará sin cargos si es un Pase Maestro (0 KC) o conforme a la política del Anticipo Protegido (40%).',
            'Your reserved spot will be released free if it is a Master Pass (0 KC) or per the Protected Deposit (40%) policy.'
          )}
          confirmLabel={tx('Cancelar Reserva', 'Cancel Reservation')}
          cancelLabel={tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel')}
          tone="amber"
          loading={isCanceling}
          onConfirm={() => {
            const target = pendingCancelPass;
            setPendingCancelPass(null);
            if (target) onCancelPass(target);
          }}
          onCancel={() => setPendingCancelPass(null)}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }
);

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrapper: { marginBottom: 6 },
  activeBlock: { marginBottom: 14 },
  carouselRow: { gap: 12, paddingVertical: 2 },
  passColumn: { gap: 8 },
  quickCancelBtn: {
    flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6,
    paddingVertical: 8, paddingHorizontal: 12, borderRadius: 11, backgroundColor: '#240B0E',
    borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#EF4444', borderTopWidth: 1.6, borderTopColor: '#FCA5A5',
    borderBottomWidth: 3.5, borderBottomColor: '#7F1D1D',
  },
  quickCancelBtnLight: {
    backgroundColor: '#FEF2F2', borderColor: '#F87171', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#B91C1C',
  },
  quickCancelTxt: { color: '#FCA5A5', fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7 },
  emptyCard: {
    flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: 14, marginBottom: 14, borderRadius: 16,
    backgroundColor: '#09111A', borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#1E293B', borderTopWidth: 1.8,
    borderTopColor: '#334155', borderBottomWidth: 4, borderBottomColor: '#020617',
  },
  emptyIconCircle: {
    width: 38, height: 38, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
    backgroundColor: '#0A1E26', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#155E75',
  },
  emptyTitle: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900', marginBottom: 2 },
  emptySub: { fontSize: 11, lineHeight: 15, fontWeight: '600' },
  exploreBtn: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 9, borderRadius: 10, backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan,
    borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#67E8F9', borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: '#CFFAFE',
    borderBottomWidth: 3.8, borderBottomColor: '#0E7490',
  },
  exploreBtnTxt: { color: '#050505', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7 },
});

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaB2BRevenueCard.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/BovedaB2BRevenueCard.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { calcularCorteSemanalB2B } from '../../services/adminB2BCutEngine';
import {
  exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado,
  exportarPDFReporteMaestroCEO,
} from '../../services/adminPdfTemplates';
import {
  buildCeoConsolidatedWeeklyReport,
  EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY,
  formatVenueCountLabel,
  IMasterPassAuditSummary,
} from '../../services/bovedaB2BDataHelper';
import { buildPartnerPeriodOptions } from '../../services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO } from '../../types';
import { KineticLoader, SkeletonPulse, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export interface IBovedaB2BRevenueCardProps {
  bv: any;
  effectiveIsAdmin: boolean;
  hasHydratedB2B?: boolean;
  isProfileIncomplete: boolean;
  metricas: { hoy: number; semana: number; mes: number; total: number; ingresosTotales?: number };
  afData: any;
  allAfiliados: any[];
  reservasB2B: any[];
  masterPassSummary?: IMasterPassAuditSummary;
  ceoWeeklyReport?: IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO | null;
  activeName: string;
  user: any;
  profile: any;
  isExportingSpei: boolean;
  setIsExportingSpei: (v: boolean) => void;
  onOpenFiscalModal: () => void;
  showTacticalAlert: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
  mountedRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
}

export const BovedaB2BRevenueCard = ({
  bv,
  effectiveIsAdmin,
  hasHydratedB2B = true,
  isProfileIncomplete,
  metricas,
  afData,
  allAfiliados,
  reservasB2B,
  masterPassSummary = EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY,
  ceoWeeklyReport,
  activeName,
  user,
  profile,
  isExportingSpei,
  setIsExportingSpei,
  onOpenFiscalModal,
  showTacticalAlert,
  mountedRef,
}: IBovedaB2BRevenueCardProps) => {
  const { themeColors, t, tx, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const noSel = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;
  const exportBusyRef = useRef(false);

  const resolvedMasterPasses = useMemo(
    () => masterPassSummary || EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY,
    [masterPassSummary],
  );

  if (!hasHydratedB2B) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          bv.balanceCardB2B,
          { elevation: 0 },
          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
          noSel,
        ]}
      >
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '58%', height: 14, borderRadius: 6, marginBottom: 12 }} />
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48%', height: 42, borderRadius: 10, marginBottom: 10 }} />
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '82%', height: 12, borderRadius: 6, marginBottom: 16 }} />
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, width: '100%', marginBottom: 12 }}>
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ flex: 1, height: 62, borderRadius: 12 }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ flex: 1, height: 62, borderRadius: 12 }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ flex: 1, height: 62, borderRadius: 12 }} />
        </View>
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '100%', height: 44, borderRadius: 13 }} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!effectiveIsAdmin && isProfileIncomplete) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          bv.balanceCardB2B,
          { borderColor: 'rgba(255, 204, 0, 0.45)', elevation: 0 },
          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#D97706' },
          noSel,
        ]}
      >
        <Feather name="lock" size={28} color={isLight ? '#D97706' : COLORS.yellow} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }} />
        <Text style={[bv.lockText, isLight && { color: '#B45309' }]}>{t('vault.commercial_locked')}</Text>
        <Text style={[bv.lockSubtext, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
          {tx(
            'Registra tu cuenta CLABE de 18 dígitos y datos fiscales para habilitar tus dispersiones semanales SPEI (80%).',
            'Register your 18-digit CLABE bank account and tax details to enable weekly SPEI payouts (80%).',
          )}
        </Text>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[bv.unlockBtn, noSel]}
          onPress={onOpenFiscalModal}
          {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any)}
        >
          <Text selectable={false} style={bv.unlockBtnText}>{t('vault.configure_clabe')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const totalKc = metricas?.total ?? metricas?.ingresosTotales ?? 0;

  const handleDownloadStatement = async () => {
    if (exportBusyRef.current || isExportingSpei) return;
    exportBusyRef.current = true;
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    setIsExportingSpei(true);
    try {
      const pdfMasterSummary = {
        dia: resolvedMasterPasses.hoy,
        semana: resolvedMasterPasses.semana,
        mes: resolvedMasterPasses.mes,
      };
      if (effectiveIsAdmin) {
        const report =
          ceoWeeklyReport || buildCeoConsolidatedWeeklyReport(allAfiliados, reservasB2B);
        await exportarPDFReporteMaestroCEO(report, pdfMasterSummary);
      } else {
        const bizPayload = afData || {
          id: user?.id,
          nombre: activeName,
          rfc: profile?.rfc_fiscal,
          regimen_fiscal: profile?.regimen_fiscal,
        };
        const curWeek = buildPartnerPeriodOptions(Date.now(), isEn)[0]?.override;
        const partnerReport = calcularCorteSemanalB2B([bizPayload], reservasB2B, curWeek);
        const partnerCut = partnerReport.cortes_por_afiliado[0];
        await exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado(
          partnerCut,
          partnerReport.periodoInicio,
          partnerReport.periodoFin,
          pdfMasterSummary,
        );
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        showTacticalAlert(
          effectiveIsAdmin
            ? tx('Reporte Maestro CEO', 'CEO Master Report')
            : tx('Estado de Cuenta SPEI', 'SPEI Statement'),
          err?.message || tx('No se pudo generar el PDF.', 'Could not generate PDF.'),
          'error',
        );
      }
    } finally {
      exportBusyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setIsExportingSpei(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        bv.balanceCardB2B,
        isLight && {
          backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
          borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderBottomWidth: 4.5,
          borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
        },
        noSel,
      ]}
    >
      <View style={bv.balanceHeader}>
        <Feather name="trending-up" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        <Text style={[bv.balanceHeaderLabel, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
          {effectiveIsAdmin
            ? tx(
                'INGRESOS GLOBALES CONSOLIDADOS (TODOS LOS AFILIADOS)',
                'CONSOLIDATED GLOBAL REVENUE (ALL AFFILIATES)',
              )
            : t('vault.accrued_revenue')}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <Text style={[bv.bigKcAmount, { lineHeight: 50 }, isLight && { color: '#0F172A' }]}>
        {totalKc} <Text style={{ fontSize: 22, color: isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan }}>KC</Text>
      </Text>

      <Text style={[bv.paridadText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
        {effectiveIsAdmin
          ? tx(
              `Consolidado de ${formatVenueCountLabel(Math.max(1, allAfiliados.length), false)} • Paridad 1 KC = $1.00 MXN • Corte Dominical 23:59`,
              `Consolidated across ${formatVenueCountLabel(Math.max(1, allAfiliados.length), true)} • Parity 1 KC = $1.00 MXN • Sunday Cut 23:59`,
            )
          : tx(
              'Paridad 1 KC = $1.00 MXN • Corte Dominical 23:59 • SPEI Miércoles',
              'Parity 1 KC = $1.00 MXN • Sunday Cut 23:59 • Wednesday SPEI',
            )}
      </Text>

      <View style={bv.b2bKpiRow}>
        <View
          style={[
            bv.b2bKpiBox,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
          ]}
        >
          <Text style={[bv.b2bKpiLabel, isLight && { color: '#475569' }]}>{tx('HOY', 'TODAY')}</Text>
          <Text style={[bv.b2bKpiVal, isLight && { color: '#0F172A' }]}>{metricas?.hoy || 0} KC</Text>
        </View>
        <View
          style={[
            bv.b2bKpiBox,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
          ]}
        >
          <Text style={[bv.b2bKpiLabel, isLight && { color: '#475569' }]}>{tx('7 DÍAS', '7 DAYS')}</Text>
          <Text style={[bv.b2bKpiVal, isLight && { color: '#0F172A' }]}>{metricas?.semana || 0} KC</Text>
        </View>
        <View
          style={[
            bv.b2bKpiBox,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
          ]}
        >
          <Text style={[bv.b2bKpiLabel, isLight && { color: '#475569' }]}>{tx('MES', 'MONTH')}</Text>
          <Text style={[bv.b2bKpiVal, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{metricas?.mes || 0} KC</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View
        style={{
          marginTop: 10,
          width: '100%',
          borderRadius: 12,
          paddingVertical: 9,
          paddingHorizontal: 12,
          borderWidth: 1,
          elevation: 0,
          backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F0F9FF' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.06)',
          borderColor: isLight ? '#BAE6FD' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.25)',
        }}
      >
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 4 }}>
          <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
            {tx('PASES MAESTROS (0 KC)', 'MASTER PASSES (0 KC)')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800' }}>
            {tx(
              `Activos: ${resolvedMasterPasses.activos} • Total: ${resolvedMasterPasses.total_emitidos}`,
              `Active: ${resolvedMasterPasses.activos} • Total: ${resolvedMasterPasses.total_emitidos}`,
            )}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '800' }}>
          {tx(
            `Día: ${resolvedMasterPasses.hoy}  •  7 Días: ${resolvedMasterPasses.semana}  •  Mes: ${resolvedMasterPasses.mes}`,
            `Day: ${resolvedMasterPasses.hoy}  •  7 Days: ${resolvedMasterPasses.semana}  •  Month: ${resolvedMasterPasses.mes}`,
          )}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <TouchableOpacity
        disabled={isExportingSpei}
        onPress={handleDownloadStatement}
        style={[
          noSel,
          {
            marginTop: 12,
            width: '100%',
            flexDirection: 'row',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            gap: 8,
            backgroundColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan,

    import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { Animated, Easing, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as Record<string, string>) : null;

export const BovedaClockwiseRefreshIndicator = memo(({ visible, isLight }: { visible: boolean; isLight?: boolean }) => {
  const { tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const spinVal = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) { spinVal.stopAnimation(); spinVal.setValue(0); return; }
    spinVal.setValue(0);
    const loop = Animated.loop(Animated.timing(spinVal, { toValue: 1, duration: 800, easing: Easing.linear, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }));
    loop.start();
    return () => loop.stop();
  }, [visible, spinVal]);
  if (!visible) return null;
  const clockwiseRotate = spinVal.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: ['0deg', '360deg'] });
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8, paddingVertical: 8, paddingHorizontal: 14, marginBottom: 12, borderRadius: 999, alignSelf: 'center', backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#080C12', borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : '#00F0FF', elevation: 0 }}>
      <Animated.View style={{ width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: 11, backgroundColor: '#080C12', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', transform: [{ rotate: clockwiseRotate }] }}>
        <Feather name="zap" size={13} color="#00F0FF" />
      </Animated.View>
      <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.9, color: isLight ? '#0369A1' : '#00F0FF' }}>
        {tx('SINCRONIZANDO BÓVEDA...', 'SYNCING VAULT...')}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
});

                />

      </>
    );
  },
);

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaReceiptModalLeaf.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/BovedaReceiptModalLeaf.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { formatLocalReservaDate } from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel, type DigitalPassRecordInput } from '../../utils/kineticDigitalPassViewModelLeaf';
import { KineticLoader } from '../SharedUI';
import { ReceiptInlineRatingBlock } from './BovedaRateEventModal';
import { KineticCalendarAndWalletActions } from './KineticCalendarAndWalletActions';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as Record<string, string>)
  : null;

export type ReceiptPassRecord = DigitalPassRecordInput & {
  id?: string;
  estado?: string;
  perdon_vip_aplicado?: boolean;
  servicios?: { fecha?: string; hora_inicio?: string; fecha_clase?: string; hora?: string };
};

export interface IReceiptModalProps {
  selectedReceipt: ReceiptPassRecord | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  formatPassDate?: (iso: string, lang?: string) => string;
  getCostoTotal?: (item: ReceiptPassRecord) => number;
  athleteName: string;
  onExportPdf?: (rec: ReceiptPassRecord, athleteName: string) => void;
  isExportingPdf?: boolean;
  onRated?: (id: string, stars: number) => void;
  showTacticalAlert?: (t: string, m: string, k?: string) => void;
  triggerHaptic?: (mode?: string) => void;
}

export const ReceiptModal = React.memo(
  ({ selectedReceipt, onClose, getCostoTotal, athleteName, onExportPdf, isExportingPdf, onRated }: IReceiptModalProps) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, language, tx, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const st = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSt, resolvedTheme, ['unlockActionBtn']), [resolvedTheme]);
    const lastValidDataRef = useRef<ReceiptPassRecord | null>(null);
    if (selectedReceipt) lastValidDataRef.current = selectedReceipt;
    const activeReceipt = selectedReceipt || lastValidDataRef.current;

    const stLower = String(activeReceipt?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const isCanceled = stLower === 'cancelada';
    const isNoShow = stLower === 'no_show' || stLower === 'penalizada';
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const passVm = useMemo(() => buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel(activeReceipt, { isEn }), [activeReceipt, isEn]);
    const totalKc = passVm.isMasterPass ? 0 : (getCostoTotal && activeReceipt ? getCostoTotal(activeReceipt) : passVm.totalKc);
    const rawEscrowDb = Number(activeReceipt?.monto_escrow || 0);
    const usedPerdon = Boolean(activeReceipt?.perdon_vip_aplicado);
    const isRetainedCancel = isCanceled && rawEscrowDb > 0 && !usedPerdon;
    const escrowKc = passVm.isMasterPass ? 0 : passVm.escrowKc;
    const isZeroKcPass = passVm.isMasterPass || (totalKc <= 0 && escrowKc <= 0);
    const puertaKc = isCanceled || isNoShow || isZeroKcPass ? 0 : passVm.puertaKc;
    const localDateStr = activeReceipt ? formatLocalReservaDate(activeReceipt, language, true) : '';

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={Boolean(selectedReceipt)}
        animationIn="fadeInUp"
        animationOut="fadeOutDown"
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        backdropOpacity={themeColors.isLight ? 0.5 : 0.92}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
        backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        backdropColor={themeColors.isLight ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        onBackdropPress={onClose}
        onBackButtonPress={onClose}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'center', padding: 18 }}
      >
        <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}>
          <View
            style={[
              st.receiptGlassCard,
              themeColors.isLight && {
                backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#C4B5FD', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED', shadowColor: '#7C3AED', shadowOpacity: 0.24,
              },
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
          >
            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
              <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 12 }}>
                <Feather
                  name={isCanceled ? 'rotate-ccw' : 'file-text'}
                  size={24}
                  color={isRetainedCancel ? (themeColors.isLight ? '#B45309' : COLORS.yellow) : isCanceled ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.purpleText}
                />
                <Text style={[st.qrTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                  {isZeroKcPass
                    ? tx('COMPROBANTE PASE MAESTRO (0 KC)', 'MASTER PASS RECEIPT (0 KC)')
                    : isRetainedCancel
                      ? tx('COMPROBANTE DE CANCELACIÓN', 'CANCELLATION RECEIPT')
                      : isCanceled
                        ? tx('COMPROBANTE DE REEMBOLSO', 'REFUND RECEIPT')
                        : isNoShow
                          ? tx('COMPROBANTE NO-SHOW (40%)', 'NO-SHOW RECEIPT (40%)')
                          : tx('RECIBO DE OPERACIÓN QR', 'QR OPERATION RECEIPT')}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[st.qrSubtitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{passVm.sessionTitle}</Text>
                <Text style={[st.qrDateTime, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{localDateStr}</Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[st.dossierInfoGrid, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}>
                <View style={st.dossierInfoRow}>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('SEDE AFILIADA', 'AFFILIATED VENUE')}</Text>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{passVm.venueName}</Text>
                </View>
                {passVm.stationBadge && (
                  <>
                    <View style={[st.dossierDivider, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
                    <View style={st.dossierInfoRow}>
                      <Text style={[st.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('ESTACIÓN 3D', '3D STATION')}</Text>
                      <Text style={[st.dossierInfoValue, { color: themeColors.green }]}>{passVm.stationBadge}</Text>
                    </View>
                  </>
                )}
                <View style={[st.dossierDivider, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
                <View style={st.dossierInfoRow}>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{passVm.depositLabel.toUpperCase()}</Text>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{escrowKc} KC</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[st.dossierDivider, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
                <View style={st.dossierInfoRow}>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{passVm.settlementLabel.toUpperCase()}</Text>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{puertaKc} KC</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[st.dossierDivider, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
                <View style={st.dossierInfoRow}>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('ESTATUS FINAL', 'FINAL STATUS')}</Text>
                  <Text style={[st.dossierInfoValue, { color: (isRetainedCancel || isNoShow) ? (themeColors.isLight ? '#B45309' : COLORS.yellow) : isCanceled ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.green }]}>
                    {isZeroKcPass && isNoShow
                      ? tx('PASE MAESTRO EXENTO (0 KC)', 'MASTER PASS EXEMPT (0 KC)')
                      : isZeroKcPass && isCanceled
                        ? tx('PASE MAESTRO CANCELADO (0 KC)', 'MASTER PASS CANCELLED (0 KC)')
                        : isRetainedCancel
                          ? tx('ANTICIPO 40% RETENIDO', '40% DEPOSIT RETAINED')
                          : isCanceled
                            ? tx('REEMBOLSADO AL 100%', '100% REFUNDED')
                            : isNoShow
                              ? tx('ANTICIPO 40% NO-SHOW', '40% NO-SHOW DEPOSIT')
                              : `${totalKc} KC ${tx('LIQUIDADOS', 'SETTLED')}`}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <ReceiptInlineRatingBlock activeReceipt={activeReceipt} onRated={onRated} />
              <KineticCalendarAndWalletActions reserva={activeReceipt} escrowKc={escrowKc} puertaKc={puertaKc} compact />
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[st.unlockActionBtn, { marginTop: 14 }, NO_SEL]}
                disabled={Boolean(isExportingPdf)}
                onPress={() => activeReceipt && onExportPdf?.(activeReceipt, athleteName)}
                {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}
              >
                {isExportingPdf ? (
                  <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" />
                ) : (
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                    <Feather name="download" size={15} color="#050505" />
                    <Text selectable={false} style={[st.unlockActionBtnText, { color: '#050505', lineHeight: 16 }]}>
                      {tx('DESCARGAR COMPROBANTE (.PDF)', 'DOWNLOAD RECEIPT (.PDF)')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[st.cancelMembershipBtn, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }, NO_SEL]}
                onPress={onClose}
              >
                <Text selectable={false} style={[st.cancelMembershipText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('CERRAR RECIBO', 'CLOSE RECEIPT')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
  }
);

const baseSt = StyleSheet.create({
