    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaTacticalAlertModal.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/BovedaTacticalAlertModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { Platform, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { useRegisterOverlayLayer } from '../../services/navigation/kineticOverlayStack';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { renderInWebPortal } from '../../utils/kineticWebPortal';
import { KineticDualConfirmModal } from '../shared/KineticDualConfirmModal';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL: Record<string, string> | undefined =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : undefined;

export interface BovedaAlertPayload {
  visible?: boolean;
  title?: string;
  message?: string;
  type?: string;
  confirmText?: string;
  cancelText?: string;
  secondaryActionText?: string;
  onSecondaryAction?: (() => void) | null;
  immediateConfirm?: boolean;
  onConfirm?: (() => void) | null;
  onCancel?: (() => void) | null;
}

type BovedaSetAlertFn<T extends BovedaAlertPayload> = {
  bivariant(next: React.SetStateAction<T>): void;
}['bivariant'];

function TacticalAlertModalInner<T extends BovedaAlertPayload = BovedaAlertPayload>({
  alertConfig,
  setAlertConfig,
  triggerHaptic: hapticProp,
  visible,
  type,
  title,
  message,
  confirmText,
  cancelText,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  inlineOverlay,
}: {
  alertConfig?: T | null;
  setAlertConfig?: BovedaSetAlertFn<T> | ((next: T) => void);
  triggerHaptic?: (mode?: string) => void;
  visible?: boolean;
  type?: string;
  title?: string;
  message?: string;
  confirmText?: string;
  cancelText?: string;
  onConfirm?: (() => void) | null;
  onCancel?: (() => void) | null;
  inlineOverlay?: boolean;
}) {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, tx, td, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const alertStyles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseAlertStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const neonDataSet: Record<string, Record<string, string>> =
      Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticNeon: 'true' } } : {};
    const lastOpenConfigRef = useRef<BovedaAlertPayload>({
      title: 'KINETIC',
      message: '',
      type: 'alert',
      confirmText: tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
      cancelText: tx('PERMANECER', 'STAY'),
      secondaryActionText: '',
      onSecondaryAction: null,
      immediateConfirm: false,
      onConfirm: null,
      onCancel: null,
    });
    const timersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);
    const actionFiredRef = useRef(false);
    const scheduleAlertTimer = (fn: () => void, ms: number) => {
      const id = setTimeout(() => {
        timersRef.current = timersRef.current.filter((t) => t !== id);
        fn();
      }, ms);
      timersRef.current.push(id);
    };
    useEffect(() => () => {
      timersRef.current.forEach(clearTimeout);
      timersRef.current = [];
    }, []);

    const effectiveConfig =
      alertConfig ||
      (visible !== undefined
        ? ({ visible, type, title, message, confirmText, cancelText, onConfirm, onCancel } as T)
        : null);
    if (effectiveConfig?.visible && (effectiveConfig?.title || effectiveConfig?.message)) {
      lastOpenConfigRef.current = { ...effectiveConfig };
    }

    const activeAlert = effectiveConfig?.visible ? effectiveConfig : lastOpenConfigRef.current;
    const isModalOpen = Boolean(effectiveConfig?.visible);
    useEffect(() => {
      if (isModalOpen) actionFiredRef.current = false;
    }, [isModalOpen, activeAlert?.title, activeAlert?.message]);

    const fireHaptic = hapticProp || triggerHaptic;
    const closeAlert = () => {
      if (setAlertConfig) setAlertConfig({ ...activeAlert, visible: false } as T);
      else onCancel?.();
    };
    const opaqueScrimColor = isLightMode ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.74)' : 'rgba(4, 6, 12, 0.90)';
    const aType = String(activeAlert.type || 'alert');
    const isErr = aType === 'error' || aType === 'confirm';
    const isWarn = aType === 'warning';
    const isSucc = aType === 'success';
    const hasDualButtons = aType === 'confirm' || Boolean(activeAlert.onConfirm && activeAlert.cancelText) || Boolean(activeAlert.cancelText && activeAlert.onCancel);
    useRegisterOverlayLayer('tactical-alert-modal', isModalOpen && !hasDualButtons, closeAlert);

    if (!effectiveConfig) return null;

    if (hasDualButtons) {
      return (
        <KineticDualConfirmModal
          visible={isModalOpen}
          title={td(activeAlert.title || 'KINETIC')}
          message={td(activeAlert.message || '')}
          confirmLabel={td(activeAlert.confirmText || tx('Confirmar', 'Confirm'))}
          cancelLabel={td(activeAlert.cancelText || tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel'))}
          tone={isErr ? 'danger' : isWarn ? 'amber' : 'cyan'}
          onCancel={() => {
            if (actionFiredRef.current) return;
            actionFiredRef.current = true;
            fireHaptic?.('Light');
            if (activeAlert.onCancel) {
              const cancelCb = activeAlert.onCancel;
              if (setAlertConfig) setAlertConfig({ ...activeAlert, visible: false } as T);
              scheduleAlertTimer(() => cancelCb(), 120);
            } else {
              closeAlert();
            }
          }}
          onConfirm={() => {
            if (actionFiredRef.current) return;
            actionFiredRef.current = true;
            fireHaptic?.('Heavy');
            if (setAlertConfig) setAlertConfig({ ...activeAlert, visible: false } as T);
            if (activeAlert.onConfirm) {
              const confirmCb = activeAlert.onConfirm;
              if (activeAlert.immediateConfirm) confirmCb();
              else scheduleAlertTimer(() => confirmCb(), 140);
            }
          }}
        />
      );
    }

    const neonLip = isErr ? '#FF3B30' : isWarn ? '#FFAB00' : isSucc ? '#00C853' : '#0284C7';
    const lightSideBorder = isErr ? '#FCA5A5' : isWarn ? '#FCD34D' : isSucc ? '#6EE7B7' : '#7DD3FC';
    const titleInk = isErr
      ? isLightMode
        ? '#B91C1C'
        : COLORS.red
      : isWarn
        ? isLightMode
          ? '#92400E'
          : COLORS.yellow
        : isSucc
          ? isLightMode
            ? '#065F46'
            : COLORS.green
          : isLightMode
            ? '#0369A1'
            : themeColors.cyanText;

    const singleBtnStyle = isLightMode
      ? {
          backgroundColor: isErr ? '#FEF2F2' : isWarn ? '#FEF3C7' : isSucc ? '#ECFDF5' : '#F0F9FF',
          borderColor: isErr ? '#F87171' : isWarn ? '#F59E0B' : isSucc ? '#10B981' : '#0284C7',
          borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderBottomWidth: 4.5,
          borderBottomColor: neonLip,
          shadowColor: neonLip,
          shadowOpacity: 0.26,
        }
      : isErr
        ? {
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 59, 48, 0.15)',
            borderColor: COLORS.red,
            borderBottomColor: '#8B1A14',
            shadowColor: COLORS.red,
            elevation: 0,
          }
        : isWarn
          ? {
              backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 204, 0, 0.15)',
              borderColor: COLORS.yellow,
              borderBottomColor: '#854D0E',
              shadowColor: COLORS.yellow,
              elevation: 0,
            }
          : isSucc
            ? {
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.14)',
                borderColor: COLORS.green,
                borderBottomColor: '#00752E',
                shadowColor: COLORS.green,
                elevation: 0,
              }
            : { elevation: 0 };

    const alertBoxNode = (
      <View
        style={[
          alertStyles.cleanAlertBox,
            isLightMode
              ? {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderColor: lightSideBorder,
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomWidth: 4.5,
                  borderBottomColor: neonLip,
                  shadowColor: '#0F172A',
                  shadowOpacity: 0.18,
                  elevation: 0,
                }
              : {
                  borderColor: isErr
                    ? COLORS.red
                    : isWarn
                      ? COLORS.yellow
                      : isSucc
                        ? COLORS.green
                        : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.45)',
                  borderBottomColor: isErr
                    ? '#8B1A14'
                    : isWarn
                      ? '#854D0E'
                      : isSucc
                        ? '#00752E'
                        : '#004650',
                  shadowColor: isErr
                    ? COLORS.red
                    : isWarn
                      ? COLORS.yellow
                      : isSucc
                        ? COLORS.green
                        : COLORS.cyan,
                  elevation: 0,
                },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
        >
          <View style={globalStyles.tacticalAlertHeader}>
            <Feather
              name={
                aType === 'error'
                  ? 'alert-octagon'
                  : isWarn
                    ? 'alert-triangle'
                    : aType === 'confirm'
                      ? 'activity'
                      : 'check-circle'
              }
              size={20}
              color={titleInk}
            />
            <Text style={[globalStyles.tacticalAlertTitle, { color: titleInk }]}>
              {td(activeAlert.title)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={[globalStyles.tacticalAlertMessage, isLightMode && { color: '#334155' }]}>
            {td(activeAlert.message)}
          </Text>
          <View>
            <TouchableOpacity
              {...neonDataSet}
              style={[globalStyles.tacticalAlertBtn, singleBtnStyle, NO_SEL]}
              onPress={() => {
                if (actionFiredRef.current) return;
                actionFiredRef.current = true;
                fireHaptic?.('Light');
                closeAlert();
                if (activeAlert.onConfirm) {
                  const confirmCb = activeAlert.onConfirm;
                  scheduleAlertTimer(() => confirmCb(), 165);
                }
              }}
            >
              <Text selectable={false} style={[globalStyles.tacticalAlertBtnText, { color: titleInk }]}>
                {td(activeAlert.confirmText || tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'))}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            {Boolean(activeAlert.secondaryActionText && activeAlert.onSecondaryAction) && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[alertStyles.secondaryLinkBtn, NO_SEL]}
                onPress={() => {
                  if (actionFiredRef.current) return;
                  actionFiredRef.current = true;
                  fireHaptic?.('Selection');
                  closeAlert();
                  activeAlert.onSecondaryAction?.();
                }}
              >
                <Text selectable={false} style={[alertStyles.secondaryLinkText, { color: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan }]}>
                  {td(activeAlert.secondaryActionText)}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
      </View>
    );

    if (inlineOverlay) {
      if (!isModalOpen) return null;
      const inlineAlertNode = (
        <Pressable
          onPress={closeAlert}
          style={{
            position: Platform.OS === 'web' ? ('fixed' as 'absolute') : 'absolute',
            top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
            ...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ width: '100vw', height: '100dvh' } as Record<string, string>) : {}),
            zIndex: 99990, backgroundColor: opaqueScrimColor, padding: 20, justifyContent: 'center', elevation: 0,
          }}
        >
          <Pressable onPress={(e) => e?.stopPropagation?.()}>{alertBoxNode}</Pressable>
        </Pressable>
      );
      return Platform.OS === 'web' ? renderInWebPortal(inlineAlertNode) : inlineAlertNode;
    }

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={isModalOpen}
        animationIn="fadeIn"
        animationOut="fadeOut"
        animationInTiming={120}
        animationOutTiming={1}
        backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : '#04060C'}
        backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.01 : 0.01}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
        backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        onBackdropPress={closeAlert}
        onBackButtonPress={closeAlert}
        style={{ margin: 0, padding: 0, justifyContent: 'center' }}
      >
        <Pressable
          onPress={closeAlert}
          style={{
            flex: 1,
            width: '100%',
            padding: 20,
            justifyContent: 'center',
            backgroundColor: isModalOpen ? opaqueScrimColor : 'transparent',
            elevation: 0,
          }}
        >
          <Pressable onPress={(e) => e?.stopPropagation?.()}>{alertBoxNode}</Pressable>
        </Pressable>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
}

export const TacticalAlertModal = React.memo(TacticalAlertModalInner) as typeof TacticalAlertModalInner;

const baseAlertStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  cleanAlertBox: {
    backgroundColor: '#050505', borderRadius: 22, padding: 20, borderWidth: 1.4, borderColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.45)',
    borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: 'rgba(184, 255, 255, 0.55)', borderBottomWidth: 4.5, borderBottomColor: '#004650',
    shadowColor: '#000000', shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 8 }, shadowOpacity: 0.5, shadowRadius: 16, elevation: 0,
  },
  confirmActionsRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10, marginTop: 16 },
  confirmStayCyanBtn: {
    flex: 1, height: 46, borderRadius: 13, backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan, borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#5CEEFF',
    borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: '#B8FFFF', borderBottomWidth: 4.5, borderBottomColor: '#00838F', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', elevation: 0,
  },
  confirmStayCyanText: { color: '#050505', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7 },

    flex: 1, height: 46, borderRadius: 13, backgroundColor: '#DC2626', borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#F87171',

  },
  confirmExitRedBtnPressed: { backgroundColor: '#991B1B', borderColor: '#EF4444', borderTopColor: '#F87171', borderBottomColor: '#450A0A', transform: [{ translateY: 1.5 }] },
  confirmExitRedText: { color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7 },
  secondaryLinkBtn: { marginTop: 12, paddingVertical: 6, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', elevation: 0 },
  secondaryLinkText: { fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: '800', textDecorationLine: 'underline' },
});

export const renderCleanFormattedText = (rawText: string, baseStyle: any, boldColor = '#FFFFFF') => {
  const parts = String(rawText || '').split(/(\*\*.*?\*\*)/g);
  return React.createElement(
    Text,
    { style: [baseStyle, { lineHeight: 22 }] },
    parts.map((part, idx) =>
      part.startsWith('**') && part.endsWith('**') && part.length > 4
        ? React.createElement(
            Text,
            { key: idx, style: { color: boldColor, fontWeight: '900', lineHeight: 22 } },
            part.slice(2, -2),
          )
        : React.createElement(React.Fragment, { key: idx }, part),
    ),
  );
};

export const buildConciergeSmartReply = (
  queryRaw: string,
  userName: string,
  isB2B: boolean,
  lang: 'es' | 'en' = 'es',
): string => buildConciergePureReply(queryRaw, userName, isB2B, lang);

    borderBottomWidth: 4.2, borderBottomColor: '#007A33', paddingHorizontal: 11, paddingVertical: 7, borderRadius: 10,
    shadowColor: COLORS.green, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 5 }, shadowOpacity: 0.36, shadowRadius: 10, elevation: 0,

    FILE: src/components/boveda/KineticCalendarAndWalletActions.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/KineticCalendarAndWalletActions.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  openGoogleCalendarDirectWeb,
  reconcileReservationCalendarSyncState,
  resolveRecentCreatedReservaId,
  subscribeCalendarSyncChanges,
  syncReservationToNativeCalendar,
} from '../../services/kineticCalendarSyncService';
import {
  fetchNativeWalletPassesEnabled,
  requestWalletCompanionPass,
  shouldRenderNativeWalletPassButton,
} from '../../services/kineticWalletCompanionService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export interface KineticCalendarAndWalletActionsProps {
  reserva: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined;
  escrowKc: number;
  puertaKc: number;
  compact?: boolean;
}

export const KineticCalendarAndWalletActions: React.FC<KineticCalendarAndWalletActionsProps> = ({
  reserva,
  escrowKc,
  puertaKc,
  compact,
}) => {
  const { profile } = useAuth();
  const { language, tx, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const [isSynced, setIsSynced] = useState(false);
  const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState(false);
  const [walletFlagEnabled, setWalletFlagEnabled] = useState(false);

  const rawReservaId = String(reserva?.id || '').trim();
  const resolvedServiceId =
    reserva?.servicio_id != null
      ? String(reserva.servicio_id)
      : reserva?.service_id != null
      ? String(reserva.service_id)
      : null;
  const resolvedReservaId = rawReservaId.startsWith('opt_')
    ? resolveRecentCreatedReservaId(
        resolvedServiceId,
        String(reserva?.fecha_clase || reserva?.fecha || reserva?.fecha_hora || '')
      ) || ''
    : rawReservaId;
  const isPendingOptimisticId = rawReservaId.startsWith('opt_') && !resolvedReservaId;
  const reservaId = resolvedReservaId || rawReservaId;
  const stLower = String(reserva?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const isInactive = stLower === 'cancelada' || stLower === 'eliminada' || stLower === 'no_show' || stLower === 'penalizada';
  const activeReservaRef = useRef(reservaId);
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  activeReservaRef.current = reservaId;

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      mountedRef.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    setIsSyncing(false);
    setIsSynced(false);
    if (!reservaId || isInactive || isPendingOptimisticId) {
      return;
    }
    const checkSync = () => {
      reconcileReservationCalendarSyncState(reservaId)
        .then((res) => {
          if (alive && mountedRef.current && activeReservaRef.current === reservaId) {
            setIsSynced(res.isSynced);
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    };
    checkSync();
    const unsubCal = subscribeCalendarSyncChanges((changedId) => {
      if (changedId === reservaId) checkSync();
    });
    fetchNativeWalletPassesEnabled()
      .then((flag) => { if (alive && mountedRef.current) setWalletFlagEnabled(flag); })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => { alive = false; unsubCal(); };
  }, [reservaId, isInactive, isPendingOptimisticId]);

  if (!reservaId || isInactive) return null;

  const title = String(reserva?.servicio || reserva?.nombre_servicio || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session'));
  const venueName = String(reserva?.negocio_nombre || tx('Sede Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Venue'));
  const locationText = reserva?.ubicacion ? String(reserva.ubicacion) : null;
  const fechaIso = String(reserva?.fecha_clase || reserva?.fecha || reserva?.fecha_hora || '');
  const horaInicio = String(reserva?.hora_inicio || reserva?.hora || '08:00');
  const rawSpotNum = reserva?.spot_number != null && String(reserva.spot_number).trim() !== '' ? Number(reserva.spot_number) : NaN;
  const spotNumber = Number.isInteger(rawSpotNum) && rawSpotNum > 0 ? rawSpotNum : null;
  const equipmentType = typeof reserva?.equipment_type === 'string'
    ? reserva.equipment_type
    : typeof reserva?.categoria === 'string'
    ? reserva.categoria
    : null;
  const calEventPayload = {
    reservaId,
    title,
    venueName,
    location: locationText,
    fechaIso,
    horaInicio,
    spotNumber,
    equipmentType,
    escrowKc,
    puertaKc,
    status: 'CONFIRMED' as const,
    language,
  };

  const handleSyncCalendar = async () => {
    if (isSyncing || isPendingOptimisticId || reservaId.startsWith('opt_')) return;
    const targetReservaId = reservaId;
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setIsSyncing(true);
    try {
      const res = await syncReservationToNativeCalendar({ input: calEventPayload, silent: false });
      if (res.ok && mountedRef.current && activeReservaRef.current === targetReservaId) {
        triggerHaptic('Success');
        setIsSynced(true);
      }
    } finally {
      if (mountedRef.current && activeReservaRef.current === targetReservaId) {
        setIsSyncing(false);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleWalletPress = async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    await requestWalletCompanionPass({
      targetWallet: Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'apple_wallet' : 'google_wallet',
      flagOverride: walletFlagEnabled,
      input: {
        reservaId,
        venueName,
        discipline: String(reserva?.categoria || 'Fitness'),
        sessionTitle: title,
        fechaIso,
        horaInicio,
        spotNumber,
        escrowKc,
        puertaKc,
        vipLevel: String(profile?.nivel || 'Acceso Base'),
        language,
      },
    });
  };

  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  return (
    <View style={[styles.wrap, compact && { marginTop: 8 }]}>
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={[
          styles.calBtn,
          isLight && {
            backgroundColor: isSynced ? '#ECFDF5' : '#F0F9FF',
            borderColor: isSynced ? '#10B981' : '#38BDF8',
            borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
            borderBottomColor: isSynced ? '#047857' : '#0284C7',
          },
          !isLight && isSynced && {
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.14)',
            borderColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.55)',
            borderBottomColor: '#065F46',
          },
        ]}
        activeOpacity={0.85}
        disabled={isSyncing || isPendingOptimisticId}
        onPress={handleSyncCalendar}
      >
        {isSyncing ? (
          <KineticLoader size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.row}>
            <Feather
              name={isSynced ? 'check-circle' : 'calendar'}
              size={14}
              color={isSynced ? (isLight ? '#047857' : '#34D399') : themeColors.cyanText}
            />
            <Text
              selectable={false}
              style={[
                styles.calBtnTxt,
                { color: isSynced ? (isLight ? '#047857' : '#34D399') : themeColors.cyanText },
              ]}
            >
              {isSynced
                ? tx('Sincronizado ✓', 'Synced ✓')
                : tx('Sincronizar con mi Calendario', 'Sync to Calendar')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {Platform.OS === 'web' && (
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.calBtn, { height: 36, backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.16)', borderBottomWidth: 2, borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#0F172A' }]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); void openGoogleCalendarDirectWeb(calEventPayload); }}
        >
          <View style={styles.row}>
            <Feather name="external-link" size={13} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.calBtnTxt, { fontSize: 10.5, color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
              {tx('Abrir en Google Calendar ↗', 'Open in Google Calendar ↗')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      )}

      {shouldRenderNativeWalletPassButton(walletFlagEnabled) && (
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.walletBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#0F172A', borderColor: '#334155' }]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={handleWalletPress}
        >
          <Feather name="credit-card" size={14} color="#FFFFFF" />
          <Text selectable={false} style={styles.walletBtnTxt}>
            {tx('Agregar a Wallet', 'Add to Wallet')}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { width: '100%', gap: 8, marginTop: 10 },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 7 },
  calBtn: {
    width: '100%',
    height: 44,
    borderRadius: 13,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.1)',
    borderWidth: 1.2,
    borderColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.42)',
    borderTopWidth: 1.8,
    borderTopColor: 'rgba(184, 255, 255, 0.5)',
    borderBottomWidth: 4.2,
    borderBottomColor: '#005A66',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    elevation: 0,
  },
  calBtnTxt: { color: COLORS.cyan, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 },
  walletBtn: {
    width: '100%',
    height: 44,
    borderRadius: 13,
    backgroundColor: '#111827',
    borderWidth: 1.2,
    borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    flexDirection: 'row',
    gap: 8,
    elevation: 0,
  },
  walletBtnTxt: { color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.6 },
});

    FILE: src/components/boveda/KineticConciergeAndSupportModals.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/KineticConciergeAndSupportModals.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Keyboard, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { askKineticConciergeCloud, fetchConciergeHistoryCloud } from '../../services/kineticConciergeCloudService';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen } from '../../utils/kineticOverlayStack';
import {
  HumanSupportModal,
  KINETIC_OFFICIAL_SUPPORT_EMAIL,
  KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP,
} from '../BovedaSupportAndReferralModals';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import {
  QUICK_PROMPTS_B2B_EN,
  QUICK_PROMPTS_B2B_ES,
  QUICK_PROMPTS_B2C_EN,
  QUICK_PROMPTS_B2C_ES,
  baseConciergeStyles,
  buildConciergeSmartReply,
  renderCleanFormattedText,
} from './conciergeBrainAndStyles';

export { HumanSupportModal, KINETIC_OFFICIAL_SUPPORT_EMAIL, KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP };
const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as Record<string, string>) : null;

interface IConciergeMessage {
  id: string;
  sender: 'bot' | 'user';
  text: string;
}

export const KineticConciergeModal = ({
  visible, onClose, isB2B = false, userName = 'Atleta Kinetic', onSwitchToHuman,
}: {
  visible: boolean; onClose: () => void; isB2B?: boolean; userName?: string; onSwitchToHuman?: () => void;
}) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseConciergeStyles, resolvedTheme, ['msgBubbleUser', 'sendCircleBtn']),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const scrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const [inputTxt, setInputTxt] = useState('');
  const [keyboardHeight, setKeyboardHeight] = useState(0);
  const frozenPaddingBottomRef = useRef<number>(insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 12 : 20);
  const displayUserName =
    !userName || userName === 'Atleta Kinetic'
      ? isB2B
        ? tx('Socio Kinetic', 'Kinetic Partner')
        : tx('Atleta Kinetic', 'Kinetic Athlete')
      : userName;

  const initialGreeting = useMemo(
    () =>
      isB2B
        ? tx(
            `¡Hola, **${displayUserName}**! ⚡ Soy tu **Concierge Operativo B2B 24/7**. Puedo asistirte con **Corte Dominical**, **Liquidación SPEI de Miércoles**, **Escáner Óptico QR TOTP (60s + Offline)**, **Configuración de Sala/Aforo 3D** y la **Garantía No-Show 40% Escrow**. ¿Qué deseas revisar hoy?`,
            `Hello, **${displayUserName}**! ⚡ I am your **24/7 B2B Operations Concierge**. I can assist with **Sunday Cuts**, **Wednesday SPEI Payouts**, **Optical QR TOTP Scanning (60s + Offline)**, **3D Studio Capacity Layouts**, and the **40% Escrow No-Show Guarantee**. How can I help your venue today?`
          )
        : tx(
            `¡Hola, **${displayUserName}**! ⚡ Soy el **Concierge IA 24/7 de Kinetic**. Puedo ayudarte con **Soporte Técnico**, cómo **reservar clases**, **Membresías y Recargas Top-Up**, **Chat de Equipos**, **Historias 24h**, **Reserva Protegida 40/60** y **Pase QR de Acceso**. ¿En qué te ayudo hoy?`,
            `Hello, **${displayUserName}**! ⚡ I am your **24/7 Kinetic AI Concierge**. I can assist with **Technical Support**, **class bookings & 3D spots**, **VIP Memberships & Rollover Top-Ups**, **Team Chat**, **24h Stories**, and **40/60 Escrow & Dynamic QR**. How can I help you today?`
          ),
    [displayUserName, isB2B, tx]
  );

  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<IConciergeMessage[]>([
    { id: 'welcome_1', sender: 'bot', text: initialGreeting },
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible || !user?.id) return;
    let active = true;
    void fetchConciergeHistoryCloud(user.id, 12).then((hist) => {
      if (!active || hist.length === 0) return;
      setMessages((prev) => (prev.length <= 1 ? [{ id: 'welcome_1', sender: 'bot', text: initialGreeting }, ...hist] : prev));
    });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [visible, user?.id, initialGreeting]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMessages((prev) =>
      prev.length === 1 && prev[0].id === 'welcome_1'
        ? [{ id: 'welcome_1', sender: 'bot', text: initialGreeting }]
        : prev
    );
  }, [initialGreeting]);

  const msgSeqRef = useRef(0);
  const reqSeqRef = useRef(0);
  const prevUidRef = useRef<string | undefined>(user?.id);
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const chatTimersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);
  const switchTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const clearChatAsync = useCallback(() => {
    if (rafRef.current !== null) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
      rafRef.current = null;
    }
    chatTimersRef.current.forEach(clearTimeout);
    chatTimersRef.current = [];
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    reqSeqRef.current += 1;
    if (prevUidRef.current !== user?.id) {
      prevUidRef.current = user?.id;
      setMessages([{ id: 'welcome_1', sender: 'bot', text: initialGreeting }]);
    }
  }, [language, user?.id, initialGreeting]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen(true);
    return () => setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen(false);
  }, [visible]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) {
      clearChatAsync();
    }
  }, [visible, clearChatAsync]);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      clearChatAsync();
      if (switchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(switchTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, [clearChatAsync]);

  const scrollToBottomSmooth = useCallback(() => {
    if (rafRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
    rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      rafRef.current = null;
      scrollRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true });
      const tid = setTimeout(() => scrollRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true }), 90);
      chatTimersRef.current.push(tid);
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    scrollToBottomSmooth();
  }, [visible, messages.length, scrollToBottomSmooth]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    const onShow = (e: { endCoordinates?: { height?: number } }) => {
      setKeyboardHeight(e?.endCoordinates?.height || 260);
      scrollToBottomSmooth();
      chatTimersRef.current.push(setTimeout(scrollToBottomSmooth, 140));
    };
    const onHide = () => setKeyboardHeight(0);
    const sDidShow = Keyboard.addListener('keyboardDidShow', onShow);
    const sDidHide = Keyboard.addListener('keyboardDidHide', onHide);
    const sWillShow = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? Keyboard.addListener('keyboardWillShow', onShow) : null;
    const sWillHide = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? Keyboard.addListener('keyboardWillHide', onHide) : null;
    return () => {
      sDidShow.remove();
      sDidHide.remove();
      sWillShow?.remove();
      sWillHide?.remove();
    };
  }, [visible, scrollToBottomSmooth]);

  const sendQuestion = (rawQuestion: string) => {
    const clean = String(rawQuestion || '').trim();
    if (!clean) return;
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    const mySeq = ++reqSeqRef.current;
    const userMsg: IConciergeMessage = {
      id: `msg_${Date.now()}_${++msgSeqRef.current}`,
      sender: 'user',
      text: clean,
    };
    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg]);
    setInputTxt('');
    scrollToBottomSmooth();
    chatTimersRef.current.push(setTimeout(scrollToBottomSmooth, 60));
    void askKineticConciergeCloud({
      query: clean,
      userName: displayUserName,
      isB2B,
      preferredLang: language === 'en' ? 'en' : 'es',
      localFallback: (q, l) => buildConciergeSmartReply(q, displayUserName, isB2B, l),
    }).then((out) => {
      if (!isMountedRef.current || mySeq !== reqSeqRef.current) return;
      const botMsg: IConciergeMessage = { id: `msg_${Date.now()}_${++msgSeqRef.current}`, sender: 'bot', text: out.text };
      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, botMsg]);
      scrollToBottomSmooth();
      chatTimersRef.current.push(setTimeout(scrollToBottomSmooth, 90));
    });
  };

  const quickPrompts = isB2B
    ? language === 'en'
      ? QUICK_PROMPTS_B2B_EN
      : QUICK_PROMPTS_B2B_ES
    : language === 'en'
    ? QUICK_PROMPTS_B2C_EN
    : QUICK_PROMPTS_B2C_ES;

  const activeBottomPadding =
    keyboardHeight > 0
      ? (Platform.OS === 'android' ? keyboardHeight + 10 : 10)
      : (insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 12 : 20);
  if (visible) {
    frozenPaddingBottomRef.current = activeBottomPadding;
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(visible)}
      onBackdropPress={onClose}
      onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      onModalHide={() => setKeyboardHeight(0)}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.55 : 0.92}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      avoidKeyboard={false}
    >
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
        keyboardVerticalOffset={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 8 : 0}
        style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        pointerEvents="box-none"
      >
        <View
          style={[
            globalStyles.bottomSheet,
            KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
            isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#0284C7' },
            {
              width: '100%',
              maxWidth: '100%',
              overflow: 'hidden',
              paddingBottom: frozenPaddingBottomRef.current,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />

          <View style={styles.conciergeTopHeader}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
              <Text style={[styles.conciergeTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {tx('CONCIERGE IA 24/7', '24/7 AI CONCIERGE')}
              </Text>
              <Text
                numberOfLines={1}
                adjustsFontSizeToFit
                minimumFontScale={0.78}
                style={[styles.conciergeSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}
              >
                {tx('Asistente Inteligente Kinetic • Soporte y Guía en Vivo', 'Kinetic Smart Assistant • Live Guide & Support')}
              </Text>
            </View>
            {onSwitchToHuman && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.humanSwitchPill,
                  isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#059669', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#047857' },
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('Selection');
                  Keyboard.dismiss();
                  onClose();
                  if (switchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(switchTimerRef.current);
                  switchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => onSwitchToHuman(), 260);
                }}
              >
                <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.humanSwitchTxt, isLightMode && { color: '#047857' }]}>
                  {tx('HUMANO ›', 'HUMAN ›')}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
            <ScrollView
              horizontal
              nestedScrollEnabled={true}
              showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
              style={{ marginHorizontal: -16 }}
              contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 8, paddingVertical: 2, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingRight: 28, alignItems: 'center' }}
            >
              {quickPrompts.map((promptTxt, idx) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={idx}
                  style={[
                    styles.quickChipBtn,
                    isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
                    NO_SEL,
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => sendQuestion(promptTxt)}
                >
                  <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} style={[styles.quickChipTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                    {promptTxt}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </ScrollView>
          </View>

          <ScrollView
            ref={scrollRef}
            style={[
              styles.chatBox,
              { flexShrink: 1 },
              isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
            ]}
            nestedScrollEnabled={true}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={true}
            bounces={false}
            keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            onLayout={() => {
              if (visible && (keyboardHeight > 0 || messages.length > 1)) scrollToBottomSmooth();
            }}
            onContentSizeChange={() => {
              if (visible && messages.length > 0) scrollToBottomSmooth();
            }}
            contentContainerStyle={{ paddingVertical: 12, paddingBottom: keyboardHeight > 0 ? 28 : 14, gap: 10 }}
          >
            {messages.map((m) => {
              const isUser = m.sender === 'user';
              return (
                <View
                  key={m.id}
                  style={[
                    styles.msgBubble,
                    isUser ? styles.msgBubbleUser : styles.msgBubbleBot,
                    !isUser && isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
                  ]}
                  {...(isUser ? ({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as unknown as Record<string, unknown>) : {})}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.msgSenderTag, { color: isUser ? '#050505' : themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                    {isUser ? tx('TÚ', 'YOU') : tx('⚡ KINETIC IA', '⚡ KINETIC AI')}
                  </Text>
                  {renderCleanFormattedText(
                    m.text,
                    [styles.msgText, { color: isUser ? '#050505' : themeColors.textSecondary }],
                    isUser ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary
                  )}
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>

          <View style={[styles.inputRow, keyboardHeight > 0 && { marginBottom: 2 }]}>
            <TextInput
              style={[
                styles.chatInput,
                { fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
              ]}
              placeholder={tx('Escribe tu duda sobre Kinetic...', 'Ask anything about Kinetic...')}
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              value={inputTxt}
              onChangeText={setInputTxt}
              onFocus={() => chatTimersRef.current.push(setTimeout(scrollToBottomSmooth, 100))}
              onSubmitEditing={() => sendQuestion(inputTxt)}
              returnKeyType="send"
            />
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.sendCircleBtn, NO_SEL]}
              onPress={() => sendQuestion(inputTxt)}
              {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)}
            >
              <Feather name="send" size={16} color="#050505" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

            )}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 14 }}>
      <View style={styles.clauseCard}>
        <Text style={styles.clauseTitle}>
          {tx(
            '1. RESPONSABLE DEL TRATAMIENTO Y DATOS RECABADOS (LFPDPPP)',
            '1. DATA CONTROLLER & COLLECTED INFORMATION (LFPDPPP)'
          )}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.clauseBody}>
          <Text style={styles.boldWhite}>KineticApp S.A.S.</Text>{' '}
          {tx(
            'es responsable del tratamiento de tus datos personales conforme a la LFPDPPP. Recabamos nombre, correo, teléfono, fotografía de perfil y datos fiscales opcionales (RFC, Régimen, CP y CLABE para socios B2B) únicamente para operar reservas, llaves QR, dispersiones SPEI y facturas CFDI 4.0.',
            'is responsible for processing your personal data under Mexican Privacy Law (LFPDPPP). We collect name, email, phone, profile photo, and optional tax details solely to operate bookings, dynamic QR passes, SPEI payouts, and CFDI 4.0 invoices.'
          )}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.clauseCard}>
        <Text style={styles.clauseTitle}>
          {tx(
            '2. PERMISOS NATIVOS (GPS Y CÁMARA) Y PRIVACIDAD EN CLASES',
            '2. NATIVE PERMISSIONS (GPS & CAMERA) & CLASS PRIVACY'
          )}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.clauseBody}>
          {tx(

          )}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.clauseCard}>
        <Text style={styles.clauseTitle}>
          {tx(
            '3. DERECHOS ARCO Y ELIMINACIÓN DEFINITIVA DE CUENTA',
            '3. ARCO PRIVACY RIGHTS & PERMANENT ACCOUNT DELETION'
          )}
        </Text>

            'We never sell your personal data. You may exercise your ARCO privacy rights or execute permanent account deletion (3-second hold button in Vault Settings) or by emailing '
          )}
          <Text style={styles.boldWhite}>soporte@kineticapp.com.mx</Text>.
        </Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

    // === src/components/boveda/ProfileDossierModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { resolveUnifiedAthleteProgression } from '../../services/kineticlubLevelsEngine';
import { getDisplayRole, isKineticMasterAdmin, resolveOfficialKineticName } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import { COLORS, RADIUS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { useIsDisciplinePickerOpen } from '../../utils/kineticOverlayStack';
import { getAvatarUrl } from '../../utils/supabaseImage';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

const KINETIC_LOGO = require('../../../assets/logo.png');
const NO_SEL: ViewStyle | null = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle) : null;

interface ProfileDossierProps {
  visible?: boolean;
  onClose?: () => void;
  user?: {
    id?: string;
    email?: string | null;
    created_at?: string | null;
    app_metadata?: { rol?: string; tipo_cuenta?: string; [k: string]: unknown } | null;
    user_metadata?: { equipo_nombre?: string; [k: string]: unknown } | null;
    [k: string]: unknown;
  } | null;
  profile?: {
    id?: unknown;
    nivel?: string | null;
    recompensas_reclamadas?: unknown;
    cajas_nivel_reclamadas?: unknown;
    racha_dias?: unknown;
    racha?: unknown;
    xp_total?: unknown;
    puntos_xp?: unknown;
    xp?: unknown;
    prestigio_nivel?: unknown;
    nombre?: string | null;
    avatar_url?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    rol?: string | null;
    tipo_cuenta?: string | null;
    created_at?: string | null;
    equipo_nombre?: string | null;
    equipo?: string | null;
    kc_membresia?: number | string | null;
    kc_topup?: number | string | null;
    [k: string]: unknown;
  } | null;
  isB2B?: boolean;
  displayRole?: string;
  totalEscrowLockedKc?: number;
  completedCount?: number;
  reservasCount?: number;
  triggerHaptic?: (type?: string) => void;
  onToggleGhostMode?: () => void;
  onOpenSettings?: () => void;
  onOpenMembership?: () => void;
  billingCycleInfo?: { nextCutDateLabel?: string; daysUntilCut?: number; [k: string]: unknown } | null;
  isGhostMode?: boolean;
  metricas?: { totalDevengado?: number | string; ingresosTotales?: number | string; total?: number | string; [k: string]: unknown } | null;
}

export const ProfileDossierModal = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  user,
  profile,
  isB2B,
  displayRole,
  totalEscrowLockedKc = 0,
  completedCount = 0,
  billingCycleInfo,
  isGhostMode = false,
  metricas,
}: ProfileDossierProps) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, fmtDate, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const isDisciplinePickerOpen = useIsDisciplinePickerOpen();

  const isAdminAccount = useMemo(() => {
    const em = String(user?.email || profile?.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const rol = String(profile?.rol || user?.app_metadata?.rol || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const tipo = String(profile?.tipo_cuenta || user?.app_metadata?.tipo_cuenta || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return (
      ['administrador@kinetic.com.mx', 'administrador@kinetic.com', 'kinetic@kinetic.com', 'admin@kinetic.com'].includes(em) ||
      rol === 'admin' ||
      rol === 'fundador' ||
      tipo === 'admin' ||
      tipo === 'fundador' ||
      isKineticMasterAdmin(em, rol, tipo)
    );
  }, [user?.email, profile?.email, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.app_metadata?.rol, user?.app_metadata?.tipo_cuenta]);

  const isMasterAdmin = isAdminAccount;
  const isRealB2BPartner = Boolean(isB2B && !isMasterAdmin);
  const dossierDisplayName = useMemo(
    () =>
      resolveOfficialKineticName(
        profile?.nombre,
        isMasterAdmin,
        isRealB2BPartner ? tx('Socio Afiliado', 'Affiliate Partner') : tx('Miembro Kinetic', 'Kinetic Member'),
      ),
    [profile?.nombre, isMasterAdmin, isRealB2BPartner, tx],
  );
  const dossierRoleLabel = useMemo(() => {
    if (isAdminAccount) {
      return getDisplayRole(profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.email, language === 'en');
    }
    return displayRole || getDisplayRole(profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.email, language === 'en');
  }, [isAdminAccount, displayRole, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.email, language]);

  const shortAccountId = useMemo(
    () => String(user?.id || '89976C19').replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(),
    [user?.id],
  );
  const formattedCreatedAt = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = profile?.created_at || user?.created_at;
    const fallback = tx('8 de septiembre de 2026', 'September 8, 2026');
    if (!raw) return fallback;
    return fmtDate(raw, { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }) || fallback;
  }, [profile?.created_at, user?.created_at, fmtDate, tx]);

  const levelState = useMemo(
    () => resolveUnifiedAthleteProgression(profile, Number(completedCount) || 0, language),
    [profile, completedCount, language],
  );
  const teamLabel = useMemo(() => {
    const rawTeam = String(
      profile?.equipo_nombre || profile?.equipo || user?.user_metadata?.equipo_nombre || '',
    ).trim();
    return rawTeam || tx('Sin equipo asignado', 'No team assigned');
  }, [profile?.equipo_nombre, profile?.equipo, user?.user_metadata?.equipo_nombre, tx]);

  const kcMembresia = Math.max(0, Number(profile?.kc_membresia) || 0);
  const kcTopup = Math.max(0, Number(profile?.kc_topup) || 0);

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(visible)}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.55 : 0.92}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
      backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      onBackdropPress={onClose}
      onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
    >
      <View
        pointerEvents={isDisciplinePickerOpen ? 'none' : 'auto'}
        style={[
          globalStyles.bottomSheet,
          KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
          {
            backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : themeColors.surface,
            borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : undefined,
            paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 14 : 26,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />

        <ScrollView
          style={{ flex: 1 }}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          bounces={false}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 8 }}
        >
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <View style={[styles.dossierAvatarRing, NO_SEL]}>
              {profile?.avatar_url ? (
                <Image
                  source={{ uri: getAvatarUrl(profile.avatar_url, 200) }}
                  style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
                  contentFit="cover"
                  cachePolicy="memory-disk"
                  transition={0}
                />
              ) : isB2B || isAdminAccount ? (
                <Image source={KINETIC_LOGO} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
              ) : (
                <Feather name="user" size={32} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              )}
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.dossierName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>
              {dossierDisplayName}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.dossierEmail, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{user?.email || ''}</Text>
            <View style={styles.dossierBadgeRow}>
              <View style={[styles.dossierRolePill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
                <Text style={[styles.dossierRolePillText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                  {dossierRoleLabel}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.dossierVerifiedPill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#D1FAE5', borderColor: '#059669' }]}>
                <Feather name="check-circle" size={11} color={isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green} />
                <Text style={[styles.dossierVerifiedText, isLight && { color: '#047857' }]}>
                  {tx('CUENTA VERIFICADA', 'VERIFIED ACCOUNT')}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View
            style={[
              styles.dossierInfoGrid,
              isLight && {
                backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8',
              },
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
          >
            <View style={styles.dossierInfoRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx('ID DE IDENTIDAD KINETIC', 'KINETIC IDENTITY ID')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.dossierInfoValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>#{shortAccountId}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.dossierDivider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
            <View style={styles.dossierInfoRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx('MIEMBRO EN LA RED DESDE', 'NETWORK MEMBER SINCE')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.dossierInfoValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{formattedCreatedAt}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.dossierDivider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
            <View style={styles.dossierInfoRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.dossierInfoLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>

    // === src/components/boveda/ReferralModals.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Keyboard, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, ScrollView, Share, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import type { KineticAuthProfile, KineticAuthUser } from '../../services/kineticAuthContextHelpers';
import { adminService } from '../../services/adminService';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { baseReferralStyles } from '../../styles/boveda/referralModals.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from '../shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { KineticLoader, triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL: Record<string, string> | undefined = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' }
  : undefined;

interface ReferralModalProps {
  visible?: boolean;
  isVisible?: boolean;
  onClose?: () => void;
  user?: KineticAuthUser | null;
  profile?: KineticAuthProfile | null;
  refreshProfile?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
  triggerHaptic?: (type?: string) => void;
}

export const ReferralModal = ({
  visible, isVisible, onClose, user, profile, refreshProfile, showTacticalAlert, triggerHaptic: hapticProp,
}: ReferralModalProps) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseReferralStyles, resolvedTheme, ['shareBtn', 'shareBtnTxt', 'redeemBtn', 'redeemBtnTxt']),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const fireHaptic = hapticProp || triggerHaptic;
  const isOpen = Boolean(visible ?? isVisible);

  const [friendCodeInput, setFriendCodeInput] = useState('');
  const [inlineNotice, setInlineNotice] = useState<{ kind: 'warning' | 'error' | 'success'; msg: string } | null>(null);
  const [isRedeeming, setIsRedeeming] = useState(false);
  const redeemBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const alertTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const attenuatedPlaceholder = isLightMode ? 'rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.46)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.44)';

  useEffect(() => {
    setInlineNotice(null);
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (alertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(alertTimerRef.current);
  }, []);

  const myReferralCode = useMemo(() => {
    if (profile?.codigo_referido) {
      return String(profile.codigo_referido).trim().toUpperCase();
    }
    const baseId = String(user?.id || 'KNT100')
      .replace(/-/g, '')
      .substring(0, 6)
      .toUpperCase();
    return `KNT-${baseId}`;
  }, [profile?.codigo_referido, user?.id]);

  const handleCopyCode = async () => {
    fireHaptic?.('Success');
    await adminService.copiarAlPortapapeles(myReferralCode);
    setInlineNotice({
      kind: 'success',
      msg: tx(
        `Código ${myReferralCode} copiado al portapapeles.`,
        `Invite code ${myReferralCode} copied to clipboard.`
      ),
    });
  };

  const handleShareReferral = async () => {
    fireHaptic?.('Light');
    const msg = tx(
      `⚡ ¡Entrena conmigo en KINETIC!\n\n🎁 Usa mi código de referido *${myReferralCode}* en tu Bóveda y ambos ganamos *+100 Kineticoins (Rollover)* y *+50 XP* al activar tu membresía.\n\n📲 Únete en:\nhttps://kineticapp.com.mx`,
      `⚡ Train with me on KINETIC!\n\n🎁 Use my referral code *${myReferralCode}* in your Vault and we both earn *+100 Kineticoins (Rollover)* and *+50 XP* when you activate your VIP membership.\n\n📲 Join at:\nhttps://kineticapp.com.mx`
    );
    try {
      await Share.share({
        title: tx('Invitación a Kinetic', 'Kinetic Invite'),
        message: msg,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      void err;
    }
  };

  const handleRedeemFriendCode = async () => {
    if (redeemBusyRef.current || isRedeeming) return;
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    const clean = friendCodeInput.trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!clean) {
      fireHaptic?.('Warning');
      setInlineNotice({
        kind: 'warning',
        msg: tx(
          'Escribe o pega el código de invitación de tu amigo antes de canjearlo.',
          'Enter or paste your friend’s invite code before redeeming.'
        ),
      });
      return;
    }
    if (!user?.id) return;

    redeemBusyRef.current = true;
    setInlineNotice(null);
    setIsRedeeming(true);
    try {
      const res = (await bovedaService.canjearCodigoReferido(user.id, clean)) as { message?: string } | null;
      await refreshProfile?.();
      setFriendCodeInput('');
      triggerArcadeHaptic('levelUp');
      onClose?.();
      if (alertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(alertTimerRef.current);
      alertTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        const okMsg = language === 'en'
          ? 'Code applied successfully. Upon activating any VIP Membership, both of you receive +100 KC (Rollover) and +50 XP.'
          : (res?.message || 'Código aplicado con éxito. Al activar cualquier Membresía VIP ambos reciben +100 KC (Rollover) y +50 XP.');
        showTacticalAlert?.(tx('¡CÓDIGO VINCULADO!', 'CODE LINKED!'), okMsg, 'success');
      }, 280);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      fireHaptic?.('Error');
      const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
      const rawErr = errMsg.toLowerCase();
      const enErr = rawErr.includes('propio')
        ? 'You cannot redeem your own referral code.'
        : rawErr.includes('ya ') || rawErr.includes('previamente')
        ? 'A referral code has already been redeemed on this account.'
        : rawErr.includes('no existe') || rawErr.includes('invalid') || rawErr.includes('inválid') || rawErr.includes('encontr')
        ? 'Invalid or unrecognized referral code.'
        : 'Could not redeem the entered code.';
      setInlineNotice({
        kind: 'error',
        msg: language === 'en' ? enErr : (errMsg || 'No se pudo canjear el código ingresado.'),
      });
    } finally {
      redeemBusyRef.current = false;
      setIsRedeeming(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={isOpen}
      onBackdropPress={onClose}
      onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      avoidKeyboard={true}
    >
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
        style={{ width: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        pointerEvents="box-none"
      >
        <View
          style={[
            globalStyles.bottomSheet,
            KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
            isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderTopColor: '#7C3AED', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' },
            { paddingBottom: 0 },
          ]}
        >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader visible={isOpen} onClose={onClose} accentColor={isLightMode ? '#7C3AED' : '#E0AAFF'} />

          <ScrollView
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            bounces={false}
            keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 12 }}
          >
            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <View style={[styles.giftIconCircle, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F3E8FF', borderColor: '#7C3AED' }]}>
                <Feather name="gift" size={22} color={isLightMode ? '#7C3AED' : '#E0AAFF'} />
              </View>
              <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, isLightMode && { color: '#6D28D9' }]}>
                {tx('PROGRAMA DE REFERIDOS (+100 KC)', 'REFERRAL PROGRAM (+100 KC)')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.modalSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx(
                  'Invita a tus amigos a Kinetic. Cuando activen su primera Membresía VIP, ambos reciben +100 KC en Billetera Rollover y +50 XP.',
                  'Invite your friends to Kinetic. When they activate their first VIP Membership, you both earn +100 KC in your Rollover Wallet and +50 XP.'
                )}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.myCodeCard,
                isLightMode && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4',
                  shadowColor: '#00B8D4', shadowOpacity: 0.2,
                },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx('TU CÓDIGO PERSONAL KINETIC', 'YOUR PERSONAL KINETIC CODE')}
              </Text>
              <View style={styles.myCodeRow}>
                <Text style={[styles.myCodeBigTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{myReferralCode}</Text>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[
                      styles.copyBtn,
                      isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0891B2', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0E7490' },
                      NO_SEL,
                    ]}
                    onPress={handleCopyCode}
                  >
                    <Feather name="copy" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.copyBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('COPIAR', 'COPY')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>

                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.shareBtn, NO_SEL]}
                    onPress={handleShareReferral}
                    {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}
                  >
                    <Feather name="share-2" size={14} color="#050505" />
                    <Text selectable={false} style={styles.shareBtnTxt}>{tx('COMPARTIR', 'SHARE')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.redeemBox,
                isLightMode && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#A855F7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED',
                  shadowColor: '#7C3AED', shadowOpacity: 0.18,
                },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {tx('¿TE INVITÓ UN AMIGO? INGRESA SU CÓDIGO AQUÍ', 'INVITED BY A FRIEND? ENTER THEIR CODE HERE')}
              </Text>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 6, width: '100%' }}>
                <TextInput
                  style={[
                    styles.codeInput,
                    { fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                    isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#A855F7', borderTopColor: '#E9D5FF', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED' },
                  ]}
                  placeholder={tx('Ej. KNT-XXXXXX', 'e.g. KNT-XXXXXX')}
                  placeholderTextColor={attenuatedPlaceholder}
                  autoCapitalize="characters"
                  value={friendCodeInput}
                  onChangeText={(txt) => {
                    if (inlineNotice) setInlineNotice(null);
                    setFriendCodeInput(txt);
                  }}
                />
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.redeemBtn, NO_SEL]}
                  disabled={isRedeeming}
                  onPress={handleRedeemFriendCode}
                  {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}
                >
                  {isRedeeming ? (
                    <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Ionicons name="flash" size={14} color="#050505" />
                      <Text selectable={false} style={styles.redeemBtnTxt}>{tx('CANJEAR', 'REDEEM')}</Text>
                    </>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
              {Boolean(inlineNotice) && (
                <View
                  style={{
                    flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginTop: 10,
                    paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 8, borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1, elevation: 0,
                    borderColor: inlineNotice?.kind === 'error' ? '#EF4444' : inlineNotice?.kind === 'success' ? '#10B981' : '#F59E0B',
                    backgroundColor:
                      inlineNotice?.kind === 'error' ? (isLightMode ? '#FEF2F2' : 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.14)')
                        : inlineNotice?.kind === 'success' ? (isLightMode ? '#ECFDF5' : 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.14)')
                        : (isLightMode ? '#FFFBEB' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.14)'),
                  }}
                >
                  <Feather
                    name={inlineNotice?.kind === 'error' ? 'alert-octagon' : inlineNotice?.kind === 'success' ? 'check-circle' : 'alert-triangle'}
                    size={14}
                    color={inlineNotice?.kind === 'error' ? (isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FCA5A5') : inlineNotice?.kind === 'success' ? (isLightMode ? '#047857' : '#6EE7B7') : (isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FCD34D')}
                  />
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      flex: 1, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '700',
                      color: inlineNotice?.kind === 'error' ? (isLightMode ? '#991B1B' : '#FECACA') : inlineNotice?.kind === 'success' ? (isLightMode ? '#065F46' : '#A7F3D0') : (isLightMode ? '#92400E' : '#FDE68A'),
                    }}
                  >
                    {inlineNotice?.msg}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>
          </ScrollView>

          <KineticErgonomicFooter onBack={() => onClose?.()} style={{ marginHorizontal: -18 }} />
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export const ReferralPromoModal = ReferralModal;

  KineticDisciplineSelectorField,
