    FILE: src/components/boveda/SettingsLegalAndArcoTab.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/SettingsLegalAndArcoTab.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Animated, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { computeHoldDeleteProgress3s } from '../../utils/kineticVolumetric3dPureLeaf';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import { KineticDualConfirmModal } from '../shared/KineticDualConfirmModal';
import { KineticLoader } from '../SharedUI';
import { baseSettingsStyles } from './SettingsModal.styles';

const NO_SEL: ViewStyle | null =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle) : null;

export interface SettingsLegalAndArcoTabProps {
  visible: boolean;
  user: { id?: string; email?: string | null } | null;
  isB2B: boolean;
  showB2BVenue: boolean;
  isMasterAdmin: boolean;
  styles: typeof baseSettingsStyles;
  groupCardTheme: ViewStyle;
  openBillingFromSettings: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  scheduleModalSwitch: (fn: () => void, ms?: number) => void;
  onOpenReferrals?: () => void;
  onOpenLegal?: () => void;
  onOpenAiConcierge?: () => void;
  onOpenHumanSupport?: () => void;
  showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
  onToast: (msg: string | null) => void;
  fireHaptic?: (type?: string) => void;
}

export const SettingsLegalAndArcoTab = memo((props: SettingsLegalAndArcoTabProps) => {
  const {
    visible, user, isB2B, showB2BVenue, isMasterAdmin, styles, groupCardTheme,
    openBillingFromSettings, onClose, scheduleModalSwitch,
    onOpenReferrals, onOpenLegal, onOpenAiConcierge, onOpenHumanSupport,
    showTacticalAlert, onToast, fireHaptic,
  } = props;
  const router = useRouter();
  const { signOut } = useAuth();
  const { themeColors, language, t, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;

  const holdProgress = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const holdTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const holdIntervalRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
  const holdStartMsRef = useRef(0);
  const lastSecondTickRef = useRef<0 | 1 | 2 | 3>(0);
  const holdCompletedRef = useRef(false);
  const purgingRef = useRef(false);

  const [isHoldingDelete, setIsHoldingDelete] = useState(false);
  const [holdCountdown, setHoldCountdown] = useState(3);
  const [holdPercent, setHoldPercent] = useState(0);
  const [holdTrackWidth, setHoldTrackWidth] = useState(280);
  const [showDeleteConfirmInline, setShowDeleteConfirmInline] = useState(false);
  const [showMasterProtectedInline, setShowMasterProtectedInline] = useState(false);
  const [isPurgingAccount, setIsPurgingAccount] = useState(false);

  const masterProtectedMsg = tx(
    'La cuenta maestra de Administrador Oficial está protegida y no puede eliminarse.',
    'The Official Master Admin account is protected and cannot be deleted.'
  );

  const clearHoldTimers = useCallback(() => {
    if (holdTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(holdTimerRef.current); holdTimerRef.current = null; }
    if (holdIntervalRef.current) { clearInterval(holdIntervalRef.current); holdIntervalRef.current = null; }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) {
      clearHoldTimers();
      holdProgress.stopAnimation();
      holdProgress.setValue(0);
      setIsHoldingDelete(false);
      setHoldCountdown(3);
      setHoldPercent(0);
      setShowDeleteConfirmInline(false);
      setShowMasterProtectedInline(false);
      holdCompletedRef.current = false;
      lastSecondTickRef.current = 0;
    }
    return clearHoldTimers;
  }, [visible, clearHoldTimers, holdProgress]);

  const handlePressInDelete = () => {
    if (isPurgingAccount || purgingRef.current) return;
    clearHoldTimers();
    holdCompletedRef.current = false;
    lastSecondTickRef.current = 0;
    holdStartMsRef.current = Date.now();
    setIsHoldingDelete(true);
    setHoldCountdown(3);
    setHoldPercent(0);
    fireHaptic?.('Medium');
    holdProgress.stopAnimation();
    holdProgress.setValue(0);
    Animated.timing(holdProgress, { toValue: 1, duration: 3000, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }).start();
    holdIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      const snap = computeHoldDeleteProgress3s(Date.now() - holdStartMsRef.current, true);
      setHoldPercent(snap.percentInt);
      setHoldCountdown(Math.max(1, 3 - snap.secondTick));
      if (snap.secondTick > lastSecondTickRef.current && snap.secondTick < 3) {
        lastSecondTickRef.current = snap.secondTick;
        fireHaptic?.('Light');
      }
    }, 50);
    holdTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      clearHoldTimers();
      holdCompletedRef.current = true;
      setHoldPercent(100);
      setIsHoldingDelete(false);
      setHoldCountdown(3);
      holdProgress.stopAnimation();
      holdProgress.setValue(0);
      setHoldPercent(0);
      if (isMasterAdmin) {
        fireHaptic?.('Warning');
        setShowDeleteConfirmInline(false);
        setShowMasterProtectedInline(true);
        onToast(masterProtectedMsg);
        return;
      }
      fireHaptic?.('Heavy');
      setShowDeleteConfirmInline(true);
    }, 3000);
  };

  const handlePressOutDelete = () => {
    const cancelledEarly = isHoldingDelete && !holdCompletedRef.current;
    clearHoldTimers();
    holdProgress.stopAnimation();
    holdProgress.setValue(0);
    setIsHoldingDelete(false);
    setHoldCountdown(3);
    setHoldPercent(0);
    lastSecondTickRef.current = 0;
    if (cancelledEarly) {
      onToast(tx('Eliminación cancelada • Tu cuenta sigue protegida', 'Account deletion cancelled • Your account remains protected'));
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmPurgeAccount = async () => {
    if (isMasterAdmin) {
      fireHaptic?.('Warning');
      setShowDeleteConfirmInline(false);
      setShowMasterProtectedInline(true);
      onToast(masterProtectedMsg);
      return;
    }
    if (!user?.id || purgingRef.current) return;
    purgingRef.current = true;
    setIsPurgingAccount(true);
    fireHaptic?.('Heavy');
    try {
      await bovedaService.eliminarCuentaDefinitiva(user.id);
      await signOut().catch(() => {});
      setShowDeleteConfirmInline(false);
      onClose();
      scheduleModalSwitch(() => {
        showTacticalAlert?.(
          tx('CUENTA ELIMINADA DEFINITIVAMENTE', 'ACCOUNT PERMANENTLY DELETED'),
          tx('Tu cuenta y datos personales han sido suprimidos conforme a Derechos ARCO y LFPDPPP.', 'Your account and personal data have been permanently deleted under ARCO Rights & LFPDPPP.'),
          'success'
        );
        router.replace('/login');
      }, 240);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setShowDeleteConfirmInline(false);
      fireHaptic?.('Error');
      const rawErrMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
      const errMsg = language === 'en'
        ? 'Could not process permanent account deletion. Verify your connection or contact Official Support.'
        : (rawErrMsg || 'No se pudo procesar la eliminación.');
      onToast(errMsg);
    } finally {
      purgingRef.current = false;
      setIsPurgingAccount(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <View>
      <TouchableOpacity
        activeOpacity={0.85}
        style={[
          styles.billingActionBtn,
          isLight && baseSettingsStyles.billingActionBtnLight,
          { justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingHorizontal: 14 },
          NO_SEL,
        ]}
        onPress={openBillingFromSettings}
      >
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 11, flex: 1 }}>
          <View
            style={{
              width: 34,
              height: 34,
              borderRadius: 10,
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              backgroundColor: isLight ? '#E0F2FE' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)',
              borderWidth: 1,
              borderColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.4)',
              elevation: 0,
            }}
          >
            <Feather name={showB2BVenue ? 'credit-card' : 'file-text'} size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </View>
          <Text
            selectable={false}
            numberOfLines={2}
            style={[styles.actionBtn3DTxt, { flex: 1, textAlign: 'left', color: themeColors.cyanText }]}
          >
            {isB2B || isMasterAdmin
              ? tx('CLABE SPEI (80%) y Facturación SAT', 'SPEI CLABE (80%) & Tax Billing')
              : tx('Datos de Facturación SAT (CFDI 4.0)', 'Tax & Billing Info (SAT CFDI 4.0)')}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {onOpenLegal && (
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          style={[
            styles.billingActionBtn,
            isLight && baseSettingsStyles.billingActionBtnLight,
            { marginTop: 10, justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingHorizontal: 14 },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
          onPress={() => {
            fireHaptic?.('Light');
            onOpenLegal();
          }}
        >
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 11, flex: 1 }}>
            <View
              style={{
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                borderRadius: 10,
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                backgroundColor: isLight ? '#E0F2FE' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)',
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.4)',
                elevation: 0,
              }}
            >
              <Feather name="shield" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </View>
            <Text
              selectable={false}
              numberOfLines={2}
              style={[styles.actionBtn3DTxt, { flex: 1, textAlign: 'left', color: themeColors.cyanText }]}
            >
              {tx('Contrato Reserva 40/60 y Waiver Legal', '40/60 Reservation & Legal Waiver Contract')}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      )}

      <View style={styles.arcoSectionWrap}>
        <Text style={[styles.arcoHeaderTxt, { color: isLight ? '#991B1B' : '#FF6B6B' }]}>
          {tx('DERECHOS ARCO Y ELIMINACIÓN DE CUENTA', 'ARCO RIGHTS & ACCOUNT DELETION')}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.arcoWarningLead, { color: isLight ? '#475569' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.62)' }]}>
          {tx(
            'Conforme a la LFPDPPP y Derechos ARCO, esta acción suprime de forma permanente e irreversible tus credenciales, perfil e historial.',
            'Under LFPDPPP & ARCO Privacy Rights, this action permanently and irreversibly purges your credentials, profile, and account records.'
          )}
        </Text>

        {showMasterProtectedInline && (
          <View style={[styles.arcoHoldCard, isLight && baseSettingsStyles.arcoHoldCardLight, { padding: 12, marginBottom: 10 }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.arcoHoldTitle, { color: isLight ? '#DC2626' : '#FF453A' }]}>
              {tx('CUENTA MAESTRA PROTEGIDA', 'PROTECTED MASTER ACCOUNT')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.arcoHoldSub, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, marginBottom: 8 }]}>{masterProtectedMsg}</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.confirmCancelBtn, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => setShowMasterProtectedInline(false)}>
              <Text style={[styles.confirmCancelBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}

        <KineticDualConfirmModal
          visible={showDeleteConfirmInline}
          title={tx('CONFIRMAR ELIMINACIÓN DEFINITIVA', 'CONFIRM PERMANENT DELETION')}
          message={tx(
            'Se cancelarán tus reservas activas, se suprimirán tus credenciales y tus datos serán desvinculados permanentemente conforme a LFPDPPP / Derechos ARCO.',
            'Active bookings will be cancelled, login credentials purged, and your identity permanently unlinked under LFPDPPP / ARCO Rights.'
          )}
          confirmLabel={tx('ELIMINAR DEFINITIVAMENTE', 'DELETE PERMANENTLY')}
          cancelLabel={tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel')}
          tone="danger"
          loading={isPurgingAccount}
          onConfirm={handleConfirmPurgeAccount}
          onCancel={() => {
            setShowDeleteConfirmInline(false);
            onToast(tx('Eliminación cancelada • Tu cuenta sigue protegida', 'Account deletion cancelled • Your account remains protected'));
          }}
        />
        {!showDeleteConfirmInline && (
          <View
            onLayout={(e) => {
              const w = Math.round(e.nativeEvent.layout.width);
              if (w > 0) setHoldTrackWidth(w);
            }}
            style={[styles.arcoHoldCard, isLight && baseSettingsStyles.arcoHoldCardLight]}
          >
            <Animated.View
              pointerEvents="none"
              style={[
                styles.arcoProgressFill,
                {
                  backgroundColor: isLight ? 'rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.24)' : 'rgba(255, 59, 48, 0.42)',
                  transform: [
                    {
                      translateX: holdProgress.interpolate({
                        inputRange: [0, 1],
                        outputRange: [-Math.max(1, holdTrackWidth) / 2, 0],
                      }),
                    },
                    { scaleX: holdProgress },
                  ],
                },
              ]}
            />
            <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.9} onPressIn={handlePressInDelete} onPressOut={handlePressOutDelete} style={[styles.arcoHoldTouch, NO_SEL]}>
              <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.arcoHoldTitle, { color: isLight ? '#DC2626' : '#FF453A', fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>
                {isHoldingDelete
                  ? tx(`MANTÉN PRESIONADO... (${holdCountdown}S • ${holdPercent}%)`, `KEEP HOLDING... (${holdCountdown}S • ${holdPercent}%)`)
                  : tx('MANTÉN PRESIONADO 3 SEGUNDOS PARA ELIMINAR CUENTA', 'HOLD FOR 3 SECONDS TO DELETE ACCOUNT')}
              </Text>
              <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.arcoHoldSub, { color: isLight ? '#64748B' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.58)' }]}>
                {isHoldingDelete
                  ? tx('Suelta antes de 3 segundos para cancelar', 'Release before 3 seconds to cancel')
                  : tx('Acción permanente e irreversible • LFPDPPP / Derechos ARCO', 'Permanent & irreversible action • LFPDPPP / ARCO Rights')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/boveda/SettingsModal.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/SettingsModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import Constants from 'expo-constants';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Animated, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, Switch, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useSettingsBoveda } from '../../hooks/useBoveda';
import type { KineticAuthProfile, KineticAuthUser } from '../../services/kineticAuthContextHelpers';
import {
  BIOMETRIC_LOGIN_ENABLED_KEY,
  BIOMETRIC_SAVED_EMAIL_KEY,
  useSettingsModalMediaAndSave,
} from '../../hooks/boveda/useSettingsModalMediaAndSave';
import { isKineticMasterAdmin } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen, useIsDisciplinePickerOpen } from '../../utils/kineticOverlayStack';
import { renderInWebPortal } from '../../utils/kineticWebPortal';
import { BillingCfdiModal } from '../BillingCfdiModal';
import { LegalTermsModal } from '../LegalTermsModal';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from '../shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { KineticThemeLanguageControl } from '../shared/KineticThemeLanguageControl';
import { SettingsB2BVenueSection } from './SettingsB2BVenueSection';
import { SettingsLegalAndArcoTab } from './SettingsLegalAndArcoTab';
import { baseSettingsStyles } from './SettingsModal.styles';
import { SettingsSecurity2FAPanel } from './SettingsSecurity2FAPanel';

const rawExpoVersion = typeof Constants?.expoConfig?.version === 'string' ? Constants.expoConfig.version.trim() : '';
export const KINETIC_APP_VERSION = `KINETIC v${rawExpoVersion || '2.8.6'}`;
export { BIOMETRIC_LOGIN_ENABLED_KEY, BIOMETRIC_SAVED_EMAIL_KEY };
export type SettingsSectionTab = 'perfil' | 'seguridad' | 'preferencias' | 'legal';

const KINETIC_LOGO = require('../../../assets/logo.png');
const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle) : null;
const MASTER_ADMIN_EMAILS = ['administrador@kinetic.com.mx', 'administrador@kinetic.com', 'kinetic@kinetic.com', 'admin@kinetic.com'];

const normalizeSettingsTab = (raw?: SettingsSectionTab | 'prefs' | 'security'): SettingsSectionTab =>
  raw === 'prefs' ? 'preferencias' : raw === 'security' ? 'seguridad' : (raw || 'perfil');

export interface SettingsModalProps {
  visible?: boolean;
  onClose?: () => void;
  user: KineticAuthUser | null;
  profile: KineticAuthProfile | null;
  isB2B?: boolean;
  initialSection?: SettingsSectionTab | 'prefs' | 'security';
  refreshProfile?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
  onOpenFiscal?: () => void;
  onOpenLegal?: () => void;
  onOpenReferrals?: () => void;
  onOpenAiConcierge?: () => void;
  onOpenHumanSupport?: () => void;
  triggerHaptic?: (type?: string) => void;
  alreadySigned?: boolean;
  onLegalAccepted?: () => void;
}

export const SettingsModal = ({
  visible, onClose, user, profile, isB2B, initialSection = 'perfil',
  refreshProfile, showTacticalAlert, onOpenFiscal, onOpenLegal, onOpenReferrals,
  onOpenAiConcierge, onOpenHumanSupport, triggerHaptic: hapticProp,
  alreadySigned = false, onLegalAccepted,
}: SettingsModalProps) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, t, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSettingsStyles, resolvedTheme, ['unlockActionBtn', 'deleteAccountBtn', 'arcoHoldCard']), [resolvedTheme]);
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const fireHaptic = hapticProp || triggerHaptic;
  const settingsScrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const [keepNativeMounted, setKeepNativeMounted] = useState(Boolean(visible));
  const [inlineSubModal, setInlineSubModal] = useState<'fiscal' | 'legal' | null>(null);

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<SettingsSectionTab>(() => normalizeSettingsTab(initialSection));
  const [nombrePerfil, setNombrePerfil] = useState<string>(String(profile?.nombre || ''));
  const [disciplinaB2B, setDisciplinaB2B] = useState<string>(String(profile?.disciplina || 'Fuerza'));
  const [horariosB2B, setHorariosB2B] = useState<string>(String(profile?.horarios || 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00'));
  const [livePrefToast, setLivePrefToast] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const isDisciplinePickerOpen = useIsDisciplinePickerOpen();

  const isMasterAdmin = useMemo(() => {
    const em = String(user?.email || profile?.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const rol = String(profile?.rol || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const tipo = String(profile?.tipo_cuenta || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return MASTER_ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(em) || rol === 'admin' || rol === 'fundador' || tipo === 'admin' || tipo === 'fundador' || isKineticMasterAdmin(em, rol, tipo);
  }, [user?.email, profile?.email, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta]);
  const showB2BVenue = Boolean(isB2B && !isMasterAdmin);

  const {
    whatsapp, setWhatsapp, ubicacion, setUbicacion, portadaUrl, setPortadaUrl,
    isSaving, setIsSaving, isUploading, setIsUploading,
    isUploadingPortada, setIsUploadingPortada, userPrefs, togglePreference,
  } = useSettingsBoveda(user, showB2BVenue, profile);

  const wasVisibleRef = useRef(false);
  const lastUserIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen(true);
    return () => setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen(false);
  }, [Boolean(visible)]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      setKeepNativeMounted(true);
      return;
    }
    setInlineSubModal(null);
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      const tid = setTimeout(() => setKeepNativeMounted(false), 280);
      return () => clearTimeout(tid);
    }
  }, [visible]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) { wasVisibleRef.current = false; return; }
    const uid = String(user?.id || '');
    if (!wasVisibleRef.current || lastUserIdRef.current !== uid) {
      wasVisibleRef.current = true;
      lastUserIdRef.current = uid;
      setActiveTab(normalizeSettingsTab(initialSection));
      setNombrePerfil(String(profile?.nombre ?? ''));
      setDisciplinaB2B(String(profile?.disciplina || 'Fuerza'));
      setHorariosB2B(String(profile?.horarios || 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00'));
      setLivePrefToast(null);
    }
  }, [visible, initialSection, user?.id, profile?.nombre, profile?.disciplina, profile?.horarios]);

  const {
    handleToggleSwitchLive,
    handleAvatarChange,
    handlePortadaChange,
    handleSaveSettings,
  } = useSettingsModalMediaAndSave({
    user,
    profile,
    sessionToken: session?.access_token,
    showB2BVenue,
    language,
    tx,
    nombrePerfil,
    disciplinaB2B,
    horariosB2B,
    whatsapp,
    ubicacion,
    userPrefs,
    togglePreference,
    setPortadaUrl,
    isSaving,
    setIsSaving,
    isUploading,
    setIsUploading,
    isUploadingPortada,
    setIsUploadingPortada,
    setLivePrefToast,
    refreshProfile: refreshProfile ? async () => { await refreshProfile(); } : undefined,
    onClose,
    fireHaptic,
  });

  const inputCardTheme = { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : themeColors.inputBg, borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : themeColors.cardBorder, borderTopColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#020306' };
  const groupCardTheme = { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : themeColors.surfaceElevated, borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : themeColors.cardBorder, borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : themeColors.cardBorderTop, borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : themeColors.cardBorderBottom };

  const timersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);
  const scheduleModalSwitch = useCallback((fn: () => void, ms = 240) => {
    const tid = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    timersRef.current.push(tid);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => () => {
    timersRef.current.forEach(clearTimeout);
    timersRef.current = [];
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!livePrefToast) return;
    const tid = setTimeout(() => setLivePrefToast(null), 2400);
    return () => clearTimeout(tid);
  }, [livePrefToast]);

  const openBillingFromSettings = useCallback(() => {
    fireHaptic?.('Light');
    void onOpenFiscal;
    setInlineSubModal('fiscal');
  }, [fireHaptic, onOpenFiscal]);

  const openLegalFromSettings = useCallback(() => {
    fireHaptic?.('Light');
    void onOpenLegal;
    setInlineSubModal('legal');
  }, [fireHaptic, onOpenLegal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    settingsScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
  }, [activeTab]);

  const [pressedTabKey, setPressedTabKey] = useState<SettingsSectionTab | null>(null);
  const tabItems: Array<{ key: SettingsSectionTab; label: string; icon: keyof typeof Feather.glyphMap }> = [
    { key: 'perfil', label: tx('Perfil', 'Profile'), icon: 'user' },
    { key: 'seguridad', label: tx('Seguridad', 'Security'), icon: 'shield' },
    { key: 'preferencias', label: tx('Ajustes', 'Prefs'), icon: 'sliders' },
    { key: 'legal', label: tx('Legal', 'Legal'), icon: 'file-text' },
  ];

  const handleCloseSheet = useCallback(() => {
    if (inlineSubModal) {
      setInlineSubModal(null);
      return;
    }
    onClose?.();
  }, [inlineSubModal, onClose]);

  const sheetBody = (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      style={{ width: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      pointerEvents={isDisciplinePickerOpen ? 'none' : 'box-none'}
    >
      <View
        pointerEvents={isDisciplinePickerOpen || Boolean(inlineSubModal) ? 'none' : 'auto'}
        style={[
          globalStyles.bottomSheet,
          KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
          {
            minHeight: 0,
            flexShrink: 1,
            backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : themeColors.surface,
            borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : undefined,
            paddingBottom: 0,
            ...(inlineSubModal ? { elevation: 0 } : null),
          },
        ]}
      >
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader visible={Boolean(visible)} onClose={handleCloseSheet} />
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 8 }}>
          <Feather name="sliders" size={20} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text style={[styles.membershipTitle, { marginTop: 4, color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{t('settings.modal_title')}</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.segmentedDock, isLight && baseSettingsStyles.segmentedDockLight]}>
          {tabItems.map((tab) => {
            const active = activeTab === tab.key;
            const isPressed = pressedTabKey === tab.key;
            const staticSurface = active
              ? isLight ? baseSettingsStyles.segmentedTabBtnActiveLight : baseSettingsStyles.segmentedTabBtnActive
              : { backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderColor: 'transparent' };
            const tabInk = active ? (isLight ? '#0284C7' : '#00F0FF') : (isLight ? '#475569' : '#CBD5E1');
            return (
              <Animated.View key={tab.key} style={{ flex: 1, transform: [{ scale: isPressed ? 0.96 : 1 }] }}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.86}
                  onPressIn={() => setPressedTabKey(tab.key)}
                  onPressOut={() => setPressedTabKey((cur) => (cur === tab.key ? null : cur))}
                  onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); setLivePrefToast(null); setActiveTab(tab.key); }}
                  style={NO_SEL}
                >
                  <View style={[styles.segmentedTabBtn, staticSurface]}>
                    <Feather name={tab.icon} size={13} color={tabInk} />
                    <Text numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.78} style={[styles.segmentedTabTxt, { color: tabInk }]}>
                      {tab.label}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </Animated.View>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        <ScrollView ref={settingsScrollRef} style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }} nestedScrollEnabled={true} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" contentContainerStyle={{ paddingTop: 12, paddingBottom: 32 }}>
          {activeTab === 'perfil' && (
            <View>
              <View style={styles.avatarEditContainer}>
                <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.avatarEditWrapper, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleAvatarChange} disabled={isUploading}>
                  <View style={[styles.avatarClipBox, { position: 'relative' }]}>
                    {profile?.avatar_url ? (
                      <Image source={{ uri: profile.avatar_url }} recyclingKey={`settings_av_${profile.avatar_url}`} style={styles.avatarEditImage} contentFit="cover" cachePolicy="memory-disk" transition={0} />
                    ) : isB2B || isMasterAdmin ? (
                      <Image source={KINETIC_LOGO} style={styles.avatarEditImage} contentFit="cover" />
                    ) : (
                      <Feather name="user" size={32} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    )}
                    {isUploading && (
                      <View style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, backgroundColor: 'rgba(5,5,5,0.45)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', elevation: 0 }}>
                        <KineticLoader size={22} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                      </View>
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.avatarEditBadge} {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}>
                    <Feather name="camera" size={12} color="#050505" />
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              <Text style={[styles.inputLabelModal, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{t('settings.kinetic_name')}</Text>
              <View style={[styles.inputFieldBox, inputCardTheme]}>
                <Feather name="user" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                <TextInput
                  style={[styles.inputFieldText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16 }]}
                  value={nombrePerfil}
                  onChangeText={setNombrePerfil}
                  maxLength={80}
                  placeholder={tx('Nombre', 'Name')}
                  placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
                />
              </View>

              {showB2BVenue && (
                <SettingsB2BVenueSection
                  venueName={nombrePerfil || String(profile?.nombre || '')} portadaUrl={portadaUrl} isUploadingPortada={isUploadingPortada}
                  onPickPortada={handlePortadaChange} whatsapp={whatsapp} onChangeWhatsapp={setWhatsapp}
                  disciplinaB2B={disciplinaB2B} onSelectDisciplina={setDisciplinaB2B}
                  ubicacion={ubicacion} onChangeUbicacion={setUbicacion} horariosB2B={horariosB2B} onChangeHorarios={setHorariosB2B}
                />
              )}
            </View>
          )}

          <View style={{ display: activeTab === 'seguridad' ? 'flex' : 'none' }}>
            <SettingsSecurity2FAPanel
              user={user} sessionAccessToken={session?.access_token} biometriaActiva={userPrefs.biometria_activa}
              onToggleBiometria={() => handleToggleSwitchLive('biometria_activa')} onToast={setLivePrefToast}
              showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert} triggerHaptic={fireHaptic}
              onRequestMfaScroll={() => settingsScrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 240, animated: true })}
            />
          </View>

          {activeTab === 'preferencias' && (
            <View>
              <KineticThemeLanguageControl onPreferenceChanged={(msg) => setLivePrefToast(msg)} />
              <Text style={[styles.settingsSectionHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{t('settings.privacy_bio_header')}</Text>
              <View style={[styles.prefsGroupCard, groupCardTheme]}>
                {!isB2B && (
                  <View style={[styles.prefRowItem, isLight && { borderBottomColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.prefTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('settings.public_visibility')}</Text>
                    <Switch value={userPrefs.radar_publico} onValueChange={() => handleToggleSwitchLive('radar_publico')} trackColor={{ false: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#334155', true: isLight ? '#BAE6FD' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.4)' }} thumbColor={userPrefs.radar_publico ? (isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan) : isLight ? '#64748B' : '#888'} />
                  </View>
                )}
                <View style={[styles.prefRowItem, isLight && { borderBottomColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.prefTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('settings.ops_notifications')}</Text>
                  <Switch value={userPrefs.notificaciones_push} onValueChange={() => handleToggleSwitchLive('notificaciones_push')} trackColor={{ false: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#334155', true: isLight ? '#BAE6FD' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.4)' }} thumbColor={userPrefs.notificaciones_push ? (isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan) : isLight ? '#64748B' : '#888'} />
                </View>
                <View style={[styles.prefRowItem, { borderBottomWidth: 0 }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.prefTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('settings.flash_alerts')}</Text>
                  <Switch value={userPrefs.alertas_ventas_flash} onValueChange={() => handleToggleSwitchLive('alertas_ventas_flash')} trackColor={{ false: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#334155', true: isLight ? '#BAE6FD' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.4)' }} thumbColor={userPrefs.alertas_ventas_flash ? (isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan) : isLight ? '#64748B' : '#888'} />
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'legal' && (
            <SettingsLegalAndArcoTab
              visible={Boolean(visible)} user={user} isB2B={Boolean(isB2B)} showB2BVenue={showB2BVenue} isMasterAdmin={isMasterAdmin}
              styles={styles} groupCardTheme={groupCardTheme} openBillingFromSettings={openBillingFromSettings} onClose={handleCloseSheet}
              scheduleModalSwitch={scheduleModalSwitch} onOpenReferrals={onOpenReferrals} onOpenLegal={openLegalFromSettings}
              onOpenAiConcierge={onOpenAiConcierge} onOpenHumanSupport={onOpenHumanSupport}
              showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert} onToast={setLivePrefToast} fireHaptic={fireHaptic}
            />
          )}
        </ScrollView>

        {Boolean(livePrefToast) && (
          <View
            pointerEvents="none"
            style={[
              styles.liveToastStrip,
              {
                position: 'absolute', left: 18, right: 18, bottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 12) + 64, zIndex: 95, marginBottom: 0,
                shadowColor: '#000000', shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 6 }, shadowOpacity: isLight ? 0.16 : 0.45, shadowRadius: 12,
              },
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#047857' },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.liveToastStripText, isLight && { color: '#047857' }]}>{livePrefToast}</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <KineticErgonomicFooter
          onBack={handleCloseSheet}
          rightAction={activeTab === 'perfil' ? { label: t('settings.save_changes'), icon: 'check', tone: 'cyan', loading: isSaving, onPress: handleSaveSettings } : undefined}
          style={{ marginHorizontal: -18 }}
        />
      </View>

      <BillingCfdiModal
        inlineOverlay={true} portalOnWeb={false} visible={inlineSubModal === 'fiscal'} onClose={() => setInlineSubModal(null)}
        user={user} profile={profile} isB2B={Boolean(isB2B)} refreshProfile={refreshProfile}
        showTacticalAlert={showTacticalAlert} triggerHaptic={fireHaptic}
      />
      {inlineSubModal === 'legal' && (
        <LegalTermsModal
          inlineOverlay={true} portalOnWeb={false} isVisible={true} onClose={() => setInlineSubModal(null)}
          initialTab={isB2B ? 'b2b' : 'atleta'} userId={user?.id} userName={profile?.nombre || ''}
          alreadySigned={alreadySigned} onAccept={() => { onLegalAccepted?.(); refreshProfile?.(); }}
        />
      )}
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );

  if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
    if (!visible) return null;
    return renderInWebPortal(
      <View {...({ dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } as Record<string, unknown>)} style={{ position: 'fixed', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, width: '100vw', height: '100dvh', zIndex: 9850, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5)' : 'rgba(5, 5, 5, 0.92)', justifyContent: 'flex-end', elevation: 0 } as unknown as ViewStyle}>
        <Pressable style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }} onPress={handleCloseSheet} />
        {sheetBody}
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!visible && !keepNativeMounted) return null;

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(visible)} style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background} backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92}
      animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false} onModalHide={() => setKeepNativeMounted(false)}
      onBackdropPress={handleCloseSheet} onBackButtonPress={handleCloseSheet}
      useNativeDriver={true} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={true} avoidKeyboard={true}
    >
      {sheetBody}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export default SettingsModal;

    FILE: src/components/boveda/SettingsSecurity2FAPanel.tsx

    // === src/components/boveda/SettingsSecurity2FAPanel.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Linking, Platform, StyleSheet, Switch, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { adminService } from '../../services/adminService';
import {
  IKineticActiveSessionItem, IKineticMfaSnapshot, extractJwtSessionId, fetchMyActiveSessions, getMfaSecuritySnapshot,
  isValidTotpSixDigitCode, revokeSpecificSession, sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode, signOutOtherDevices,
  startTotpEnrollment, unenrollTotpFactor, verifyAndActivateTotpFactor,
} from '../../services/kineticSecurityMfaService';
import { COLORS, RADIUS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticLoader } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as Record<string, string>) : undefined;
const DARK_INK: Record<string, unknown> = { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } };

export interface SettingsSecurity2FAPanelProps {
  user: { id?: string; email?: string | null; [k: string]: unknown } | null | undefined; sessionAccessToken?: string | null; biometriaActiva: boolean; onToggleBiometria: () => void;
  onToast?: (msg: string) => void; showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void; triggerHaptic?: (type: string) => void;
  onRequestMfaScroll?: () => void;
}

export const SettingsSecurity2FAPanel = memo((props: SettingsSecurity2FAPanelProps) => {
  const { user, sessionAccessToken, biometriaActiva, onToggleBiometria, onToast, showTacticalAlert, triggerHaptic, onRequestMfaScroll } = props;
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, t, tx, fmtDate } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, ['cyanBtn', 'greenBadge']), [resolvedTheme]);

  const [mfaSnap, setMfaSnap] = useState<IKineticMfaSnapshot>({
    hasVerifiedTotp: false, verifiedFactors: [], unverifiedFactors: [], currentLevel: 'aal1', nextLevel: 'aal1', requiresMfaChallenge: false,
  });
  const [enrollment, setEnrollment] = useState<{ factorId: string; totpUri: string; secret: string } | null>(null);
  const [totpCode, setTotpCode] = useState('');
  const [confirmUnenroll, setConfirmUnenroll] = useState(false);
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<IKineticActiveSessionItem[]>([]);
  const [isLoadingMfa, setIsLoadingMfa] = useState(false);
  const [isLoadingSessions, setIsLoadingSessions] = useState(true);
  const [revokingId, setRevokingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const busyRef = useRef(false);
  const reqIdRef = useRef(0);
  const currentSessionId = useMemo(() => extractJwtSessionId(sessionAccessToken), [sessionAccessToken]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => { mountedRef.current = false; };
  }, []);

  const loadSecurityState = useCallback(async () => {
    const reqId = ++reqIdRef.current;
    if (!user?.id) {
      setMfaSnap({ hasVerifiedTotp: false, verifiedFactors: [], unverifiedFactors: [], currentLevel: 'aal1', nextLevel: 'aal1', requiresMfaChallenge: false });
      setSessions([]); setIsLoadingSessions(false); return;
    }
    setIsLoadingSessions(true);
    const [snap, activeList] = await Promise.all([getMfaSecuritySnapshot(), fetchMyActiveSessions(sessionAccessToken, isEn)]);
    if (!mountedRef.current || reqId !== reqIdRef.current) return;
    setMfaSnap(snap); setSessions(activeList); setIsLoadingSessions(false);
  }, [user?.id, sessionAccessToken, isEn]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setEnrollment(null);
    setTotpCode('');
    setConfirmUnenroll(false);
    loadSecurityState();
  }, [loadSecurityState]);

  const handleStartEnroll = async () => {
    if (busyRef.current || isLoadingMfa) return;
    busyRef.current = true;
    setIsLoadingMfa(true);
    triggerHaptic?.('Selection');
    try {
      const enrolled = await startTotpEnrollment('Kinetic Authenticator');
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setEnrollment({ factorId: enrolled.factorId, totpUri: enrolled.totpUri, secret: enrolled.secret });
        setTotpCode('');
        setTimeout(() => onRequestMfaScroll?.(), 90);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      triggerHaptic?.('Error');
      const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
      onToast?.(isEn ? 'Sign in with an active cloud connection to link a 2FA authenticator.' : (errMsg || 'Inicia sesión con conexión activa para vincular tu autenticador 2FA.'));
    } finally {
      busyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setIsLoadingMfa(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmTotp = async () => {
    if (!enrollment?.factorId || busyRef.current || isLoadingMfa) return;
    const clean = sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode(totpCode);
    if (!isValidTotpSixDigitCode(clean)) {
      triggerHaptic?.('Warning');
      onToast?.(tx('Ingresa los 6 dígitos numéricos del autenticador', 'Enter the 6-digit code from your authenticator'));
      return;
    }
    busyRef.current = true;
    setIsLoadingMfa(true);
    try {
      await verifyAndActivateTotpFactor(enrollment.factorId, clean);
      triggerHaptic?.('Success');
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setEnrollment(null);
        setTotpCode('');
        onToast?.(tx('✓ Autenticador 2FA activado', '✓ 2FA Authenticator enabled'));
      }
      await loadSecurityState();
    } catch {
      triggerHaptic?.('Error');
      onToast?.(tx('Código de 6 dígitos incorrecto o expirado', 'Invalid or expired 6-digit code'));
    } finally {
      busyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setIsLoadingMfa(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDisableTotp = async () => {
    const activeFactor = mfaSnap.verifiedFactors[0];
    if (!activeFactor?.id || busyRef.current || isLoadingMfa) return;
    busyRef.current = true;
    setIsLoadingMfa(true);
    try {
      await unenrollTotpFactor(String(activeFactor.id));
      triggerHaptic?.('Success');
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setConfirmUnenroll(false);
        onToast?.(tx('Verificación en 2 pasos desactivada', 'Two-factor authentication disabled'));
      }
      await loadSecurityState();
    } catch {
      triggerHaptic?.('Error');
      onToast?.(tx('No se pudo desactivar 2FA en este momento', 'Unable to disable 2FA right now'));
    } finally {
      busyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setIsLoadingMfa(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCopySecret = async () => {
    if (!enrollment?.secret) return;
    await adminService.copiarAlPortapapeles(enrollment.secret);
    triggerHaptic?.('Success');
    onToast?.(tx('✓ Clave manual de autenticador copiada', '✓ Manual authenticator key copied'));
  };

  const handleRevokeSession = async (sessionId: string) => {
    if (busyRef.current || revokingId) return;
    busyRef.current = true;
    setRevokingId(sessionId);
    try {
      const resolvedCurId = currentSessionId || sessions.find((s) => s.isCurrent)?.id || null;
      const ok = await revokeSpecificSession(sessionId, resolvedCurId);
      if (!ok) {
        onToast?.(tx('No puedes revocar la sesión actual', 'Cannot revoke current session'));
        return;
      }
      triggerHaptic?.('Success');
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== sessionId));
        onToast?.(tx('✓ Sesión remota revocada', '✓ Remote session revoked'));
      }
    } catch {
      triggerHaptic?.('Error');
      onToast?.(tx('No se pudo revocar la sesión remota', 'Could not revoke remote session'));
    } finally {
      busyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setRevokingId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleSignOutOthers = async () => {
    if (busyRef.current || isLoadingSessions) return;
    busyRef.current = true;
    setIsLoadingSessions(true);
    try {
      await signOutOtherDevices();
      triggerHaptic?.('Success');
      onToast?.(tx('✓ Otras sesiones cerradas correctamente', '✓ Signed out of all other devices'));
      await loadSecurityState();
    } catch {
      triggerHaptic?.('Error');
      if (mountedRef.current) setIsLoadingSessions(false);
    } finally {
      busyRef.current = false;
    }
  };

  const cardSurface = isLightMode ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' } : null;
  const isMfaActive = mfaSnap.hasVerifiedTotp;

  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 12 }}>
      {/* Bloque 1: Biometría & Llave QR 60s */}
      <View style={[styles.card3D, cardSurface]}>
        <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
            <Text style={[styles.cardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('settings.biometric_qr')}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.cardSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('Protege el acceso rápido y tu llave dinámica QR 60s en puerta.', 'Protects quick sign-in and your 60s dynamic reception QR key.')}
            </Text>
            {Platform.OS === 'web' && (
              <Text style={[styles.cardSub, { color: isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24', marginTop: 4, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
                {tx('Solo disponible en app móvil iOS / Android', 'Available on iOS / Android mobile app only')}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
          <Switch
            value={Platform.OS === 'web' ? false : biometriaActiva}
            disabled={Platform.OS === 'web'}
            onValueChange={Platform.OS === 'web' ? undefined : onToggleBiometria}
            trackColor={{ false: isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : '#334155', true: isLightMode ? '#BAE6FD' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.4)' }}
            thumbColor={Platform.OS !== 'web' && biometriaActiva ? (isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan) : isLightMode ? '#64748B' : '#888888'}
          />
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Bloque 2: Verificación en 2 Pasos (2FA) */}
      <View style={[styles.card3D, cardSurface]}>
        <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flex: 1 }}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={16} color={isMfaActive ? (isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[styles.cardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Verificación en 2 Pasos (2FA)', 'Two-Factor Auth (2FA)')}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.statusBadge, isMfaActive ? styles.greenBadge : styles.cyanBadge]}>
            <Text style={[styles.statusBadgeTxt, { color: isMfaActive ? (isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {isMfaActive ? tx('2FA Activo', '2FA Active') : tx('Estándar', 'Standard')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <Text style={[styles.cardSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted, marginTop: 4 }]}>
          {tx(
            'Vincula Google Authenticator, Authy o 1Password para proteger tu Bóveda con código dinámico cada 30s.',
            'Link Google Authenticator, Authy, or 1Password to protect your Vault with dynamic 30s codes.'
          )}
        </Text>

        {isMfaActive ? (
          <View style={{ marginTop: 10, gap: 8 }}>
            <View style={[styles.linkedBox, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#6EE7B7' }]}>
              <Feather name="check-circle" size={15} color={isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green} />
              <Text style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textPrimary }}>
                {String(mfaSnap.verifiedFactors[0]?.friendly_name || 'Kinetic Authenticator')}
              </Text>
            </View>
            {confirmUnenroll ? (
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
                <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.ghostBtn, { flex: 1 }]} onPress={() => setConfirmUnenroll(false)} disabled={isLoadingMfa}>
                  <Text style={[styles.ghostBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL')}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.dangerBtn, { flex: 1 }]} onPress={handleDisableTotp} disabled={isLoadingMfa}>
                  {isLoadingMfa ? <KineticLoader size={15} color="#FFFFFF" /> : <Text style={styles.dangerBtnTxt}>{tx('CONFIRMAR BAJA', 'CONFIRM DISABLE')}</Text>}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.ghostBtn, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic?.('Warning'); setConfirmUnenroll(true); }} disabled={isLoadingMfa}>
                <Text style={[styles.ghostBtnTxt, { color: isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF6B6B' }]}>{tx('DESACTIVAR 2FA', 'DISABLE 2FA')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
        ) : enrollment ? (
          <View style={{ marginTop: 12, alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
            <View style={[styles.qrFrame, isLightMode && { borderColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
              <QRCode value={enrollment.totpUri} size={132} backgroundColor="#FFFFFF" color="#050505" />
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.secretCopyRow,
                { justifyContent: 'center' },
                isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic?.('Selection');
                Linking.openURL(enrollment.totpUri).catch(() => {
                  onToast?.(tx('Copia la clave manual abajo e ingrésala en tu App Autenticadora.', 'Copy the manual key below into your Authenticator App.'));
                });
              }}
            >
              <Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', color: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan }}>
                {tx('📲 Abrir en App Autenticadora', '📲 Open in Authenticator App')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.secretCopyRow, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' }, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleCopySecret}>
              <Text numberOfLines={1} style={[styles.secretMonoTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{enrollment.secret}</Text>
              <Feather name="copy" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TextInput
              style={[styles.codeInput, { fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary, borderColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.45)', backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : 'rgba(5,7,11,0.9)' }]}
              value={totpCode}
              onChangeText={(v) => setTotpCode(sanitizeTotpSixDigitCode(v))}
              onFocus={() => setTimeout(() => onRequestMfaScroll?.(), 120)}
              placeholder="000000"
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              keyboardType="number-pad"
              maxLength={12}
              autoComplete="one-time-code"
            />
            <TouchableOpacity {...DARK_INK} style={[styles.cyanBtn, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleConfirmTotp} disabled={isLoadingMfa}>
              {isLoadingMfa ? <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" /> : <Text style={styles.cyanBtnTxt}>{tx('CONFIRMAR CÓDIGO DE 6 DÍGITOS', 'CONFIRM 6-DIGIT CODE')}</Text>}
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => { unenrollTotpFactor(enrollment.factorId).catch(() => {}); setEnrollment(null); setTotpCode(''); }} disabled={isLoadingMfa}>
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textMuted }}>{tx('Cancelar vinculación', 'Cancel setup')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <TouchableOpacity {...DARK_INK} style={[styles.cyanBtn, { marginTop: 10 }, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleStartEnroll} disabled={isLoadingMfa}>
            {isLoadingMfa ? <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" /> : <Text style={styles.cyanBtnTxt}>{tx('ACTIVAR AUTENTICADOR 2FA', 'ENABLE 2FA AUTHENTICATOR')}</Text>}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Bloque 3: Sesiones Activas y Dispositivos */}
      <View style={[styles.card3D, cardSurface]}>
        <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
          <Text style={[styles.cardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Sesiones Activas y Dispositivos', 'Active Sessions & Devices')}</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={loadSecurityState} disabled={isLoadingSessions} hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}>
            <Feather name="refresh-cw" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {isLoadingSessions ? (
          <View style={{ paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center' }}><KineticLoader size={20} color={themeColors.cyanText} /></View>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ marginTop: 8, gap: 8 }}>
            {sessions.map((item) => (
              <View key={item.id} style={[styles.sessionRow, isLightMode && { borderColor: '#E2E8F0', backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC' }]}>
                <Feather name={item.deviceIcon} size={16} color={item.isCurrent ? (isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : themeColors.cyanText} />
                <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textPrimary }} numberOfLines={1}>{item.deviceLabel}</Text>
                    {item.isCurrent && (
                      <View style={styles.thisDevicePill}>
                        <Text style={{ fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '900', color: isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green }}>{tx('ESTE DISPOSITIVO', 'THIS DEVICE')}</Text>
                      </View>
                    )}
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.cyanText }}>{item.aalBadge}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: themeColors.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {item.ipMasked} • {fmtDate(item.updatedAt)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {!item.isCurrent && (
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.revokeMiniBtn} onPress={() => handleRevokeSession(item.id)} disabled={revokingId === item.id}>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF6B6B' }}>
                      {revokingId === item.id ? '...' : tx('Revocar', 'Revoke')}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            ))}
            {sessions.length > 1 && (
              <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.ghostBtn, { marginTop: 4 }, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleSignOutOthers} disabled={isLoadingSessions}>
                <Text style={[styles.ghostBtnTxt, { color: isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF6B6B' }]}>{tx('CERRAR OTRAS SESIONES', 'SIGN OUT OTHER SESSIONS')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
});

    import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { getLevelNumericWeight } from '../../constants/memberships';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { localizeVipTierName } from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { topUpSectionBaseStyles as baseStyles } from './TopUpPackagesSection.styles';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle) : null;

export const TOPUP_NON_MEMBER_SURCHARGE_RATE = 0.2;

export const formatMxnCurrency = (amount: unknown, includeSuffix = true): string => {
  const raw = typeof amount === 'string' ? amount.replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, '') : amount;
  const num = Number(raw);
  const safe = Number.isFinite(num) && num >= 0 ? num : 0;
  const [intPart, decPart] = safe.toFixed(2).split('.');
  const withCommas = intPart.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
  return includeSuffix ? `$${withCommas}.${decPart}\u00A0MXN` : `$${withCommas}.${decPart}`;
};

export const TOPUP_PACKAGES = [
  {

    kc: 100,

    descEn: 'Book multiple single sessions with permanent validity (Guaranteed Rollover).',
  },

    id: 'topup_500',
    kc: 500,
    precio_mxn: 500,
    etiqueta: 'Recargar Kineticoins',

    precio_mxn: 1000,
    etiqueta: 'Bóveda Máxima',
    etiquetaEn: 'Maximum Vault',
    desc: 'Máximo respaldo de Kineticoins en pago único con vigencia permanente (Rollover).',
    descEn: 'Maximum Kineticoins reserve in a one-time payment with permanent validity (Rollover).',
  },
];

export type TopUpPackageItem = (typeof TOPUP_PACKAGES)[number];

export const resolveCleanVipLevel = (
  rawLevel?: string | null,
  kcMembresia?: number | null,
): string => {
  const clean = String(rawLevel || '').trim();
  const lower = clean.toLowerCase();
  if (lower === 'acceso base' || lower === 'base access' || lower === 'ninguno') return 'Acceso Base';
  if (lower.startsWith('top-up') || lower.startsWith('topup') || !clean) {
    const memKc = Number(kcMembresia) || 0;
    if (memKc >= 3600) return 'Platinum';
    if (memKc >= 2500) return 'Gold';
    if (memKc >= 1500) return 'Silver';
    if (memKc >= 600) return 'Bronze';
    return 'Acceso Base';
  }
  if (lower.includes('platino') || lower.includes('platinum')) return 'Platinum';
  if (lower.includes('oro') || lower.includes('gold')) return 'Gold';
  if (lower.includes('plata') || lower.includes('silver')) return 'Silver';
  if (lower.includes('bronce') || lower.includes('bronze')) return 'Bronze';
  return clean;
};

export const getTopUpPricingForLevel = (
  baseMxn: number,
  rawLevel?: string | null,
  kcMembresia?: number | null,
) => {
  const cleanBase = Number.isFinite(Number(baseMxn)) && Number(baseMxn) > 0 ? Math.round(Number(baseMxn)) : 0;
  const resolvedLvl = resolveCleanVipLevel(rawLevel, kcMembresia);
  const isVipMember = getLevelNumericWeight(resolvedLvl) >= 1;
  const surchargeMxn = isVipMember ? 0 : Math.round(cleanBase * TOPUP_NON_MEMBER_SURCHARGE_RATE);
  const totalMxn = cleanBase + surchargeMxn;
  const nonMemberReferenceMxn = cleanBase + Math.round(cleanBase * TOPUP_NON_MEMBER_SURCHARGE_RATE);

  return {
    baseMxn: cleanBase,
    surchargeMxn,
    totalMxn,
    nonMemberReferenceMxn,
    isVipMember,
  };
};

              'RECARGAS SUELTAS TOP-UP (ROLLOVER • NUNCA EXPIRAN)',

            styles.rolloverNoticeBox,

          ]}

          {hasActiveMembership ? (

              {tx('Al tener membresía activa (', 'With your active VIP membership (')}
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                {localizeVipTierName(effectiveLevel, isEn)}
              </Text>
              {tx('), tus recargas Top-Up tienen ', '), your Top-Up packages enjoy ')}
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                {tx(
                  'paridad 1:1 (100 KC = $100 MXN • 0% comisión)',

                )}
              </Text>{' '}
              {tx(
                'y se depositan en tu billetera Rollover sin caducidad.',
                'and never expire in your Rollover wallet.',
              )}
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <Text style={[styles.rolloverNoticeText, isLight && { color: '#334155' }]}>
              <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FFCC00', fontWeight: '900' }}>
                {tx('Modo Acceso Base (+20% cargo app):', 'Base Access Mode (+20% app fee):')}
              </Text>{' '}
              {tx(
                'Puedes recargar Kineticoins sueltos que nunca caducan, o activar cualquier Membresía VIP para obtener paridad 1:1 (0% comisión).',

              )}
            </Text>
          )}
        </View>
