          const isThisTopupLoading =
            internalProcessingId === pkg.id || processingPlanId === pkg.id;
          const pricing = getTopUpPricingForLevel(pkg.precio_mxn, effectiveLevel, kcMembresia);
          const isConfirmOpen = confirmPkgId === pkg.id;
          const formattedTotal = formatMxnCurrency(pricing.totalMxn);
          const formattedBase = formatMxnCurrency(pricing.baseMxn, false);
          const formattedSurcharge = formatMxnCurrency(pricing.surchargeMxn, false);
          const formattedRef = formatMxnCurrency(pricing.nonMemberReferenceMxn);

          return (
            <View
              key={pkg.id}
              onLayout={(e) => {
                pkgOffsetsRef.current[pkg.id] = Number(e?.nativeEvent?.layout?.y || 0);
              }}
              style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}
            >
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.topupCardItem,
                  { marginBottom: 0 },
                  isLight && {
                    backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderColor: '#CBD5E1',
                    borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED',
                  },
                  isConfirmOpen && {
                    borderColor: '#10B981',
                    borderTopColor: isLight ? '#6EE7B7' : '#34D399',
                    borderBottomColor: '#059669',
                  },
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
              >
                <View style={{ width: '100%' }}>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginBottom: 4 }}>
                    <View
                      style={[styles.topupKcPill, NO_SEL]}
                      {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'light', kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}
                    >
                      <Text selectable={false} style={styles.topupKcPillTxt}>+{pkg.kc} KC</Text>
                    </View>
                    <Text style={[styles.topupCardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                      {isEn ? pkg.etiquetaEn : pkg.etiqueta}
                    </Text>
                  </View>

                  <Text style={[styles.topupCardDesc, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                    {isEn ? pkg.descEn : pkg.desc}
                  </Text>

                  {pricing.isVipMember ? (
                    <View style={{ marginTop: 5 }}>
                      <Text
                        style={[
                          styles.topupPriceTag,
                          isLight && { color: '#047857' },
                        ]}
                      >
                        {formattedTotal} • {tx('Paridad VIP 1:1 (0% comisión)', 'VIP 1:1 Parity (0% fee)')}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.topupSurchargeBreakdown, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                        {tx('Sin Membresía VIP (+20%):', 'Without VIP Membership (+20%):')} {formattedRef}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  ) : (
                    <View style={{ marginTop: 5 }}>
                      <Text
                        style={[
                          styles.topupPriceTag,
                          { color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FFCC00' },
                        ]}
                      >
                        {tx(
                          `Total: ${formattedTotal} (${formattedBase} base + ${formattedSurcharge} cuota app)`,
                          `Total: ${formattedTotal} (${formattedBase} base + ${formattedSurcharge} app fee)`,
                        )}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    styles.topupBuyBtn,
                    isLight && {
                      backgroundColor: '#7C3AED',
                      borderColor: '#9333EA',
                      borderTopColor: '#EDE9FE',
                      borderBottomColor: '#4C1D95',
                      shadowColor: '#7C3AED',
                      shadowOpacity: 0.32,
                    },
                    NO_SEL,
                  ]}
                  disabled={isBusy}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    setConfirmPkgId(pkg.id);
                    if (rafScrollRef.current != null) cancelAnimationFrame(rafScrollRef.current);
                    rafScrollRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                      rafScrollRef.current = null;
                      const yOffset = sectionOffsetYRef.current + (pkgOffsetsRef.current[pkg.id] ?? 0);
                      scrollViewRef?.current?.scrollTo({
                        y: Math.max(0, yOffset - 14),
                        animated: true,
                      });
                    });
                  }}
                  {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'light', kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}
                >
                  {isThisTopupLoading ? (
                    <KineticLoader size={16} color="#FFFFFF" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Feather name="zap" size={13} color="#FFFFFF" />
                      <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', flexShrink: 1 }}>
                        <Text selectable={false} style={styles.topupBuyBtnLabel} numberOfLines={1}>
                          {tx('RECARGAR', 'TOP UP')}
                        </Text>
                        <Text
                          selectable={false}
                          style={styles.topupBuyBtnPrice}
                          numberOfLines={1}
                          adjustsFontSizeToFit
                          minimumFontScale={0.72}
                        >
                          {formattedTotal}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    </>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              {isConfirmOpen && (
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.inlineConfirmCard,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: isLight ? '#ECFDF5' : 'rgba(6, 78, 59, 0.35)',
                      elevation: 0,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.inlineConfirmOverline}>
                    {tx('PAGO ÚNICO • CERO SUSCRIPCIÓN OCULTA', 'ONE-TIME PAYMENT • ZERO HIDDEN SUBSCRIPTION')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.inlineConfirmDesc, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                    {tx(
                      `Confirmar recarga única +${pkg.kc} KC por ${formattedTotal}.`,
                      `Confirm one-time +${pkg.kc} KC top-up for ${formattedTotal}.`,
                    )}
                  </Text>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={styles.inlineConfirmPayBtn}
                      disabled={isBusy}
                      onPress={() => {
                        triggerHaptic('Medium');
                        setConfirmPkgId(null);
                        onSelectPackage(pkg);
                      }}
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.inlineConfirmPayTxt} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>
                        {tx(`PAGAR ${formattedTotal}`, `PAY ${formattedTotal}`)}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={[styles.inlineConfirmCancelBtn, { borderColor: themeColors.cardBorder }]}
                      disabled={isBusy}
                      onPress={() => {
                        triggerHaptic('Selection');
                        setConfirmPkgId(null);
                      }}
                    >
                      <Text style={[styles.inlineConfirmCancelTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                        {tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel')}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>
          );
        })}
      </View>
    );
  },
);

    FILE: src/components/boveda/VipMembershipPlansSection.tsx

    import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { IKineticMembership, getLevelNumericWeight } from '../../constants/memberships';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { localizeVipTierName } from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { baseStyles } from '../MembershipModal.styles';
import { KineticLoader } from '../SharedUI';
import { formatMxnCurrency } from './TopUpPackagesSection';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;

const getPlanColor = (nivel: string) => {
  const n = String(nivel || '').toUpperCase();
  if (n.includes('BRONZE') || n.includes('BRONCE')) return '#CD7F32';
  if (n.includes('SILVER') || n.includes('PLATA')) return '#A8B2C1';
  if (n.includes('GOLD') || n.includes('ORO')) return COLORS.yellow;
  if (n.includes('PLATINUM') || n.includes('PLATINO') || n === 'PRO' || n.includes('DIAMOND')) return COLORS.cyan;
  if (n.includes('BLACK') || n.includes('ÉLITE') || n.includes('ELITE') || n.includes('LEYENDA')) return '#E0AAFF';
  return COLORS.textLight;
};

const getPlanLipColor = (nivel: string) => {
  const n = String(nivel || '').toUpperCase();
  if (n.includes('BRONZE') || n.includes('BRONCE')) return '#7C4700';
  if (n.includes('SILVER') || n.includes('PLATA')) return '#64748B';
  if (n.includes('GOLD') || n.includes('ORO')) return '#B45309';
  if (n.includes('BLACK') || n.includes('ÉLITE') || n.includes('ELITE') || n.includes('LEYENDA')) return '#5B21B6';
  return '#008B99';
};

const getPlanTextColor = (nivel: string, isLight: boolean) => {
  if (!isLight) return getPlanColor(nivel);
  const n = String(nivel || '').toUpperCase();
  if (n.includes('BRONZE') || n.includes('BRONCE') || n.includes('GOLD') || n.includes('ORO')) return '#92400E';
  if (n.includes('SILVER') || n.includes('PLATA')) return '#475569';
  if (n.includes('PLATINUM') || n.includes('PLATINO') || n === 'PRO') return '#006B82';
  if (n.includes('BLACK') || n.includes('ÉLITE') || n.includes('ELITE') || n.includes('LEYENDA')) return '#6D28D9';
  return '#334155';
};

interface VipMembershipPlansProps {
  paidMembershipPlans: IKineticMembership[];
  hasActiveMembership: boolean;
  userWeight: number;
  currentMembershipObj: IKineticMembership | undefined;
  processingPlanId?: string | null;
  internalProcessingId: string | null;
  isBusy: boolean;
  handleMembershipAction: (
    plan: IKineticMembership,
    isDowngrade: boolean,
    kcDeltaToCredit: number,
    priceDeltaToPay: number,
  ) => void;
  onSwitchToTopUp: () => void;
}

export const VipMembershipPlansSection = memo(
  ({
    paidMembershipPlans,
    hasActiveMembership,
    userWeight,
    currentMembershipObj,
    processingPlanId,
    internalProcessingId,
    isBusy,
    handleMembershipAction,
    onSwitchToTopUp,
  }: VipMembershipPlansProps) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, ['planKcBadge', 'directTopUpFromCurrentBtn']),
      [resolvedTheme],
    );
    const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
    const isEnMode = language === 'en';
    const numLocale = isEnMode ? 'en-US' : 'es-MX';

    return (
      <>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.sectionCategoryBanner,
            isLight && {
              backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE',
              borderColor: '#0284C7',
              borderBottomColor: '#0369A1',
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Feather name="award" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text
            style={[styles.sectionCategoryText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}
            numberOfLines={2}
          >
            {tx(
              'ESCALERA OFICIAL VIP (BILLETERA MEMBRESÍA • 30 DÍAS)',
              'OFFICIAL VIP LADDER (MEMBERSHIP WALLET • 30 DAYS)',
            )}
          </Text>
        </View>
        {paidMembershipPlans.map((plan) => {
          const planColor = getPlanColor(plan.nivel);
          const planWeight = getLevelNumericWeight(plan.nivel);
          const localizedPlanTier = localizeVipTierName(plan.nivel, isEnMode);
          const isCurrentLevel = hasActiveMembership && planWeight === userWeight;
          const isUpgrade = !hasActiveMembership || planWeight > userWeight;
          const isDowngrade = hasActiveMembership && planWeight < userWeight;
          const activePlanRef =
            currentMembershipObj || paidMembershipPlans.find((p) => getLevelNumericWeight(p.nivel) === userWeight);
          const currentPlanKc = hasActiveMembership ? Number(activePlanRef?.kc_otorgados) || 0 : 0;
          const currentPlanPrice = hasActiveMembership ? Number(activePlanRef?.precio_mensual_mxn) || 0 : 0;
          const kcDeltaToCredit = isDowngrade
            ? 0
            : isUpgrade && hasActiveMembership
            ? Math.max(0, Number(plan.kc_otorgados) - currentPlanKc)
            : Number(plan.kc_otorgados);
          const priceDeltaToPay = isDowngrade
            ? 0
            : isUpgrade && hasActiveMembership
            ? Math.max(0, Number(plan.precio_mensual_mxn) - currentPlanPrice)
            : Number(plan.precio_mensual_mxn);
          const isThisPlanLoading =
            processingPlanId === plan.nivel ||
            processingPlanId === plan.id ||
            internalProcessingId === plan.id ||
            internalProcessingId === plan.nivel;

          const buttonLabel = isCurrentLevel
            ? isEnMode ? 'CURRENT TIER' : 'NIVEL ACTUAL'
            : isUpgrade
            ? hasActiveMembership
              ? isEnMode ? `UPGRADE (+${kcDeltaToCredit} KC)` : `SUBIR (+${kcDeltaToCredit} KC)`
              : isEnMode ? `ACTIVATE ${localizedPlanTier.toUpperCase()}` : `ACTIVAR ${localizedPlanTier.toUpperCase()}`
            : isEnMode ? 'SWITCH AT CYCLE' : 'CAMBIAR AL CORTE';

          const formattedFullPrice = formatMxnCurrency(plan.precio_mensual_mxn, false).replace(/^\$/, '');
          const formattedDeltaPrice = formatMxnCurrency(priceDeltaToPay, false).replace(/^\$/, '');

          return (
            <View
              key={plan.id}
              style={[
                styles.planCard,
                { borderColor: isCurrentLevel ? COLORS.green : `${planColor}66` },
                isLight && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderColor: isCurrentLevel ? '#059669' : '#CBD5E1',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomColor: isCurrentLevel ? '#047857' : '#94A3B8',
                  shadowColor: '#0F172A',
                  shadowOpacity: 0.12,
                },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.planHeaderRow}>
                <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.planTierLabel,
                        { color: getPlanTextColor(plan.nivel, isLight) },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {localizedPlanTier.toUpperCase()}
                    </Text>
                    {isCurrentLevel && (
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.activeLevelPill,
                          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#D1FAE5', borderColor: '#059669' },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Feather
                          name="check-circle"
                          size={10}
                          color={isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green}
                        />
                        <Text
                          style={[
                            styles.activeLevelPillTxt,
                            isLight && { color: '#047857' },
                          ]}
                        >
                          {tx('ACTIVO', 'ACTIVE')}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.planCommercialTitle,
                      { color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                    ]}
                    numberOfLines={2}
                  >
                    {isEnMode
                      ? plan.nombre_comercial_en || plan.nombre_en || plan.nombre_comercial
                      : plan.nombre_comercial}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View
                  style={[styles.planKcBadge, { backgroundColor: planColor }]}
                  {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any)}
                >
                  <Feather name="zap" size={12} color="#050505" />
                  <Text
                    selectable={false}
                    style={[
                      styles.planKcText,
                      { color: '#050505', fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {isUpgrade && hasActiveMembership
                      ? `+${kcDeltaToCredit} KC ${tx('DIF', 'DIFF')}`
                      : `${plan.kc_otorgados} KC`}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.planPillsRow}>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.planMiniPill,
                    isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FEF3C7', borderColor: '#F59E0B' },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.planMiniPillTxt,
                      isLight && { color: '#B45309' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    ★ {tx(`1 a ${plan.acceso_estrellas_max} Estrellas`, `1 to ${plan.acceso_estrellas_max} Stars`)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.planMiniPill,
                    isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F3E8FF', borderColor: '#A855F7' },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.planMiniPillTxt,
                      { color: isLight ? '#7C3AED' : COLORS.purpleLight },
                    ]}
                  >
                    ⚡ Flash -{plan.descuento_flash}%
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {plan.perdones_mensuales > 0 && (
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.planMiniPill,
                      isLight && { backgroundColor: '#D1FAE5', borderColor: '#10B981' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.planMiniPillTxt,
                        { color: isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green },
                      ]}
                    >
                      🛡️ {plan.perdones_mensuales}{' '}
                      {isEnMode
                        ? 'VIP Forgiveness <72h'
                        : `${plan.perdones_mensuales === 1 ? 'Perdón' : 'Perdones'} <72h`}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>

              <View
                style={[
                  styles.planBenefitsBox,
                  isLight && { borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' },
                ]}
              >
                {(isEnMode ? plan.beneficios_en || plan.beneficios : plan.beneficios || []).map((ben, idx) => (
                  <View key={idx} style={styles.benefitItemRow}>
                    <Feather
                      name="check"
                      size={13}
                      color={isLight ? themeColors.cyanText : planColor}
                    />
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.benefitItemText,
                        isLight && { color: '#334155' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {ben}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                ))}
              </View>

              <View
                style={[
                  styles.planFooterRow,
                  isLight && { borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={{ width: '100%' }}>
                  {isUpgrade && hasActiveMembership ? (
                    <>
                      <Text
                        style={[styles.planPrice, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
                        numberOfLines={1}
                        adjustsFontSizeToFit
                      >
                        +${formattedDeltaPrice}{' '}
                        <Text style={[styles.planCurrency, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                          {tx('MXN diferencia', 'MXN diff')}
                        </Text>
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.planIvaSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                        {tx(
                          `Plan ${localizedPlanTier}: $${formattedFullPrice}/mes • Recibes +${kcDeltaToCredit} KC netos`,
                          `${localizedPlanTier} Plan: $${formattedFullPrice}/mo • Receive +${kcDeltaToCredit} net KC`,

                      <Text
                        style={[styles.planPrice, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
                        numberOfLines={1}
                        adjustsFontSizeToFit
                      >
                        ${formattedFullPrice}{' '}
                        <Text style={[styles.planCurrency, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                          {tx('MXN / próximo mes', 'MXN / next month')}
                        </Text>
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.planIvaSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                        {tx(
                          'Cambio sin cargo hoy • Conservas tu saldo actual',
                          'No charge today • Keep your current balance',
                        )}
                      </Text>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Text
                        style={[styles.planPrice, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
                        numberOfLines={1}
                        adjustsFontSizeToFit
                      >
                        ${formattedFullPrice}{' '}
                        <Text style={[styles.planCurrency, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                          {tx('MXN / mes', 'MXN / month')}
                        </Text>
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.planIvaSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                        {tx(
                          'IVA Incluido • Prioridad 1 (Ciclo 30 días)',
                          'Tax Included • Priority 1 (30-day Cycle)',
                        )}
                      </Text>
                    </>
                  )}
                </View>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    styles.buyBtn,
                    isCurrentLevel
                      ? [
                          styles.buyBtnLockedCurrent,
                          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#059669' },
                        ]
                      : isDowngrade
                      ? [
                          styles.buyBtnDowngrade,
                          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                        ]
                      : {
                          backgroundColor: planColor,
                          borderColor: planColor,
                          borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                          borderBottomColor: getPlanLipColor(plan.nivel),
                          shadowColor: planColor,
                        },

                  onPress={() =>
                    handleMembershipAction(plan, isDowngrade, kcDeltaToCredit, priceDeltaToPay)
                  }
                  disabled={isBusy || isCurrentLevel}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  {...(!isCurrentLevel && !isDowngrade ? ({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any) : {})}
                >
                  {isThisPlanLoading ? (
                    <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Feather
                        name={isCurrentLevel ? 'check-circle' : isDowngrade ? 'refresh-cw' : 'zap'}
                        size={13}
                        color={isCurrentLevel ? (isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : isDowngrade ? themeColors.textPrimary : '#050505'}
                      />
                      <Text
                        selectable={false}
                        style={[
                          styles.buyBtnText,
                          isCurrentLevel && { color: isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green },
                          isDowngrade && { color: themeColors.textPrimary },
                        ]}
                      >
                        {buttonLabel}
                      </Text>
                    </>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              {isCurrentLevel && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.directTopUpFromCurrentBtn, { width: '100%', alignSelf: 'stretch' }, NO_SEL]}
                  onPress={onSwitchToTopUp}
                  {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any)}
                >
                  <Feather name="plus-circle" size={14} color="#050505" />
                  <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.78} style={[styles.directTopUpFromCurrentTxt, { flexShrink: 1 }]}>
                    {tx('SUBIR DE NIVEL / RECARGAR (+)', 'LEVEL UP / TOP UP (+)')}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>
          );
        })}
      </>
    );
  },
);

import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';

import { buildQrCancelCtaLabel, evaluateCancellationWindowPolicy } from '../services/kineticCancellationPolicyHelper';

  buildCancelDualConfirmCopy,
  buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel,
  type DigitalPassRecordInput,
} from '../utils/kineticDigitalPassViewModelLeaf';
import { ReceiptModal, type IReceiptModalProps } from './boveda/BovedaReceiptModalLeaf';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from './boveda/BovedaTacticalAlertModal';
import { KineticCalendarAndWalletActions } from './boveda/KineticCalendarAndWalletActions';
import { KineticDualConfirmModal } from './shared/KineticDualConfirmModal';
import { KineticBoardingPassWalletCard } from './shared/KineticVaultAndChest3D';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';

export { ReceiptModal, TacticalAlertModal, buildQrCancelCtaLabel };
export type { IReceiptModalProps };

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as Record<string, string>) : null;

type PassModalRecord = DigitalPassRecordInput & {
  id?: string;
  estado?: string;
  qr_token?: string;
  created_at?: string;
  perdon_vip_aplicado?: boolean;
  servicios?: { fecha?: string; hora_inicio?: string; fecha_clase?: string; hora?: string };
};

export interface IQRModalProps {
  selectedQR: PassModalRecord | null;
  isQRReady?: boolean;
  isWithinWindow?: boolean;
  qrPayload: string;
  isRegeneratingQR?: boolean;
  windowMessage?: string;
  qrTimeLeft: number;
  isCanceling?: boolean;
  isAdminPass?: boolean;
  formatPassDate?: (iso: string, lang?: string) => string;
  getCostoTotal?: (item: PassModalRecord) => number;
  cancelarReserva?: (item: PassModalRecord) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  triggerHaptic?: typeof triggerHaptic;
}

export const QRModal = React.memo(
  ({ selectedQR, qrPayload, qrTimeLeft, isCanceling, isAdminPass, getCostoTotal, cancelarReserva, onClose }: IQRModalProps) => {
    const { profile } = useAuth();
    const { resolvedTheme, language, tx, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const prStyles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(basePrStyles, resolvedTheme, ['unlockActionBtn']), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [confirmCancelOpen, setConfirmCancelOpen] = useState(false);
    useEffect(() => { setConfirmCancelOpen(false); }, [selectedQR?.id]);
    const lastValidDataRef = useRef<PassModalRecord | null>(null);
    if (selectedQR) lastValidDataRef.current = selectedQR;
    const activeQR = selectedQR || lastValidDataRef.current;

    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const passVm = useMemo(
      () => buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel(activeQR, { isEn, isAdminOverride: isAdminPass }),
      [activeQR, isEn, isAdminPass]
    );
    const effectiveSecs = Number(qrTimeLeft) > 0 && Number(qrTimeLeft) <= 60 ? Number(qrTimeLeft) : 60;
    const fallbackQrId = String(activeQR?.qr_token || activeQR?.id || 'knt').trim();
    const offlineSlot = Math.floor((Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs()) / 60_000);
    const effectivePayload = qrPayload || `KINETIC_PASS|${fallbackQrId}|${offlineSlot}`;
    const totalCosto = passVm.isMasterPass ? 0 : (getCostoTotal && activeQR ? getCostoTotal(activeQR) : passVm.totalKc);
    const montoEscrow = passVm.isMasterPass ? 0 : passVm.escrowKc;
    const montoPuerta = passVm.isMasterPass ? 0 : passVm.puertaKc;
    const isZeroKcPass = passVm.isMasterPass || (totalCosto <= 0 && montoEscrow <= 0);
    const localDateLabel = activeQR ? formatLocalReservaDate(activeQR, language, true) : passVm.fechaDisplayLabel;

    const cancelPolicy = useMemo(() => {
      const nowMs = Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
      const evMs = activeQR ? parseReservaLocalDateTimeMs(activeQR, activeQR?.servicios) : NaN;
      const hoursUntilEvent = Number.isFinite(evMs) ? (evMs - nowMs) / 3600000 : 999;
      const createdAtMs = activeQR?.created_at ? new Date(activeQR.created_at).getTime() : NaN;
      const minutesSinceCreated = Number.isFinite(createdAtMs) ? (nowMs - createdAtMs) / 60000 : 999;
      const dt = new Date(nowMs);
      const curM = `${dt.getFullYear()}-${String(dt.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      const usedForg = String(profile?.mes_perdones_control || '').trim() === curM ? Number(profile?.perdones_mensuales_usados) || 0 : 0;
      return evaluateCancellationWindowPolicy({
        hoursUntilEvent,
        minutesSinceCreated,
        vipLevel: profile?.nivel,
        usedForgivenessCount: usedForg,
        isAdminUser: Boolean(isAdminPass),
        isZeroKcPass,
        montoEscrow,
        isEn,
      });
    }, [activeQR, profile?.nivel, profile?.perdones_mensuales_usados, profile?.mes_perdones_control, isAdminPass, isZeroKcPass, montoEscrow, isEn]);

    const cancelDialog = useMemo(
      () => buildCancelDualConfirmCopy(cancelPolicy, montoEscrow, isEn),
      [cancelPolicy, montoEscrow, isEn]
    );
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={Boolean(selectedQR)}
        animationIn="fadeInUp"
        animationOut="fadeOutDown"
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.5 : 0.92}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
        backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        backdropColor={isLight ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        onBackdropPress={onClose}
        onBackButtonPress={onClose}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'center', padding: 14 }}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            prStyles.qrGlassCard,
            isLight && {
              backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
              borderBottomColor: '#0284C7', shadowColor: '#0284C7', shadowOpacity: 0.24,
            },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
        >
          <ScrollView style={{ width: '100%', maxHeight: '88%' }} contentContainerStyle={{ alignItems: 'center' }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
            <View style={prStyles.boardingTopBadge}>
              <Feather name="shield" size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <Text style={[prStyles.boardingTopBadgeTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {passVm.headerTitle} • QR 60S
              </Text>
            </View>

            <KineticBoardingPassWalletCard pass={activeQR} qrSecondsLeft={effectiveSecs} escrowKc={montoEscrow} puertaKc={montoPuerta}>
              <View style={prStyles.perforatedDividerRow}>
                <View style={[prStyles.perforatedNotchLeft, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECF1F8' }]} />
                <View style={[prStyles.perforatedDashLine, isLight && { borderColor: '#94A3B8' }]} />
                <View style={[prStyles.perforatedNotchRight, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECF1F8' }]} />
              </View>

              <View style={prStyles.fichaHeaderWrap}>
                {passVm.stationBadge && (
                  <View style={[prStyles.stationPill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#059669' }]}>
                    <Feather name="map-pin" size={11} color={isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green} />
                    <Text style={[prStyles.stationPillTxt, { color: isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green }]}>
                      {passVm.stationBadge}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                )}
                {passVm.masterPassBadge && (
                  <View style={[prStyles.masterPill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F5F3FF', borderColor: '#7C3AED' }]}>
                    <Text style={[prStyles.stationPillTxt, { color: isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#E0AAFF' }]}>
                      {passVm.masterPassBadge}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>

              <Text style={[prStyles.qrDateTime, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {localDateLabel || passVm.fechaDisplayLabel} • {passVm.scheduleLabel}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[prStyles.qrZoneSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={2}>
                📍 {passVm.zoneAndCoachLabel}
              </Text>

              <View style={prStyles.qrCodeBox}>
                <QRCode value={effectivePayload} size={172} backgroundColor="#FFFFFF" color="#050505" />
              </View>

              <View style={prStyles.symmetricSplitRow}>
                <View style={[prStyles.symmetricSplitCol, isLight && prStyles.symmetricSplitColLight]}>
                  <Text style={[prStyles.splitLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={2}>
                    {passVm.depositLabel}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[prStyles.splitVal, { color: passVm.isMasterPass ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                    {passVm.depositValueLabel}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[prStyles.symmetricSplitCol, isLight && prStyles.symmetricSplitColLight]}>
                  <Text style={[prStyles.splitLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={2}>
                    {passVm.settlementLabel}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[prStyles.splitVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                    {passVm.settlementValueLabel}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            </KineticBoardingPassWalletCard>

            <KineticCalendarAndWalletActions reserva={activeQR} escrowKc={montoEscrow} puertaKc={montoPuerta} />
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[prStyles.unlockActionBtn, NO_SEL]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={onClose}
              {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as Record<string, unknown>)}
            >
              <Text selectable={false} style={[prStyles.unlockActionBtnText, { color: '#050505', lineHeight: 16 }]}>
                {tx('OCULTAR LLAVE QR', 'HIDE QR KEY')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            {cancelarReserva && activeQR && (
              <>
                {!isZeroKcPass && (
                  <Text
                    selectable={false}
                    style={{
                      fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 4,
                      color: cancelPolicy.shouldRefund ? (isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : (isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24'),
                    }}
                  >
                    {cancelPolicy.windowMessage}
                  </Text>
                )}
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    prStyles.cancelTicketBtn,
                    isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FEF2F2', borderColor: '#DC2626', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#991B1B', shadowColor: '#DC2626', shadowOpacity: 0.22 },
                    NO_SEL,
                  ]}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic(cancelPolicy.shouldRefund ? 'Medium' : 'Warning');
                    setConfirmCancelOpen(true);
                  }}
                  disabled={isCanceling}
                >
                  {isCanceling ? (
                    <KineticLoader size={16} color={isLight ? '#DC2626' : COLORS.red} />
                  ) : (
                    <Text
                      selectable={false}
                      numberOfLines={1}
                      adjustsFontSizeToFit
                      minimumFontScale={0.78}
                      style={[prStyles.cancelTicketText, isLight && { color: '#B91C1C' }]}
                    >
                      {isZeroKcPass ? tx('Liberar Pase (0 KC)', 'Release Pass (0 KC)') : tx('Cancelar Reserva', 'Cancel Reservation')}
                    </Text>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </>
            )}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
        <KineticDualConfirmModal
          visible={Boolean(selectedQR && confirmCancelOpen)}
          tone={cancelDialog.tone}
          title={cancelDialog.title}
          message={cancelDialog.message}
          confirmLabel={cancelDialog.confirmLabel}
          cancelLabel={cancelDialog.cancelLabel}
          loading={Boolean(isCanceling)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            setConfirmCancelOpen(false);
            if (activeQR && cancelarReserva) cancelarReserva(activeQR);
          }}
          onCancel={() => setConfirmCancelOpen(false)}
        />
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
  }
);

const basePrStyles = StyleSheet.create({

    FILE: src/components/BovedaSupportAndReferralModals.tsx

    // === src/components/BovedaSupportAndReferralModals.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Linking, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { openExternalLinkSafe } from '../utils/externalLinks';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen } from '../utils/kineticOverlayStack';
import { baseConciergeStyles } from './boveda/conciergeBrainAndStyles';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from './shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';

export const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP = '';
export const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_SUPPORT_EMAIL = 'soporte@kineticapp.com.mx';

export const hasConfiguredOfficialWhatsApp = (raw?: string | null): boolean =>
  String(raw ?? '').replace(/\D/g, '').trim().length >= 10;

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle) : null;

export const HumanSupportModal = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  userName = 'Miembro Kinetic',
  userEmail = '',
  isB2B = false,
  onSwitchToConcierge,
}: {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  userName?: string;
  userEmail?: string;
  isB2B?: boolean;
  onSwitchToConcierge?: () => void;
}) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseConciergeStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const [unregisteredPhoneNotice, setUnregisteredPhoneNotice] = useState(false);
  const switchTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const showWhatsAppChannel = hasConfiguredOfficialWhatsApp(KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen(true);
    return () => setFullScreenModalOverlayOpen(false);
  }, [visible]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (switchTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(switchTimerRef.current);
      switchTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);
  const displaySupportName =
    !userName || userName === 'Miembro Kinetic' ? tx('Miembro Kinetic', 'Kinetic Member') : userName;

  const handleCloseSupport = () => {
    if (switchTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(switchTimerRef.current);
      switchTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    setUnregisteredPhoneNotice(false);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleOpenWhatsApp = () => {
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    const cleanPhone = String(KINETIC_OFFICIAL_WHATSAPP || '').replace(/\D/g, '').trim();
    if (!hasConfiguredOfficialWhatsApp(cleanPhone)) {
      setUnregisteredPhoneNotice(true);
      return;
    }
    const roleTag = isB2B ? tx('Socio B2B', 'B2B Partner') : tx('Atleta', 'Athlete');
    const text = encodeURIComponent(`Kinetic Support ⚡\n${displaySupportName} (${roleTag} • ${userEmail}):`);
    openExternalLinkSafe(`https://wa.me/${cleanPhone}?text=${text}`);
  };

  const handleOpenSupportEmail = async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    const subject = encodeURIComponent(`Kinetic Support — ${displaySupportName}`);
    const body = encodeURIComponent(
      `${tx('Hola Equipo de Soporte Kinetic', 'Hello Kinetic Support Team')},\n\n${displaySupportName} (${userEmail}):\n\n`,
    );
    const mailtoUrl = `mailto:${KINETIC_OFFICIAL_SUPPORT_EMAIL}?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      try {
        await Linking.openURL(mailtoUrl);
        return;
      } catch (err) {
        void err;
      }
    }
    openExternalLinkSafe(mailtoUrl);
  };

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(visible)}
      onBackdropPress={handleCloseSupport}
      onBackButtonPress={handleCloseSupport}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.55 : 0.92}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
      backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
    >
      <View
        style={[
          globalStyles.bottomSheet,
          KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
          isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#0284C7' },
          { paddingBottom: 0 },
        ]}
      >
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader
          visible={Boolean(visible)}
          onClose={handleCloseSupport}
          accentColor={isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan}
        />

        <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.headsetCircle,
                isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
              ]}
            >
              <Feather name="headphones" size={22} color={isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan} />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.conciergeTitle, { color: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan }]}>
              {tx('HABLAR CON UN HUMANO', 'TALK TO A HUMAN')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.conciergeSub, { textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 4, color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx(
                'Selecciona el canal directo por el que prefieres ser atendido por el equipo de soporte ejecutivo de KineticApp S.A.S.:',
                'Select your preferred direct channel to connect with KineticApp S.A.S. Executive Support:',
              )}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {showWhatsAppChannel && unregisteredPhoneNotice && (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.unregisteredPhoneAlertBox,
                isLightMode && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFBEB',
                  borderColor: '#F59E0B',
                  borderTopColor: '#FEF3C7',
                  borderBottomColor: '#D97706',
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginBottom: 4 }}>
                <Ionicons name="alert-circle" size={16} color={isLightMode ? '#B45309' : COLORS.yellow} />
                <Text style={[styles.unregisteredPhoneAlertTitle, isLightMode && { color: '#B45309' }]}>
                  {tx('AÚN NO SE HA REGISTRADO UN NÚMERO', 'WHATSAPP LINE NOT YET REGISTERED')}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={[styles.unregisteredPhoneAlertSub, isLightMode && { color: '#334155' }]}>
                {tx(
                  'Aún no se ha registrado un número oficial de WhatsApp para soporte en vivo. Por favor utiliza el Correo Electrónico Oficial abajo o el Concierge IA 24/7.',
                  'An official live WhatsApp support number is not yet active. Please use Official Email below or the 24/7 AI Concierge.',
                )}
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}

          {showWhatsAppChannel && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.channelCardGreen,
                isLightMode && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderColor: '#10B981',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderBottomColor: '#059669',
                },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={handleOpenWhatsApp}
            >
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.channelIconCircleGreen,
                  isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#059669' },
                ]}
              >
                <Ionicons name="logo-whatsapp" size={18} color={isLightMode ? '#047857' : themeColors.green} />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={[styles.channelCardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                  {tx('WhatsApp Soporte Kinetic', 'Kinetic WhatsApp Support')}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.channelCardSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                  {tx('Atención directa en tiempo real por mensaje', 'Direct real-time messaging support')}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={isLightMode ? '#047857' : themeColors.green} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              styles.channelCardCyan,
              isLightMode && {
                backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderColor: '#0284C7',
                borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderBottomColor: '#0369A1',
              },
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={handleOpenSupportEmail}
          >
            <View
              style={[
                styles.channelIconCircleCyan,
                isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
              ]}
            >
              <Feather name="mail" size={17} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[styles.channelCardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {tx('Correo Electrónico Oficial', 'Official Support Email')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.channelCardSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {KINETIC_OFFICIAL_SUPPORT_EMAIL} • {tx('Aclaraciones y facturación', 'Billing & account inquiries')}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {Boolean(onSwitchToConcierge) && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.channelCardCyan,
                {
                  borderColor: isLightMode ? '#7C3AED' : COLORS.purple,
                  borderBottomColor: isLightMode ? '#6D28D9' : '#5B21B6',
                  marginBottom: 8,
                },
                isLightMode && {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                handleCloseSupport();
                switchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => onSwitchToConcierge?.(), 220);
              }}
            >
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.channelIconCircleCyan,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F3E8FF' : 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.14)',
                    borderColor: isLightMode ? '#7C3AED' : COLORS.purple,
                    elevation: 0,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Feather name="cpu" size={17} color={isLightMode ? '#6D28D9' : themeColors.purpleText} />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={[styles.channelCardTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                  {tx('Concierge IA 24/7 (Respuesta Inmediata)', '24/7 AI Concierge (Instant Help)')}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.channelCardSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                  {tx('Asistencia inteligente al instante en Bóveda, QR y Escrow', 'Instant guidance on Vault, QR Passes & Escrow')}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={isLightMode ? '#6D28D9' : themeColors.purpleText} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </ScrollView>

        <KineticErgonomicFooter onBack={handleCloseSupport} style={{ marginHorizontal: -18 }} />
      </View>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

    FILE: src/components/KineticStripeProvider.tsx

    // === src/components/KineticStripeProvider.tsx === //
// Kinetic OS v2.5 — Proveedor Resiliente de Stripe (Web / Native / Offline Safe)
import React, { createContext, useContext, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { getNativeStripeProvider } from '../services/nativeStripeBridge';
import { stripeService } from '../services/stripeService';

interface KineticStripeContextValue {
  publishableKey: string;
  isLiveMode: boolean;
  isStripeConfigured: boolean;
  merchantIdentifier: string;
  urlScheme: string;
}

const DEFAULT_MERCHANT_ID = 'merchant.com.kineticapp.sas';
const DEFAULT_URL_SCHEME = 'kinetic';

const KineticStripeContext = createContext<KineticStripeContextValue>({
  publishableKey: '',
  isLiveMode: false,
  isStripeConfigured: false,
  merchantIdentifier: DEFAULT_MERCHANT_ID,
  urlScheme: DEFAULT_URL_SCHEME,
});

export const useKineticStripe = () => useContext(KineticStripeContext);

interface KineticStripeProviderProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  publishableKey?: string;
  merchantIdentifier?: string;
  urlScheme?: string;
}

/**
 * Proveedor de Stripe de Grado Bancario para Kinetic OS.
 * - En Native (iOS/Android): Prepara la inicialización de Payment Sheet y Apple/Google Pay.
 * - En Web: Aísla el árbol de fallos nativos permitiendo operar sin excepciones silenciosas.
 * - Cero bloqueos de renderizado si la clave no está presente en el arranque.
 */
export function KineticStripeProvider({
  children,
  publishableKey: explicitKey,
  merchantIdentifier = DEFAULT_MERCHANT_ID,
  urlScheme = DEFAULT_URL_SCHEME,
}: KineticStripeProviderProps) {
  const activeKey = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      explicitKey ||
      stripeService.getPublishableKey() ||
      process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ||
      ''
    ).trim();
  }, [explicitKey]);

  const isLiveMode = useMemo(() => {
    return activeKey.startsWith('pk_live_');
  }, [activeKey]);

  const isStripeConfigured = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      Boolean(activeKey) &&
      (activeKey.startsWith('pk_live_') || activeKey.startsWith('pk_test_')) &&
      !activeKey.startsWith('sk_')
    );
  }, [activeKey]);

  const contextValue = useMemo(
    () => ({
      publishableKey: activeKey,
      isLiveMode,
      isStripeConfigured,
      merchantIdentifier,
      urlScheme,
    }),
    [activeKey, isLiveMode, isStripeConfigured, merchantIdentifier, urlScheme]
  );

  const NativeStripeProvider = Platform.OS !== 'web' ? getNativeStripeProvider() : null;

  if (NativeStripeProvider && isStripeConfigured) {
    return (
      <NativeStripeProvider
        publishableKey={activeKey}
        merchantIdentifier={merchantIdentifier}
        urlScheme={urlScheme}
      >
        <KineticStripeContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
          {children}
        </KineticStripeContext.Provider>
      </NativeStripeProvider>
    );
  }

  return (
    <KineticStripeContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
      {children}
    </KineticStripeContext.Provider>
  );
}

    FILE: src/components/LegalTermsModal.tsx

    // === src/components/LegalTermsModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Animated,
  Platform,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { COLORS } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { renderInWebPortal } from '../utils/kineticWebPortal';
import { baseLegalStyles } from './LegalTermsModal.styles';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import { LegalTermsClausesBody } from './boveda/LegalTermsClausesBody';

const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;

export const WAIVER_CURRENT_VERSION = 'v2.5';
export const getWaiverStorageKey = (userId?: string | null) =>
  `@kinetic_waiver_signed_v2_${userId || 'global'}`;

interface LegalTermsModalProps {
  isVisible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAccept?: () => void;
  initialTab?: 'atleta' | 'b2b' | 'privacidad';
  userId?: string | null;
  userName?: string | null;
  alreadySigned?: boolean;
  requireSignature?: boolean;
  inlineOverlay?: boolean;
  portalOnWeb?: boolean;
}

export const LegalTermsModal = ({
  isVisible, onClose, onAccept, initialTab = 'atleta', userId, userName, alreadySigned = false, requireSignature = false,
  inlineOverlay = false, portalOnWeb = true,
}: LegalTermsModalProps) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { tx, fmtDate, language, resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseLegalStyles, resolvedTheme, ['acceptBtn', 'acceptBtnText']),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'atleta' | 'b2b' | 'privacidad'>(initialTab);
  const [isChecked, setIsChecked] = useState<boolean>(alreadySigned);
  const [isSignedConfirmed, setIsSignedConfirmed] = useState<boolean>(alreadySigned);
  const [signedDateText, setSignedDateText] = useState<string>('');
  const [isSavingSignature, setIsSavingSignature] = useState(false);
  const swipeHintAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const closeTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (closeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(closeTimerRef.current);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    const loadSignatureState = async () => {
      if (!alreadySigned && isMounted) {
        setIsSignedConfirmed(false);
        setIsChecked(false);
        setSignedDateText('');
      }
      try {
        const storageKey = getWaiverStorageKey(userId);
        const savedRaw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(storageKey);
        if (savedRaw) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(savedRaw);
          if (isMounted && parsed?.signed) {
            setIsSignedConfirmed(true);
            setIsChecked(true);
            if (parsed.timestamp) {
              const dt = fmtDate(parsed.timestamp, { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
              setSignedDateText(tx(`Firmado el ${dt}`, `Signed on ${dt}`));
            } else {
              setSignedDateText(tx('Firma digital registrada', 'Digital signature recorded'));
            }
            return;
          }
        }
        if (alreadySigned) {
          if (isMounted) {
            setIsSignedConfirmed(true);
            setIsChecked(true);
            setSignedDateText(
              tx(
                'Firma verificada en Bóveda',
                'Signature verified in Vault'
              )
            );
          }
          return;
        }
        if (userId) {
          const { data } = await supabase
            .from('perfiles')
            .select('waiver_firmado, waiver_fecha, waiver_version')
            .eq('id', userId)
            .maybeSingle();
          if (isMounted && data?.waiver_firmado) {
            setIsSignedConfirmed(true);
            setIsChecked(true);
            if (data?.waiver_fecha) {
              const dt = fmtDate(data.waiver_fecha, {
                day: '2-digit',
                month: 'short',
                year: 'numeric',
              });
              setSignedDateText(
                tx(`Firmado el ${dt}`, `Signed on ${dt}`)
              );
            } else {
              setSignedDateText(
                tx(
                  'Firma verificada en Bóveda',
                  'Signature verified in Vault'
                )
              );
            }
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Silencioso
      }
    };
    if (isVisible) {
      setActiveTab(initialTab);
      loadSignatureState();
      swipeHintAnim.setValue(0);
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.delay(160),
        Animated.timing(swipeHintAnim, {
          toValue: 8,
          duration: 150,
          useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
        }),
        Animated.spring(swipeHintAnim, {
          toValue: 0,
          friction: 5,
          useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
        }),
      ]).start();
    } else if (!alreadySigned) {
      setIsSignedConfirmed(false);
      setIsChecked(false);
      setSignedDateText('');
    }
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [isVisible, initialTab, userId, alreadySigned, swipeHintAnim, fmtDate, tx, language]);

  const switchTab = (tab: 'atleta' | 'b2b' | 'privacidad') => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    setActiveTab(tab);
    scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
  };

  const handleConfirmSignature = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!isChecked || isSavingSignature) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      return;
    }
    setIsSavingSignature(true);
    triggerHaptic('Success');
    const now = new Date();
    const nowIso = now.toISOString();
    const dateLabel = fmtDate(now, {
      day: '2-digit',
      month: 'short',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
    try {
      const storageKey = getWaiverStorageKey(userId);
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(
        storageKey,
        JSON.stringify({
          signed: true,
          version: WAIVER_CURRENT_VERSION,
          dateLabel,
          timestamp: nowIso,
        })
      );
      if (userId) {
        const { error: perfErr } = await supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .update({
            waiver_firmado: true,
            waiver_fecha: nowIso,
            waiver_version: WAIVER_CURRENT_VERSION,
          })
          .eq('id', userId);
        if (perfErr) {
          await supabase
            .from('perfiles')
            .update({ waiver_firmado: true })
            .eq('id', userId);
        }
      }
      setIsSignedConfirmed(true);
      setSignedDateText(
        tx(
          `Firmado el ${dateLabel}`,
          `Signed on ${dateLabel}`
        )
      );
      onAccept?.();
      if (requireSignature) {
        if (closeTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(closeTimerRef.current);
        closeTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          onClose();
        }, 400);
      }
    } catch {
      setIsSignedConfirmed(true);
      setSignedDateText(
        tx(
          `Firmado el ${dateLabel}`,
          `Signed on ${dateLabel}`
        )
      );
      onAccept?.();
    } finally {
      setIsSavingSignature(false);
    }
  }, [isChecked, isSavingSignature, userId, onAccept, requireSignature, onClose, fmtDate, tx]);

  const canDismissModal = !requireSignature || isSignedConfirmed;

  const sheetNode = (
    <Animated.View
      style={[
        styles.sheetContainer,
        KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
        isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#00B8D4' },
        {
          minHeight: 0,
          flexShrink: 1,
          paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 14 : 26,
          transform: [{ translateY: swipeHintAnim }],
        },
      ]}
    >
      <KineticSwipeCloseHeader
        onClose={canDismissModal ? onClose : null}
        labelEs={canDismissModal ? 'DESLIZA PARA CERRAR' : 'ACEPTA LOS TÉRMINOS PARA CONTINUAR'}
        labelEn={canDismissModal ? 'SWIPE TO CLOSE' : 'ACCEPT TERMS TO CONTINUE'}
      />

      <View style={styles.headerBox}>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.iconCircle,
            isSignedConfirmed && { borderColor: isLightMode ? '#059669' : COLORS.green, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#ECFDF5' : 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.12)', elevation: 0 },
          ]}
        >
          <Feather
            name={isSignedConfirmed ? 'check-circle' : 'shield'}
            size={22}
            color={isSignedConfirmed ? themeColors.green : themeColors.cyanText}
          />
        </View>
        <Text style={[styles.headerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
          {tx(
            'MARCO LEGAL Y WAIVER KINETIC',
            'KINETIC LEGAL TERMS & WAIVER'
          )}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>
          {tx(
            'KineticApp S.A.S. • Exención deportiva B2C, Contrato de Adhesión B2B y Aviso de Privacidad LFPDPPP.',
            'KineticApp S.A.S. • B2C Athletic Waiver, B2B Partner Agreement & LFPDPPP Privacy Notice.'
          )}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={[styles.tabsRow, { gap: 6 }]}>
        {(['atleta', 'b2b', 'privacidad'] as const).map((tabKey) => {
          const active = activeTab === tabKey;
          const tabFlex = tabKey === 'atleta' ? 1.28 : tabKey === 'b2b' ? 1.04 : 0.78;
          const label =
            tabKey === 'atleta'
              ? tx('ATLETA Y WAIVER', 'ATHLETE & WAIVER')
              : tabKey === 'b2b'
              ? tx('SOCIOS B2B', 'B2B PARTNERS')
              : tx('PRIVACIDAD', 'PRIVACY');
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={tabKey}
              style={[
                styles.tabPill,
                { flex: tabFlex, paddingHorizontal: 4 },
                isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                active && styles.tabPillActive,
                active && isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
              onPress={() => switchTab(tabKey)}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} style={[styles.tabPillText, { fontSize: 9.2, letterSpacing: 0.2 }, active && styles.tabPillTextActive, active && isLightMode && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {label}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        ref={scrollViewRef}
        bounces={false}
        nestedScrollEnabled={true}
        directionalLockEnabled={true}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        style={[styles.scrollArea, { flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }]}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
      >
        <LegalTermsClausesBody activeTab={activeTab} styles={styles} tx={tx} />
      </ScrollView>

      <View style={[styles.signatureSection, isSignedConfirmed && { paddingTop: 8, marginTop: 6 }, isLightMode && { borderTopColor: '#CBD5E1' }]}>
        {isSignedConfirmed ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.signedCompactBar,
              isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981' },
              NO_SEL,
            ]}
          >
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
              <Feather name="check-circle" size={14} color={isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green} />
              <Text
                selectable={false}
                numberOfLines={1}
                style={[styles.signedBannerTitle, { flexShrink: 1, letterSpacing: 0.4 }, isLightMode && { color: '#047857' }]}
              >
                {tx('TÉRMINOS ACEPTADOS ✓', 'TERMS ACCEPTED ✓')}
                {signedDateText ? ` • ${signedDateText}` : ''}
              </Text>
            </View>
            {canDismissModal && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.signedCompactBackChip,
                  isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' },
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); onClose(); }}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
              >
                <Feather name="arrow-left" size={12} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
                <Text selectable={false} style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.textPrimary }}>
                  {tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
        ) : (
          <>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.checkboxAgreementRow,
                isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                isChecked && styles.checkboxAgreementRowActive,
                isChecked && isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
                NO_SEL,
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
              onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsChecked(!isChecked); }}
            >
              <View style={[styles.checkboxSquare, isLightMode && !isChecked && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#64748B' }, isChecked && styles.checkboxSquareChecked]}>
                {isChecked && <Feather name="check" size={14} color="#050505" />}
              </View>
              <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.checkboxAgreementText, isLightMode && { color: '#1E293B' }]}>
                {tx('Declaro ser mayor de 18 años y acepto el ', 'I confirm I am 18+ years old and accept the ')}
                <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                  {tx(
                    'Waiver Deportivo B2C, Contrato Marco B2B y Aviso de Privacidad',
                    'B2C Athletic Waiver, B2B Master Agreement & Privacy Notice'
                  )}
                </Text>
                .
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <View style={styles.footerActions}>
              {canDismissModal && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.closeBtn, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }, NO_SEL]}
                  onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); onClose(); }}
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                >
                  <Feather name="arrow-left" size={14} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
                  <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.closeBtnText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.acceptBtn,
                  !isChecked && styles.acceptBtnDisabled,
                  !isChecked && isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#F8FAFC', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8', shadowOpacity: 0, elevation: 0 },
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
                disabled={!isChecked || isSavingSignature}
                onPress={handleConfirmSignature}
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                {...(isChecked ? ({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as Record<string, unknown>) : {})}
              >
                {isSavingSignature ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#050505" />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Feather
                      name="check-circle"
                      size={16}
                      color={isChecked ? '#050505' : themeColors.textMuted}
                      style={{ marginRight: 8 }}
                    />
                    <Text selectable={false} style={[styles.acceptBtnText, !isChecked && { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                      {tx('ACEPTAR Y FIRMAR', 'ACCEPT & SIGN')}
                    </Text>
                  </>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </>
        )}
      </View>
    </Animated.View>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    if (!isVisible) return null;
    const inlineWrap = (
      <View
        {...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } as any) : {})}
        style={{
          position: (Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? 'fixed' : 'absolute') as any,
          top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0,
          ...(Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? ({ width: '100vw', height: '100dvh' } as any) : {}),
          zIndex: 9940,
          backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5)' : 'rgba(5, 5, 5, 0.92)',
          justifyContent: 'flex-end',
          elevation: 0,
        }}
      >
        <Pressable
          style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }}
          onPress={() => { if (canDismissModal) onClose(); }}
        />
        {sheetNode}
      </View>
    );
    return Platform.OS === 'web' && portalOnWeb ? renderInWebPortal(inlineWrap) : inlineWrap;
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(isVisible)}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      onBackdropPress={() => { if (canDismissModal) onClose(); }}
      onBackButtonPress={() => { if (canDismissModal) onClose(); }}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
    >
      {sheetNode}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export default LegalTermsModal;
