    // === src/components/MembershipModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import {
  IKineticMembership,
  KINETIC_MEMBERSHIPS,
  getLevelNumericWeight,
  getMembershipByLevel,
} from '../constants/memberships';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { isStrictB2BPartnerAccount } from '../services/kineticlubHelpers';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel } from '../hooks/boveda/useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel';
import { localizeVipTierName } from '../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { baseStyles } from './MembershipModal.styles';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import {
  TOPUP_NON_MEMBER_SURCHARGE_RATE,
  TOPUP_PACKAGES,
  TopUpPackagesSection,
  formatMxnCurrency,
  getTopUpPricingForLevel,
  resolveCleanVipLevel,
} from './boveda/TopUpPackagesSection';
import { VipMembershipPlansSection } from './boveda/VipMembershipPlansSection';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from './shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

export {
  TOPUP_NON_MEMBER_SURCHARGE_RATE,
  TOPUP_PACKAGES,
  formatMxnCurrency,
  getTopUpPricingForLevel,
  resolveCleanVipLevel,
};

const NO_SEL: Record<string, string> | undefined = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' }
  : undefined;
const DARK_INK: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } : {};
const LIGHT_MEDIA_INK: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? { dataSet: { kineticInk: 'light', kineticOnMedia: 'true' } }
  : {};
const SHEET_OPEN_ATTR: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } : {};

interface MembershipModalProps {
  isVisible?: boolean;
  isMembershipModalOpen?: boolean;
  onClose?: () => void;
  onPurchase?: (payload: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<void> | void;
  handlePurchase?: (payload: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<void> | void;
  isProcessingPayment?: boolean;
  processingPlanId?: string | null;
  currentLevel?: string | null;
  initialTab?: 'membresias' | 'topup';
  showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
  triggerHaptic?: (type?: string) => void;
  inlineOverlay?: boolean;
}

export const MembershipModal = ({
  isVisible,
  isMembershipModalOpen,
  onClose,
  onPurchase,
  handlePurchase,
  isProcessingPayment,
  processingPlanId,
  currentLevel,
  initialTab = 'membresias',
  showTacticalAlert,
  triggerHaptic: hapticProp,
  inlineOverlay,
}: MembershipModalProps) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user, profile, refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(
    () =>
      adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, [
        'topSwitchTabBtnCyan',
        'topSwitchTabBtnPurple',
        'directTopUpFromCurrentBtn',
      ]),
    [resolvedTheme],
  );
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const fallbackTimersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);
  const fireHaptic = hapticProp || triggerHaptic;
  const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'membresias' | 'topup'>(
    initialTab === 'topup' ? 'topup' : 'membresias',
  );
  const [headerScrolled, setHeaderScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [internalProcessingId, setInternalProcessingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      fallbackTimersRef.current.forEach((tid) => clearTimeout(tid));
      fallbackTimersRef.current = [];
    };
  }, []);

  const modalVisible = Boolean(isVisible ?? isMembershipModalOpen);
  const triggerPurchase = onPurchase || handlePurchase;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (modalVisible) {
      setActiveTab(initialTab === 'topup' ? 'topup' : 'membresias');
    }
  }, [modalVisible, initialTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!modalVisible) return;
    scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
    const resetTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
    }, 55);
    return () => clearTimeout(resetTimer);
  }, [activeTab, modalVisible]);

  const effectiveLevel = useMemo(
    () => resolveCleanVipLevel(currentLevel || profile?.nivel, profile?.kc_membresia),
    [currentLevel, profile?.nivel, profile?.kc_membresia],
  );
  const userWeight = getLevelNumericWeight(effectiveLevel);
  const hasActiveMembership = userWeight >= 1;
  const paidMembershipPlans = useMemo<IKineticMembership[]>(
    () => (KINETIC_MEMBERSHIPS || []).filter((p) => p.precio_mensual_mxn > 0),
    [],
  );
  const currentMembershipObj = useMemo(
    () =>
      getMembershipByLevel(effectiveLevel) ||
      (hasActiveMembership
        ? paidMembershipPlans.find((p) => getLevelNumericWeight(p.nivel) === userWeight)
        : undefined),
    [effectiveLevel, hasActiveMembership, paidMembershipPlans, userWeight],
  );

  const isPurchasingRef = useRef(false);
  const isBusy = Boolean(isProcessingPayment || internalProcessingId);

  const { handlePurchase: fallbackPurchase } = useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel({
    user,
    isEn,
    tx,
    cleanVipLevel: effectiveLevel,
    refreshProfile,
    fetchData: async () => {},
    getCostoTotal: () => 0,
    isProcessingPayment: isBusy,
    setIsProcessingPayment: () => {},
    setProcessingPlanId: setInternalProcessingId,
    isCanceling: false,
    setIsCanceling: () => {},
    setIsMembershipModalOpen: (open) => {
      if (!open) onClose?.();
    },
    setSelectedQR: () => {},
    scheduleBovedaTimeout: (fn, ms) => {
      const tid = setTimeout(() => {
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
        fn();
      }, ms);
      fallbackTimersRef.current.push(tid);
    },
    showTacticalAlert: (title, msg, type) => {
      showTacticalAlert?.(title, msg, type);
    },
  });
  const executePurchase = triggerPurchase || fallbackPurchase;

  const handleSafeClose = () => {
    if (isBusy || isPurchasingRef.current) return;
    fireHaptic?.('Light');
    onClose?.();
  };

  const handleMembershipAction = async (
    plan: IKineticMembership,
    isDowngrade: boolean,
    kcDeltaToCredit: number,
    priceDeltaToPay: number,
  ) => {
    if (isStrictB2BPartnerAccount(user, profile)) {
      fireHaptic?.('Error');
      showTacticalAlert?.(tx('SOLO CUENTAS DE ATLETA', 'ATHLETE ACCOUNTS ONLY'), tx('Las cuentas operativas B2B no pueden comprar membresías ni Kineticoins.', 'B2B partner accounts cannot buy memberships or Kineticoins.'), 'error');
      return;
    }
    if (isBusy || isPurchasingRef.current) return;
    isPurchasingRef.current = true;
    fireHaptic?.('Medium');
    const netKcDelta = isDowngrade
      ? 0
      : hasActiveMembership && getLevelNumericWeight(plan.nivel) > userWeight
        ? Math.max(0, Number(plan.kc_otorgados) - (Number(currentMembershipObj?.kc_otorgados) || 0))
        : Math.max(0, Number(kcDeltaToCredit) || Number(plan.kc_otorgados) || 0);
    const payload = {
      id: plan.nivel,
      nivel: plan.nivel,
      nombre: plan.nivel,
      precio_mxn: priceDeltaToPay,
      precio_mensual_mxn: plan.precio_mensual_mxn,
      kc: netKcDelta,
      kc_otorgados: netKcDelta,
      diferencia_kc: netKcDelta,
      tipo: 'membresia',
      esTopUp: false,
      isUpgrade: !isDowngrade && hasActiveMembership,
      isDowngrade,
    };
    try {
      await executePurchase(payload);
    } finally {
      isPurchasingRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) setInternalProcessingId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleTopUpPurchase = async (pkg: { id?: string; precio_mxn?: number; kc?: number }) => {
    if (isStrictB2BPartnerAccount(user, profile)) {
      fireHaptic?.('Error');
      showTacticalAlert?.(tx('SOLO CUENTAS DE ATLETA', 'ATHLETE ACCOUNTS ONLY'), tx('Las cuentas operativas B2B no pueden adquirir Kineticoins.', 'B2B partner accounts cannot buy Kineticoins.'), 'error');
      return;
    }
    if (isBusy || isPurchasingRef.current) return;
    isPurchasingRef.current = true;
    fireHaptic?.('Medium');
    const pkgPrice = Number(pkg.precio_mxn) || 0;
    const pkgKc = Number(pkg.kc) || 0;
    const pricing = getTopUpPricingForLevel(pkgPrice, effectiveLevel, profile?.kc_membresia);
    const payload = {
      id: pkg.id,
      nivel: effectiveLevel,
      nombre: `Top-Up +${pkgKc} KC`,
      precio_mxn: pricing.totalMxn,
      precio_mensual_mxn: pricing.totalMxn,
      precio_base_mxn: pricing.baseMxn,
      cargo_plataforma_mxn: pricing.surchargeMxn,
      kc: pkgKc,
      kc_otorgados: pkgKc,
      tipo: 'topup',
      esTopUp: true,
      billetera: 'topup',
    };
    try {
      await executePurchase(payload);
    } finally {
      isPurchasingRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) setInternalProcessingId(null);
    }
  };

  const displayTier = localizeVipTierName(effectiveLevel, isEn).toUpperCase();

  const sheetContent = (
    <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, { paddingBottom: 0 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' }]}>
      <KineticSwipeCloseHeader visible={modalVisible} onClose={handleSafeClose} />
      <View style={styles.membershipHeader}>
        <Text style={[styles.membershipTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={2}>
          {tx('MEMBRESÍAS VIP Y RECARGAS', 'VIP MEMBERSHIPS & TOP-UPS')}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.membershipDesc, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
          {tx(`Tu rango actual es ${displayTier} • `, `Your current tier is ${displayTier} • `)}
          {hasActiveMembership ? (
            <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green, fontWeight: '900' }}>
              {tx('Paridad Preferencial 1 KC = $1.00 MXN (0% Comisión)', 'Preferential Parity 1 KC = $1.00 MXN (0% Fee)')}
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FFCC00', fontWeight: '900' }}>
              {tx('Recargas Top-Up con +20% sobre el valor recargado', 'Top-Up packages include +20% platform fee')}
            </Text>
          )}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={[styles.topSwitchTabsRow, headerScrolled && { borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.22)', paddingBottom: 10 }]}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            styles.topSwitchTabBtn,
            isLight && activeTab !== 'membresias' && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
            activeTab === 'membresias' && styles.topSwitchTabBtnCyan,
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); setActiveTab('membresias'); setHeaderScrolled(false); scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true }); }}
          {...(activeTab === 'membresias' ? DARK_INK : {})}
        >
          <Feather name="award" size={13} color={activeTab === 'membresias' ? '#050505' : themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text
            selectable={false}
            numberOfLines={1}
            adjustsFontSizeToFit
            minimumFontScale={0.78}
            style={[styles.topSwitchTabTxt, { flexShrink: 1, color: activeTab === 'membresias' ? '#050505' : isLight ? '#334155' : COLORS.cyan }]}
          >
            {tx('MEMBRESÍAS VIP', 'VIP MEMBERSHIPS')}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            styles.topSwitchTabBtn,
            isLight && activeTab !== 'topup' && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
            activeTab === 'topup' && styles.topSwitchTabBtnPurple,
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); setActiveTab('topup'); setHeaderScrolled(false); scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true }); }}
          {...(activeTab === 'topup' ? LIGHT_MEDIA_INK : {})}
        >
          <Feather name="zap" size={13} color={activeTab === 'topup' ? '#FFFFFF' : isLight ? '#7C3AED' : '#E0AAFF'} />
          <Text
            selectable={false}
            numberOfLines={1}
            adjustsFontSizeToFit
            minimumFontScale={0.78}
            style={[styles.topSwitchTabTxt, { flexShrink: 1, color: activeTab === 'topup' ? '#FFFFFF' : isLight ? '#334155' : '#E0AAFF' }]}
          >
            {tx('RECARGAR KINETICOINS', 'TOP UP KINETICOINS')}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        ref={scrollViewRef}
        style={{ flex: 1 }}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={true}
        nestedScrollEnabled={true}
        bounces={false}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        onScroll={(e) => {
          const y = Number(e?.nativeEvent?.contentOffset?.y || 0);
          setHeaderScrolled((prev) => (y > 4 ? true : prev ? false : prev));
        }}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
      >
        {activeTab === 'topup' ? (
          <TopUpPackagesSection
            effectiveLevel={effectiveLevel}
            hasActiveMembership={hasActiveMembership}
            kcMembresia={profile?.kc_membresia}
            internalProcessingId={internalProcessingId}
            processingPlanId={processingPlanId}
            isBusy={isBusy}
            scrollViewRef={scrollViewRef}
            onSelectPackage={handleTopUpPurchase}
          />
        ) : (
          <VipMembershipPlansSection
            paidMembershipPlans={paidMembershipPlans}
            hasActiveMembership={hasActiveMembership}
            userWeight={userWeight}
            currentMembershipObj={currentMembershipObj}
            processingPlanId={processingPlanId}
            internalProcessingId={internalProcessingId}
            isBusy={isBusy}
            handleMembershipAction={handleMembershipAction}
            onSwitchToTopUp={() => { fireHaptic?.('Selection'); setActiveTab('topup'); scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true }); }}
          />
        )}
      </ScrollView>

      <KineticErgonomicFooter onBack={handleSafeClose} style={{ marginHorizontal: -18 }} />
    </View>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    if (!modalVisible) return null;
    return (
      <Pressable
        onPress={handleSafeClose}
        {...SHEET_OPEN_ATTR}
        style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, zIndex: 985, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5)' : 'rgba(5, 5, 5, 0.92)', justifyContent: 'flex-end', elevation: 0 }}
      >
        <Pressable onPress={(e) => e?.stopPropagation?.()} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
          {sheetContent}
        </Pressable>
      </Pressable>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={modalVisible}
      onBackdropPress={handleSafeClose}
      onBackButtonPress={handleSafeClose}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92}
      animationInTiming={200}
      animationOutTiming={180}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
      backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
    >
      {sheetContent}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export default MembershipModal;

/**
 * Obtiene la configuración oficial del nivel del atleta (con fallback seguro a Acceso Base).
 */
export const getMembershipByLevel = (rawLevel?: string | null): IKineticMembership => {
  const norm = String(rawLevel || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (
    norm.includes('platinum') ||
    norm.includes('platino') ||
    norm.includes('leyenda') ||
    norm.includes('black')
  ) {
    return KINETIC_MEMBERSHIPS[4];
  }
  if (norm.includes('gold') || norm.includes('oro')) return KINETIC_MEMBERSHIPS[3];
  if (norm.includes('silver') || norm.includes('plata')) return KINETIC_MEMBERSHIPS[2];
  if (norm.includes('bronze') || norm.includes('bronce')) return KINETIC_MEMBERSHIPS[1];
  return KINETIC_MEMBERSHIPS[0];
};

/**
 * Peso jerárquico numérico para validaciones de acceso (0 = Free/Acceso Base ... 4 = Platinum).
 */
export const getLevelNumericWeight = (rawLevel?: string | null): number => {
  const norm = String(rawLevel || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (
    norm.includes('platinum') ||
    norm.includes('platino') ||
    norm.includes('leyenda') ||
    norm.includes('black')
  ) return 4;
  if (norm.includes('gold') || norm.includes('oro')) return 3;
  if (norm.includes('silver') || norm.includes('plata')) return 2;
  if (norm.includes('bronze') || norm.includes('bronce')) return 1;
  return 0;
};

/**
 * Devuelve la cantidad máxima de Perdones Mensuales (<72h a 4h) según el nivel VIP del atleta.
 */
export const getMaxForgivenessForLevel = (rawLevel?: string | null): number => {
  return getMembershipByLevel(rawLevel).perdones_mensuales;
};

/**
 * MOTOR MATEMÁTICO DE VENTAS FLASH (<3 HORAS) CON GUARD CLAUSE INQUEBRANTABLE
 * Garantiza que el descuento aplicado al comprador (5% Bronze, 10% Silver, 15% Gold, 20% Platinum)
 * jamás supere el 40% retenido en custodia (monto_escrow), protegiendo el 100% del margen B2B
 * y dejando un excedente operativo de entre +20% y +35% en cupos penalizados.
 */
export const calculateFlashSalePricing = (
  precioBaseKc: number,
  userLevel?: string | null,
  isFlashWindowActive: boolean = false
) => {
  const basePrice = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(precioBaseKc) || 0));
  const escrowOriginal40 = Math.floor(basePrice * 0.4);
  const membership = getMembershipByLevel(userLevel);

  // Guard Clause: Tope estricto al 20% máximo y nunca mayor al fondo Escrow del 40%
  const rawPct = isFlashWindowActive ? Math.min(20, Math.max(0, membership.descuento_flash)) : 0;
  const rawDiscountKc = Math.floor(basePrice * (rawPct / 100));
  const descuentoAplicadoKc = Math.min(rawDiscountKc, escrowOriginal40);

  const precioFinalKc = Math.max(0, basePrice - descuentoAplicadoKc);
  const nuevoEscrowKc = Math.floor(precioFinalKc * 0.4);
  const nuevoPuertaKc = Math.max(0, precioFinalKc - nuevoEscrowKc);

  return {
    precioBaseKc: basePrice,
    descuentoPorcentaje: rawPct,
    descuentoAplicadoKc,
    precioFinalKc,
    montoEscrow: nuevoEscrowKc,
    montoPuerta: nuevoPuertaKc,
    subsidioCubiertoPorEscrow: descuentoAplicadoKc <= escrowOriginal40,
  };
};

    FILE: src/hooks/boveda/useBovedaActions.ts

    // === src/hooks/boveda/useBovedaActions.ts === //
import { MutableRefObject, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Share } from 'react-native';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';

export type TacticalAlertFn = (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;

export interface IUseBovedaActionsParams {
  user: any;
  profile: any;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void>;
  isMountedRef: MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  isTogglingGhostRef: MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  redeemBusyRef: MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  copyFeedbackTimerRef: MutableRefObject<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>;
  isGhostMode: boolean;
  setIsGhostMode: (val: boolean) => void;
  referralCode: string;
  referralCodeInput: string;
  setReferralCodeInput: (val: string) => void;
  setIsReferralCopied: (val: boolean) => void;
  isRedeemingReferral: boolean;
  setIsRedeemingReferral: (val: boolean) => void;
  selectedReceipt: any | null;
  isExportingReceiptPdf: boolean;
  setIsExportingReceiptPdf: (val: boolean) => void;
}

export function useBovedaActions(params: IUseBovedaActionsParams) {
  const {
    user,
    profile,
    refreshProfile,
    isMountedRef,
    isTogglingGhostRef,
    redeemBusyRef,
    copyFeedbackTimerRef,
    isGhostMode,
    setIsGhostMode,
    referralCode,
    referralCodeInput,
    setReferralCodeInput,
    setIsReferralCopied,
    isRedeemingReferral,
    setIsRedeemingReferral,
    selectedReceipt,
    isExportingReceiptPdf,
    setIsExportingReceiptPdf,
  } = params;

  const toggleGhostModeBoveda = useCallback(
    async (alertFn?: TacticalAlertFn) => {
      if (!user?.id || isTogglingGhostRef.current) return;
      isTogglingGhostRef.current = true;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      const prevGhost = isGhostMode;
      const nextGhost = !prevGhost;
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      setIsGhostMode(nextGhost);
      try {
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .update({ radar_publico: !nextGhost })
          .eq('id', user.id);
        if (error) throw error;
        await refreshProfile?.();
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsGhostMode(prevGhost);
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        alertFn?.(
          isEn ? 'Network Friction' : 'Fricción de Red',
          err?.message ||
            (isEn
              ? 'Could not update your radar privacy.'
              : 'No se pudo actualizar tu privacidad en el radar.'),
          'error',
        );
      } finally {
        isTogglingGhostRef.current = false;
      }
    },
    [user?.id, isGhostMode, refreshProfile, isMountedRef, isTogglingGhostRef, setIsGhostMode],
  );

  const copyReferralCodeSilently = useCallback(
    async (customCode?: string) => {
      const targetCode = String(customCode || referralCode).trim();
      triggerHaptic('Success');
      await bovedaService.copiarAlPortapapelesSilencioso(targetCode);
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setIsReferralCopied(true);
        if (copyFeedbackTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(copyFeedbackTimerRef.current);
        copyFeedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          if (isMountedRef.current) setIsReferralCopied(false);
        }, 2500);
      }
      return true;
    },
    [referralCode, isMountedRef, setIsReferralCopied, copyFeedbackTimerRef],
  );

  const shareReferralCodeNative = useCallback(
    async (customCode?: string) => {
      const targetCode = String(customCode || referralCode).trim();
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      const msg = isEn
        ? `⚡ Train with me on KINETIC — The Sports & Wellness Network!\n\n🎁 Use my invite code when registering or in your Vault:\n👉 ${targetCode}\n\nWhen you activate your first VIP Membership, we both receive +100 KC in Top-Up (Rollover) + 50 Prestige XP.\n\n🔗 Join here:\nhttps://kineticapp.com.mx`
        : `⚡ ¡Entrena conmigo en KINETIC — La Red Deportiva y de Bienestar!\n\n🎁 Usa mi código único de invitación al registrarte o en tu Bóveda:\n👉 ${targetCode}\n\nAl activar tu primera Membresía VIP con cuenta verificada, ambos recibimos +100 KC en Billetera Top-Up (Rollover) + 50 XP de Prestigio.\n\n🔗 Entra aquí:\nhttps://kineticapp.com.mx`;
      try {
        await Share.share({
          title: isEn
            ? `Kinetic Invite — Code ${targetCode}`
            : `Invitación Kinetic — Código ${targetCode}`,
          message: msg,
        });
      } catch {}
    },
    [referralCode],
  );

  const redeemReferralCode = useCallback(
    async (rawCode?: string, alertFn?: TacticalAlertFn) => {
      if (!user?.id || isRedeemingReferral || redeemBusyRef.current) return false;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      const codeToRedeem = String(rawCode ?? referralCodeInput).trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!codeToRedeem) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        alertFn?.(
          isEn ? 'Code Required' : 'Código Requerido',
          isEn
            ? 'Enter your team invite code to link it.'
            : 'Escribe el código de invitación de tu equipo para vincularlo.',
          'error',
        );
        return false;
      }
      redeemBusyRef.current = true;
      setIsRedeemingReferral(true);
      try {
        const res = await bovedaService.canjearCodigoReferido(user.id, codeToRedeem);
        await refreshProfile?.();
        if (isMountedRef.current) setReferralCodeInput('');
        const creditedNow = Boolean(res?.bono_inmediato || res?.status === 'credited_now');
        if (creditedNow) triggerArcadeHaptic('levelUp');
        else triggerHaptic('Success');
        alertFn?.(
          creditedNow
            ? isEn
              ? 'BONUS CREDITED (+100 KC + 50 XP)!'
              : '¡BONO ACREDITADO (+100 KC + 50 XP)!'
            : isEn
              ? 'CODE LINKED SUCCESSFULLY'
              : 'CÓDIGO VINCULADO CON ÉXITO',
          res?.message ||
            (creditedNow
              ? isEn
                ? '+100 KC (Rollover) and +50 XP were credited to both your Vault and your host!'
                : '¡Se acreditaron +100 KC (Rollover) y +50 XP en tu Bóveda y en la de tu anfitrión!'
              : isEn
                ? 'When you activate any VIP Membership, both you and your teammate will automatically receive +100 KC (Rollover) and +50 XP.'
                : 'Al activar cualquier Membresía VIP con tu cuenta verificada, tanto tú como tu compañero recibirán automáticamente +100 KC (Rollover) y +50 XP.'),
          'success',
        );
        return true;
      } catch (err: any) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        alertFn?.(
          isEn ? 'Could Not Redeem' : 'No se pudo canjear',
          err?.message ||
            (isEn ? 'Verify the code is typed correctly.' : 'Verifica que el código esté bien escrito.'),
          'error',
        );
        return false;
      } finally {
        redeemBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsRedeemingReferral(false);
      }
    },
    [
      user?.id,
      isRedeemingReferral,
      referralCodeInput,
      refreshProfile,
      redeemBusyRef,
      setIsRedeemingReferral,
      isMountedRef,
      setReferralCodeInput,
    ],
  );

  const exportReceiptToPdf = useCallback(
    async (reservaTarget?: any, alertFn?: TacticalAlertFn) => {
      const target = reservaTarget || selectedReceipt;
      if (!target || isExportingReceiptPdf) return false;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      setIsExportingReceiptPdf(true);
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      try {
        await bovedaService.exportarReciboReservaPDF(
          target,
          profile?.nombre || user?.email || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Athlete' : 'Atleta Kinetic'),
        );
        triggerHaptic('Success');
        return true;
      } catch (err: any) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        alertFn?.(
          isEn ? 'PDF Export Error' : 'Error al exportar PDF',
          err?.message ||
            (isEn
              ? 'Could not generate receipt PDF.'
              : 'No se pudo generar el documento PDF del recibo.'),
          'error',
        );
        return false;
      } finally {
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsExportingReceiptPdf(false);
      }
    },
    [
      selectedReceipt,
      isExportingReceiptPdf,
      profile?.nombre,
      user?.email,
      setIsExportingReceiptPdf,
      isMountedRef,
    ],
  );

  return {
    toggleGhostModeBoveda,
    copyReferralCodeSilently,
    shareReferralCodeNative,
    redeemReferralCode,
    exportReceiptToPdf,
  };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/boveda/useBovedaB2BAndSettings.ts

    import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import {
  buildB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot,
  buildCeoConsolidatedWeeklyReport,
  EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY,
  formatVenueCountLabel,
  IB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot,
  IMasterPassAuditSummary,
  resolveReservaB2BMonto,
} from '../../services/bovedaB2BDataHelper';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import { getB2BDashboardSwrSync, setB2BDashboardSwrSync, type SwrRecord } from '../../services/kineticSwrMemoryHub';
import { getMazatlanNowParts, parseMazatlanTimestampMs } from '../../services/kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';
import { registerSessionCleanupHook } from '../../services/kineticSessionPersistence';
import { CIPHER_PREFIX, decryptKineticString, ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync, encryptKineticString } from '../../services/kineticVaultCrypto';
import { getDisplayRole, isKineticMasterAdmin, resolveOfficialKineticName } from '../../styles/boveda.styles';
import { createDebouncedEventRunner, subscribeKineticRadarUpdated, subscribeKineticRootRefresh } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { useSettingsBoveda } from './useSettingsBovedaHook';

export { formatVenueCountLabel, resolveReservaB2BMonto, useSettingsBoveda };

const B2B_SWR_CACHE_KEY_PREFIX = '@kinetic_b2b_swr_v1_';
const B2B_EGRESS_TTL_MS = 120_000;
type B2BDynRow = Record<string, unknown>;

export const computeRolling30DayCutInfo = (createdAtRaw?: string | null) => {
  const langEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
  const mesesCortos = langEn
    ? ['JAN', 'FEB', 'MAR', 'APR', 'MAY', 'JUN', 'JUL', 'AUG', 'SEP', 'OCT', 'NOV', 'DEC']
    : ['ENE', 'FEB', 'MAR', 'ABR', 'MAY', 'JUN', 'JUL', 'AGO', 'SEP', 'OCT', 'NOV', 'DIC'];
  const mzNow = getMazatlanNowParts();
  const todayUtcMs = Date.UTC(mzNow.year, mzNow.month - 1, mzNow.day);
  const parsedMs = createdAtRaw ? parseMazatlanTimestampMs(createdAtRaw) : NaN;
  const baseParts = Number.isFinite(parsedMs) ? getMazatlanNowParts(new Date(parsedMs)) : mzNow;
  const baseUtcMs = Date.UTC(baseParts.year, baseParts.month - 1, baseParts.day);
  const msPerDay = 86_400_000;
  const elapsedDays = Math.max(0, Math.floor((todayUtcMs - baseUtcMs) / msPerDay));
  const cutDate = new Date(baseUtcMs + ((Math.floor(elapsedDays / 30) + 1) * 30 - 1) * msPerDay);
  const daysUntilCut = Math.max(1, Math.min(30, Math.round((cutDate.getTime() - todayUtcMs) / msPerDay) + 1));
  const nextCutDateLabel = langEn ? `${mesesCortos[cutDate.getUTCMonth()]} ${cutDate.getUTCDate()}` : `${cutDate.getUTCDate()} DE ${mesesCortos[cutDate.getUTCMonth()]}`;
  return { daysUntilCut, nextCutDateLabel, cutDateIso: cutDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10) };
};

const b2bDashboardCache: Record<string, IB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot> = {};

const redactAffiliateForSwrCache = (af: B2BDynRow | null | undefined): B2BDynRow | null => {
  if (!af || typeof af !== 'object') return af ?? null;
  const { clabe_bancaria: _clabe, rfc: _rfc, whatsapp: _wa, ...safeRest } = af;
  return safeRest;
};

export const clearInMemoryB2BDashboardCache = (): void => {
  for (const key of Object.keys(b2bDashboardCache)) delete b2bDashboardCache[key];
};
registerSessionCleanupHook(clearInMemoryB2BDashboardCache);

export interface IUseDashboardB2BUser {
  id?: string;
  email?: string | null;
  user_metadata?: { nombre_negocio?: string; [k: string]: unknown };
  [k: string]: unknown;
}

export interface IUseDashboardB2BProfile {
  rol?: string | null;
  tipo_cuenta?: string | null;
  nombre?: string | null;
  [k: string]: unknown;
}

export function useDashboardB2B(user: IUseDashboardB2BUser | null | undefined, profile: IUseDashboardB2BProfile | null | undefined) {
  const isAdmin = useMemo(() => isKineticMasterAdmin(user?.email, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta), [user?.email, profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta]);
  const roleMode: 'ceo' | 'b2b' = isAdmin ? 'ceo' : 'b2b';
  const memHit = user?.id ? b2bDashboardCache[user.id] : undefined;
  const hubHit = user?.id && !memHit ? (getB2BDashboardSwrSync(user.id) as unknown as IB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot | undefined) : undefined;
  const cached = (memHit?.roleMode === roleMode ? memHit : undefined) || (hubHit?.roleMode === roleMode ? hubHit : undefined);
  if (user?.id && cached && !b2bDashboardCache[user.id]) b2bDashboardCache[user.id] = cached;

  const lastValidDataRef = useRef(cached || null);
  const [hasHydratedB2B, setHasHydratedB2B] = useState<boolean>(() => Boolean(cached));
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [isProfileIncomplete, setIsProfileIncomplete] = useState(Boolean(cached?.isProfileIncomplete));
  const [afData, setAfData] = useState<B2BDynRow | null>(cached?.afData ?? null);
  const [allAfiliados, setAllAfiliados] = useState<B2BDynRow[]>(cached?.allAfiliados || []);
  const [reservasB2B, setReservasB2B] = useState<B2BDynRow[]>(cached?.reservasB2B || []);
  const [metricas, setMetricas] = useState(cached?.metricas || { hoy: 0, semana: 0, mes: 0, total: 0 });
  const [desglosePorPeriodo, setDesglosePorPeriodo] = useState<{ hoy: B2BDynRow[]; semana: B2BDynRow[]; mes: B2BDynRow[]; total: B2BDynRow[] }>(
    cached?.desglosePorPeriodo || { hoy: [], semana: [], mes: [], total: [] }
  );
  const [masterPassSummary, setMasterPassSummary] = useState<IMasterPassAuditSummary>(
    cached?.masterPassSummary || { ...EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY }
  );
  const [validServiceSets, setValidServiceSets] = useState<{ ids?: Set<string>; names?: Set<string> }>({});
  const isFetchingRef = useRef(false);
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => { isMountedRef.current = false; };
  }, []);

  const displayRole = useMemo(() => getDisplayRole(profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.email), [profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.email]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const c = (user?.id ? b2bDashboardCache[user.id] : undefined) || (user?.id ? (getB2BDashboardSwrSync(user.id) as unknown as IB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot | undefined) : undefined);
    if (c && c.roleMode === roleMode) {
      b2bDashboardCache[user!.id!] = c;
      setAfData(c.afData ?? null);
      setAllAfiliados(c.allAfiliados || []);
      setReservasB2B(c.reservasB2B || []);
      setMetricas(c.metricas || { hoy: 0, semana: 0, mes: 0, total: 0 });
      setDesglosePorPeriodo(c.desglosePorPeriodo || { hoy: [], semana: [], mes: [], total: [] });
      if (c.masterPassSummary) setMasterPassSummary(c.masterPassSummary);
      setIsProfileIncomplete(Boolean(c.isProfileIncomplete));
      setHasHydratedB2B(true);
    } else {
      setAfData(null);
      setAllAfiliados([]);
      setReservasB2B([]);
      setMetricas({ hoy: 0, semana: 0, mes: 0, total: 0 });
      setDesglosePorPeriodo({ hoy: [], semana: [], mes: [], total: [] });
      setHasHydratedB2B(false);
    }
  }, [user?.id, roleMode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const curHit = user?.id ? b2bDashboardCache[user.id] : undefined;
    if (!user?.id || (curHit && curHit.roleMode === roleMode)) return;
    let active = true;
    const swrKey = `${B2B_SWR_CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}${user.id}_${isAdmin ? 'ceo' : 'b2b'}`;
    AsyncStorage.getItem(swrKey)
      .then(async (raw) => {
        if (!active || !raw) return;
        let jsonStr = raw;
        if (raw.startsWith(CIPHER_PREFIX)) {
          const salt = await ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync();
          jsonStr = decryptKineticString(raw, salt) || '';
        } else if (raw.trim().startsWith('{')) {
          await AsyncStorage.removeItem(swrKey).catch(() => {});
        }
        if (!active || !jsonStr) return;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonStr) as Partial<IB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot>;
        if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') return;
        if (parsed.afData !== undefined) setAfData(parsed.afData);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.allAfiliados)) setAllAfiliados(parsed.allAfiliados);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.reservasB2B)) setReservasB2B(parsed.reservasB2B);
        if (parsed.metricas) setMetricas(parsed.metricas);
        if (parsed.desglosePorPeriodo) setDesglosePorPeriodo(parsed.desglosePorPeriodo);
        if (parsed.masterPassSummary) setMasterPassSummary(parsed.masterPassSummary);
        setIsProfileIncomplete(Boolean(parsed.isProfileIncomplete));
        setHasHydratedB2B(true);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [user?.id, isAdmin, roleMode]);

  const fetchB2BData = useCallback(async (force = false) => {
    if (!user?.id || isFetchingRef.current) return;
    const cachedHit = b2bDashboardCache[user.id];
    const hit = cachedHit?.roleMode === roleMode ? cachedHit : undefined;
    if (!force && hit && Date.now() - hit.ts < B2B_EGRESS_TTL_MS) {
      if (isMountedRef.current) { setHasHydratedB2B(true); setRefreshing(false); }
      return;
    }
    isFetchingRef.current = true;
    try {
      const rawNegocio = String(profile?.nombre || user?.user_metadata?.nombre_negocio || '');
      const negocioNombre = resolveOfficialKineticName(rawNegocio, isAdmin, '');
      const res = (await bovedaService.fetchB2BData(user.id, isAdmin, negocioNombre, user?.email ?? undefined)) as Parameters<typeof buildB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot>[0] | null;
      if (!isMountedRef.current || !res) return;
      const snap = buildB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot(res, isAdmin);
      const svcIds = Array.isArray(res?.validServiceIds) ? res.validServiceIds.map(String) : [];
      const svcNames = Array.isArray(res?.validServiceNames) ? res.validServiceNames.map(String) : [];
      setValidServiceSets({ ids: svcIds.length > 0 ? new Set(svcIds) : undefined, names: svcNames.length > 0 ? new Set(svcNames) : undefined });
      b2bDashboardCache[user.id] = snap;
      setB2BDashboardSwrSync(user.id, snap as unknown as SwrRecord);
      lastValidDataRef.current = snap;
      const sanitizedSwrSnap = { ...snap, afData: redactAffiliateForSwrCache(snap.afData), allAfiliados: snap.allAfiliados.map(redactAffiliateForSwrCache) };
      ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync()
        .then((salt) => AsyncStorage.setItem(`${B2B_SWR_CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}${user.id}_${isAdmin ? 'ceo' : 'b2b'}`, encryptKineticString(JSON.stringify(sanitizedSwrSnap), salt)))
        .catch(() => {});
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setAfData(snap.afData); setAllAfiliados(snap.allAfiliados); setReservasB2B(snap.reservasB2B);
        setMetricas(snap.metricas); setDesglosePorPeriodo(snap.desglosePorPeriodo);
        setMasterPassSummary(snap.masterPassSummary); setIsProfileIncomplete(snap.isProfileIncomplete);
      }
    } catch {
    } finally {
      isFetchingRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) { setHasHydratedB2B(true); setRefreshing(false); }
    }
  }, [user?.id, user?.email, user?.user_metadata?.nombre_negocio, profile?.nombre, isAdmin, roleMode]);

  const ceoWeeklyReport = useMemo(
    () =>
      isAdmin
        ? buildCeoConsolidatedWeeklyReport(
            allAfiliados,
            reservasB2B,
            undefined,
            validServiceSets.ids,
            validServiceSets.names,
          )
        : null,
    [isAdmin, allAfiliados, reservasB2B, validServiceSets.ids, validServiceSets.names],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user?.id) {
      bovedaService.purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(user.id).catch(() => {});
    }
    fetchB2BData(false);
    const debouncedMutate = createDebouncedEventRunner(() => {
      if (user?.id && b2bDashboardCache[user.id]) b2bDashboardCache[user.id].ts = 0;
      fetchB2BData(true);
    }, 150);
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(debouncedMutate.trigger);
    const unsubRoot = subscribeKineticRootRefresh(debouncedMutate.trigger);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function') {
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
      return () => {
        debouncedMutate.cancel();
        unsubRadar();
        unsubRoot();
        window.removeEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
      };
    }
    return () => {
      debouncedMutate.cancel();
      unsubRadar();
      unsubRoot();
    };
  }, [fetchB2BData, user?.id]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    setRefreshing(true);
    await fetchB2BData(true);
  }, [fetchB2BData]);

  return {
    hasHydratedB2B,
    refreshing,
    isProfileIncomplete,
    afData,
    allAfiliados,
    reservasB2B,
    metricas,
    desglosePorPeriodo,
    masterPassSummary,
    ceoWeeklyReport,
    isAdmin,
    displayRole,
    onRefresh,
    fetchB2BData,
  };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/boveda/useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel.ts

    // === src/hooks/boveda/useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel.ts === //
import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { getVipLevelWeight } from '../../services/bovedaHelpers';
import { localizeVipTierName } from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import { isStrictB2BPartnerAccount } from '../../services/kineticlubHelpers';
import { resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan } from '../../services/kineticFintechSecurityGuards';
import { stripeService } from '../../services/stripeService';
import { notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed } from '../../utils/eventModalActions';

export interface IUseBovedaPurchaseAndCancelParams {
  user: any;
  profile?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  isEn: boolean;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  cleanVipLevel: string;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void>;
  fetchData: (force?: boolean, silent?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  getCostoTotal: (r: any) => number;
  isProcessingPayment: boolean;
  setIsProcessingPayment: (v: boolean) => void;
  setProcessingPlanId: (id: string | null) => void;
  isCanceling: boolean;
  setIsCanceling: (v: boolean) => void;
  setIsMembershipModalOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  setSelectedQR: (q: any | null) => void;
  scheduleBovedaTimeout: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => void;
  showTacticalAlert: (title: string, message: string, type?: string) => void;
}

export function useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel(params: IUseBovedaPurchaseAndCancelParams) {
  const {
    user,
    profile,
    isEn,
    tx,
    cleanVipLevel,
    refreshProfile,
    fetchData,
    getCostoTotal,
    isProcessingPayment,
    setIsProcessingPayment,
    setProcessingPlanId,
    isCanceling,
    setIsCanceling,
    setIsMembershipModalOpen,
    setSelectedQR,
    scheduleBovedaTimeout,
    showTacticalAlert,
  } = params;

  const isPurchasingRef = useRef(false);
  const isCancelingRef = useRef(false);

  const handlePurchase = useCallback(
    async (planPayload: any) => {
      if (!user?.id || isPurchasingRef.current || isProcessingPayment) return;
      if (isStrictB2BPartnerAccount(user, profile)) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert(tx('SOLO CUENTAS DE ATLETA', 'ATHLETE ACCOUNTS ONLY'), tx('Las cuentas operativas B2B no pueden adquirir Kineticoins ni membresías.', 'B2B partner accounts cannot purchase Kineticoins or memberships.'), 'error');
        return;
      }
      isPurchasingRef.current = true;
      const planId = planPayload?.id || 'plan';
      const isTopUp = Boolean(
        planPayload?.esTopUp || String(planPayload?.tipo || '').toLowerCase() === 'topup',
      );
      const isDowngrade = Boolean(planPayload?.isDowngrade);
      const kcInjected = isDowngrade
        ? 0
        : Math.max(0, Number(planPayload?.kc ?? planPayload?.kc_otorgados) || 0);
      const targetLevel = isTopUp
        ? cleanVipLevel
        : planPayload?.nivel || planPayload?.nombre || 'Bronze';
      const targetLevelLabel = localizeVipTierName(targetLevel, isEn);
      const currentTierLabel = localizeVipTierName(cleanVipLevel, isEn);
      const targetWallet: 'membresia' | 'topup' = isTopUp ? 'topup' : 'membresia';

      setIsProcessingPayment(true);
      setProcessingPlanId(planId);
      triggerHaptic('Medium');

      try {
        if (isDowngrade) {
          const { error: downRpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('cambiar_nivel_downgrade_propio', {
            p_nuevo_nivel: targetLevel,
          });
          if (downRpcErr) {
            if (
              downRpcErr.code === 'PGRST202' &&
              getVipLevelWeight(targetLevel) < getVipLevelWeight(cleanVipLevel)
            ) {
              const { error: downErr } = await supabase
                .from('perfiles')
                .update({ nivel: targetLevel })
                .eq('id', user.id);
              if (downErr) throw new Error(downErr.message);
            } else {
              throw new Error(downRpcErr.message);
            }
          }
        } else {
          const canonical = resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan({
            tipo: isTopUp ? 'topup' : 'membresia',
            planId: String(planPayload?.id || targetLevel),
            targetLevel,
            currentLevel: cleanVipLevel,
          });
          let paymentId: string | undefined;
          if (canonical.kcOtorgados > 0 || canonical.precioMxn > 0) {
            const payRes = await stripeService.processCheckout({
              userId: user.id,
              tipo: isTopUp ? 'topup' : 'membresia',
              planId: canonical.canonicalPlanId,
              nombre: isTopUp
                ? `Top-Up +${canonical.kcOtorgados} KC`
                : String(canonical.canonicalLevel || targetLevel),
              kcOtorgados: canonical.kcOtorgados,
              precioMxn: canonical.precioMxn,
            });
            if (!payRes.success) {
              if (payRes.canceled) return;
              throw new Error(
                payRes.error ||
                  tx('No se pudo autorizar el pago con Stripe.', 'Could not authorize Stripe payment.'),
              );
            }
            paymentId = payRes.paymentId;
          }
          await bovedaService.comprarMembresia(
            user.id,
            canonical.kcOtorgados,
            isTopUp ? undefined : canonical.canonicalLevel || targetLevel,
            targetWallet,
            false,
            paymentId,
            canonical.canonicalPlanId,
          );
        }

        setIsMembershipModalOpen(false);
        scheduleBovedaTimeout(() => {
          (async () => {
            try {
              await refreshProfile?.();
            } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
            if (isDowngrade) {
              triggerHaptic('Success');
              showTacticalAlert(
                tx('CAMBIO DE NIVEL APLICADO', 'TIER CHANGE APPLIED'),
                tx(
                  `Tu rango fue ajustado a ${targetLevelLabel} sin cargo adicional hoy. Conservas íntegro tu saldo actual de Kineticoins.`,
                  `Your tier was switched to ${targetLevelLabel} with no charge today. You keep your current Kineticoins balance intact.`,
                ),
                'success',
              );
            } else {
              triggerArcadeHaptic('levelUp');
              showTacticalAlert(
                tx('¡BÓVEDA ACTUALIZADA!', 'VAULT UPDATED!'),
                isTopUp
                  ? tx(
                      `Se acreditaron +${kcInjected} KC en tu billetera Top-Up (Rollover) conservando tu rango ${currentTierLabel}.`,
                      `+${kcInjected} KC were credited to your Top-Up (Rollover) wallet while keeping your ${currentTierLabel} tier.`,
                    )
                  : planPayload?.isUpgrade
                    ? tx(
                        `¡Subiste a ${targetLevelLabel}! Se acreditó la diferencia de +${kcInjected} KC en tu billetera Membresía.`,
                        `Upgraded to ${targetLevelLabel}! The +${kcInjected} KC difference was credited to your Membership wallet.`,
                      )
                    : tx(
                        `Se activó tu Membresía ${targetLevelLabel} con +${kcInjected} KC en tu Bóveda.`,
                        `Your ${targetLevelLabel} Membership was activated with +${kcInjected} KC in your Vault.`,
                      ),
                'success',
              );
            }
          })();
        }, 340);
      } catch (err: any) {
        setIsMembershipModalOpen(false);
        scheduleBovedaTimeout(() => {
          showTacticalAlert(
            tx('AVISO DE BÓVEDA', 'VAULT NOTICE'),
            err?.message ||
              tx(
                'No se pudo procesar la actualización de tu Bóveda.',
                'Could not process your Vault update.',
              ),
            'error',
          );
        }, 320);
      } finally {
        isPurchasingRef.current = false;
        setIsProcessingPayment(false);
        setProcessingPlanId(null);
      }
    },
    [
      user,
      profile,
      isProcessingPayment,
      cleanVipLevel,
      isEn,
      tx,
      setIsProcessingPayment,
      setProcessingPlanId,
      setIsMembershipModalOpen,
      scheduleBovedaTimeout,
      refreshProfile,
      showTacticalAlert,
    ],
  );

  const cancelarReserva = useCallback(
    async (reservaTarget: any) => {
      if (!reservaTarget?.id || !user?.id || isCancelingRef.current || isCanceling) return;
      isCancelingRef.current = true;
      const rawEscrow = reservaTarget?.monto_escrow;
      const reembolso =
        rawEscrow != null && Number.isFinite(Number(rawEscrow))
          ? Math.max(0, Number(rawEscrow))
          : Math.round(getCostoTotal(reservaTarget) * 0.4);
      setIsCanceling(true);
      try {
        let result: any;
        try {
          result = await bovedaService.cancelarReserva(reservaTarget.id, user.id, reembolso);
        } catch (innerErr: any) {
          if (reembolso <= 0) {
            const { error: fbErr } = await supabase
              .from('reservas')
              .update({ estado: 'cancelada' })
              .eq('id', reservaTarget.id)
              .eq('usuario_id', user.id);
            if (fbErr) throw innerErr;
            result = {
              refunded: true,
              message: tx('Pase liberado correctamente.', 'Pass released successfully.'),
            };
          } else {
            throw innerErr;
          }
        }
        notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
        await Promise.all([fetchData(true, true), refreshProfile?.()]);
        setSelectedQR(null);
        scheduleBovedaTimeout(() => {
          if (result.refunded) {
            triggerHaptic('Success');
            showTacticalAlert(
              result.usedVipForgiveness
                ? tx('🛡️ PERDÓN VIP APLICADO', '🛡️ VIP FORGIVENESS APPLIED')
                : tx('RESERVA CANCELADA', 'RESERVATION CANCELLED'),
              result.message ||
                (isEn
                  ? `+${reembolso} KC (40% Protected Deposit) were refunded in full to your Vault.`
                  : `Se reembolsaron +${reembolso} KC íntegros (Anticipo Protegido 40%) a tu Bóveda.`),
              'success',
            );
          } else {
            triggerHaptic('Warning');
            showTacticalAlert(
              tx('SESIÓN LIBERADA (GARANTÍA 40%)', 'SESSION RELEASED (40% GUARANTEE)'),
              result.message ||
                (isEn
                  ? 'Pass canceled: the 40% Protected Deposit was retained for the studio.'
                  : 'Sesión liberada aplicando Anticipo Protegido del 40%.'),
              'warning',
            );
          }
        }, 180);
      } catch (err: any) {
        setSelectedQR(null);
        scheduleBovedaTimeout(() => {
          showTacticalAlert(
            tx('FRICCIÓN', 'NOTICE'),
            err?.message ||
              tx('No se pudo cancelar la reserva.', 'Could not cancel the reservation.'),
            'error',
          );
        }, 180);
      } finally {
        isCancelingRef.current = false;
        setIsCanceling(false);
      }
    },
    [
      user?.id,
      isCanceling,
      getCostoTotal,
      setIsCanceling,
      tx,
      fetchData,
      refreshProfile,
      setSelectedQR,
      scheduleBovedaTimeout,
      showTacticalAlert,
      isEn,
    ],
  );

  return { handlePurchase, cancelarReserva };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/boveda/useSettingsBovedaHook.ts

    // === src/hooks/boveda/useSettingsBovedaHook.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import { resolvePlatformBiometricAvailabilityState } from '../../services/kineticBiometricVaultService';
import { CIPHER_PREFIX, decryptKineticString, ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync, encryptKineticString } from '../../services/kineticVaultCrypto';
import { IUserPreferences } from '../../types';

const PREFS_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX = '@kinetic_user_prefs_v1_';
const FISCAL_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX = '@kinetic_user_fiscal_v1_';

const isWebPlatform = Platform.OS === 'web';
const DEFAULT_PREFERENCES: IUserPreferences = {
  radar_publico: true,
  notificaciones_push: true,
  recordatorios_entrenamiento: true,
  alertas_ventas_flash: true,
  biometria_activa: !isWebPlatform,
};

export interface ISettingsBovedaUser {
  id?: string;
  email?: string | null;
  [k: string]: unknown;
}

export interface ISettingsBovedaProfile {
  radar_publico?: boolean | null;
  [k: string]: unknown;
}

export function useSettingsBoveda(
  user: ISettingsBovedaUser | null | undefined,
  isB2B: boolean,
  profile?: ISettingsBovedaProfile | null
) {
  const [whatsapp, setWhatsapp] = useState('');
  const [clabe, setClabe] = useState('');
  const [ubicacion, setUbicacion] = useState('');
  const [portadaUrl, setPortadaUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [rfc, setRfc] = useState('');
  const [razonSocial, setRazonSocial] = useState('');
  const [regimenFiscal, setRegimenFiscal] = useState('');
  const [codigoPostalFiscal, setCodigoPostalFiscal] = useState('');
  const [usoCfdi, setUsoCfdi] = useState('G03 - Gastos en general');
  const [emailFacturacion, setEmailFacturacion] = useState('');
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const [isUploadingPortada, setIsUploadingPortada] = useState(false);
  const [userPrefs, setUserPrefs] = useState<IUserPreferences>(() => ({
    ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES,
    radar_publico: profile?.radar_publico !== false,
    biometria_activa: isWebPlatform ? false : DEFAULT_PREFERENCES.biometria_activa,
  }));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profile?.radar_publico !== undefined) {
      setUserPrefs((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        radar_publico: profile.radar_publico !== false,
        biometria_activa: isWebPlatform ? false : prev.biometria_activa,
      }));
    }
  }, [profile?.radar_publico]);

  const activeUidRef = useRef<string>(String(user?.id || ''));
  activeUidRef.current = String(user?.id || '');

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    const activeUid = String(user?.id || '');
    setWhatsapp(''); setClabe(''); setUbicacion(''); setPortadaUrl(null);
    setRfc(''); setRazonSocial(''); setRegimenFiscal(''); setCodigoPostalFiscal(''); setEmailFacturacion('');
    if (!activeUid) return () => { isMounted = false; };
    const canApply = () => isMounted && activeUidRef.current === activeUid;
    const loadAllSettings = async () => {
      try {
        const [storedPrefsRaw, storedFiscalRaw, bioCapable] = await Promise.all([
          AsyncStorage.getItem(`${PREFS_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${activeUid}`),
          AsyncStorage.getItem(`${FISCAL_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${activeUid}`),
          resolvePlatformBiometricAvailabilityState().catch(() => ({ isSupported: false })),
        ]);
        const allowBiometrics = !isWebPlatform && bioCapable.isSupported;
        if (canApply() && storedPrefsRaw) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(storedPrefsRaw) as Partial<IUserPreferences>;
          if (canApply()) {
            setUserPrefs((prev) => ({
              ...prev,
              ...parsed,
              radar_publico:
                profile?.radar_publico !== undefined ? profile.radar_publico !== false : parsed.radar_publico ?? true,
              biometria_activa: isWebPlatform ? false : allowBiometrics ? parsed.biometria_activa ?? prev.biometria_activa : false,
            }));
          }
        } else if (canApply() && isWebPlatform) {
          setUserPrefs((prev) => ({ ...prev, biometria_activa: false }));
        }
        if (canApply() && storedFiscalRaw) {
          const salt = storedFiscalRaw.startsWith(CIPHER_PREFIX) ? await ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync() : '';
          const plainFiscal = storedFiscalRaw.startsWith(CIPHER_PREFIX) ? decryptKineticString(storedFiscalRaw, salt) : storedFiscalRaw;
          const parsedFiscal = plainFiscal ? (JSON.parse(plainFiscal) as Record<string, string>) : null;
          if (parsedFiscal && canApply()) {
            if (parsedFiscal.rfc) setRfc(parsedFiscal.rfc);
            if (parsedFiscal.razonSocial) setRazonSocial(parsedFiscal.razonSocial);
            if (parsedFiscal.regimenFiscal) setRegimenFiscal(parsedFiscal.regimenFiscal);
            if (parsedFiscal.codigoPostalFiscal) setCodigoPostalFiscal(parsedFiscal.codigoPostalFiscal);
            if (parsedFiscal.usoCfdi) setUsoCfdi(parsedFiscal.usoCfdi);
            if (parsedFiscal.emailFacturacion) setEmailFacturacion(parsedFiscal.emailFacturacion);
          }
        }
      } catch {}
      if (!isB2B || !canApply()) return;
      try {
        const data = (await bovedaService.fetchSettingsB2B(activeUid)) as Record<string, unknown> | null;
        if (!canApply() || !data) return;
        const wa = String(data.whatsapp || '');
        setWhatsapp(wa.includes('1234567') ? '' : wa);
        setClabe(String(data.clabe_bancaria || ''));
        setUbicacion(String(data.ubicacion || ''));
        setPortadaUrl(typeof data.portada_url === 'string' ? data.portada_url : null);
        if (typeof data.rfc === 'string' && data.rfc) setRfc(data.rfc);
        if (typeof data.regimen_fiscal === 'string' && data.regimen_fiscal) setRegimenFiscal(data.regimen_fiscal);
      } catch {}
    };
    void loadAllSettings();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [user?.id, isB2B, profile?.radar_publico]);

  const togglePreference = useCallback(
    async (key: keyof IUserPreferences) => {
      if (isWebPlatform && key === 'biometria_activa') {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        setUserPrefs((prev) => ({ ...prev, biometria_activa: false }));
        return;
      }
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      setUserPrefs((prev) => {
        const next = { ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] };
        if (isWebPlatform) next.biometria_activa = false;
        if (user?.id) {
          AsyncStorage.setItem(`${PREFS_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${user.id}`, JSON.stringify(next)).catch(() => {});
        }
        return next;
      });
    },
    [user?.id]
  );

  const saveFiscalCache = useCallback(
    async (fiscalPayload: {
      rfc?: string;
      razonSocial?: string;
      regimenFiscal?: string;
      codigoPostalFiscal?: string;
      usoCfdi?: string;
      emailFacturacion?: string;
    }) => {
      if (!user?.id) return;
      const salt = await ensureDeviceCipherSaltAsync();
      const encrypted = encryptKineticString(JSON.stringify(fiscalPayload), salt);
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(`${FISCAL_STORAGE_KEY_PREFIX}${user.id}`, encrypted).catch(() => {});
    },
    [user?.id]
  );

  return {
    whatsapp, setWhatsapp, clabe, setClabe, ubicacion, setUbicacion, portadaUrl, setPortadaUrl,
    rfc, setRfc, razonSocial, setRazonSocial, regimenFiscal, setRegimenFiscal,
    codigoPostalFiscal, setCodigoPostalFiscal, usoCfdi, setUsoCfdi, emailFacturacion, setEmailFacturacion,
    saveFiscalCache, isSaving, setIsSaving, isUploading, setIsUploading, isUploadingPortada, setIsUploadingPortada,
    userPrefs, setUserPrefs, togglePreference,
  };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/boveda/useSettingsModalMediaAndSave.ts

    // === src/hooks/boveda/useSettingsModalMediaAndSave.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { Image as ExpoImage } from 'expo-image';
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { Keyboard } from 'react-native';
import { openKineticImageFrame, preloadImageInMemory } from '../../utils/kineticImageFramePureLeaf';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import type { KineticAuthProfile, KineticAuthUser } from '../../services/kineticAuthContextHelpers';
import {
  emitBiometricPreferenceChanged,
  persistBiometricRefreshTokenIfAllowedAsync,
  purgeBiometricRefreshVaultAsync,
} from '../../services/kineticBiometricVaultService';
import type { IMfaSessionLike } from '../../services/kineticMfaPureGuards';
import { getMfaSecuritySnapshot } from '../../services/kineticSecurityMfaService';
import { markTransientNativeOverlayActive } from '../../utils/kineticBiometricGateMachineLeaf';
import {
  decodeBase64OrDataUriToUint8Array,
  readMediaUriAsUint8Array,
  validateSafeStorageImagePayload,
} from '../../utils/kineticBinaryMediaHelper';
import { validateStrictWhatsappPhone } from '../../utils/kineticPhoneValidatorLeaf';

export const BIOMETRIC_LOGIN_ENABLED_KEY = '@kinetic_biometric_login_enabled_v1';
export const BIOMETRIC_SAVED_EMAIL_KEY = '@kinetic_biometric_email_v1';

type SettingsTogglePrefKey = 'notificaciones_push' | 'alertas_ventas_flash' | 'biometria_activa' | 'radar_publico';

interface UseSettingsModalMediaAndSaveParams {
  user: KineticAuthUser | null;
  profile: KineticAuthProfile | null;
  sessionToken?: string | null;
  showB2BVenue: boolean;
  language: string;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  nombrePerfil: string;
  disciplinaB2B: string;
  horariosB2B: string;
  whatsapp: string;
  ubicacion: string;
  userPrefs: { biometria_activa?: boolean; notificaciones_push?: boolean; alertas_ventas_flash?: boolean; radar_publico?: boolean };
  togglePreference: (k: SettingsTogglePrefKey) => void;
  setPortadaUrl: (v: string) => void;
  isSaving: boolean;
  setIsSaving: (v: boolean) => void;
  isUploading: boolean;
  setIsUploading: (v: boolean) => void;
  isUploadingPortada: boolean;
  setIsUploadingPortada: (v: boolean) => void;
  setLivePrefToast: (msg: string | null) => void;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void>;
  onClose?: () => void;
  fireHaptic?: (mode?: string) => void;
}

const preloadFinalUrl = async (url: string): Promise<void> => {
  await preloadImageInMemory(url);
  const imgApi = ExpoImage as unknown as { prefetch?: (urls: string[]) => Promise<boolean> };
  if (typeof imgApi.prefetch === 'function' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) {
    await imgApi.prefetch([url]).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
  }
};

export function useSettingsModalMediaAndSave(params: UseSettingsModalMediaAndSaveParams) {
  const {
    user, profile, sessionToken, showB2BVenue, language, tx,
    nombrePerfil, disciplinaB2B, horariosB2B, whatsapp, ubicacion,
    userPrefs, togglePreference, setPortadaUrl,
    setIsSaving, isUploading, setIsUploading, isUploadingPortada, setIsUploadingPortada,
    setLivePrefToast, refreshProfile, onClose, fireHaptic,
  } = params;

  const mediaBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const saveBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const bioToggleSeqRef = useRef(0);

  const handleToggleSwitchLive = async (key: SettingsTogglePrefKey) => {
    fireHaptic?.('Selection');
    const nextVal = !userPrefs[key];
    togglePreference(key);
    if (key !== 'biometria_activa') {
      setLivePrefToast(tx('✓ Preferencia actualizada en vivo', '✓ Preference updated live'));
      return;
    }
    const seq = ++bioToggleSeqRef.current;
    await Promise.all([
      AsyncStorage.setItem(BIOMETRIC_LOGIN_ENABLED_KEY, nextVal ? 'true' : 'false').catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
      AsyncStorage.removeItem('@kinetic_biometric_enabled_v1').catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
      nextVal && user?.email
        ? AsyncStorage.setItem(BIOMETRIC_SAVED_EMAIL_KEY, String(user.email).trim().toLowerCase()).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; })
        : AsyncStorage.removeItem(BIOMETRIC_SAVED_EMAIL_KEY).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
    ]);
    emitBiometricPreferenceChanged();
    if (bioToggleSeqRef.current !== seq) return;
    if (!nextVal) {
      await purgeBiometricRefreshVaultAsync(user?.email || user?.id || profile?.email);
      if (bioToggleSeqRef.current === seq) {
        setLivePrefToast(tx('Acceso Biométrico desactivado', 'Biometric Login disabled'));
      }
      return;
    }
    let sealed = false;
    try {
      const [sessRes, mfaSnap] = await Promise.all([supabase.auth.getSession(), getMfaSecuritySnapshot()]);
      if (bioToggleSeqRef.current !== seq) return;
      const liveSess = sessRes?.data?.session as unknown as IMfaSessionLike | null;
      if (!mfaSnap.checkFailed && !mfaSnap.requiresMfaChallenge) {
        sealed = await persistBiometricRefreshTokenIfAllowedAsync({
          session: liveSess, hasVerifiedTotpFactor: mfaSnap.hasVerifiedTotp, biometriaActiva: true,
        });
      } else {
        await purgeBiometricRefreshVaultAsync(user?.email || user?.id || profile?.email);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    if (bioToggleSeqRef.current !== seq) return;
    setLivePrefToast(
      sealed
        ? tx('✓ Acceso con Huella Biométrica activado', '✓ Biometric Fingerprint Login enabled')
        : tx('✓ Preferencia guardada (verifica 2FA activo en app móvil para sellar huella)', '✓ Preference saved (verify active 2FA on mobile app to seal biometrics)')
    );
  };

  const uploadBinaryToAvatarsBucket = useCallback(
    async (prefix: 'avatar' | 'cover', framedUri: string, fileName?: string | null, mimeType?: string | null): Promise<string | null> => {
      const targetUid = String(user?.id || '').trim();
      if (!targetUid) return null;
      const { data: sessionRes } = await supabase.auth.getSession().catch((err: unknown) => { void err; return { data: { session: null } }; });
      const activeToken = String(sessionRes?.session?.access_token || sessionToken || '');
      const authUid = String(sessionRes?.session?.user?.id || '').trim();
      const bytes = framedUri.startsWith('data:image/')
        ? decodeBase64OrDataUriToUint8Array(framedUri)
        : await readMediaUriAsUint8Array(framedUri);
      const effectiveMime = framedUri.startsWith('data:image/jpeg') ? 'image/jpeg' : mimeType || 'image/jpeg';
      const check = validateSafeStorageImagePayload({ bytes, uriOrName: fileName || framedUri, declaredMime: effectiveMime });
      if (!check.ok || !bytes || !authUid || authUid !== targetUid || !activeToken || activeToken.startsWith('kinetic-')) return null;
      const storagePath = `${authUid}/${prefix}_${Date.now()}.${check.ext || 'jpg'}`;
      const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
        .from('avatars')
        .upload(storagePath, bytes, { contentType: check.contentType || 'image/jpeg', upsert: true });
      if (upErr) return null;
      const { data: pubData } = supabase.storage.from('avatars').getPublicUrl(storagePath);
      return pubData?.publicUrl ? String(pubData.publicUrl) : null;
    },
    [sessionToken, user?.id]
  );

  const handleAvatarChange = async () => {
    if (!user?.id || isUploading || mediaBusyRef.current) return;
    mediaBusyRef.current = true;
    markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', true);
    try {
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ['images'], allowsEditing: false, quality: 0.84, base64: false,
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]) {
        const asset = res.assets[0];
        const framedUri = await openKineticImageFrame({
          uri: asset.uri, aspect: '1:1', lockAspect: true, titleEs: 'Foto de Perfil (1:1)', titleEn: 'Profile Photo (1:1)',
        });
        if (!framedUri) return;
        setIsUploading(true);
        const finalUrl = await uploadBinaryToAvatarsBucket('avatar', framedUri, asset.fileName, asset.mimeType);
        if (!finalUrl) {
          setLivePrefToast(tx('Verifica tu conexión para subir tu foto 1:1.', 'Check your connection to upload your 1:1 photo.'));
          return;
        }
        await preloadFinalUrl(finalUrl);
        await bovedaService.updateAvatar(user.id, finalUrl, showB2BVenue);
        await refreshProfile?.();
        fireHaptic?.('Success');
        setLivePrefToast(tx('✓ Foto 1:1 actualizada en la nube', '✓ 1:1 photo updated in cloud'));
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      void err;
      setLivePrefToast(tx('No se pudo actualizar la foto. Intenta nuevamente.', 'Could not update photo. Please try again.'));
    } finally {
      markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', false);
      mediaBusyRef.current = false;
      setIsUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePortadaChange = async () => {
    if (!user?.id || isUploadingPortada || mediaBusyRef.current) return;
    mediaBusyRef.current = true;
    markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', true);
    try {
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ['images'], allowsEditing: false, quality: 0.84, base64: false,
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]) {
        const asset = res.assets[0];
        const framedUri = await openKineticImageFrame({
          uri: asset.uri, aspect: '16:9', lockAspect: true, titleEs: 'Portada de Sede (16:9)', titleEn: 'Venue Cover (16:9)',
        });
        if (!framedUri) return;
        setIsUploadingPortada(true);
        const finalUrl = await uploadBinaryToAvatarsBucket('cover', framedUri, asset.fileName, asset.mimeType);
        if (!finalUrl) {
          setLivePrefToast(tx('Verifica tu conexión para subir tu portada.', 'Check your connection to upload your cover.'));
          return;
        }
        await preloadFinalUrl(finalUrl);
        await bovedaService.updatePortada(user.id, finalUrl);
        setPortadaUrl(finalUrl);
        fireHaptic?.('Success');
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally {
      markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', false);
      mediaBusyRef.current = false;
      setIsUploadingPortada(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveSettings = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    if (!user?.id || saveBusyRef.current) return;
    const cleanName = nombrePerfil.trim().slice(0, 80);
    if (!cleanName) {
      fireHaptic?.('Warning');
      setLivePrefToast(tx('Ingresa un nombre válido antes de guardar.', 'Enter a valid name before saving.'));
      return;
    }
    const cleanWa = whatsapp.trim();
    const phoneCheck = cleanWa ? validateStrictWhatsappPhone(cleanWa) : null;
    if (showB2BVenue && phoneCheck && !phoneCheck.valid) {
      fireHaptic?.('Warning');
      setLivePrefToast(tx('Ingresa un WhatsApp real (10 a 15 dígitos sin secuencias ni repetidos).', 'Enter a valid WhatsApp number (no sequences or repeated digits).'));
      return;
    }
    saveBusyRef.current = true;
    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      const { error: pErr } = await supabase.from('perfiles').update({ nombre: cleanName }).eq('id', user.id);
      if (pErr) throw pErr;
      if (showB2BVenue) {
        const baseAfUpdate = { nombre: cleanName, whatsapp: phoneCheck?.valid ? phoneCheck.normalized10 : cleanWa, ubicacion: ubicacion.trim(), categoria: disciplinaB2B };
        const { error: afErr } = await supabase.from('afiliados').update({ ...baseAfUpdate, horarios: horariosB2B.trim() }).eq('propietario_id', user.id);
        if (afErr) {
          if (!/horarios|column/i.test(String(afErr.message || ''))) throw afErr;
          const { error: fallbackErr } = await supabase.from('afiliados').update(baseAfUpdate).eq('propietario_id', user.id);
          if (fallbackErr) throw fallbackErr;
        }
      }
      await refreshProfile?.();
      fireHaptic?.('Success');
      onClose?.();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
      fireHaptic?.('Error');
      setLivePrefToast(language === 'en' ? 'Unable to save settings. Check your connection.' : (msg || 'Verifica tu conexión.'));
    } finally {
      saveBusyRef.current = false;
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return { handleToggleSwitchLive, handleAvatarChange, handlePortadaChange, handleSaveSettings };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/useBoveda.ts

    import type React from 'react';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import { getMaxForgivenessForLevel } from '../constants/memberships';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { bovedaService } from '../services/bovedaService';
import { consumeOrphanRefundFlag, evaluateQrWindowStatus, fetchOwnReservaQrSeed, isValidUuid, partitionAthleteReservas, purgeGhostPassStorageKeys, subscribePurgedBovedaPass } from '../services/bovedaHelpers';
import { hydrateOfflineReservasAtleta, mergeLocalRatingsIntoReservas, persistOfflineReservasAtleta, subscribeRatingUpdates } from '../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { getActivePassesListSnapshotSync } from '../services/bovedaRealtimeSync';
import { BOVEDA_OPTIMISTIC_TTL_MS, getBovedaSWRReservas, reconcileBovedaSwrWithServerRows, setBovedaSWRReservas, subscribeBovedaSWR } from '../services/kineticOptimisticStore';
import { getMazatlanNowParts } from '../services/kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';
import { type IGamificationProfileRow, type IGamificationReservaRow, evaluateMonthlyPassTruth } from '../services/kineticGamificationSqlTruthLeaf';
import { getLocalServerClockOffsetMs, hydrateServerClockOffsetMs, persistServerClockOffsetMs } from '../services/terminalOfflineService';
import { createDebouncedEventRunner, subscribeKineticRadarUpdated, subscribeKineticRootRefresh } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { useBovedaActions } from './boveda/useBovedaActions';
