export { computeRolling30DayCutInfo, resolveReservaB2BMonto, useDashboardB2B, useSettingsBoveda };

const BOVEDA_EGRESS_TTL_MS = 45_000;
let bovedaQrChanSeq = 0;

type BovedaRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
type BovedaUserInput = { id?: string; created_at?: string; user_metadata?: BovedaRecord } | null | undefined;
type BovedaProfileInput = BovedaRecord | null | undefined;

const makeBovedaFieldSetter = <T extends Record<string, unknown>, K extends keyof T>(
  setter: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<T>>,
  key: K
): React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<T[K]>> => (action) => {
  setter((prev) => ({
    ...prev,
    [key]: typeof action === 'function' ? (action as (p: T[K]) => T[K])(prev[key]) : action,
  }));
};

export function useBovedaAtleta(user: BovedaUserInput, profile: BovedaProfileInput, refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void>) {
  const syncSWR = getBovedaSWRReservas(user?.id) ?? getActivePassesListSnapshotSync(user?.id);
  const [reservas, setReservas] = useState<BovedaRecord[]>(() => (Array.isArray(syncSWR) ? (syncSWR as BovedaRecord[]) : []));
  const hasLoadedInitialRef = useRef<boolean>(Array.isArray(syncSWR));
  const lastFetchedAtRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const pendingForcedRefreshRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const [selectedQR, setSelectedQR] = useState<BovedaRecord | null>(null);
  const selectedQRRef = useRef<BovedaRecord | null>(null);
  selectedQRRef.current = selectedQR;
  const [selectedReceipt, setSelectedReceipt] = useState<BovedaRecord | null>(null);
  const [syncFlags, setSyncFlags] = useState(() => ({
    refreshing: false,
    hasInitialLoaded: Array.isArray(syncSWR),
    isFetchingReservas: !Array.isArray(syncSWR),
    isGhostMode: profile?.radar_publico === false,
  }));
  const [qrState, setQrState] = useState({
    isQRReady: false, qrTimeLeft: 60, qrPayload: '', isRegeneratingQR: false, isWithinWindow: true, windowMessage: '',
  });
  const [uiState, setUiState] = useState<{
    isHistorialExpanded: boolean; activeFilter: string; isProcessingPayment: boolean; processingPlanId: string | null;
    isCanceling: boolean; referralCodeInput: string; isRedeemingReferral: boolean; isReferralCopied: boolean; isExportingReceiptPdf: boolean;
  }>({
    isHistorialExpanded: false, activeFilter: 'Todas', isProcessingPayment: false, processingPlanId: null,
    isCanceling: false, referralCodeInput: '', isRedeemingReferral: false, isReferralCopied: false, isExportingReceiptPdf: false,
  });

  const { refreshing, hasInitialLoaded, isFetchingReservas, isGhostMode } = syncFlags;
  const { isQRReady, qrTimeLeft, qrPayload, isRegeneratingQR, isWithinWindow, windowMessage } = qrState;
  const {
    isHistorialExpanded, activeFilter, isProcessingPayment, processingPlanId,
    isCanceling, referralCodeInput, isRedeemingReferral, isReferralCopied, isExportingReceiptPdf,
  } = uiState;

  const setters = useMemo(() => ({
    setRefreshing: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setSyncFlags, 'refreshing'), setHasInitialLoaded: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setSyncFlags, 'hasInitialLoaded'),
    setIsFetchingReservas: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setSyncFlags, 'isFetchingReservas'), setIsGhostMode: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setSyncFlags, 'isGhostMode'),
    setIsQRReady: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setQrState, 'isQRReady'), setQrTimeLeft: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setQrState, 'qrTimeLeft'),
    setQrPayload: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setQrState, 'qrPayload'), setIsRegeneratingQR: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setQrState, 'isRegeneratingQR'),
    setIsWithinWindow: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setQrState, 'isWithinWindow'), setWindowMessage: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setQrState, 'windowMessage'),
    setIsHistorialExpanded: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'isHistorialExpanded'), setActiveFilter: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'activeFilter'),
    setIsProcessingPayment: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'isProcessingPayment'), setProcessingPlanId: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'processingPlanId'),
    setIsCanceling: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'isCanceling'), setReferralCodeInput: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'referralCodeInput'),
    setIsRedeemingReferral: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'isRedeemingReferral'), setIsReferralCopied: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'isReferralCopied'),
    setIsExportingReceiptPdf: makeBovedaFieldSetter(setUiState, 'isExportingReceiptPdf'),
  }), []);
  const {
    setRefreshing, setHasInitialLoaded, setIsFetchingReservas, setIsGhostMode,
    setIsQRReady, setQrTimeLeft, setQrPayload, setIsRegeneratingQR, setIsWithinWindow, setWindowMessage,
    setIsHistorialExpanded, setActiveFilter, setIsProcessingPayment, setProcessingPlanId,
    setIsCanceling, setReferralCodeInput, setIsRedeemingReferral, setIsReferralCopied, setIsExportingReceiptPdf,
  } = setters;

  const serverOffsetMsRef = useRef<number>(getLocalServerClockOffsetMs());
  const lastTotpBucketRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const isFetchingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const isTogglingGhostRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const redeemBusyRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const regenTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const copyFeedbackTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const receiptSwapTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    hydrateServerClockOffsetMs().then((off) => { serverOffsetMsRef.current = off; }).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      if (regenTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(regenTimerRef.current);
      if (copyFeedbackTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(copyFeedbackTimerRef.current);
      if (receiptSwapTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(receiptSwapTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profile && !isTogglingGhostRef.current) {
      setIsGhostMode(profile?.radar_publico === false);
    }
  }, [profile?.radar_publico, setIsGhostMode]);

  const referralCode = useMemo(() => {
    if (profile?.codigo_referido) {
      return String(profile.codigo_referido).trim().toUpperCase();
    }
    const cleanUid = String(user?.id || '0000').replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 6).toUpperCase();
    return `KNT-${cleanUid}`;
  }, [profile?.codigo_referido, user?.id]);

  const syncServerClock = useCallback(async () => {
    serverOffsetMsRef.current = await hydrateServerClockOffsetMs();
    try {
      const t0 = Date.now();
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_tiempo_servidor_ms');
      if (!error && typeof data === 'number') {
        serverOffsetMsRef.current = data + Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 2) - Date.now();
        persistServerClockOffsetMs(serverOffsetMsRef.current);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
  }, []);

  const getCostoTotal = useCallback((reserva: BovedaRecord): number => {
    return resolveReservaB2BMonto(reserva, false);
  }, []);

  const fetchMisReservas = useCallback(async (uid: string) => {
    try {
      const data = await bovedaService.fetchReservasAtleta(uid);
      const rows = (Array.isArray(data) ? data : []).filter(
        (r) => r?.id && !r?.isOptimistic && !String(r.id).startsWith('opt_')
      );
      const merged = reconcileBovedaSwrWithServerRows(
        getBovedaSWRReservas(uid) || [],
        rows,
        Date.now(),
        BOVEDA_OPTIMISTIC_TTL_MS
      );
      setBovedaSWRReservas(uid, rows, true, true);
      persistOfflineReservasAtleta(uid, rows).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
      lastFetchedAtRef.current = Date.now();
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setHasInitialLoaded((p) => (p ? p : true));
        setIsFetchingReservas((p) => (p ? false : p));
        setReservas(merged as BovedaRecord[]);
      }
      if (consumeOrphanRefundFlag(uid)) refreshProfile?.().catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
      return merged;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      void err;
      const cached = await hydrateOfflineReservasAtleta(uid);
      const mergedCached = await mergeLocalRatingsIntoReservas(cached);
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setHasInitialLoaded((p) => (p ? p : true));
        if (mergedCached.length > 0) {
          setReservas((prev) => (prev.length > 0 ? prev : mergedCached));
        }
      }
      return mergedCached;
    }
  }, [refreshProfile, setHasInitialLoaded, setIsFetchingReservas]);

  const fetchData = useCallback(
    async (isSilent = false, force = false) => {
      if (!user?.id) return;
      if (isFetchingRef.current) {
        if (force) pendingForcedRefreshRef.current = true;
        return;
      }
      if (!force && hasLoadedInitialRef.current && Date.now() - lastFetchedAtRef.current < BOVEDA_EGRESS_TTL_MS) {
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setHasInitialLoaded((p) => (p ? p : true));
          setIsFetchingReservas((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
        }
        return;
      }
      isFetchingRef.current = true;
      if (!hasLoadedInitialRef.current && !isSilent && isMountedRef.current) {
        setIsFetchingReservas(true);
      }
      try {
        await Promise.all([syncServerClock(), fetchMisReservas(user.id)]);
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally {
        isFetchingRef.current = false;
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        if (pendingForcedRefreshRef.current && user?.id) {
          pendingForcedRefreshRef.current = false;
          fetchMisReservas(user.id).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
        }
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setHasInitialLoaded((p) => (p ? p : true));
          setIsFetchingReservas((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
        }
      }
    },
    [user?.id, syncServerClock, fetchMisReservas, setHasInitialLoaded, setIsFetchingReservas, setRefreshing],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) {
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = false;
      lastFetchedAtRef.current = 0;
      setReservas([]);
      setIsFetchingReservas(false);
      return;
    }
    const cachedSync = getBovedaSWRReservas(user.id);
    if (Array.isArray(cachedSync)) {
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
      setHasInitialLoaded(true);
      setIsFetchingReservas(false);
      setReservas(cachedSync);
      fetchData(true, false);
    } else {
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = false;
      lastFetchedAtRef.current = 0;
      fetchData(false, true);
    }
    purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(user.id).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });

    const unsubPurged = subscribePurgedBovedaPass((purgedId) => {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      setReservas((prev) => prev.filter((r) => String(r?.id || '') !== purgedId));
      if (selectedQRRef.current && String(selectedQRRef.current.id) === purgedId) setSelectedQR(null);
    });
    const unsubRatings = subscribeRatingUpdates((ratedId, stars, comment) => {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      setReservas((prev) => prev.map((r) => String(r?.id) === ratedId
        ? { ...r, calificacion_atleta: stars, comentario_atleta: comment } : r));
      setSelectedReceipt((prev: BovedaRecord | null) => prev && String(prev.id) === ratedId
        ? { ...prev, calificacion_atleta: stars, comentario_atleta: comment } : prev);
    });

    const debouncedMutate = createDebouncedEventRunner(() => {
      lastFetchedAtRef.current = 0;
      fetchData(true, true);
    }, 150);

    const unsubSWR = subscribeBovedaSWR(user.id, (rows, meta) => {
      if (!isMountedRef.current || !Array.isArray(rows)) return;
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
      setHasInitialLoaded(true);
      setIsFetchingReservas(false);
      setReservas(rows as BovedaRecord[]);
      if (!meta?.fromServerSync && rows.some((r) => r?.id && !r?.isOptimistic)) debouncedMutate.trigger();
    });
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(debouncedMutate.trigger);
    const unsubRoot = subscribeKineticRootRefresh(debouncedMutate.trigger);
    const hasWin = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function';
    if (hasWin) {
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:stories-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
    }
    return () => {
      debouncedMutate.cancel();
      unsubPurged();
      unsubRatings();
      unsubSWR();
      unsubRadar();
      unsubRoot();
      if (hasWin) {
        window.removeEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
        window.removeEventListener('kinetic:stories-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
      }
    };
  }, [user?.id, fetchData]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    if (!user?.id) {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setRefreshing(false);
      return;
    }
    if (isMountedRef.current) setRefreshing(true);
    try {
      await Promise.all([
        fetchData(true, true),
        Promise.resolve(refreshProfile?.()).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
      ]);
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [user?.id, fetchData, refreshProfile, setRefreshing]);

  const { toggleGhostModeBoveda, copyReferralCodeSilently, shareReferralCodeNative, redeemReferralCode, exportReceiptToPdf } = useBovedaActions({
    user, profile, refreshProfile, isMountedRef, isTogglingGhostRef, redeemBusyRef, copyFeedbackTimerRef,
    isGhostMode, setIsGhostMode, referralCode, referralCodeInput, setReferralCodeInput, setIsReferralCopied,
    isRedeemingReferral, setIsRedeemingReferral, selectedReceipt, isExportingReceiptPdf, setIsExportingReceiptPdf,
  });

  const { activeReservations, historyReservations, completedCount, canceledCount, totalEscrowLockedKc } =
    useMemo(() => partitionAthleteReservas(reservas, getCostoTotal), [reservas, getCostoTotal]);

  const billingCycleInfo = useMemo(() => {
    const kcMembresia = Math.max(0, Number(profile?.kc_membresia ?? user?.user_metadata?.kc_membresia) || 0);
    const kcTopup = Math.max(0, Number(profile?.kc_topup ?? user?.user_metadata?.kc_topup) || 0);
    const saldoTotal = kcMembresia + kcTopup;
    const monthlyTruth = evaluateMonthlyPassTruth(
      { ...(profile as IGamificationProfileRow | null), kc_membresia: kcMembresia },
      reservas as unknown as IGamificationReservaRow[],
      0
    );
    const rawActivation = monthlyTruth.hasActiveMembership && profile?.fecha_activacion_membresia
      ? profile.fecha_activacion_membresia
      : monthlyTruth.nextCutDateIso;
    const activationSource = typeof rawActivation === 'string' ? rawActivation : null;
    const { daysUntilCut: rawCutDays, nextCutDateLabel } = computeRolling30DayCutInfo(activationSource);
    const daysUntilCut = monthlyTruth.hasActiveMembership ? monthlyTruth.daysUntilCut : monthlyTruth.daysLeftInMonth;
    const cutLabelOut = monthlyTruth.hasActiveMembership ? nextCutDateLabel : (monthlyTruth.nextCutDateIso || nextCutDateLabel);
    void rawCutDays;
    const currentMonthKey = getMazatlanNowParts().isoDate.slice(0, 7);
    const storedMonthKey = String(profile?.mes_perdones_control || '').trim();
    const perdonesUsados = storedMonthKey === currentMonthKey ? Math.max(0, Number(profile?.perdones_mensuales_usados) || 0) : 0;
    const perdonesMaximos = getMaxForgivenessForLevel(typeof profile?.nivel === 'string' ? profile.nivel : undefined);
    const perdonesDisponibles = Math.max(0, perdonesMaximos - perdonesUsados);
    return {
      kcMembresia,
      kcTopup,
      saldoTotal,
      daysUntilCut,
      nextCutDateLabel: cutLabelOut,
      hasActiveMembership: monthlyTruth.hasActiveMembership,
      perdonesMaximos,
      perdonesUsados,
      perdonesDisponibles,
    };
  }, [profile, reservas, user?.user_metadata?.kc_membresia, user?.user_metadata?.kc_topup]);

  const evaluateWindowAndGenerateQR = useCallback((reserva: BovedaRecord) => {
    if (!reserva) return;
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    const offset = Number.isFinite(serverOffsetMsRef.current) ? serverOffsetMsRef.current : getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
    const nowSynced = Date.now() + offset;
    lastTotpBucketRef.current = Math.floor(nowSynced / 60_000);
    const { withinWindow, message, qrPayload: nextPayload, secondsRemaining } = evaluateQrWindowStatus(reserva, nowSynced, isEn);
    setIsWithinWindow(withinWindow);
    setWindowMessage(message);
    setQrPayload(nextPayload);
    setQrTimeLeft(secondsRemaining || 60);
    setIsQRReady(true);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedQR) {
      setIsQRReady(false); setQrPayload(''); setQrTimeLeft(60);
      return;
    }
    evaluateWindowAndGenerateQR(selectedQR);
    const activeResId = String(selectedQR.id || '').trim();
    if (!selectedQR.qr_token && isValidUuid(activeResId)) {
      fetchOwnReservaQrSeed(activeResId).then((seed) => {
        if (!seed || !isMountedRef.current || String(selectedQRRef.current?.id || '').trim() !== activeResId) return;
        const hydratedQr: BovedaRecord = { ...selectedQRRef.current, qr_token: seed };
        selectedQRRef.current = hydratedQr;
        evaluateWindowAndGenerateQR(hydratedQr);
        setSelectedQR((prev) => (prev && String(prev.id || '').trim() === activeResId && !prev.qr_token ? { ...prev, qr_token: seed } : prev));
        setReservas((prev) => prev.map((item) => (String(item.id || '').trim() === activeResId ? { ...item, qr_token: seed } : item)));
      }).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
    }
    hydrateServerClockOffsetMs().then((off) => {
      serverOffsetMsRef.current = off;
      if (isMountedRef.current && selectedQRRef.current) evaluateWindowAndGenerateQR(selectedQRRef.current);
    }).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const curQr = selectedQRRef.current;
      if (!curQr || !isMountedRef.current) return;
      const offset = Number.isFinite(serverOffsetMsRef.current) ? serverOffsetMsRef.current : getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
      const nowSynced = Date.now() + offset;
      const bucket = Math.floor(nowSynced / 60_000);
      if (bucket !== lastTotpBucketRef.current) {
        setIsRegeneratingQR(true);
        triggerHaptic('Light');
        evaluateWindowAndGenerateQR(curQr);
        if (regenTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(regenTimerRef.current);
        regenTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { if (isMountedRef.current) setIsRegeneratingQR(false); }, 220);
      } else {
        setQrTimeLeft(Math.max(1, 60 - Math.floor(((nowSynced % 60_000) + 60_000) % 60_000 / 1000)));
      }
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [selectedQR, evaluateWindowAndGenerateQR]);

  const activeQrId = selectedQR?.id ? String(selectedQR.id) : '';
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeQrId) return;
    const channel = supabase
      .channel(`boveda-live-qr-${activeQrId}-${++bovedaQrChanSeq}`)
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'UPDATE', schema: 'public', table: 'reservas', filter: `id=eq.${activeQrId}` }, async (payload) => {
        const updated = (payload?.new as BovedaRecord | undefined) ?? undefined;
        if (updated?.estado !== 'completada') return;
        const baseQr = selectedQRRef.current || {};
        triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn');
        setReservas((prev) => prev.map((item) => (String(item.id) === activeQrId ? { ...item, ...updated, estado: 'completada' } : item)));
        await Promise.resolve(refreshProfile?.()).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
        setSelectedQR(null);
        if (receiptSwapTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(receiptSwapTimerRef.current);
        receiptSwapTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          if (isMountedRef.current) setSelectedReceipt({ ...baseQr, ...updated, estado: 'completada' });
        }, 180);
      })
      .subscribe();
    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel); };
  }, [activeQrId, user?.id, refreshProfile, setIsQRReady]);

    return {
      refreshing, reservas, setReservas, activeReservations, historyReservations, completedCount,
      canceledCount, totalEscrowLockedKc, billingCycleInfo, isGhostMode, toggleGhostModeBoveda,
      isFetchingReservas, hasInitialLoaded, selectedQR, setSelectedQR, isQRReady, setIsQRReady, selectedReceipt,
    setSelectedReceipt, isHistorialExpanded, setIsHistorialExpanded, activeFilter, setActiveFilter,
    isProcessingPayment, setIsProcessingPayment, processingPlanId, setProcessingPlanId,
    isCanceling, setIsCanceling, qrTimeLeft, qrPayload, isRegeneratingQR, isWithinWindow,

    getCostoTotal, onRefresh,
  };
}

    FILE: src/services/bovedaB2BDataHelper.ts

    // === src/services/bovedaB2BDataHelper.ts === //
import {
  isSupabaseQuotaExceeded,
  isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error,
  setSupabaseQuotaExceeded,
  supabase,
} from '../lib/supabase';
import { IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO } from '../types';
import { fetchAllDeterministicRangePages } from '../utils/kineticRangePaginator';
import {
  calcularCorteSemanalB2B,
  IB2BPeriodOverride,
  isAdminOrB2BAccount,
  isMasterPass0Kc,
  isSandboxAccountOrReservation,
  isOrphanEscrowReservation,
} from './adminB2BCutEngine';
import { getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds, parseMazatlanTimestampMs } from './kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';

type B2BRow = Record<string, unknown>;

export interface IMasterPassAuditSummary {
  total_emitidos: number;
  activos: number;
  liquidados: number;
  cancelados: number;
  hoy: number;
  semana: number;
  mes: number;
}

export const EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY: IMasterPassAuditSummary = {
  total_emitidos: 0,
  activos: 0,
  liquidados: 0,
  cancelados: 0,
  hoy: 0,
  semana: 0,
  mes: 0,
};

const RESERVAS_B2B_COLS_LEGACY =
  'id, usuario_id, negocio_id, negocio_nombre, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, categoria, estado, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, precio_kc, costo_total, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup, fecha_hora, fecha_clase, perdon_vip_aplicado, penalizacion_aplicada, calificacion_atleta, created_at, updated_at';
const RESERVAS_B2B_COLS = `${RESERVAS_B2B_COLS_LEGACY}, es_cuenta_sandbox`;

const _memoryB2BData = new Map<
  string,
  {
    afData: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    allAfiliados: Record<string, unknown>[];
    reservasData: Record<string, unknown>[];
    masterPassSummary: IMasterPassAuditSummary;
    validServiceIds?: string[];
    validServiceNames?: string[];
  }
>();

export const formatVenueCountLabel = (count: number, isEn = false): string => {
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.trunc(Number(count) || 0));
  if (isEn) return n === 1 ? '1 venue' : `${n} venues`;
  return n === 1 ? '1 sede' : `${n} sedes`;
};

export const isZeroKcMasterPassRow = (r: B2BRow | null | undefined): boolean => {
  if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') return false;
  if (r.es_pase_maestro || r.isMasterPass) return true;
  const precio = Number(r.precio_kc ?? r.costo_total ?? 0) || 0;
  const escrow = Number(r.monto_escrow ?? 0) || 0;
  const puerta = Number(r.monto_puerta ?? 0) || 0;
  return precio <= 0 && escrow <= 0 && puerta <= 0;
};

export function computeMasterPassWindowCounts(reservasRows: B2BRow[]): Pick<IMasterPassAuditSummary, 'hoy' | 'semana' | 'mes'> {
  const mz = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds();
  let hoy = 0;
  let semana = 0;
  let mes = 0;
  for (const r of reservasRows || []) {
    if (!isZeroKcMasterPassRow(r) || isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r)) continue;
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (st === 'cancelada' || st === 'rechazada' || st === 'eliminada') continue;
    const rawRef = r?.updated_at || r?.fecha_clase || r?.fecha_hora || r?.created_at;
    const ts = parseMazatlanTimestampMs(rawRef);
    if (Number.isNaN(ts)) continue;
    if (ts >= mz.monthStartMs && ts <= mz.monthEndMs) mes += 1;
    if (ts >= mz.startMs && ts <= mz.endMs) semana += 1;
    if (ts >= mz.startOfTodayMs && ts <= mz.endOfTodayMs) hoy += 1;
  }
  return { hoy, semana, mes };
}

export async function fetchMasterPassesAuditSummary(
  clubFiltro?: string | null,
  isAdmin = false,
  reservasFallback: B2BRow[] = [],
  hasActiveBusiness = true
): Promise<IMasterPassAuditSummary> {
  const nonSandboxFallback = (reservasFallback || []).filter((r) => !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r));
  const windowCounts = computeMasterPassWindowCounts(nonSandboxFallback);
  const canQueryMasterPassesRpc = isAdmin || (Boolean(clubFiltro && String(clubFiltro).trim()) && hasActiveBusiness);
  if (canQueryMasterPassesRpc) {
    try {
      const rpcArgs = isAdmin || !clubFiltro ? {} : { p_negocio_nombre: clubFiltro };
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_resumen_pases_maestros', rpcArgs);
      if (!error && data && typeof data === 'object') {
        const rpcData = data as B2BRow;
        return {
          total_emitidos: Math.max(0, Number(rpcData.total_emitidos) || 0),
          activos: Math.max(0, Number(rpcData.activos) || 0),
          liquidados: Math.max(0, Number(rpcData.liquidados) || 0),
          cancelados: Math.max(0, Number(rpcData.cancelados) || 0),
          hoy: Math.max(windowCounts.hoy, Number(rpcData.hoy) || 0),
          semana: Math.max(windowCounts.semana, Number(rpcData.semana) || 0),
          mes: Math.max(windowCounts.mes, Number(rpcData.mes) || 0),
        };
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  let activos = 0;
  let liquidados = 0;
  let cancelados = 0;
  for (const r of nonSandboxFallback) {
    if (!isZeroKcMasterPassRow(r)) continue;
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (st === 'completada') liquidados += 1;
    else if (st === 'cancelada') cancelados += 1;
    else if (st === 'activa' || st === 'pendiente' || st === 'confirmada') activos += 1;
  }
  return {
    total_emitidos: activos + liquidados + cancelados,
    activos,
    liquidados,
    cancelados,
    ...windowCounts,
  };
}

export function buildCeoConsolidatedWeeklyReport(
  allAfiliados: B2BRow[],
  reservasData: B2BRow[],
  periodOverride?: IB2BPeriodOverride,
  validServiceIds?: Set<string>,
  validServiceNames?: Set<string>
): IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO {
  const safeAfiliados =
    Array.isArray(allAfiliados) && allAfiliados.length > 0
      ? allAfiliados
      : Array.from(
          new Set(
            (reservasData || [])
              .map((r) => String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim())
              .filter(Boolean)
          )
        ).map((nombre, idx) => ({
          id: `af_virtual_${idx}`,
          nombre,
          disciplina: 'Fitness',
          categoria: 'Club',
          regimen_fiscal: 'sin_rfc',
        }));
  const mz = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds();
  const effectiveOverride: IB2BPeriodOverride = periodOverride || {
    startMs: mz.startMs,
    endMs: mz.endMs,
    inicioIso: mz.periodoInicio,
    finIso: mz.periodoFin,
    strictWindow: true,
  };
  return calcularCorteSemanalB2B(
    safeAfiliados,
    reservasData || [],
    effectiveOverride,
    validServiceIds,
    validServiceNames
  );
}

export async function fetchB2BDataHelper(
  userId: string,
  isAdmin: boolean,
  negocioNombre?: string,
  _userEmail?: string
) {
  let afData: B2BRow | null = null;
  let allAfiliados: B2BRow[] = [];
  const cols =
    'id, propietario_id, nombre, estado, disciplina, categoria, clabe_bancaria, whatsapp, ubicacion, portada_url, rfc, regimen_fiscal, csf_validada';

  if (isAdmin) {
    const afPageRes = await fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<B2BRow>(
      (from, to) =>
        supabase
          .from('afiliados')
          .select(cols)
          .order('nombre', { ascending: true })
          .order('id', { ascending: true })
          .range(from, to),
      { pageSize: 500 }
    );
    allAfiliados = afPageRes.rows;
    afData = allAfiliados.find((a) => String(a?.nombre || '').toLowerCase() === 'kinetic oficial') || allAfiliados[0] || null;
  } else {
    const { data: rowsByOwner, error: afError } = await supabase
      .from('afiliados')
      .select(cols)
      .eq('propietario_id', userId)
      .limit(1);
    if (afError) {
      throw new Error(`Error cargando datos del afiliado: ${afError.message}`);
    }
    afData = (Array.isArray(rowsByOwner) ? rowsByOwner[0] || null : rowsByOwner) as B2BRow | null;
    if (afData) allAfiliados = [afData];
  }

  const clubFiltro = isAdmin ? String(afData?.nombre || negocioNombre || '') : String(afData?.nombre || '');
  if (!isAdmin && (!afData || !clubFiltro)) {
    return {
      afData: null,
      allAfiliados: [],
      reservasData: [],
      masterPassSummary: { ...EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY },
    };
  }

  const b2bCacheKey = `${userId}_${isAdmin ? 'ceo_global' : clubFiltro}`;
  if (isSupabaseQuotaExceeded() && _memoryB2BData.has(b2bCacheKey)) {
    return _memoryB2BData.get(b2bCacheKey)!;
  }

  const runB2BReservasPaginated = async (colsStr: string) =>
    fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<B2BRow>(
      async (from, to) => {
        let q = supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .select(colsStr)
          .in('estado', ['completada', 'no_show', 'penalizada', 'cancelada', 'activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada'])
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
          .order('id', { ascending: true })
          .range(from, to);
        if (!isAdmin && clubFiltro) q = q.eq('negocio_nombre', clubFiltro);
        const res = await q;
        return { data: (res.data as unknown as B2BRow[] | null) || null, error: res.error };
      },
      { pageSize: 500 }
    );

  let reservasRes = await runB2BReservasPaginated(RESERVAS_B2B_COLS);
  if (reservasRes.error && /es_cuenta_sandbox/i.test(String((reservasRes.error as { message?: string })?.message || ''))) {
    reservasRes = await runB2BReservasPaginated(RESERVAS_B2B_COLS_LEGACY);
  }
  const reservasData = reservasRes.rows;
  const error = reservasRes.error as { message?: string; code?: string | number; status?: number } | null;
  if (error) {
    if (isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(error)) {
      setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
      return (
        _memoryB2BData.get(b2bCacheKey) || {
          afData,
          allAfiliados,
          reservasData: [],
          masterPassSummary: { ...EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY },
        }
      );
    }
    throw error;
  }

  const sandboxUserIds = new Set<string>();
  try {
    const sbxRes = await fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<B2BRow>(
      (from, to) =>
        supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .select('id')
          .eq('es_cuenta_sandbox', true)
          .order('id', { ascending: true })
          .range(from, to),
      { pageSize: 500 }
    );
    if (!sbxRes.error) {
      sbxRes.rows.forEach((sp) => {
        if (sp?.id) sandboxUserIds.add(String(sp.id));
      });
    }
  } catch {}

  let validServiceIds: string[] = [];
  let validServiceNames: string[] = [];
  try {
    const svcRes = await fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<B2BRow>(
      (from, to) =>
        supabase
          .from('servicios')
          .select('id, nombre_servicio, estado')
          .order('id', { ascending: true })
          .range(from, to),
      { pageSize: 500 }
    );
    if (!svcRes.error) {
      const deletedSvc = new Set(['eliminado', 'eliminada', 'borrado', 'cancelado', 'cancelada', 'rechazado']);
      for (const sv of svcRes.rows) {
        const est = String(sv?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (deletedSvc.has(est)) continue;
        if (sv?.id) validServiceIds.push(String(sv.id).trim());
        if (sv?.nombre_servicio) validServiceNames.push(String(sv.nombre_servicio).trim().toLowerCase());
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  const validSvcIdSet = validServiceIds.length > 0 ? new Set(validServiceIds) : undefined;
  const validSvcNameSet = validServiceNames.length > 0 ? new Set(validServiceNames) : undefined;

  const cleanRows = reservasData
    .map((r) => (r?.usuario_id && sandboxUserIds.has(String(r.usuario_id)) ? { ...r, es_cuenta_sandbox: true } : r))
    .filter((r) => !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r) && !isOrphanEscrowReservation(r, validSvcIdSet, validSvcNameSet));
  const hasActiveBiz = Boolean(afData?.id && String(afData?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'activo');
  const masterPassSummary = await fetchMasterPassesAuditSummary(clubFiltro, isAdmin, cleanRows, hasActiveBiz);
  const b2bSnapshot = { afData, allAfiliados, reservasData: cleanRows, masterPassSummary, validServiceIds, validServiceNames };
  _memoryB2BData.set(b2bCacheKey, b2bSnapshot);
  return b2bSnapshot;
}

export const resolveReservaB2BMonto = (r: B2BRow | null | undefined, honorNoShow40 = false): number => {
  if (!r) return 0;
  const rawCosto = Number(r?.costo_total) || 0;
  const rawPrecio = Number(r?.precio_kc) || 0;
  const rawEscrow = Number(r?.monto_escrow) || 0;
  const rawPuerta = Number(r?.monto_puerta) || 0;
  const directo = Math.max(rawCosto, rawPrecio);
  const sumaPartes = rawEscrow + rawPuerta;
  const fullTotal =
    directo > 0 && directo >= sumaPartes ? directo : sumaPartes > 0 ? sumaPartes : directo;
  if (honorNoShow40) {
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (st === 'no_show' || st === 'penalizada') {
      return rawEscrow > 0 ? rawEscrow : Math.round(fullTotal * 0.4);
    }
    if (st !== 'completada') return 0;
  }
  return fullTotal;
};

export interface IB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot {
  ts: number;
  roleMode: 'ceo' | 'b2b';
  isProfileIncomplete: boolean;
  afData: B2BRow | null;
  allAfiliados: B2BRow[];
  reservasB2B: B2BRow[];
  metricas: { hoy: number; semana: number; mes: number; total: number };
  desglosePorPeriodo: { hoy: B2BRow[]; semana: B2BRow[]; mes: B2BRow[]; total: B2BRow[] };
  masterPassSummary: IMasterPassAuditSummary;
  validServiceIds?: string[];
  validServiceNames?: string[];
}

export function buildB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot(
  res: {
    afData?: B2BRow | null;
    allAfiliados?: B2BRow[];
    reservasData?: B2BRow[];
    masterPassSummary?: IMasterPassAuditSummary;
    validServiceIds?: string[];
    validServiceNames?: string[];
  } | null | undefined,
  isAdmin: boolean
): IB2BDashboardSwrSnapshot {
  const loadedAf = res?.afData ?? null;
  const loadedAfiliados = Array.isArray(res?.allAfiliados) ? res.allAfiliados : loadedAf ? [loadedAf] : [];
  const loadedMasterPasses: IMasterPassAuditSummary = res?.masterPassSummary || { ...EMPTY_MASTER_PASS_SUMMARY };
  const reservasData = (Array.isArray(res?.reservasData) ? res.reservasData : []).filter(
    (r) => r?.id && !r?.isOptimistic && !String(r.id).startsWith('opt_')
  );
  let isProfileIncomplete = false;
  if (!isAdmin && loadedAf) {
    const missingClabe = !loadedAf.clabe_bancaria || String(loadedAf.clabe_bancaria).trim().length < 18;
    const missingWhatsapp = !loadedAf.whatsapp || String(loadedAf.whatsapp).trim().length < 8;
    isProfileIncomplete = missingClabe || missingWhatsapp;
  }
  const mz = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds();
  let hoy = 0; let semana = 0; let mes = 0; let total = 0;
  const listHoy: B2BRow[] = []; const listSemana: B2BRow[] = [];
  const listMes: B2BRow[] = []; const listTotal: B2BRow[] = [];
  for (const r of reservasData) {
    if (isMasterPass0Kc(r) || isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r)) continue;
    const pRec = (r?.perfiles as B2BRow | null | undefined) || {
      nombre: r?.atleta_nombre, email: r?.atleta_email || r?.email, rol: r?.atleta_rol,
    };
    if (isAdminOrB2BAccount(pRec as { email?: string | null; rol?: string | null; tipo_cuenta?: string | null })) continue;
    const monto = resolveReservaB2BMonto(r, true);
    const eventMs = parseMazatlanTimestampMs(r?.updated_at || r?.fecha_clase || r?.created_at || r?.fecha_hora);
    if (Number.isNaN(eventMs)) continue;
    const item = { ...r, monto_calculado_kc: monto };
    total += monto; listTotal.push(item);
    if (eventMs >= mz.monthStartMs && eventMs <= mz.monthEndMs) { mes += monto; listMes.push(item); }
    if (eventMs >= mz.startMs && eventMs <= mz.endMs) { semana += monto; listSemana.push(item); }
    if (eventMs >= mz.startOfTodayMs && eventMs <= mz.endOfTodayMs) { hoy += monto; listHoy.push(item); }
  }
  return {
    ts: Date.now(),
    roleMode: isAdmin ? 'ceo' : 'b2b',
    isProfileIncomplete,
    afData: loadedAf,
    allAfiliados: loadedAfiliados,
    reservasB2B: reservasData,
    metricas: { hoy, semana, mes, total },
    desglosePorPeriodo: { hoy: listHoy, semana: listSemana, mes: listMes, total: listTotal },
    masterPassSummary: loadedMasterPasses,
    validServiceIds: Array.isArray(res?.validServiceIds) ? res.validServiceIds.map(String) : undefined,
    validServiceNames: Array.isArray(res?.validServiceNames) ? res.validServiceNames.map(String) : undefined,
  };
}

    FILE: src/services/bovedaCancelService.ts

    // === src/services/bovedaCancelService.ts === //
import { getMaxForgivenessForLevel } from '../constants/memberships';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import {
  evaluateCancellationWindowPolicy,
  resolveMazatlanMonthKey,
  resolveMazatlanSessionInstantMs,
} from './kineticCancellationPolicyHelper';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import {
  creditWalletSafe,
  isValidUuid,
  notifyPurgedBovedaPass,
  purgeGhostPassStorageKeys,
  refundAndPurgeOrphanReserva,
} from './bovedaHelpers';
import { invalidateBovedaPassesCache } from './bovedaRealtimeSync';
import { DELETED_SERVICE_STATES } from './eventosHelpers';
import { removeCanceledReservationFromCalendar } from './kineticCalendarSyncService';
import { cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva } from './kineticTripleClassRemindersService';
import { getLocalServerClockOffsetMs, purgePassById } from './terminalOfflineService';

export interface ICancelReservationResult {
  ok: boolean;
  success?: boolean;
  refunded: boolean;
  refundAmount: number;
  usedVipForgiveness: boolean;
  remainingForgiveness: number;
  newState: 'cancelada' | 'no_show';
  message: string;
}

const _inFlightCancelIds = new Set<string>();

async function fetchServerClockMs(): Promise<number> {
  try {
    const t0 = Date.now();
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_tiempo_servidor_ms');
    if (error || typeof data !== 'number') return Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
    return data + Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 2);
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    void err;
    return Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
  }
}

export async function cancelarReservaBoveda(
  reservaId: string,
  userId: string,
  cantidadReembolso: number
): Promise<ICancelReservationResult> {
  const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
  const cleanReservaId = String(reservaId || '').trim();
  if (_inFlightCancelIds.has(cleanReservaId)) {
    throw new Error(isEn ? 'Cancellation already in progress.' : 'Cancelación en curso.');
  }
  _inFlightCancelIds.add(cleanReservaId);

  try {
    const validResUuid = isValidUuid(cleanReservaId);
    const [nowMs, resLookup, perfLookup] = await Promise.all([
      fetchServerClockMs(),
      validResUuid
        ? supabase
            .from('reservas')
            .select(
              'id, estado, costo_total, precio_kc, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, fecha_clase, hora_inicio, fecha_hora, created_at, usuario_id, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, negocio_nombre, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup'
            )
            .eq('id', cleanReservaId)
            .eq('usuario_id', userId)
            .maybeSingle()
        : Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: null }),
      userId && isValidUuid(userId)
        ? supabase
            .from('perfiles')
            .select('id, nivel, rol, perdones_mensuales_usados, mes_perdones_control')
            .eq('id', userId)
            .maybeSingle()
        : Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: null }),
    ]);

    const perf = perfLookup.data;
    const currentMonthKey = resolveMazatlanMonthKey(nowMs);
    const storedMonthKey = String(perf?.mes_perdones_control || '').trim();
    const usedForgivenessCount =
      storedMonthKey === currentMonthKey
        ? Math.max(0, Number(perf?.perdones_mensuales_usados) || 0)
        : 0;
    const maxForgiveness = getMaxForgivenessForLevel(perf?.nivel);
    const defaultRemaining = Math.max(0, maxForgiveness - usedForgivenessCount);

    const buildOrphanResult = (refundAmount = 0): ICancelReservationResult => ({
      ok: true,
      success: true,
      refunded: true,
      refundAmount,
      usedVipForgiveness: false,
      remainingForgiveness: defaultRemaining,
      newState: 'cancelada',
      message: isEn
        ? 'The orphan pass was cleanly removed from your Vault.'
        : 'El pase huérfano fue eliminado limpiamente de tu Bóveda.',
    });

    const currentRes = (resLookup.data as Record<string, unknown> | null) ?? null;
    if (validResUuid) {
      const { data: rpcCancelData, error: rpcCancelErr } = await supabase.rpc('cancelar_reserva_escrow_72h', {
        p_reserva_id: cleanReservaId,
      });
      if (!rpcCancelErr && rpcCancelData?.ok) {
        await Promise.all([purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(userId), invalidateBovedaPassesCache(userId)]);
        purgePassById(cleanReservaId).catch(() => {});
        removeCanceledReservationFromCalendar(cleanReservaId).catch(() => {});
        cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva(cleanReservaId).catch(() => {});
        const refunded = Boolean(rpcCancelData.refunded);
        const refundAmount = Math.max(0, Number(rpcCancelData.refundAmount) || 0);
        if (!currentRes || Boolean(rpcCancelData.orphanService)) {
          notifyPurgedBovedaPass(cleanReservaId, userId);
          return buildOrphanResult(refundAmount);
        }
        const usedVip = Boolean(rpcCancelData.usedVipForgiveness);
        const remForgive = Math.max(0, Number(rpcCancelData.remainingForgiveness ?? defaultRemaining) || 0);
        const newState: 'cancelada' | 'no_show' = rpcCancelData.newState === 'no_show' ? 'no_show' : 'cancelada';
        const prevSt = String(currentRes?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        const evMs = currentRes ? resolveMazatlanSessionInstantMs(currentRes, null) : NaN;
        const hrsLeft = !Number.isNaN(evMs) ? (evMs - nowMs) / 3600000 : 999;
        const heldKc = Math.max(0, Number(currentRes?.monto_escrow) || Number(cantidadReembolso) || 0);
        const msg = !currentRes
          ? buildOrphanResult(0).message
          : !refunded && ['cancelada', 'completada', 'no_show'].includes(prevSt)
            ? (isEn ? 'This reservation was already processed in your Vault.' : 'Esta reserva ya había sido procesada en tu Bóveda.')
            : refunded && refundAmount <= 0
              ? (isEn ? 'Master Pass (0 KC) released and cleanly removed from your Vault.' : 'Pase Maestro (0 KC) liberado y retirado limpiamente de tu Bóveda.')
              : refunded && usedVip
                ? (isEn
                    ? `1 VIP Forgiveness (${perf?.nivel || 'VIP'}) applied and +${refundAmount} KC refunded to your Vault (${remForgive} left this month).`
                    : `Se aplicó 1 Perdón VIP de tu rango ${perf?.nivel || 'VIP'} y se reembolsaron +${refundAmount} KC íntegros a tu Bóveda (${remForgive} perdones restantes este mes).`)
                : refunded
                  ? (isEn ? `+${refundAmount} KC (40% Protected Deposit) refunded in full to your Vault.` : `Se reembolsaron +${refundAmount} KC íntegros (Anticipo Protegido 40%) a tu Bóveda.`)
                  : hrsLeft < 4
                    ? (isEn
                        ? `Pass canceled with less than 4 hours before start. Per the 40/60 agreement, the ${heldKc} KC Protected Deposit was retained for the venue.`
                        : `La sesión fue liberada a menos de 4 horas de su inicio. Conforme a la regla de protección, el Anticipo Protegido 40% (${heldKc} KC) se aplicó para la sede.`)
                    : (isEn
                        ? `Pass canceled within 72 hours with no VIP Forgiveness available on your tier. The ${heldKc} KC Protected Deposit was retained for the venue.`
                        : `La sesión fue cancelada a menos de 72 horas sin Perdones VIP disponibles en tu rango actual. El Anticipo Protegido 40% (${heldKc} KC) se aplicó para la sede.`);
        return { ok: true, success: true, refunded, refundAmount, usedVipForgiveness: usedVip, remainingForgiveness: remForgive, newState, message: msg };
      }
      if (rpcCancelErr && rpcCancelErr.code !== 'PGRST202') throw new Error(rpcCancelErr.message);
      if (!rpcCancelErr && rpcCancelData && !rpcCancelData.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          String(
            rpcCancelData.error ||
              rpcCancelData.message ||
              (isEn ? 'Unable to cancel reservation.' : 'No se pudo cancelar la reserva.')
          )
        );
      }
    }

    if (!validResUuid || !currentRes) {
      if (validResUuid && userId) {
        try { await supabase.from('reservas').delete().eq('id', cleanReservaId).eq('usuario_id', userId); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
      }
      await Promise.all([
        purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(userId),
        invalidateBovedaPassesCache(userId),
        removeCanceledReservationFromCalendar(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
        cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
      ]);
      purgePassById(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
      notifyPurgedBovedaPass(cleanReservaId, userId);
      return buildOrphanResult(0);
    }

    const rawSid = String(currentRes.servicio_id || '').trim();
    let servRow: Record<string, unknown> | null = null;
    let servLookupOk = true;
    if (rawSid && isValidUuid(rawSid)) {
      const servReq = await supabase.from('servicios').select('id, nombre_servicio, estado, fecha, hora_inicio').eq('id', rawSid).maybeSingle();
      servLookupOk = !servReq.error;
      servRow = (servReq.data as Record<string, unknown> | null) ?? null;
    }

    const servMissingOrDeleted = !rawSid || !isValidUuid(rawSid) ||
      (servLookupOk && (!servRow || DELETED_SERVICE_STATES.has(String(servRow.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim())));
    if (servMissingOrDeleted) {
      const { refundAmount } = await refundAndPurgeOrphanReserva(currentRes, userId);
      await Promise.all([
        purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(userId),
        invalidateBovedaPassesCache(userId),
        removeCanceledReservationFromCalendar(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
        cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; }),
      ]);
      purgePassById(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
      return buildOrphanResult(refundAmount);
    }

    const currentState = String(currentRes.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (currentState === 'cancelada' || currentState === 'completada' || currentState === 'no_show') {
      await Promise.all([purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(userId), invalidateBovedaPassesCache(userId)]);
      purgePassById(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
      cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
      return {
        ok: true,
        success: true,
        refunded: false,
        refundAmount: 0,
        usedVipForgiveness: false,
        remainingForgiveness: defaultRemaining,
        newState: currentState === 'no_show' ? 'no_show' : 'cancelada',
        message: isEn ? 'This reservation was already processed in your Vault.' : 'Esta reserva ya había sido procesada en tu Bóveda.',
      };
    }

    const eventTimeMs = resolveMazatlanSessionInstantMs(currentRes, servRow);
    const createdAtMs = currentRes.created_at ? new Date(String(currentRes.created_at)).getTime() : NaN;
    const minutesSinceCreated = !Number.isNaN(createdAtMs) ? (nowMs - createdAtMs) / 60000 : 999;
    const isWithin5MinGrace = minutesSinceCreated >= 0 && minutesSinceCreated <= 5;
    const hoursUntilEvent = !Number.isNaN(eventTimeMs) ? (eventTimeMs - nowMs) / 3600000 : 999;
    const userRole = String(perf?.rol || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const isAdminUser = userRole === 'admin' || userRole === 'fundador';
    const memOrigin = Math.max(0, Number(currentRes?.escrow_membresia) || 0);
    const topupOrigin = Math.max(0, Number(currentRes?.escrow_topup) || 0);
    const montoSeguro = Math.max(0, Number(currentRes?.monto_escrow) || memOrigin + topupOrigin);
    const isZeroKcPass = montoSeguro <= 0 && Number(currentRes?.precio_kc ?? currentRes?.costo_total ?? 0) <= 0;

    const policyEval = evaluateCancellationWindowPolicy({
      hoursUntilEvent,
      minutesSinceCreated,
      vipLevel: typeof perf?.nivel === 'string' ? perf.nivel : null,
      usedForgivenessCount,
      isAdminUser,
      isZeroKcPass,
      montoEscrow: montoSeguro,
      isEn,
    });
    const shouldRefund = policyEval.shouldRefund;
    const useVipForgiveness = policyEval.usedVipForgiveness;

    const nextDbState = 'cancelada' as const;
    const targetOwnerId = String(currentRes.usuario_id || userId);
    let rowHandled = false;

    try {
      const payloadFull: Record<string, unknown> = { estado: nextDbState };
      if (useVipForgiveness) payloadFull.perdon_vip_aplicado = true;
      if (shouldRefund) payloadFull.monto_escrow = 0;
      const { data: updatedRows, error: updateErr } = await supabase
        .from('reservas')
        .update(payloadFull)
        .eq('id', cleanReservaId)
        .eq('usuario_id', userId)
        .in('estado', ['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'])
        .select('id');
      if (!updateErr && Array.isArray(updatedRows) && updatedRows.length > 0) rowHandled = true;
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }

    if (!rowHandled) {
      try {
        const { data: minimalRows, error: minimalErr } = await supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .update({ estado: nextDbState })
          .eq('id', cleanReservaId)
          .eq('usuario_id', userId)
          .in('estado', ['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'])
          .select('id');
        if (!minimalErr && Array.isArray(minimalRows) && minimalRows.length > 0) rowHandled = true;
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    }

    if (!rowHandled) {
      try {
        const { data: deletedRows, error: delErr } = await supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .delete()
          .eq('id', cleanReservaId)
          .eq('usuario_id', userId)
          .in('estado', ['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'])
          .select('id');
        if (!delErr && Array.isArray(deletedRows) && deletedRows.length > 0) rowHandled = true;
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    }

    await Promise.all([purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(userId), invalidateBovedaPassesCache(userId)]);
    purgePassById(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
    removeCanceledReservationFromCalendar(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
    cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva(cleanReservaId).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });

    if (isZeroKcPass) {
      return {
        ok: true,
        success: true,
        refunded: true,
        refundAmount: 0,
        usedVipForgiveness: false,
        remainingForgiveness: defaultRemaining,
        newState: nextDbState,
        message: isEn ? 'Master Pass (0 KC) released and cleanly removed from your Vault.' : 'Pase Maestro (0 KC) liberado y retirado limpiamente de tu Bóveda.',
      };
    }

    if (shouldRefund && rowHandled) {
      if (memOrigin > 0 || topupOrigin > 0) {
        if (memOrigin > 0) await creditWalletSafe(targetOwnerId, memOrigin, 'membresia');
        if (topupOrigin > 0) await creditWalletSafe(targetOwnerId, topupOrigin, 'topup');
      } else if (montoSeguro > 0) {
        await creditWalletSafe(targetOwnerId, montoSeguro, 'membresia');
      }

      if (useVipForgiveness) {
        const nextUsed = usedForgivenessCount + 1;
        try {
          await supabase
            .from('perfiles')
            .update({ perdones_mensuales_usados: nextUsed, mes_perdones_control: currentMonthKey })
            .eq('id', targetOwnerId);
        } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
        const remaining = Math.max(0, maxForgiveness - nextUsed);
        return {
          ok: true,
          success: true,
          refunded: true,
          refundAmount: montoSeguro,
          usedVipForgiveness: true,
          remainingForgiveness: remaining,
          newState: nextDbState,
          message: isEn
            ? `1 VIP Forgiveness (${perf?.nivel || 'VIP'}) applied and +${montoSeguro} KC refunded to your Vault (${remaining} left this month).`
            : `Se aplicó 1 Perdón VIP de tu rango ${perf?.nivel || 'VIP'} y se reembolsaron +${montoSeguro} KC íntegros a tu Bóveda (${remaining} perdones restantes este mes).`,
        };
      }

      return {
        ok: true,
        success: true,
        refunded: true,
        refundAmount: montoSeguro,
        usedVipForgiveness: false,
        remainingForgiveness: defaultRemaining,
        newState: nextDbState,
        message: isEn
          ? `+${montoSeguro} KC (40% Protected Deposit) refunded in full to your Vault.`
          : `Se reembolsaron +${montoSeguro} KC íntegros (Anticipo Protegido 40%) a tu Bóveda.`,
      };
    }

    return {
      ok: true,
      success: true,
      refunded: false,
      refundAmount: 0,
      usedVipForgiveness: false,
      remainingForgiveness: policyEval.remainingForgiveness,
      newState: nextDbState,
      message: policyEval.resultMessage,
    };
  } finally {
    _inFlightCancelIds.delete(cleanReservaId);
  }
}

export const cancelarReservaConRegla72hBoveda = (
  reservaId: string,
  userId: string,
  cantidadReembolso: number
): Promise<ICancelReservationResult> => cancelarReservaBoveda(reservaId, userId, cantidadReembolso);

    FILE: src/services/bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers.ts

    // === src/services/bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers.ts === //
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import {
  isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error,
  setSupabaseQuotaExceeded,
  supabase,
} from '../lib/supabase';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { exportarPDFReciboReservaAtleta } from './adminPdfTemplates';
import { fetchB2BDataHelper } from './bovedaB2BDataHelper';
import {
  RESERVAS_BOVEDA_COLS,
  executeBovedaMutation,
  fetchOwnReservaQrSeed,
  formatBovedaPassLocalDate,
  refundAndPurgeOrphanReserva,
} from './bovedaHelpers';
import {
  DELETED_SERVICE_STATES,
  hasReservaBeenProcessed,
  isValidUuid,
  purgeGhostPassStorageKeys,
} from './eventosHelpers';
import {
  isMissingColumnPostgrestError,
  resolveAffiliateWriteOnceGeoPatch,
} from './kineticCloudAiAndGeoService';
import {
  isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn,
  reconcileExpiredNoShowPassesSilent,
} from './kineticNoShowEngine';
import { cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva, reconcileReservasTripleReminders } from './kineticTripleClassRemindersService';
import {
  extractSpotNumberFromReserva,
  normalizeLocalIsoDate,
} from './spotCollisionService';

type DynamicRow = Record<string, unknown>;

const extractNonZeroTime = (val?: unknown): string | null => {
  const m = String(val || '').match(/T(\d{2}:\d{2})/);
  return m && m[1] !== '00:00' ? m[1] : null;
};

export const bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers = {
  fetchB2BData: fetchB2BDataHelper,

  async fetchReservasAtleta(userId: string): Promise<DynamicRow[]> {
    await purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(userId);
    if (!userId) return [];
    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.onLine === false) {
      throw new Error('OFFLINE_RESERVAS_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    void reconcileExpiredNoShowPassesSilent();

    const resReq = await supabase
      .from('reservas')
      .select(RESERVAS_BOVEDA_COLS)
      .eq('usuario_id', userId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(60);

    if (resReq?.error) {
      if (isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(resReq.error)) setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
      throw new Error(resReq.error.message || 'TRANSIENT_RESERVAS_ERROR');
    }
    const reservas: DynamicRow[] = Array.isArray(resReq.data)
      ? (resReq.data as DynamicRow[])
      : [];
    if (reservas.length === 0) return [];

    const validServiceIds = Array.from(
      new Set(
        reservas
          .map((r) => String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim())
          .filter((id: string) => isValidUuid(id))
      )
    );
    const serviceNames = Array.from(
      new Set(
        reservas
          .flatMap((r) => [r?.nombre_servicio, r?.servicio, r?.titulo_clase])
          .map((n) => String(n || '').trim())
          .filter((name: string) => name.length > 0)
      )
    );
    const hasNullServiceActive = reservas.some((r) => {
      const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      return (st === 'pendiente' || st === 'activa' || st === 'confirmada') && !r?.servicio_id;
    });

    const servCols =
      'id, nombre_servicio, categoria, estado, fecha, hora_inicio, duracion_minutos, instructor_nombre, ubicacion, precio_kc';
    const [servByIdReq, servByNameReq] = await Promise.all([
      validServiceIds.length > 0
        ? supabase.from('servicios').select(servCols).in('id', validServiceIds)
        : Promise.resolve({ data: [] as DynamicRow[], error: null as Error | null }),
      hasNullServiceActive
        ? supabase.from('servicios').select(servCols).limit(300)
        : serviceNames.length > 0
          ? supabase.from('servicios').select(servCols).in('nombre_servicio', serviceNames)
          : Promise.resolve({ data: [] as DynamicRow[], error: null as Error | null }),
    ]).catch(() => [
      { data: [] as DynamicRow[], error: new Error('network') },
      { data: [] as DynamicRow[], error: new Error('network') },
    ]);

    const canVerifyOrphans = !servByIdReq.error && !servByNameReq.error;
    const servicios: DynamicRow[] = [
      ...((servByIdReq.data as DynamicRow[] | null) || []),
      ...((servByNameReq.data as DynamicRow[] | null) || []),
    ];
    const servById = new Map<string, DynamicRow>();
    const servByName = new Map<string, DynamicRow>();
    servicios.forEach((s) => {
      const sState = String(s?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (DELETED_SERVICE_STATES.has(sState)) return;
      if (s?.id) servById.set(String(s.id).trim(), s);
      [s?.nombre_servicio, s?.titulo, s?.nombre].forEach((rawName) => {
        const norm = String(rawName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (norm) servByName.set(norm, s);
      });
    });

    const orphansToPurge: DynamicRow[] = [];
    const survivingReservas = reservas.filter((r) => {
      const rid = String(r?.id || '').trim();
      if (!rid || r?.isOptimistic || hasReservaBeenProcessed(rid)) return false;
      const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const isActive = st === 'pendiente' || st === 'activa' || st === 'confirmada';
      if (!isActive || !canVerifyOrphans) return true;

      const rawSid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
      const existsInServicios = rawSid
        ? isValidUuid(rawSid) && servById.has(rawSid)
        : [r?.nombre_servicio, r?.servicio, r?.titulo_clase]
            .map((v) => String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase())
            .filter(Boolean)
            .some((nm) => servByName.has(nm));

      if (!existsInServicios) {
        orphansToPurge.push(r);
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    });

    if (orphansToPurge.length > 0) {
      await Promise.all(orphansToPurge.map(async (orp) => {
        const oid = String(orp?.id || '').trim();
        if (oid) await cancelLocalTripleRemindersForReserva(oid);
        return refundAndPurgeOrphanReserva(orp, userId);
      }));
    }

    const qrTokenByReservaId = new Map<string, string>();
    const activeMissingIds = survivingReservas
      .filter((r) => {
        const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        return ['activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada'].includes(st) && !r?.qr_token && isValidUuid(String(r?.id || ''));
      })
      .map((r) => String(r.id).trim());
    if (activeMissingIds.length > 0) {
      try {
        const { data: rpcTokens, error: rpcTokErr } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_mis_qr_tokens_activos');
        if (!rpcTokErr && Array.isArray(rpcTokens)) {
          (rpcTokens as DynamicRow[]).forEach((row) => {
            if (row?.id && row?.qr_token) qrTokenByReservaId.set(String(row.id), String(row.qr_token));
          });
        }
        const stillMissing = activeMissingIds.filter((id) => !qrTokenByReservaId.has(id)).slice(0, 12);
        if (stillMissing.length > 0) {
          await Promise.all(
            stillMissing.map(async (rid) => {
              const seed = await fetchOwnReservaQrSeed(rid);
              if (seed) qrTokenByReservaId.set(rid, seed);
            })
          );
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    }

    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    const enrichedList = survivingReservas.map((r) => {
      const servIdKey = r?.servicio_id ? String(r.servicio_id).trim() : '';
      const servNameKey = String(r?.nombre_servicio || r?.servicio || r?.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const matchedServ: DynamicRow | null =
        (servIdKey ? servById.get(servIdKey) : undefined) ||
        (servNameKey ? servByName.get(servNameKey) : undefined) ||
        null;
      const exactLocalYmd = normalizeLocalIsoDate(
        String(r?.fecha_clase || r?.fecha_programada || r?.fecha || matchedServ?.fecha || r?.fecha_hora || r?.created_at || '')
      );
      const exactHora = String(
        r?.hora_inicio || r?.hora || matchedServ?.hora_inicio ||
          extractNonZeroTime(r?.fecha_programada) || extractNonZeroTime(r?.fecha_hora) || '08:00'
      ).substring(0, 5);
      const fechaProg = `${exactLocalYmd}T${exactHora}:00`;
      const precioReal =
        r?.precio_kc !== null && r?.precio_kc !== undefined
          ? Number(r.precio_kc)
          : Number(r?.costo_total ?? matchedServ?.precio_kc ?? 0);
      const enrichedPass: DynamicRow = {
        ...r,
        qr_token: r?.qr_token || qrTokenByReservaId.get(String(r?.id || '')) || undefined,
        categoria: r?.categoria || matchedServ?.categoria || 'Fuerza',
        fecha_clase: exactLocalYmd,
        fecha: exactLocalYmd,
        hora: exactHora,
        hora_inicio: exactHora,
        fecha_programada: fechaProg,
        fecha_display: formatBovedaPassLocalDate(exactLocalYmd, isEn ? 'en' : 'es'),
        spot_number: r?.spot_number ?? extractSpotNumberFromReserva(r) ?? null,
        es_pase_maestro: Boolean(r?.es_pase_maestro),
        duracion_minutos: r?.duracion_minutos ?? matchedServ?.duracion_minutos ?? 60,
        instructor_nombre: r?.instructor_nombre || matchedServ?.instructor_nombre || (isEn ? 'Certified Coach' : 'Coach Certificado'),
        ubicacion: r?.ubicacion || matchedServ?.ubicacion || (isEn ? 'Official Kinetic Venue' : 'Sede Oficial Kinetic'),
        precio_kc: Math.max(0, precioReal || 0),
      };
      if (isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(enrichedPass, matchedServ)) {
        return { ...enrichedPass, estado: 'no_show', penalizacion_aplicada: true };
      }
      return enrichedPass;
    });
    void reconcileReservasTripleReminders(enrichedList, isEn ? 'en' : 'es');
    return enrichedList;
  },

  async copiarAlPortapapelesSilencioso(texto: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const cleanText = String(texto || '').trim();
    if (!cleanText) return false;
    try {
      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(cleanText);
        return true;
      }
      try {
        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
        const ExpoClipboard = require('expo-clipboard') as { setStringAsync?: (value: string) => Promise<void> };
        if (ExpoClipboard?.setStringAsync) {
          await ExpoClipboard.setStringAsync(cleanText);
          return true;
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
      try {
        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
        const RN = require('react-native') as { Clipboard?: { setString?: (value: string) => void } };
        if (RN?.Clipboard?.setString) {
          RN.Clipboard.setString(cleanText);
          return true;
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
      return true;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      void err;
      return false;
    }
  },

  async exportarReciboReservaPDF(reserva: DynamicRow | null | undefined, athleteName?: string) {
    if (!reserva) {
      throw new Error('No se encontró la información del recibo para exportar.');
    }
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    return exportarPDFReciboReservaAtleta(
      reserva,
      athleteName || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Athlete' : 'Atleta Kinetic')
    );
  },

  async fetchSettingsB2B(userId: string) {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('afiliados')
      .select('whatsapp, clabe_bancaria, ubicacion, portada_url, rfc, regimen_fiscal')
      .eq('propietario_id', userId)
      .limit(1);
    if (error) throw error;
    return Array.isArray(data) ? data[0] || null : data;
  },

  async updateAvatar(userId: string, base64Img: string, isB2B?: boolean) {
    await executeBovedaMutation(
      supabase.from('perfiles').update({ avatar_url: base64Img }).eq('id', userId),
      'Error actualizando avatar'
    );
    if (isB2B) {
      await executeBovedaMutation(
        supabase.from('afiliados').update({ avatar_url: base64Img }).eq('propietario_id', userId),
        'Error sincronizando logotipo de la marca'
      );
    }
    return true;
  },

  async updatePortada(userId: string, base64Img: string) {
    return executeBovedaMutation(
      supabase.from('afiliados').update({ portada_url: base64Img }).eq('propietario_id', userId),
      'Error actualizando portada'
    );
  },

  async saveSettingsB2B(
    userId: string,
    whatsapp: string,
    clabe: string,
    ubicacion: string,
    rfc?: string,
    regimenFiscal?: string
  ) {
    const cleanClabe = String(clabe || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 18);
    const updatePayload: Record<string, string> = {
      whatsapp: String(whatsapp || '').trim(),
      clabe_bancaria: cleanClabe,
      ubicacion: String(ubicacion || '').trim(),
    };
    if (rfc !== undefined) updatePayload.rfc = String(rfc || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (regimenFiscal !== undefined) updatePayload.regimen_fiscal = String(regimenFiscal || '').trim();
    const geoPatch = await resolveAffiliateWriteOnceGeoPatch({ ownerUserId: userId, ubicacion: String(updatePayload.ubicacion || '') });
    const fullUpdate = Object.keys(geoPatch).length > 0 ? { ...updatePayload, ...geoPatch } : updatePayload;
    try {
      return await executeBovedaMutation(supabase.from('afiliados').update(fullUpdate).eq('propietario_id', userId), 'Error guardando parámetros operativos B2B');
    } catch (err) {
      if (fullUpdate === updatePayload || !isMissingColumnPostgrestError(err)) throw err;
      return executeBovedaMutation(supabase.from('afiliados').update(updatePayload).eq('propietario_id', userId), 'Error guardando parámetros operativos B2B');
    }
  },

  async updateParametrosOperativos(userId: string, clabe: string, whatsapp: string, ubicacion: string) {
    return bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers.saveSettingsB2B(userId, whatsapp, clabe, ubicacion);
  },
};

export const formatBovedaPassLocalDate = (rawDate?: string | Date | null, lang?: string): string => {
  const s = typeof rawDate === 'string' ? rawDate.trim().split('T')[0].split(' ')[0] : '';
  const [y, m, d] = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s) ? s.split('-').map(Number) : [0, 0, 0];
  const dt = y && m && d ? new Date(y, m - 1, d, 12, 0, 0) : parseIsoLocal(rawDate, 12, 0);
  if (!dt) return String(rawDate || '');
  const isEn = String(lang || getActiveKineticLanguage() || 'es').toLowerCase().startsWith('en');
  return dt.toLocaleDateString(isEn ? 'en-US' : 'es-MX', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
};
