export const fetchOwnReservaQrSeed = async (reservaId: string): Promise<string | null> => {
  const cleanId = String(reservaId || '').trim();
  if (!isValidUuid(cleanId)) return null;
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_semilla_qr_reserva_propia', {
      p_reserva_id: cleanId,
    });
    if (error || typeof data !== 'string') return null;
    const cleanTok = data.trim();
    return cleanTok.length > 0 ? cleanTok : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

const _pendingProfileRefreshUsers = new Set<string>();
const _purgedPassListeners = new Set<(reservaId: string, userId: string) => void>();

export const subscribePurgedBovedaPass = (
  cb: (reservaId: string, userId: string) => void
): (() => void) => {
  _purgedPassListeners.add(cb);
  return () => {
    _purgedPassListeners.delete(cb);
  };
};

export const notifyPurgedBovedaPass = (reservaId: string, userId: string): void => {
  const cleanId = String(reservaId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return;
  markReservaProcessed(cleanId);
  if (userId) removeBovedaOptimisticPass(userId, cleanId);
  _purgedPassListeners.forEach((cb) => {
    try {
      cb(cleanId, userId);
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
  });
};

export const consumeOrphanRefundFlag = (userId?: string): boolean => {
  if (!userId || !_pendingProfileRefreshUsers.has(userId)) return false;
  _pendingProfileRefreshUsers.delete(userId);
  return true;
};

export const refundAndPurgeOrphanReserva = async (
  r: BovedaRecord,
  fallbackUserId: string
): Promise<{ refunded: boolean; refundAmount: number }> => {
  const resId = String(r?.id || '').trim();
  const ownerId = String(r?.usuario_id || fallbackUserId || '').trim();
  if (!resId || !isValidUuid(resId)) {
    if (resId) notifyPurgedBovedaPass(resId, ownerId);
    return { refunded: false, refundAmount: 0 };
  }
  const outcome = await claimRefundAndDeleteOrphanRow(r, ownerId, 0);
  if (outcome.refunded && ownerId) _pendingProfileRefreshUsers.add(ownerId);
  notifyPurgedBovedaPass(resId, ownerId);
  return outcome;
};

export const executeBovedaMutation = async <T = unknown>(
  query: PromiseLike<{ data?: T | null; error?: { message?: string } | null }>,
  errorPrefix = 'Fricción'
): Promise<T | boolean> => {
  const { data, error } = await query;
  if (error) throw new Error(`${errorPrefix}: ${error.message || 'Error desconocido'}`);
  return data || true;
};

export const getVipLevelWeight = (level?: string): number => {
  const l = String(level || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (l.includes('leyenda') || l.includes('black') || l.includes('élite') || l.includes('elite')) return 5;
  if (l.includes('platinum') || l.includes('platino') || l.includes('pro')) return 4;
  if (l.includes('gold') || l.includes('oro')) return 3;
  if (l.includes('silver') || l.includes('plata')) return 2;
  if (l.includes('bronze') || l.includes('bronce')) return 1;
  return 0;
};

export const partitionAthleteReservas = <T extends BovedaRecord>(
  reservas: T[],
  getCostoTotal: (r: T) => number
) => {
  const active: T[] = [];
  const history: T[] = [];
  let lockedKc = 0;
  let completed = 0;
  let canceled = 0;
  for (const r of reservas) {
    const rid = String(r?.id || '').trim();
    if (!rid || r?.isOptimistic || rid.startsWith('opt_') || hasReservaBeenProcessed(rid)) {
      continue;
    }
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const isPendingState = st === 'pendiente' || st === 'activa' || st === 'confirmada';
    if (isPendingState && !isPassExpiredWithoutCheckIn(r)) {
      active.push(r);
      const rawEscrow =
        r?.monto_escrow !== null && r?.monto_escrow !== undefined
          ? Number(r.monto_escrow)
          : Math.round(getCostoTotal(r) * 0.4);
      lockedKc += Math.max(0, rawEscrow || 0);
    } else {
      const normalizedItem: T = isPendingState
        ? { ...r, estado: 'no_show', penalizacion_aplicada: true }
        : r;
      history.push(normalizedItem);
      const effSt = String(normalizedItem.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (effSt === 'completada') completed += 1;
      if (effSt === 'cancelada' || effSt === 'no_show' || effSt === 'expirada' || effSt === 'penalizada') {
        canceled += 1;
      }
    }
  }
  return {
    activeReservations: active,
    historyReservations: history,
    completedCount: completed,
    canceledCount: canceled,
    totalEscrowLockedKc: lockedKc,
  };
};

export const evaluateQrWindowStatus = (reserva: BovedaRecord, nowSynced: number, isEn: boolean) => {
  let withinWindow = true;
  let message = isEn ? 'Dynamic key active for reception' : 'Llave dinámica activa para recepción';
  const eventTimeMs = resolveMazatlanSessionInstantMs(reserva);
  if (!Number.isNaN(eventTimeMs)) {
    const diffMinutes = (eventTimeMs - nowSynced) / 60000;
    if (diffMinutes > 60) {
      withinWindow = false;
      message = isEn
        ? 'Code unlocks 1 hour before session start.'
        : 'El código se habilitará 1 hora antes del inicio del evento.';
    } else if (diffMinutes < -180) {
      withinWindow = false;
      message = isEn
        ? 'Check-in window for this session has closed.'
        : 'La ventana de check-in de esta sesión ha concluido.';
    }
  }
  const qrIdentifier = String(reserva?.qr_token || reserva?.id || '').trim();
  const totpBucket = Math.floor(nowSynced / 60_000);
  const qrNonce = sha256HexSync(`${qrIdentifier}:${totpBucket}`).slice(0, 12);
  const secondsRemaining = Math.max(1, 60 - Math.floor((nowSynced % 60_000) / 1000));
  return { withinWindow, message, qrPayload: `KINETIC_PASS|${qrIdentifier}|${totpBucket}|${qrNonce}`, secondsRemaining };
};

    FILE: src/services/bovedaRatingService.ts

    // === src/services/bovedaRatingService.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';

export const RATED_RESERVATIONS_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_rated_reservations_v25';
const OFFLINE_RESERVAS_PREFIX = '@kinetic_boveda_offline_res_v25_';
const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

export interface IRatedReservaEntry {
  stars: number;
  comment: string;
  updatedAt: number;
}

type RatingListener = (reservaId: string, stars: number, comment: string) => void;
const _memoryRatedMap: Record<string, IRatedReservaEntry> = {};
let _hydratedOnce = false;
const _ratingListeners = new Set<RatingListener>();

export const subscribeRatingUpdates = (cb: RatingListener): (() => void) => {
  _ratingListeners.add(cb);
  return () => { _ratingListeners.delete(cb); };
};

export const getRatedEntrySync = (reservaId?: string | null): IRatedReservaEntry | null => {
  const cleanId = String(reservaId || '').trim();
  return cleanId && _memoryRatedMap[cleanId] ? _memoryRatedMap[cleanId] : null;
};

export const hydrateLocalRatingsMap = async (): Promise<Record<string, IRatedReservaEntry>> => {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(RATED_RESERVATIONS_STORAGE_KEY);
    const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
      Object.entries(parsed).forEach(([k, v]: [string, any]) => {
        const st = Number(v?.stars);
        if (k && st >= 1 && st <= 5) {
          _memoryRatedMap[k] = {
            stars: Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Math.round(st))),
            comment: String(v?.comment || '').trim(),
            updatedAt: Number(v?.updatedAt) || Date.now(),
          };
        }
      });
    }
    _hydratedOnce = true;
  } catch {}
  return { ..._memoryRatedMap };
};

export const resolveReservaRating = (reserva: any, cacheMap?: Record<string, IRatedReservaEntry>) => {
  const rid = String(reserva?.id || '').trim();
  const local = (rid && cacheMap?.[rid]) || getRatedEntrySync(rid);
  const rawStars = Number(local?.stars ?? reserva?.calificacion_atleta ?? reserva?.calificacion_estrellas ?? reserva?.calificacion ?? 0);
  const stars = rawStars >= 1 && rawStars <= 5 ? Math.round(rawStars) : null;
  const comment = String(local?.comment ?? reserva?.comentario_atleta ?? reserva?.comentario_resena ?? reserva?.comentario ?? '').trim();
  return { stars, comment };
};

export const mergeLocalRatingsIntoReservas = async (reservas: any[]): Promise<any[]> => {
  if (!Array.isArray(reservas) || reservas.length === 0) return [];
  const map = _hydratedOnce ? _memoryRatedMap : await hydrateLocalRatingsMap();
  return reservas.map((r) => {
    const { stars, comment } = resolveReservaRating(r, map);
    return stars ? { ...r, calificacion_atleta: stars, comentario_atleta: comment } : r;
  });
};

export const parseLocalClassDate = (rawDate?: unknown): Date | null => {
  if (!rawDate) return null;
  if (rawDate instanceof Date) return Number.isNaN(rawDate.getTime()) ? null : rawDate;
  const str = String(rawDate).trim();
  if (!str) return null;
  const ymd = str.split('T')[0].split(' ')[0];
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(ymd)) {
    const isRealUtcTs = str.includes('T') && /([zZ]|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/.test(str) && !/T00:00(:00(\.000)?)?([zZ]|[+-]00:?00)$/.test(str);
    if (isRealUtcTs) {
      const dt = new Date(str);
      return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : new Date(dt.getFullYear(), dt.getMonth(), dt.getDate(), 12, 0, 0);
    }
    const [y, m, d] = ymd.split('-').map(Number);
    if (y && m && d) return new Date(y, m - 1, d, 12, 0, 0);
  }
  const fallback = new Date(str);
  return Number.isNaN(fallback.getTime()) ? null : fallback;
};

export const formatLocalReservaDate = (input: any, lang?: string, includeTime = false): string => {
  const isObj = input && typeof input === 'object' && !(input instanceof Date);
  const rawDate = isObj ? (input.fecha_clase || input.fecha || input.fecha_programada || input.fecha_hora || input.created_at) : input;
  const dt = parseLocalClassDate(rawDate);
  if (!dt) return String(rawDate || '');
  const isEn = String(lang || getActiveKineticLanguage() || 'es').toLowerCase().startsWith('en');
  const formatted = dt.toLocaleDateString(isEn ? 'en-US' : 'es-MX', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
  if (includeTime && isObj) {
    const rawHora = String(input.hora_inicio || input.hora || '').trim().slice(0, 5);
    if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(rawHora)) return `${formatted} • ${rawHora}`;
  }
  return formatted;
};

export const localizeVipTierName = (rawTier?: string | null, isEn = false): string => {
  const clean = String(rawTier || '').trim();
  const low = clean.toLowerCase();
  if (!clean || low === 'acceso base' || low === 'base access' || low === 'ninguno') return isEn ? 'Base Access' : 'Acceso Base';
  if (low === 'bronze' || low === 'bronce') return isEn ? 'Bronze' : 'Bronce';
  if (low === 'silver' || low === 'plata') return isEn ? 'Silver' : 'Plata';
  if (low === 'gold' || low === 'oro') return isEn ? 'Gold' : 'Oro';
  if (low === 'platinum' || low === 'platino') return isEn ? 'Platinum' : 'Platino';
  if (low === 'pro') return 'Pro';
  if (low === 'black' || low === 'élite' || low === 'elite' || low === 'black/élite') return isEn ? 'Black / Elite' : 'Black / Élite';
  if (low === 'leyenda' || low === 'legend') return isEn ? 'Legend' : 'Leyenda';
  return clean;
};

export const persistOfflineReservasAtleta = async (userId: string, rows: any[]): Promise<void> => {
  if (!userId || !Array.isArray(rows)) return;
  try { await AsyncStorage.setItem(`${OFFLINE_RESERVAS_PREFIX}${userId}`, JSON.stringify(rows.slice(0, 40))); } catch {}
};

export const hydrateOfflineReservasAtleta = async (userId: string): Promise<any[]> => {
  if (!userId) return [];
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(`${OFFLINE_RESERVAS_PREFIX}${userId}`);
    const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
  } catch { return []; }
};

export const calificarReservaAtleta = async (reserva: any, stars: number, comment?: string) => {
  const reservaId = String(reserva?.id || '').trim();
  const cleanStars = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(stars) || 5)));
  const cleanComment = String(comment || '').trim().slice(0, 240);

  if (reservaId) {
    _memoryRatedMap[reservaId] = { stars: cleanStars, comment: cleanComment, updatedAt: Date.now() };
    try {
      const existing = await hydrateLocalRatingsMap();
      existing[reservaId] = { stars: cleanStars, comment: cleanComment, updatedAt: Date.now() };
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(RATED_RESERVATIONS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(existing));
    } catch {}
    _ratingListeners.forEach((cb) => { try { cb(reservaId, cleanStars, cleanComment); } catch {} });
  }

  let clubRating: number | null = null;
  if (reservaId && UUID_RE.test(reservaId)) {
    try {
      const { error: fullErr } = await supabase.from('reservas').update({ calificacion_atleta: cleanStars, comentario_atleta: cleanComment }).eq('id', reservaId);
      if (fullErr) await supabase.from('reservas').update({ calificacion_atleta: cleanStars }).eq('id', reservaId);
    } catch {}
  }

  try {
    const candidateAfId = String(reserva?.afiliado_id || reserva?.negocio_id || '').trim();
    const clubName = String(reserva?.negocio_nombre || '').trim();
    let targetAfId = UUID_RE.test(candidateAfId) ? candidateAfId : '';
    let resolvedClubName = clubName;

    if (targetAfId) {
      const { data: afById } = await supabase.from('afiliados').select('id, nombre').eq('id', targetAfId).maybeSingle();
      if (afById?.id) { targetAfId = String(afById.id); resolvedClubName = String(afById.nombre || clubName).trim(); } else targetAfId = '';
    }
    if (!targetAfId && clubName) {
      const { data: afByName } = await supabase.from('afiliados').select('id, nombre').ilike('nombre', clubName).limit(1);
      const afRow = Array.isArray(afByName) ? afByName[0] : afByName;
      if (afRow?.id) { targetAfId = String(afRow.id); resolvedClubName = String(afRow.nombre || clubName).trim(); }
    }

    let ratingsRows: any[] = [];
    if (resolvedClubName) {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('reservas').select('id, calificacion_atleta').eq('negocio_nombre', resolvedClubName).not('calificacion_atleta', 'is', null).limit(300);
      if (Array.isArray(data)) ratingsRows = data;
    } else if (UUID_RE.test(candidateAfId)) {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('reservas').select('id, calificacion_atleta').eq('negocio_id', candidateAfId).not('calificacion_atleta', 'is', null).limit(300);
      if (Array.isArray(data)) ratingsRows = data;
    }

    const scoreMap = new Map<string, number>();
    ratingsRows.forEach((r: any) => { const v = Number(r?.calificacion_atleta); if (r?.id && v >= 1 && v <= 5) scoreMap.set(String(r.id), v); });
    if (reservaId) scoreMap.set(reservaId, cleanStars);
    const scores = Array.from(scoreMap.values());
    if (scores.length > 0) {
      clubRating = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Number((scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scores.length).toFixed(2))));
      if (targetAfId) await supabase.from('afiliados').update({ rating: clubRating }).eq('id', targetAfId);
      else if (resolvedClubName) await supabase.from('afiliados').update({ rating: clubRating }).ilike('nombre', resolvedClubName);
    }

    const servicioId = String(reserva?.servicio_id || '').trim();
    if (UUID_RE.test(servicioId)) {
      const { data: srvRatings } = await supabase.from('reservas').select('id, calificacion_atleta').eq('servicio_id', servicioId).not('calificacion_atleta', 'is', null).limit(200);
      const srvMap = new Map<string, number>();
      (srvRatings || []).forEach((r: any) => { const v = Number(r?.calificacion_atleta); if (r?.id && v >= 1 && v <= 5) srvMap.set(String(r.id), v); });
      if (reservaId) srvMap.set(reservaId, cleanStars);
      const srvVals = Array.from(srvMap.values());
      if (srvVals.length > 0) {
        const srvAvg = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Number((srvVals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / srvVals.length).toFixed(2))));
        await supabase.from('servicios').update({ rating: srvAvg }).eq('id', servicioId);
      }
    }
  } catch {}

  return { ok: true, reservaId, stars: cleanStars, comment: cleanComment, clubRating };
};

    // === src/services/bovedaRealtimeSync.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { AppState, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

export const BOVEDA_PASSES_CACHE_KEY = '@kinetic_boveda_passes_cache_v1';

const INACTIVE_PASS_STATES = new Set([
  'cancelada',
  'cancelado',
  'no_show',
  'completada',
  'completado',
  'finalizada',
  'expirada',
  'eliminada',
]);

export type RealtimePassRow = Record<string, unknown> & {
  id?: string | number | null;
  usuario_id?: string | null;
  servicio_id?: string | number | null;
  nombre_servicio?: string | null;
  servicio?: string | null;
  titulo?: string | null;
  fecha?: string | null;
  fecha_clase?: string | null;
  fecha_hora?: string | null;
  fecha_seleccionada?: string | null;
  fecha_proyectada?: string | null;
  estado?: string | null;
};

const _memoryActiveReservasByUser = new Map<string, RealtimePassRow[]>();
const _memoryActiveKeysByUser = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
const _snapshotListeners = new Set<() => void>();

const normalizePassTitle = (raw: unknown): string =>
  String(raw ?? '')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

const MAZATLAN_TZ_OFFSET_HOURS = -7;

const extractIsoDate = (raw: unknown): string => {
  const str = String(raw ?? '').trim();
  if (!str) return '';
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return str;
  if (str.includes('T') && (str.endsWith('Z') || /[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2}$/.test(str))) {
    const ms = Date.parse(str);
    if (Number.isFinite(ms)) {
      const mz = new Date(ms + MAZATLAN_TZ_OFFSET_HOURS * 3600_000);
      return `${mz.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(mz.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(mz.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
  }
  const s = str.split('T')[0].split(' ')[0].trim();
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s) ? s : '';
};

const emitPassesSnapshotChanged = (): void => {
  _snapshotListeners.forEach((fn) => {
    try { fn(); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
  });
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.dispatchEvent === 'function') {
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kinetic:boveda-updated')); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
  }
};

export const extractReservationMatchKeys = (res: RealtimePassRow | null | undefined): string[] => {
  if (!res || typeof res !== 'object') return [];
  const estado = String(res.estado || 'confirmada').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (INACTIVE_PASS_STATES.has(estado)) return [];
  const keys: string[] = [];
  const sid = String(res.servicio_id || '').trim();
  const title = normalizePassTitle(res.nombre_servicio || res.servicio || res.titulo);
  const iso = extractIsoDate(res.fecha_clase || res.fecha || res.fecha_hora);
  if (!iso) return keys;
  if (sid) {
    keys.push(`sid:${sid}::${iso}`);
  } else if (title) {
    keys.push(`title:${title}::${iso}`);
  }
  return keys;
};

export const syncActivePassesSnapshot = (userId: string | null | undefined, reservas: RealtimePassRow[]): Set<string> => {
  const uid = String(userId || '').trim();
  if (!uid) return new Set<string>();
  const activeRows = (Array.isArray(reservas) ? reservas : []).filter((r) => {
    const est = String(r?.estado || 'confirmada').trim().toLowerCase();
    return !INACTIVE_PASS_STATES.has(est);
  });
  const nextSet = new Set<string>();
  activeRows.forEach((r) => {
    extractReservationMatchKeys(r).forEach((k) => nextSet.add(k));
  });
  const prevSet = _memoryActiveKeysByUser.get(uid);
  _memoryActiveReservasByUser.set(uid, activeRows);
  _memoryActiveKeysByUser.set(uid, nextSet);
  const changed = !prevSet || prevSet.size !== nextSet.size || Array.from(nextSet).some((k) => !prevSet.has(k));
  if (changed) emitPassesSnapshotChanged();
  return nextSet;
};

export const registerOptimisticActivePass = (
  userId: string | null | undefined,
  ev: RealtimePassRow | null | undefined,
  dateIso?: string
): void => {
  const uid = String(userId || '').trim();
  if (!uid || !ev) return;
  const prevRows = _memoryActiveReservasByUser.get(uid) || [];
  const iso = extractIsoDate(dateIso || ev.fecha_seleccionada || ev.fecha_proyectada || ev.fecha_clase || ev.fecha);
  const optimisticRow: RealtimePassRow = {
    id: `optimistic-${ev.id || Date.now()}`,
    usuario_id: uid,
    servicio_id: ev.servicio_id || ev.id || null,
    nombre_servicio: ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo || ev.servicio || '',
    fecha_clase: iso,
    estado: 'confirmada',
  };
  syncActivePassesSnapshot(uid, [optimisticRow, ...prevRows]);
};

export const getActivePassKeysSnapshotSync = (userId?: string | null): Set<string> => {
  const uid = String(userId || '').trim();
  if (!uid) return new Set<string>();
  return _memoryActiveKeysByUser.get(uid) || new Set<string>();
};

export const getActivePassesListSnapshotSync = (userId?: string | null): RealtimePassRow[] | undefined => {
  const uid = String(userId || '').trim();
  if (!uid) return undefined;
  return _memoryActiveReservasByUser.get(uid);
};

export const hasActivePassForEventSync = (
  userId: string | null | undefined,
  ev: RealtimePassRow | null | undefined,
  targetDateIso?: string,
  keysOverride?: Set<string>
): boolean => {
  const uid = String(userId || '').trim();
  if (!uid || !ev) return false;
  const set = keysOverride || _memoryActiveKeysByUser.get(uid);
  if (!set || set.size === 0) return false;
  const sid = String(ev.id || ev.servicio_id || '').trim();
  const title = normalizePassTitle(ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo || ev.servicio);
  const iso = extractIsoDate(targetDateIso || ev.fecha_seleccionada || ev.fecha_proyectada || ev.fecha_clase || ev.fecha);
  if (!iso) return false;
  if (sid) return set.has(`sid:${sid}::${iso}`);
  return Boolean(title) && set.has(`title:${title}::${iso}`);
};

export const invalidateBovedaPassesCache = async (userId?: string | null): Promise<void> => {
  const uid = String(userId || '').trim();
  const keysToRemove = [BOVEDA_PASSES_CACHE_KEY];
  if (uid) keysToRemove.push(`${BOVEDA_PASSES_CACHE_KEY}_${uid}`);
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage) {
    keysToRemove.forEach((k) => {
      try { window.localStorage.removeItem(k); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    });
  }
  await AsyncStorage.multiRemove(keysToRemove).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
  emitPassesSnapshotChanged();
};

export const subscribeBovedaPassesSnapshot = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
  _snapshotListeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    _snapshotListeners.delete(listener);
  };
};

export const subscribeUserReservasRealtimeAndFocus = (
  userId: string | null | undefined,
  onRevalidate: () => void
): (() => void) => {
  const uid = String(userId || '').trim();
  if (!uid) return () => {};

  let lastTriggerTs = 0;
  let qrChanSeq = 0;
  const triggerRefresh = (forceInvalidate = false) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (!forceInvalidate && now - lastTriggerTs < 1500) return;
    lastTriggerTs = now;
    if (forceInvalidate) {
      void invalidateBovedaPassesCache(uid);
    }
    onRevalidate();
  };

  const handleRealtimePayload = () => {
    triggerRefresh(true);
  };

  const channelName = `kinetic-reservas-sync-${uid}-${++qrChanSeq}-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;
  const channel = supabase
    .channel(channelName)
    .on(
      'postgres_changes',
      { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'reservas', filter: `atleta_id=eq.${uid}` },
      handleRealtimePayload
    )
    .on(
      'postgres_changes',
      { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'reservas', filter: `usuario_id=eq.${uid}` },
      handleRealtimePayload
    )
    .subscribe();

  const onWebFocus = () => triggerRefresh(false);
  const onWebVisChange = () => {
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
      triggerRefresh(false);
    }
  };

  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.addEventListener('focus', onWebFocus);
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onWebVisChange);
    }
  }

  const appStateSub =
    Platform.OS !== 'web'
      ? AppState.addEventListener('change', (nextState) => {
          if (nextState === 'active') triggerRefresh(false);
        })
      : null;

  return () => {
    try { supabase.removeChannel(channel); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.removeEventListener('focus', onWebFocus);
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
        document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onWebVisChange);
      }
    }
    appStateSub?.remove?.();
  };
};

const _activeWalletPurchaseUsers = new Set<string>();

export function evaluateAccountDeletionResponse(res: {
  data?: unknown;
  error?: unknown;
  status?: number;
}): { ok: true; deletedUserId: string } | { ok: false; errorMessage: string } {
  if (typeof res.status === 'number' && res.status >= 400) {
    return { ok: false, errorMessage: `HTTP_${res.status}` };
  }
  if (res.error) {
    const errObj = res.error as { code?: unknown; message?: unknown; context?: { status?: number } };
    const code = String(errObj?.code || '').trim();
    const msg = String(errObj?.message || '').trim();
    const ctxStatus = Number(errObj?.context?.status || 0);
    if (code === 'PGRST202') return { ok: false, errorMessage: 'ACCOUNT_DELETE_UNAVAILABLE_PGRST202' };
    if (ctxStatus >= 400) return { ok: false, errorMessage: msg || `HTTP_${ctxStatus}` };
    return { ok: false, errorMessage: msg || code || 'ACCOUNT_DELETE_FAILED' };
  }
  if (!res.data || typeof res.data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(res.data)) {
    return { ok: false, errorMessage: 'EMPTY_DELETION_RESPONSE' };
  }
  const body = res.data as { ok?: unknown; deletedUserId?: unknown; error?: unknown; code?: unknown };
  if (body.ok !== true) {
    const errTxt = String(body.code || body.error || 'ACCOUNT_DELETE_REJECTED').trim();
    return { ok: false, errorMessage: errTxt };
  }
  const deletedUserId = typeof body.deletedUserId === 'string' ? body.deletedUserId.trim() : '';
  return { ok: true, deletedUserId };
}

export const bovedaService = {

  async fetchReservasAtleta(userId: string) {
    const rows = await bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers.fetchReservasAtleta(userId);
    const merged = await mergeLocalRatingsIntoReservas(rows);
    syncActivePassesSnapshot(userId, merged);
    return merged;
  },

  async obtenerTiempoServidorMs(): Promise<number> {
    try {
      const t0 = Date.now();
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_tiempo_servidor_ms');
      if (error || typeof data !== 'number') return Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
      return data + Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 2);
    } catch {
      return Date.now() + getLocalServerClockOffsetMs();
    }
  },

  cancelarReserva: cancelarReservaBoveda,
  cancelarReservaConRegla72h: cancelarReservaConRegla72hBoveda,

  async comprarMembresia(
    userId: string,
    cantidadInyectada: number,
    nivelNombre?: string,
    billetera: 'membresia' | 'topup' = 'membresia',
    allowDowngrade: boolean = true,
    stripePaymentId?: string | null,
    planIdHint?: string | null
  ) {
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    if (_activeWalletPurchaseUsers.has(userId)) {
      throw new Error(isEn ? 'Vault transaction in progress. Please wait a second.' : 'Operación de Bóveda en curso. Espera un segundo.');
    }
    _activeWalletPurchaseUsers.add(userId);
    try {
      const jwtCheck = await reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession(supabase, 3500);
      const authUid = String(jwtCheck.user?.id || '').trim();
      if (jwtCheck.status !== 'valid' || !authUid || authUid !== String(userId || '').trim()) {
        throw new Error(isEn ? 'Cloud session expired. Sign in again to continue.' : 'Tu sesión en la nube expiró. Inicia sesión de nuevo para continuar.');
      }
      const { data: curRows } = await supabase.from('perfiles').select('nivel').eq('id', authUid).limit(1);
      const curPerf = Array.isArray(curRows) ? curRows[0] : curRows;
      const canonical = resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan({
        tipo: billetera,
        planId: planIdHint || (billetera === 'topup' ? `topup_${Math.round(Number(cantidadInyectada) || 0)}` : nivelNombre),
        targetLevel: nivelNombre,
        currentLevel: curPerf?.nivel,
      });
      if (billetera === 'membresia' && canonical.isDowngrade) {
        if (!allowDowngrade) {
          throw new Error(isEn ? 'Tier downgrade is not allowed in this flow.' : 'El descenso de nivel no está permitido en esta operación.');
        }
        const { error: downRpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('cambiar_nivel_downgrade_propio', {
          p_nuevo_nivel: canonical.canonicalLevel,
        });
        if (downRpcErr) {
          throw new Error(downRpcErr.message || (isEn ? 'Error updating membership tier.' : 'Error actualizando nivel de membresía.'));
        }
        return true;
      }
      if (billetera === 'membresia') {
        const curWeight = getLevelNumericWeight(curPerf?.nivel);
        const nextWeight = getLevelNumericWeight(canonical.canonicalLevel);
        if (curWeight >= 1 && nextWeight <= curWeight) {
          throw new Error('MEMBERSHIP_DOWNGRADE_OR_SAME_TIER_FORBIDDEN');
        }
      }
      assertValidKcMultipleOfTen(canonical.kcOtorgados, 'Membresia/TopUp');
      const cleanPayId = String(stripePaymentId || '').trim();
      if (cleanPayId && (_consumedPaymentIds.has(cleanPayId) || _inflightPaymentIds.has(cleanPayId))) {
        throw new Error(isEn ? 'Payment already processed (idempotency guard).' : 'Pago ya procesado (candado de idempotencia activo).');
      }
      if (cleanPayId) _inflightPaymentIds.add(cleanPayId);

      try {
        // [V15-01] Sandbox Pre-Stripe (Web y .APK sin pk_live_): acredita KC al instante vía RPC seguro
        if (!stripeService.isLiveModeEnabled()) {
          const { data: sandboxData, error: sandboxErr } = await supabase.rpc('procesar_compra_boveda_sandbox', {
            p_plan_id: canonical.canonicalPlanId,
            p_tipo: billetera,
          });
          if (sandboxErr) {
            throw new Error(
              sandboxErr.message ||
                (isEn ? 'Error crediting Kineticoins in Sandbox mode.' : 'Error acreditando Kineticoins en modo Sandbox.')
            );
          }
          if (cleanPayId) _consumedPaymentIds.add(cleanPayId);
          return sandboxData || true;
        }

        if (!isKineticDevRuntime()) {
          if (!/^pi_[A-Za-z0-9_]{8,}$/.test(cleanPayId)) {
            throw new Error(
              isEn ? 'Verified Stripe payment required to credit Kineticoins.' : 'Se requiere pago verificado con Stripe para acreditar Kineticoins.'
            );
          }
          const { data: verifyData, error: verifyErr } = await supabase.functions.invoke('stripe-verify-payment', {
            body: { paymentIntentId: cleanPayId, planId: canonical.canonicalPlanId, tipo: billetera },
          });
          if (verifyErr) {
            throw new Error(verifyErr.message || (isEn ? 'Error verifying Stripe payment.' : 'Error verificando pago con Stripe.'));
          }
          _consumedPaymentIds.add(cleanPayId);
          return verifyData || true;
        }

        const { data: rpcData4, error: rpcErr4 } = await supabase.rpc('inyectar_saldo_boveda', {
          p_user_id: authUid,
          p_cantidad: canonical.kcOtorgados,
          p_billetera: billetera,
          p_nuevo_nivel: billetera === 'membresia' ? canonical.canonicalLevel : null,
        });
        if (rpcErr4) {
          throw new Error(rpcErr4.message || (isEn ? 'Error crediting Kineticoins in Vault' : 'Error acreditando Kineticoins en bóveda'));
        }
        if (cleanPayId) _consumedPaymentIds.add(cleanPayId);
        return rpcData4 || true;
      } finally {
        if (cleanPayId) _inflightPaymentIds.delete(cleanPayId);
      }
    } finally {
      _activeWalletPurchaseUsers.delete(userId);
    }
  },

  async canjearCodigoReferido(userId: string, rawCode: string) {
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    const cleanCode = String(rawCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!cleanCode || cleanCode.length < 4) {
      throw new Error(isEn ? 'Enter a valid referral code.' : 'Ingresa un código de invitación válido.');
    }
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('canjear_codigo_referido', {
      p_user_id: userId,
      p_codigo: cleanCode,
    });
    if (error) {
      throw new Error(error.message || (isEn ? 'Could not validate referral code.' : 'No se pudo validar el código ingresado.'));
    }
    if (data && typeof data === 'object' && data.ok === false) {
      throw new Error(
        data.message || (isEn ? 'Invalid or already redeemed referral code.' : 'El código de invitación no es válido o ya fue utilizado.')
      );
    }
    return (
      data || {
        ok: true,
        message: isEn
          ? 'Referral code linked! When either of you activates a VIP Membership, both earn +100 KC (Rollover) & +50 XP.'
          : 'Código vinculado con éxito. Al activar cualquier Membresía VIP, ambos recibirán +100 KC (Rollover) y +50 XP.',
      }
    );
  },

  async eliminarCuentaDefinitiva(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    const cleanUid = String(userId || '').trim();
    if (!cleanUid) throw new Error(isEn ? 'Invalid user identifier.' : 'Identificador de usuario inválido.');
    const jwtCheck = await reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession(supabase, 4000);
    const callerEmail = String(jwtCheck.user?.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (jwtCheck.status !== 'valid' || !jwtCheck.user?.id || String(jwtCheck.user.id) !== cleanUid) {
      throw new Error(
        isEn ? 'Active authenticated session required to delete account.' : 'Se requiere una sesión activa autenticada para eliminar tu cuenta.'
      );
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('delete-own-account', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: { confirmDelete: true },
    });
    const verdict = evaluateAccountDeletionResponse({ data, error });
    if (!verdict.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        isEn
          ? `Account purge rejected: ${verdict.errorMessage}`
          : `No se pudo eliminar la cuenta (${verdict.errorMessage}).`
      );
    }

    await Promise.allSettled([
      purgeBiometricRefreshVaultAsync(),
      purgeGhostPassStorageKeys(cleanUid),
      clearPersistedKineticSessionExplicit(),
      AsyncStorage.multiRemove([
        '@kinetic_biometric_login_enabled_v1',
        '@kinetic_biometric_enabled_v1',
        '@kinetic_biometric_email_v1',
        '@kinetic_stories_24h_v4_cloud',
        `@kinetic_waiver_signed_v2_${cleanUid}`,
      ]),
    ]);
    if (callerEmail) {
      try {
        const loaded = await loadSanitizedLoginAccounts();
        await removeSanitizedLoginAccount(callerEmail, loaded.sanitizedMap);
        const rawSaved = await AsyncStorage.getItem(SAVED_ACCOUNTS_REAL_V3_KEY);
        if (rawSaved) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(rawSaved) as Record<string, unknown>;
          if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
            delete parsed[callerEmail];
            await AsyncStorage.setItem(SAVED_ACCOUNTS_REAL_V3_KEY, JSON.stringify(parsed));
          }
        }
        void DEVICE_VERIFIED_LOGINS_V4_KEY;
      } catch {}
    }
    await supabase.auth.signOut({ scope: 'local' }).catch(() => {});
    return true;
  },
};

export default bovedaService;

    FILE: src/services/kineticStripeWebBridge.ts

    // === src/services/kineticStripeWebBridge.ts === //

const STRIPE_JS_URL = 'https://js.stripe.com/v3/';
const REAL_PK_RE = /^pk_(test|live)_[A-Za-z0-9]{24,}$/;
const REAL_CLIENT_SECRET_RE = /^pi_[A-Za-z0-9]+_secret_[A-Za-z0-9]+$/;

export interface IStripeWebFactory {
  (publishableKey: string): {
    confirmCardPayment?: (clientSecret: string, data?: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<{ error?: { message?: string; code?: string }; paymentIntent?: { id?: string; status?: string } }>;
    confirmPayment?: (opts: { clientSecret?: string; confirmParams: { return_url: string }; redirect?: 'if_required' | 'always' }) => Promise<{ error?: { message?: string; code?: string }; paymentIntent?: { id?: string; status?: string } }>;
  };
}

export type StripeExecutionMode =
  | 'pre_stripe_sandbox'
  | 'stripe_live_web'
  | 'stripe_live_native'
  | 'blocked_missing_key';

const assertNoServerSecretInKey = (val: string): void => {
  const lower = val.toLowerCase();
  if (
    /^(sk_live_|sk_test_|rk_live_|rk_test_|whsec_)/i.test(val) ||
    lower.includes('service_role') ||
    lower.includes('gemini_api_key')
  ) {
    throw new Error('FATAL_SECURITY_GUARD: Private credential rejected in client Stripe bridge.');
  }
};

export const isGenuineStripePublishableKey = (rawKey: unknown): boolean => {
  const key = typeof rawKey === 'string' ? rawKey.trim() : '';
  if (!key) return false;
  assertNoServerSecretInKey(key);
  if (key.includes('<REDACTED_') || /KineticAppPro|placeholder|mock|demo/i.test(key)) {
    return false;
  }
  return REAL_PK_RE.test(key);
};

export const validateStripeClientSecretFormat = (rawSecret: unknown): boolean => {
  const secret = typeof rawSecret === 'string' ? rawSecret.trim() : '';
  return REAL_CLIENT_SECRET_RE.test(secret);
};

export function resolveStripeCheckoutExecutionMode(params: {
  allowPreStripeSandbox: boolean;
  publishableKey?: unknown;
  platformOs: string;
}): StripeExecutionMode {
  if (params.allowPreStripeSandbox) return 'pre_stripe_sandbox';
  if (!isGenuineStripePublishableKey(params.publishableKey)) return 'blocked_missing_key';
  return params.platformOs === 'web' ? 'stripe_live_web' : 'stripe_live_native';
}

export const sanitizeStripeWebReturnUrl = (rawReturnUrl?: string): string => {
  const fallback = 'https://kineticapp.com.mx/boveda';
  const candidate = String(rawReturnUrl || '').trim();
  if (!candidate) return fallback;
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(candidate);
    if (
      parsed.protocol === 'https:' &&
      (parsed.hostname === 'kineticapp.com.mx' || parsed.hostname === 'www.kineticapp.com.mx')
    ) {
      return parsed.toString();
    }
    if (parsed.protocol === 'http:' && parsed.hostname === 'localhost') {
      return parsed.toString();
    }
    return fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
};

interface BrowserDocLike {
  querySelector?: (sel: string) => { getAttribute?: (name: string) => string | null; addEventListener?: (ev: string, fn: () => void) => void } | null;
  createElement?: (tag: string) => { src: string; async: boolean; crossOrigin?: string | null; onload: (() => void) | null; onerror: (() => void) | null };
  head?: { appendChild?: (el: unknown) => void };
}

let _stripeScriptPromise: Promise<IStripeWebFactory | null> | null = null;

export async function loadStripeJsV3Script(): Promise<IStripeWebFactory | null> {
  const g = globalThis as { window?: { Stripe?: IStripeWebFactory }; document?: BrowserDocLike };
  if (typeof g.window?.Stripe === 'function') return g.window.Stripe;
  if (!g.document?.createElement || !g.document.head?.appendChild) return null;
  if (_stripeScriptPromise) return _stripeScriptPromise;

  _stripeScriptPromise = new Promise<IStripeWebFactory | null>((resolve) => {
    try {
      const parsed = new URL(STRIPE_JS_URL);
      if (parsed.protocol !== 'https:' || parsed.hostname !== 'js.stripe.com') {
        resolve(null);
        return;
      }
      const existing = g.document?.querySelector?.('script[src^="https://js.stripe.com/v3"]');
      if (existing && typeof g.window?.Stripe === 'function') {
        resolve(g.window.Stripe);
        return;
      }
      const script = g.document!.createElement!('script');
      script.src = STRIPE_JS_URL;
      script.async = true;
      script.onload = () => resolve(typeof g.window?.Stripe === 'function' ? g.window.Stripe : null);
      script.onerror = () => {
        _stripeScriptPromise = null;
        resolve(null);
      };
      g.document!.head!.appendChild!(script);
    } catch {
      _stripeScriptPromise = null;
      resolve(null);
    }
  });
  return _stripeScriptPromise;
}

export async function confirmStripeWebPayment(params: {
  publishableKey: string;
  clientSecret: string;
  returnUrl?: string;
}): Promise<{ ok: boolean; paymentIntentId?: string; status?: string; errorMessage?: string; canceled?: boolean }> {
  if (!isGenuineStripePublishableKey(params.publishableKey)) {
    return { ok: false, errorMessage: 'Stripe publishable key is unavailable or invalid.' };
  }
  if (!validateStripeClientSecretFormat(params.clientSecret)) {
    return { ok: false, errorMessage: 'Stripe bank authorization token format is invalid.' };
  }
  const StripeFactory = await loadStripeJsV3Script();
  if (!StripeFactory) {
    return { ok: false, errorMessage: 'Unable to load secure Stripe Web SDK (https://js.stripe.com/v3/).' };
  }
  const stripe = StripeFactory(params.publishableKey.trim());
  const safeReturnUrl = sanitizeStripeWebReturnUrl(params.returnUrl);
  if (typeof stripe?.confirmPayment === 'function') {
    const res = await stripe.confirmPayment({
      clientSecret: params.clientSecret.trim(),
      confirmParams: { return_url: safeReturnUrl },
      redirect: 'if_required',
    });
    if (res?.error) {
      const isCancel = res.error.code === 'canceled' || /cancel/i.test(String(res.error.message || ''));
      return { ok: false, canceled: isCancel, errorMessage: res.error.message || 'Stripe payment declined.' };
    }
    return { ok: true, paymentIntentId: res?.paymentIntent?.id, status: res?.paymentIntent?.status || 'succeeded' };
  }
  if (typeof stripe?.confirmCardPayment === 'function') {
    const res = await stripe.confirmCardPayment(params.clientSecret.trim());
    if (res?.error) {
      return { ok: false, errorMessage: res.error.message || 'Stripe card authorization declined.' };
    }
    return { ok: true, paymentIntentId: res?.paymentIntent?.id, status: res?.paymentIntent?.status || 'succeeded' };
  }
  return { ok: false, errorMessage: 'Stripe Web payment confirmation API is unavailable.' };
}

    FILE: src/services/stripeService.ts

    // === src/services/stripeService.ts === //
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { assertValidKcMultipleOfTen, resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan } from './kineticFintechSecurityGuards';
import { reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession } from './kineticSessionReconciler';
import {
  confirmStripeWebPayment,
  isGenuineStripePublishableKey,
  resolveStripeCheckoutExecutionMode,
  validateStripeClientSecretFormat,
} from './kineticStripeWebBridge';
import { isKineticDevRuntime } from './kineticVaultCrypto';
import { getNativeStripeSdk } from './nativeStripeBridge';

const _inFlightStripeUsers = new Set<string>();
let _cachedSandboxFlag: { value: boolean; fetchedAt: number } | null = null;
const SANDBOX_FLAG_TTL_MS = 60_000;

const assertNoSecretInPublicEnv = (val?: string) => {
  const v = String(val || '').trim();
  if (/^(sk_live_|sk_test_|rk_live_|rk_test_|whsec_)/i.test(v) || v.includes('"service_role"')) {
    throw new Error('FATAL_SECURITY_GUARD: Llave secreta detectada en variable EXPO_PUBLIC_*.');
  }
};

assertNoSecretInPublicEnv(process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);
assertNoSecretInPublicEnv(process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY);

const STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY || '';

export interface CheckoutItemPayload {
  userId: string;
  tipo: 'membresia' | 'topup';
  planId: string;
  nombre: string;
  kcOtorgados: number;
  precioMxn: number;
}

const getLevelWeight = (level?: string): number => {
  const l = String(level || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (l.includes('leyenda') || l.includes('black')) return 5;
  if (l.includes('platinum')) return 4;
  if (l.includes('gold')) return 3;
  if (l.includes('silver')) return 2;
  if (l.includes('bronze')) return 1;
  return 0;
};

export const stripeService = {
  getPublishableKey(): string {
    const key = (process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY || STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY || '').trim();
    assertNoSecretInPublicEnv(key);
    return key;
  },

  getMerchantIdentifier(): string {
    return 'merchant.com.kineticapp.sas';
  },

  getUrlScheme(): string {
    return 'kinetic';
  },

  async fetchAllowPreStripeSandbox(forceRefresh = false): Promise<boolean> {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (!forceRefresh && _cachedSandboxFlag && now - _cachedSandboxFlag.fetchedAt < SANDBOX_FLAG_TTL_MS) {
      return _cachedSandboxFlag.value;
    }
    try {
      const { data: cfgRow, error: cfgErr } = await supabase
        .from('app_config')
        .select('bool_value')
        .eq('config_key', 'allow_pre_stripe_sandbox')
        .maybeSingle();
      if (!cfgErr && cfgRow && typeof (cfgRow as { bool_value?: unknown }).bool_value === 'boolean') {
        const val = Boolean((cfgRow as { bool_value?: boolean }).bool_value);
        _cachedSandboxFlag = { value: val, fetchedAt: now };
        return val;
      }
      const { data: flagRow } = await supabase
        .from('kinetic_system_flags')
        .select('enabled')
        .eq('flag_key', 'allow_pre_stripe_sandbox')
        .maybeSingle();
      if (flagRow && typeof (flagRow as { enabled?: unknown }).enabled === 'boolean') {
        const val = Boolean((flagRow as { enabled?: boolean }).enabled);
        _cachedSandboxFlag = { value: val, fetchedAt: now };
        return val;
      }
      return true;
    } catch {
      return true;
    }
  },

  isLiveModeEnabled(): boolean {
    const k = this.getPublishableKey();
    if (!isGenuineStripePublishableKey(k)) return false;
    return k.startsWith('pk_live_') || (isKineticDevRuntime() && k.startsWith('pk_test_'));
  },

  async createPaymentIntent(
    payload: CheckoutItemPayload
  ): Promise<{ clientSecret?: string; client_secret?: string; id?: string; status?: string; mode?: string; allow_pre_stripe_sandbox?: boolean }> {
    const uid = String(payload?.userId || '').trim();
    if (!uid) throw new Error('Usuario requerido para iniciar checkout Stripe.');
    if (_inFlightStripeUsers.has(uid)) throw new Error('Operación de pago ya en curso.');
    _inFlightStripeUsers.add(uid);
    try {
      const liveMode = this.isLiveModeEnabled();
      let resolvedCurrentLevel: string | null = null;
      if (liveMode || !isKineticDevRuntime()) {
        const jwtCheck = await reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession(supabase, 3500);
        const authUid = String(jwtCheck.user?.id || '').trim();
        if (jwtCheck.status !== 'valid' || !authUid || authUid !== uid) {
          throw new Error('Sesión autenticada requerida para iniciar checkout Stripe.');
        }
        const { data: perfilRow } = await supabase.from('perfiles').select('nivel').eq('id', authUid).maybeSingle();
        resolvedCurrentLevel = (perfilRow as { nivel?: string | null } | null)?.nivel ?? null;
      }
      const canonical = resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan({
        tipo: payload.tipo,
        planId: payload.planId,
        targetLevel: payload.nombre || payload.planId,
        currentLevel: resolvedCurrentLevel,
      });
      assertValidKcMultipleOfTen(canonical.kcOtorgados, 'StripeCheckout');
      if (!liveMode) {
        const timestamp = Date.now();
        const secret = 'sandbox_secret_' + timestamp;
        return { clientSecret: secret, client_secret: secret, id: 'sandbox_pre_stripe_' + timestamp, status: 'succeeded', mode: 'pre_stripe_sandbox', allow_pre_stripe_sandbox: true };
      }
      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('stripe-create-payment', {
        body: { planId: canonical.canonicalPlanId, tipo: payload.tipo },
      });
      if (error) throw error;
      return (data || {}) as { clientSecret?: string; client_secret?: string; id?: string; status?: string; mode?: string; allow_pre_stripe_sandbox?: boolean };
    } finally {
      _inFlightStripeUsers.delete(uid);
    }
  },

  async registrarTransaccionEnBoveda(payload: CheckoutItemPayload, stripePaymentId?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!isKineticDevRuntime()) {
      throw new Error('La acreditación directa desde cliente está bloqueada en producción.');
    }
    if (!payload?.userId) {
      throw new Error('Identificador de usuario requerido para acreditar la Bóveda.');
    }

    const kcInyectados = Math.max(0, Number(payload.kcOtorgados) || 0);
    const isTopup = payload.tipo === 'topup';
    const targetWallet: 'membresia' | 'topup' = isTopup ? 'topup' : 'membresia';

    let nextTier: string | null = null;
    if (!isTopup && payload.nombre) {
      const { data: perfilActual } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles')
        .select('nivel')
        .eq('id', payload.userId)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (getLevelWeight(payload.nombre) >= getLevelWeight(perfilActual?.nivel)) {
        nextTier = payload.nombre;
      }
    }

    // 1. Inyección atómica de Kineticoins vía RPC SECURITY DEFINER (Cumplimiento RLS / ACID)
    if (kcInyectados > 0 || nextTier) {
      const { error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('inyectar_saldo_boveda', {
        p_user_id: payload.userId,
        p_cantidad: kcInyectados,
        p_billetera: targetWallet,
        p_nuevo_nivel: nextTier,
      });

      if (rpcErr) throw new Error(`Error acreditando Bóveda: ${rpcErr.message}`);
    }

    // 2. Registramos la auditoría financiera en transacciones_pagos
    try {
      const folioAuditoria = stripePaymentId || `sim_stripe_${Date.now()}`;

      await supabase.from('transacciones_pagos').insert({
        usuario_id: payload.userId,
        tipo_operacion: payload.tipo,
        paquete_id: payload.planId,
        monto_mxn: payload.precioMxn,
        kc_inyectados: kcInyectados,
        stripe_payment_intent_id: folioAuditoria,
        estado: 'completado',
      });
    } catch {
      // Silencioso si aún no se ha creado la tabla opcional de bitácora transaccional
    }

    return true;
  },

  async processCheckout(payload: CheckoutItemPayload): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    canceled?: boolean;
    error?: string;
    paymentId?: string;
  }> {
    const uid = String(payload?.userId || '').trim();
    if (!uid) return { success: false, error: 'Usuario requerido.' };
    assertValidKcMultipleOfTen(Number(payload?.kcOtorgados), 'StripeCheckout');
    const allowSandbox = await this.fetchAllowPreStripeSandbox();
    const execMode = resolveStripeCheckoutExecutionMode({
      allowPreStripeSandbox: allowSandbox,
      publishableKey: this.getPublishableKey(),
      platformOs: typeof window !== 'undefined' && Boolean(window.document) ? 'web' : 'native',
    });
    if (execMode === 'pre_stripe_sandbox') {
      return { success: true, paymentId: `sandbox_pre_stripe_${Date.now()}` };
    }
    if (execMode === 'blocked_missing_key') {
      return { success: false, error: 'Pasarela Stripe Live requerida con llave pública válida.' };
    }

    try {
      const intentData = await this.createPaymentIntent(payload);
      if (intentData?.mode === 'pre_stripe_sandbox' || intentData?.allow_pre_stripe_sandbox) {
        return { success: true, paymentId: String(intentData?.id || `sandbox_pre_stripe_${Date.now()}`) };
      }
      const clientSecret = String(intentData?.clientSecret || intentData?.client_secret || '');
      const paymentId = String(intentData?.id || '').trim();
      if (!validateStripeClientSecretFormat(clientSecret)) {
        return { success: false, error: 'No se recibió clientSecret válido de Stripe.' };
      }
      if (!/^pi_[A-Za-z0-9_]{8,}$/.test(paymentId)) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Identificador PaymentIntent inválido de Stripe.' };
      }

      if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.document) {
        const stripeSdk = getNativeStripeSdk();
        if (!stripeSdk?.initPaymentSheet || !stripeSdk?.presentPaymentSheet) {
          return { success: false, error: 'SDK nativo de Stripe no disponible.' };
        }
        const initRes = await stripeSdk.initPaymentSheet({
          paymentIntentClientSecret: clientSecret,
          merchantDisplayName: 'KineticApp S.A.S.',
          applePay: { merchantCountryCode: 'MX' },
          googlePay: { merchantCountryCode: 'MX', testEnv: false },
          style: 'alwaysDark',
        });
        if (initRes.error) return { success: false, error: initRes.error.message };

        const presentRes = await stripeSdk.presentPaymentSheet();
        if (presentRes.error) {
          if (presentRes.error.code === 'Canceled' || presentRes.error.message?.toLowerCase().includes('cancel')) {
            return { success: false, canceled: true };
          }
          return { success: false, error: presentRes.error.message };
        }
        return { success: true, paymentId };
      }

      if (intentData?.status === 'succeeded') {
        return { success: true, paymentId };
      }
      const webRes = await confirmStripeWebPayment({
        publishableKey: this.getPublishableKey(),
        clientSecret,
        returnUrl: 'https://kineticapp.com.mx/boveda',
      });
      if (!webRes.ok) {
        return { success: false, canceled: webRes.canceled, error: webRes.errorMessage || 'Pago Web Stripe no confirmado.' };
      }
      return { success: true, paymentId: webRes.paymentIntentId || paymentId };
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : '';
      return { success: false, error: msg || 'Error en el procesamiento de Stripe.' };
    }
  },
};

import { StyleSheet } from 'react-native';

export const CFDI_USES_CATALOG = [
  { code: 'G03', es: 'G03 - Gastos en general', en: 'G03 - General expenses' },
  { code: 'D01', es: 'D01 - Honorarios médicos y hospitalarios', en: 'D01 - Medical, dental & hospital fees' },
  { code: 'S01', es: 'S01 - Sin efectos fiscales', en: 'S01 - No tax effects' },

export const SAT_REGIMES_CATALOG = [
  { code: '626', es: '626 - Régimen Simplificado de Confianza (RESICO)', en: '626 - Simplified Trust Regime (RESICO)' },
  { code: '612', es: '612 - Persona Física con Actividad Empresarial', en: '612 - Individual w/ Business & Professional Activity' },

  { code: '616', es: '616 - Sin obligaciones fiscales', en: '616 - Without Tax Obligations' },
] as const;

export const getCfdiUseEntry = (rawVal?: string) => {
  const clean = String(rawVal || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  return (
    CFDI_USES_CATALOG.find(
      (item) => clean === item.code || clean.startsWith(`${item.code} `) || clean.startsWith(`${item.code}-`),
    ) || CFDI_USES_CATALOG[0]
  );
};

export const getSatRegimeEntry = (rawVal?: string) => {
  const clean = String(rawVal || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!clean) return null;
  return SAT_REGIMES_CATALOG.find((item) => clean === item.code || clean.startsWith(item.code)) || null;
};

const OMISO_REGIME_TOKENS = new Set([
  'sin_rfc_omiso',
  'sin_rfc',
  'sin rfc',
  'omiso',
  'null',
  'undefined',
  'n/a',
  'na',
]);

export const formatSatRegimenLabel = (code?: string | null, isEn = false): string => {
  const raw = String(code || '').trim();
  const entry = getSatRegimeEntry(raw);
  if (entry) return isEn ? entry.en : entry.es;

  const fallbackOmiso = isEn
    ? 'No Tax ID on file (Withholding 36.0%)'
    : 'Sin RFC registrado (Retención 36.0%)';
  const low = raw.toLowerCase();
  if (low === 'persona_moral') {
    return isEn ? 'Corporate Entity (0% Tax)' : 'P. Moral (0% Ret.)';
  }
  if (low === 'persona_fisica_con_rfc' || low === 'persona_fisica') {
    return isEn ? 'Individual w/ RFC (2.1%/8%)' : 'P. Física con RFC (2.1%/8%)';
  }
  if (!low || OMISO_REGIME_TOKENS.has(low) || low.includes('sin_rfc_omiso') || low.includes('sin_rfc') || low === 'omiso') {
    return fallbackOmiso;
  }
  return raw.replace(/sin_rfc_omiso/gi, fallbackOmiso);
};

    import { StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { COLORS, RADIUS, SPACING } from './globalStyles';
import { sanitizeVerifiedDisplayName } from '../utils/kineticDisplayNamePureLeaf';
import { formatKineticDate, getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';

export const isKineticMasterAdmin = (
  email?: string | null,
  rol?: string | null,
  tipo?: string | null,
): boolean => {
  const mail = String(email || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const r = String(rol || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const t = String(tipo || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  return (
    mail === 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx' ||
    mail === 'administrador@kinetic.com' ||
    mail === 'kinetic@kinetic.com' ||
    mail === 'admin@kinetic.com' ||
    r === 'admin' ||
    r === 'fundador' ||
    t === 'admin' ||
    t === 'fundador'
  );
};

export const resolveOfficialKineticName = (
  rawName?: string | null,
  isAdmin?: boolean,
  fallback = 'Socio Afiliado',
): string =>
  sanitizeVerifiedDisplayName(rawName, {
    isOfficial: Boolean(isAdmin),
    isEn: getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en',
    fallback,
  });

export const formatPassDate = (iso?: string, langOverride?: string) => {
  const lang = langOverride === 'en' || langOverride === 'es' ? langOverride : getActiveKineticLanguage();
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  if (!iso) return isEn ? 'Today' : 'Hoy';
  const cleanIso = String(iso).trim();
  const parts = cleanIso.split('T')[0].split(' ')[0].split('-').map(Number);
  if (
    parts.length === 3 &&
    parts.every((n) => Number.isFinite(n)) &&
    parts[0] > 1900 &&
    parts[1] >= 1 &&
    parts[1] <= 12 &&
    parts[2] >= 1 &&
    parts[2] <= 31
  ) {
    const [y, m, d] = parts;
    const localDate = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
    if (!Number.isNaN(localDate.getTime())) {
      const weekdays = isEn
        ? ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat']
        : ['Dom', 'Lun', 'Mar', 'Mié', 'Jue', 'Vie', 'Sáb'];
      const months = isEn
        ? ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec']
        : ['Ene', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Abr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Ago', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dic'];
      return isEn
        ? `${weekdays[localDate.getDay()]}, ${months[m - 1]} ${d}`
        : `${weekdays[localDate.getDay()]} ${d} ${months[m - 1]}`;
    }
  }
  return formatKineticDate(cleanIso, lang, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }) || cleanIso;
};

interface IDisplayRoleInputObj {
  email?: string | null;
  rol?: string | null;
  tipo?: string | null;
  tipo_cuenta?: string | null;
  language?: string | null;
  isEn?: boolean;
  user?: { email?: string | null } | null;
  profile?: { rol?: string | null; tipo_cuenta?: string | null } | null;
}

export const getDisplayRole = (
  rolOrEmailOrObj?: string | IDisplayRoleInputObj | null,
  tipoOrProfile?: string | IDisplayRoleInputObj | boolean | null,
  emailOrLang?: string | boolean | null,
  isEnOrLang?: boolean | string,
) => {
  let rol = '';
  let tipo = '';
  let email = '';
  let langParam: boolean | string | null | undefined = isEnOrLang;

  if (rolOrEmailOrObj && typeof rolOrEmailOrObj === 'object') {
    email = String(rolOrEmailOrObj.email || rolOrEmailOrObj.user?.email || '');
    rol = String(rolOrEmailOrObj.rol || rolOrEmailOrObj.profile?.rol || '');
    tipo = String(rolOrEmailOrObj.tipo || rolOrEmailOrObj.tipo_cuenta || rolOrEmailOrObj.profile?.tipo_cuenta || '');
    langParam = rolOrEmailOrObj.language ?? rolOrEmailOrObj.isEn ?? (typeof tipoOrProfile === 'object' ? undefined : tipoOrProfile);
  } else if (tipoOrProfile && typeof tipoOrProfile === 'object') {
    email = String(rolOrEmailOrObj || tipoOrProfile.email || '');
    rol = String(tipoOrProfile.rol || '');
    tipo = String(tipoOrProfile.tipo_cuenta || tipoOrProfile.tipo || '');
    langParam = emailOrLang ?? isEnOrLang;
  } else {
    const firstStr = String(rolOrEmailOrObj || '').trim();
    if (firstStr.includes('@') && !emailOrLang) {
      email = firstStr;
      langParam = (typeof tipoOrProfile === 'string' || typeof tipoOrProfile === 'boolean' ? tipoOrProfile : undefined) ?? isEnOrLang;
    } else {
      rol = firstStr;
      tipo = String(tipoOrProfile || '').trim();
      email = String(emailOrLang || '').trim();
    }
  }

  const isEn =
    typeof langParam === 'boolean'
      ? langParam
      : typeof langParam === 'string'
      ? langParam.toLowerCase().startsWith('en')
      : getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';

  if (isKineticMasterAdmin(email, rol, tipo)) return isEn ? 'FOUNDER / CEO' : 'FUNDADOR / CEO';
  const r = String(rol || tipo || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (r.includes('especialista')) return isEn ? 'VERIFIED SPECIALIST' : 'ESPECIALISTA VERIFICADO';
  if (['socio', 'negocio', 'estadio', 'club', 'afiliado', 'partner b2b'].includes(r)) {
    return isEn ? 'B2B AFFILIATE PARTNER' : 'SOCIO AFILIADO B2B';
  }
  return isEn ? 'VERIFIED ATHLETE' : 'ATLETA VERIFICADO';
};
